Chapter 1071 - Tracked Down

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Anna glanced at the people by the shop entrance. She lightly tapped the
bottom of Olivia's foot, and the latter walked outside.

Back on the lifeless street, Olivia sounded a bit reluctant as she said,
"Three days ago, they killed a family of three and threw their bodies
into the sea. The reason for the killing was to obtain the daughter's
moving doll."

"A robbery with homicide? So they're a group of robbers?" Anna guessed.

"I wouldn't say it's homicide. They accepted a commission, so it was
premeditated."

Upon hearing that, Jackal instantly deduced their identity and said,
"Those guys are the so-called hunters. They're people who do dirty work.
They specifically target isolated individuals in our circles. Of course,
they accept commissions."

"So they're like mercenaries?"

"Something like that. Anyone with connections can hire them. Of course,
don't expect them to be extremely loyal. In fact, some just take the
deposit and run. It's a mixed crowd with all sorts of people.

"When I warned you about some people earlier, I was mostly talking about
those hunters. Compared to the IMF's shadows, they are more dangerous.
After all, the IMF will only either contain you or try to make you join
their side.

"They aren't going to rob and murder you."

Beneath the ground, Anna fell into deep contemplation before asking,
"Does the IMF not care about them at all?"

"They're like stubborn flies around Subterranean London. It is simply
impossible to drive them away. They're also targeting representatives of
others. You know the situation here. Most people won't come here to buy
and sell things themselves, and it gives those hunters an opportunity to
obtain some profits.

"They're basically here with the connivance of the IMF. The IMF
tolerates their existence because they believe that there's no need to
intervene in matters that don't affect ordinary civilians.

"And since those people are fighting each other like wild dogs, the IMF
has fewer problems to handle."

Anna quietly took note of that fact. These hunters were numerous, and
their relationships were quite complex, so if Anna somehow became a
hunter in Subterranean London, not only would she be able to receive
information quickly, but she would also be able to use her identity as
an excellent camouflage.

Anna carefully considered the idea, and the more she thought about it,
the more feasible it became.

As a hunter, there would be no issues even if the IMF's shadows and
agents paid attention to her. After all, a group of greedy hunters
motivated by money couldn't possibly be connected to the deceased 315.

While Anna was refining the details of that plan in her mind, Olivia
suddenly stopped and said, "Wait, that's my classmate! Why would she
come to a dangerous place like this?"

Olivia's eyes behind the sunglasses cast a worried gaze at a young woman
dressed like her. "I'm familiar with the memories in her mind. That's
definitely Sariah!"

Anna couldn't care less about why Olivia's classmate was here. She
tapped on the bottom of Olivia's foot and said, "Don't cause any
trouble. Keep walking. Jackal, is there a place here that sells
information?"

"Yes." Jackal nodded. "See that newsstand ahead? Their newspapers
contain most of the news within our world, but they're quite expensive—a
thousand dollars each."

Anna led the group toward the newsstand. Now that she thought about it,
Anna had no information network to relay information to her, and it was
the reason why she had no idea about what was going on out there.

The newsstand before the group was exactly what Anna urgently needed, so
of course, she had to check it out for herself.

Upon paying for a copy, Jackal received a newspaper. The front page
featured an eye-catching banner headline that said, "Our End Is Near!
Move Now or Everyone Will Die!"

Hello, readers. I believe that once this issue is released, you must be
aware of how to fuse with an Anomaly, but that is not necessarily a good
thing! Just think about it! Who has the most Anomalies on hand? The IMF!

According to my reliable sources, the IMF has started training new
Mobile Task Forces where its members have fused with an Anomaly. Once
their training is complete, who do you think they will deal with? It's
none other than us!

We move now and find a way out of this predicament! I'm sure you've had
enough of eking out an existence in the dark. They can fuse with
Anomalies, but we can do it, too! We're at the same starting line, and
this is a rare opportunity!

Who decided that Anomalies must stay hidden in the dark? We must unite
and establish our very own empire! We can definitely live proudly in the
sun with our heads held high!

Dogs of the IMF! I know you're reading this—you people are pieces of
shit! You say we're heinous criminals, but who do you think you are?
Heroes? All of you are a bunch of hypocritical scums. Can you truly say
that you're clean? How about we talk about who allowed you to conduct
human experiments?

Your glory days are over! Once we've overthrown you, I'll kick your
leaders' heads around like they're balls!

Contributor: Anonymous

Anna's lips curved slightly upward upon hearing about the article's
contents. That guy sure is full of himself.

The page was turned, and another article was found. The article stated
that an IMF site in South Africa was breached by certain individuals,
and the contained Anomalies had disappeared into thin air.

There was another article above that article, and it contained news
about her encounter with the vampires.

The newspaper was expensive, but it contained authentic news. This place
truly had information that couldn't be found on the Internet.

"It looks like things are becoming really chaotic. In the past, no one
was daring enough to provoke the IMF like this," Jackal remarked while
reading the newspaper's contents to Anna.

"It's not chaotic enough; we need more chaos," Anna remarked. She was
capable of transforming humans into zealous followers of God Fhtagn, but
the ritual could only be done in the Indian Ocean.

The IMF's satellites were certainly monitoring the Indian Ocean around
the clock, which meant visiting the Indian Ocean to do a ritual was
tantamount to suicide.

However, it would be a different story once things became so chaotic
that the IMF couldn't spare any resources to monitor the Indian Ocean.
By then, Anna would take advantage of that opportunity to rapidly expand
the number of her subordinates.

Gao Zhiming standing still like a puppet curiously watched the crowd as
if he were a security guard. His right hand would occasionally graze the
holster at his waist, and he looked quite eager to try it out.

"I have a gun now. I can shoot those who dare to harm my big sister,"
Gao Zhiming muttered to himself, and his face beneath the hood revealed
a tinge of ruthlessness.

Just then, Anna frowned. Someone's gaze had just swept across her.

This was strange. She was hidden underground, but someone actually saw
her?

Anna's heart raced as she immediately declared, "Change of plans. We
need to leave now."

The others were confused about why they needed to leave so soon when
they had just arrived, but no one objected.

Anna paid close attention to their surroundings and quickly spotted the
one tracking her down.

"Olivia, don't turn around. Check the memories of the person thirty
meters behind us. I want to know where they're from," Anna ordered.

"Miss Anna, I can't see anything," Olivia replied, "It's like they don't
have any memories at all."

Chapter 1072 - A Familiar Face

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

The footsteps of Gao Zhiming and his companions echoed through the
spacious subway tunnel as they slowly made their way out of the
underground city.

No one spoke, but everyone had quietly drawn their weapons.

According to Olivia, the person who had been following them on the
street was now behind them in the darkness, and they were getting closer
and closer.

Their identity and motive, as well as if they had any accomplices, were
unknown to Gao Zhiming and the others. Thus, a tense air hung above them
within the dark tunnel.

Regardless of whether the one following them was aware of her true
identity or not, Anna had already decided to strike first and ask
questions later.

Swoosh!

Anna thrust her hands skyward, grabbing the person's foot. Immediately
afterward, green corrosive flames manifested and moved toward the
stranger's face.

Bang! Bang!

Two gunshots echoed as bullets flew toward the stranger. The stranger
ignored the bullets, allowing them to phase through their figure, but
they were clearly afraid of Anna's corrosive flames.

The stranger pulled a black cloth from their sleeve and covered the
flames with it.

Unfortunately, the green flames held and engulfed the cloth instead,
transforming it into a fireball.

Just as Anna's group was about to launch another round of attacks, the
stranger quickly raised a hand and removed the hood covering their face.

All attacks ceased immediately afterward.

Looking at the "face" being illuminated by the green flames, Gao Zhiming
excitedly ran over while shouting, "Uncle Mask! So you're okay?! Thank
goodness!"

The stranger turned out to be the clown. No wonder Olivia couldn't find
any memories within him. Without a brain, how could there be any
memories?

Anna rose slowly from beneath the ground, and she stared wide-eyed at
her former companion. She had never expected to encounter him under such
circumstances and in this place.

The clown placed one hand on his chest and bowed, greeting Anna in a
gentlemanly manner.

"Didn't the IMF capture you?" Anna asked in surprise.

"I was captured, but I escaped. If I could escape once, of course, I
could escape a second time," the clown replied by conjuring playing
cards with letters written on them.

Just then, footsteps echoed from the darkness in the distance. It seemed
that people were leaving Subterranean London.

"Let's go. This isn't the right place to talk. We'll go somewhere safe."

With that, they brought the clown with them to their lodgings, and the
clown recounted his experience to Anna's group.

"I was detained at a containment site on a deserted island somewhere in
the Atlantic. One day, people appeared out of nowhere and attacked the
site."

Recalling the recent news, Anna instantly discovered the identity of
those attackers. "It's a good thing you ran away immediately. Otherwise,
someone would have absorbed you."

"I escaped while they were fighting," the clown remarked. Then, he
tugged at the corners of his mouth, stretching it into a smile before
saying, "I knew you wouldn't die. And I was right."

He was genuinely happy to see that Anna was still alive.

After briefly rejoicing at her companion's return to her side, Anna
decided to ask more questions. "Tell me more about the situation back
then. Do you know what happened to the others before you were
contained?"

"There's not much to tell. They rushed to contain me, but I knew that
they were after me, so I hid your other fleshly body as well as Gao
Zhiming."

"I took Li Long with me and used him to draw their attention."

"What about Li Long? Did he get captured as well?"

The clown unexpectedly shook his head at that question. "I killed him
before I got captured. That guy's mind was too weak; I was afraid that
he would stab you in the back."

The words written on the playing cards sent chills down everyone's
spine, but Anna's gaze toward the clown softened.

The clown was extremely thoughtful—far more thoughtful than she thought.
For her sake, he not only acted as bait but had even considered the
possibility that Li Long could betray her during the interrogations.

Before all this, they shared a superior-subordinate relationship, but
now, they could be considered friends with whom they had overcome
life-or-death battles.

Anna rarely trusted anyone, and the clown was now one of those few
people.

"Thank you, Mask. Upon my return to the Subterranean Sea, I'll make sure
you'll get a share of whatever I have there."

"It is my honor."

While the two were basking in their heartwarming reunion, Gao Zhiming's
eyes were red, and his voice was choked with tears as he asked, "Uncle
Li Long is dead? Why did you kill him? He was a good person..."

Anna stared at him and patiently explained, "There are times when you'll
have to make a decision, and Mask made the right decision back then. And
you must remember that sometimes, what determines a person's fate isn't
whether they're a good person or not, but whether they have any value."

Gao Zhiming stood frozen and in a daze. He felt that something wasn't
exactly right, but he couldn't pinpoint it.

I hope my words won't turn him into a murderer when he grows up, Anna
thought, worried about Gao Zhiming's future. However, she remembered
something and added, Well, his memories will ultimately be deleted,
anyway.

With that, Anna was finally at ease.

After comforting Gao Zhiming, she turned to the clown and asked, "What
are you doing in Subterranean London? Aren't you afraid that the IMF's
shadows hidden among the crowd might end up seeing you?"

"It's because Tobba told me to meet him. I was passing through, so I
thought I'd look around to see if I could find anyone else."

"Tobba?" Anna was surprised upon hearing that.

"He managed to escape as well? Wait, could this be a trap created by the
IMF?"

"Probably not. If the IMF knew where I was, they could just send a
mobile task force to capture me rather than setting a trap."

Anna frowned deeply, carefully weighing the clown's words.

Unlike the clown, Tobba had never been useful to her since he was born.

Anna's death back then made her understand that even though Tobba would
survive until the Foundation descended into the Subterranean Sea, Tobba
couldn't necessarily help others survive along with him.

Since he wasn't helpful, why would she make any contact with him again?

"I advise you not to go there. The risk is too high, especially since
Tobba is completely useless. He's just going to be a burden.

"Anyway, since he believes that I'm dead, then we should let him keep
believing that I'm dead. Let him run around out there. It'd be great if
he can divert the IMF's attention for us."

However, Mask actually shook his head. "It appears as though he doesn't
have any special abilities, but he managed to free his mother from a
containment site without anyone noticing it.

"That's enough proof of his prowess. His special ability might be very
unstable, but I believe he's going to be helpful in the future.

"Moreover, he easily deduced my location, which is already quite
astonishing."

Upon hearing that, Anna thought, Could it be that his special ability
ended up awakening after that incident?

It was possible. If it was true, Tobba's foresight would certainly be
useful to her.

"Where are they?"

"In Norway."

Chapter 1073 - Tobba

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

In the end, Anna decided to accompany the clown to Tobba's location. She
was gambling on the possibility that he had finally awakened his ability
to foresee the future.

If he had yet to awaken that power, then it wouldn't be too late to
abandon the burden.

The scenery outside the window featured nothing but a vast expanse of
white, and it was all due to its close proximity to the Arctic Circle.
The mere sight of it alone was capable of making anyone feel cold.

"Since he managed to escape, why can't he just leave on his own? Why
does he need you to pick him up?" Anna asked the clown sitting next to
her.

"I don't know, but there must be a reason behind why he wants me to meet
him," the clown replied with words written on playing cards. He then
glanced at Jackal and Olivia sitting nearby.

He had been curious about the two new faces since he first met them.

Noticing the clown's curiosity, Anna promptly introduced them to the
former, saying, "That young woman has fused with an Anomaly, and her
special ability is to read memories. She's a helper I found myself.

"As for the other one... he's a Fhtagnist."

Anna had no idea how to explain Jackal's background.

Jackal immediately corrected her, saying, "No, my position is a bit
higher than a mere follower. An ordinary follower couldn't possibly see
the Great One at all times, unlike me. I believe I'm an apostle of the
Great One."

"You can see him?"

Jackal remained calm upon seeing the words written on the playing card
that the clown had thrown over to him. Instead of replying, he raised
his orange juice to his face and stared reverently at the liquid within.

He was staring at it as if something worthy of his respect was inside
it.

"God is everywhere," Jackal said and drained the glass in one gulp.

The clown shrugged at Anna. "That guy is weird."

Then, he turned his attention to Olivia sitting beside him.

The young woman was always wearing a pair of sunglasses, piquing the
clown's interest.

Olivia could feel the clown's white-gloved hand being extended toward
her, and she nervously reached out to shake it. She grabbed it and
attempted to shake it but was horrified to find that she had shaken the
hand off the clown's wrist.

It was just an empty glove; the gentleman had no physical form. The
eerie sight frightened her so much that she cried out, attracting the
gazes of the other passengers.

"That's enough. No more talking. We'll soon be at the airport, so get
ready to gather your belongings."

It was only three in the afternoon, but it was dark outside the airport
since it was polar night.

Anna and her group decisively followed the clown to Tobba's location—an
old apartment building.

To ensure that they wouldn't get ambushed, the clown opened the door
first while Anna and the others waited at the corner of the hall. It was
better to be safe than sorry.

Soon, Tobba's distinct cheering voice erupted from behind the door. Anna
realized just then that her worries were unfounded. With that, she
entered the room with Gao Zhiming and saw Tobba sitting cross-legged on
the table.

Tobba was like a groundhog, gnawing away at a large piece of watermelon
in his arms using his recently erupted front teeth.

Upon seeing Anna and her group, Tobba's infant-like eyes brightened
almost immediately. "Hey, hey, hey! Look who's here! It's none other
than our Anna! Long time no see. And who's that beauty behind you? Does
she have a boyfriend?"

However, Anna's attention wasn't on him. Her gaze was on the man being
tended to by Li Lu on the nearby bed. "I was wondering how you managed
to escape. It turns out it was the help of an insider rather than
Tobba."

The man was none other than Tobba's father, Roy. He was the shadow that
ultimately led to Anna's capture by the IMF. He was severely wounded.
His face and lips were extremely pale as if there were no blood coursing
through him, and bloody bandages were wrapped around his chest.

As soon as Anna saw Roy, she immediately understood why Tobba couldn't
leave this place on his own. Unless he abandoned his father, he had no
way out of this place.

"Y-y-you're still alive?! How is this possible?! I definitely saw your
corpse!" Roy exclaimed. Clutching his wound, he used all his strength to
reach for the pistol on the nearby table, but Li Lu held him back.

Anna's arrival made Li Lu feel complicated. She was happy because her
husband would most likely survive, but she was worried about getting
involved with such a dangerous woman once again.

"Um... actually, I was the one who called them here. You can't move
right now, and we can't just keep dragging things out," Tobba said. His
mouth was red, as he had been gnawing on a piece of watermelon.

Anna looked at him and lifted him off the table.

"So tell me, how did you know where the clown was?" Anna asked.

"It was, of course, all thanks to my invincible and ultimate power of
foresight! I'm an omniscient prophet!" Tobba exclaimed and gesticulated.

"Is that so? Then how did your father get injured? If you can foresee
the future, couldn't you have helped him avoid getting injured?"

Tobba's face wrinkled, and he seemed to be having trouble as he replied,
"Uh... this is a bit difficult to explain, so listen to me, all right?

"Have you played any games before? Let me make an analogy. Skills like
mine are extremely powerful, high-level skills that consume a lot of
mana and have a very long cooldown time.

"In other words, I need to rest for a long time before I can use it
again. Yeah, that's it."

"Really?" Anna said without any change in her expression. She then
grabbed Tobba by the ear and lifted him into the air.

"Ahhh! Granny, I was wrong! I just made that up. I don't know what's
going on, either. Scenes would pop into my mind sometimes, and one of
them showed me the clown's location.

"And I was just making a last-ditch effort. I didn't expect that you'll
actually come here."

Anna had no idea what to say as she stared at Tobba. He truly had an
amazing ability, but no one had any idea how it could be activated.

When Anna was stabbed in the back by the Shattered God, Tobba's
foresight hadn't activated. To make matters worse, no one knew whether
he was telling the truth or not.

Anna regretted coming here. Tobba was still Tobba; he was good at
nothing save for talking a bunch of nonsense.

Despite her harsh thoughts, Anna was actually a bit happy to see Tobba.
After all, he was her one and only connection to the Subterranean Sea.
Of course, she would never admit that she was feeling such weak human
emotions.

"Stay away from my son! You demon!" Roy roared. He disengaged the safety
on his pistol with one hand and pointed the trembling muzzle at Anna.

Anna completely ignored his threat and simply patted Tobba's sparse
head.

"You'd better change your attitude. You've already betrayed the IMF for
your son's sake, so you're in the same boat as us now. We're on the same
side."

"I betrayed them for him, but that doesn't mean that I'll work with you!
Leave! Leave now! I'm not going to tell the IMF that you're still
alive," Roy remarked, and he was left profusely sweating and gasping for
breath due to the exertion.

"Hehe, you're not the same age as your son, so let me tell you this—now
that you both know that I'm still alive, do you really think that I'll
let you go that easily?"

Chapter 1074 - Apology

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Sensing the increasingly tense atmosphere between his parents and Anna,
Tobba quickly stood between them and tried to mediate, saying, "Don't do
this, okay? We're all on the same side."

Tobba showed a big smile toward Anna, and then he walked up to his
father's bedside.

He reached for the gun in Roy's hands while saying, "She doesn't really
want to destroy the world; she just wants to go back home. Be good and
listen to me, all right?"

The listless Roy's face was as yellow as gold, and he was too weak to
overpower even an infant. In the end, he could do nothing but sprawl out
on the bed and gasp for air.

Anna looked at the bloody bandages and chuckled. "I probably don't need
to do anything—you won't last much longer at this rate. You can keep up
the tough act. I wasn't planning on helping you out anyway."

"Don't say that, Anna! You can't just let him die. Can't you just take
pity on a child less than a year old? I can't lose my father this
young!" Tobba howled and stretched his arms toward Anna's legs, but the
latter kicked him away.

Li Lu walked over and hugged her son. Her face was full of worry as she
said, "Anna, please save him. We're all in the same boat. If you save
him, won't you have one more ally?

"And you've dealt with my husband before, so you know his abilities are
exceptional. We'll be a lot safer with him on our side."

Unfortunately, Anna remained indifferent. Upon seeing that, Li Lu
decided to sweeten the pot, saying, "He knows what's happening in the
IMF. He knows all sorts of useful information."

Anna looked at the family of three with resignation. In the end, she
snapped her fingers at the clown and said, "Take him with you. This
place is too cold—I don't like this place at all, so let's go back."

Fortunately, the trip wasn't in vain. She had picked up a family of
three. Whether they were useful or not was yet to be seen, but at the
very least, their numbers had increased.

Including Jackal, Olivia, and Gao Zhiming, they now had seven people.

Tobba finally breathed a sigh of relief upon hearing that. Fortunately,
the argument hadn't escalated to the point of exchanging blows. He wiped
away the sweat from his forehead and remarked, "Ah, the things I do for
this family."

In response to Anna's order, the clown walked up to Roy.

Under Roy's astonished gaze, the clown removed his mask and placed it on
Roy's face. Then, he took off his black top hat and placed it on Roy's
head. In no time, Roy's severely injured body was under the clown's
control.

He stood up, stretched vigorously, and removed the bandages around him
to reveal a cut that stretched across his abdomen.

The clown flipped his hand, and a zipper head painted with fancy colors
appeared in his palm. The clown shoved the zipper head at the other end
of the wound and closed the zipper. Miraculously, Roy's wound closed
along with the zipper.

"We must hurry. What he did is just a small trick, and it is only done
during emergencies. We still need to find a real doctor to treat him,"
Anna remarked. She reached for the door and grabbed the door knob.

Before she could twist the door open, Tobba appeared out of nowhere and
clung to her arm. For some reason, Anna felt like something bad was
about to happen when she saw Tobba's ingratiating smile.

"Um, I need to tell you something. We can't just leave like this because
we're being followed. If we walk out just like this, we'll instantly get
recognized."

Anna lifted Tobba up to her face with one hand and smiled. "Why did you
not tell Mask about something so important?"

Tobba looked uncomfortable as he scratched his head and replied,
"Well... would you have come here if you had known that? You wouldn't
come here, right? Hehehe."

Anna really felt like strangling Tobba to death. For the sake of his
survival, Tobba had deliberately endangered Anna.

"Don't worry, I won't forget everything you've done for me. If anything
happens to you in the future, I'll definitely risk everything to help
you," Tobba said, making a promise while patting his small chest.

"Let's go. Tell me more as we walk. Who's following you?" Anna turned
the doorknob and led the others into the hallway.

At this point, it was too late for further discussion. They could only
move forward.

"It's a special Mobile Task Force, codenamed Sigma-61 'Wild Wolves.'
They're highly skilled in tracking targets."

"Be careful of all animals in the city. One of their special abilities
is to communicate with animals to gather intelligence from them."

Upon confirming the details about the Mobile Task Force Sigma-61 "Wild
Wolves," Anna quickly decided on their next course of action.

Since they knew that someone was tracking them, Anna had no plans of
brazenly boarding a plane with Tobba. Instead, she hijacked a bright red
off-road vehicle and drove the others into the vast, snow-covered land.

The hood of Anna's down jacket covered her hair, and thick goggles
concealed her eyes. The others were dressed in the same way as her.
𝓯𝓻𝒆𝙚𝒘𝓮𝙗𝓷𝒐𝓿𝙚𝒍.𝙘𝓸𝙢

"This isn't going to work. They'll catch up to us in no time. These
tracks are too easy to see," Tobba remarked, looking at the tire tracks
in the snow outside the window.

Just then, a wolf howl echoed throughout the white expanse. Clearly,
their pursuers had decided to chase after them.

Anna pressed the accelerator to the floor, and her eyes gleamed with
killing intent as she remarked, "I know. But we can't shake them off, so
we're just going to kill them all!

"I've had enough of hiding and running away. I need to find something to
vent my frustrations on!"

Anna glanced at Olivia and Gao Zhiming in the back seat through the
rearview mirror. "You usually don't get this kind of opportunity, so you
two should go out there and toughen yourself up."

The falling snow had gotten heavier, and the visibility had gotten so
low that Anna could barely see the road ahead.

Meanwhile, they could still hear wolf howls around them.

"Are they bad people?" Gao Zhiming asked curiously.

"Of course, they're bad people. Those trying to stop us from leaving
this place are bad people!" Anna exclaimed. The anticipation for the
upcoming slaughter made Anna part her lips into a smile, revealing her
pearly whites.

Boom!

The windshield abruptly shattered, and a hole appeared exactly where
Anna's forehead had been not even a second ago. The bullet shattered the
rearview mirror before phasing through her head.

Just then, a towering polar bear charged at them from the side, slamming
violently into the vehicle. Despite its powerful charge, the polar bear
couldn't overturn the car. Instead, it "melted" into the cabin.

Squelch!

Anna ruthlessly drove a sharp dagger into the polar bear's neck. A
stream of hot blood spurted from the wound, drenching Anna's down jacket
in a dark red hue.

Anna had quicker methods to kill, but she had chosen a more brutal
approach. At first, she thought she could control her emotions
perfectly, but she later discovered that she couldn't do that at all.

After all, she was human now; she was no longer a Dioite.

Humans ought to vent their frustrations whenever they were feeling
repressed, or they would eventually find themselves grappling with a
malady.

Chapter 1075 - Slaughter

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Despite the late night, darkness didn't reign supreme due to the thick
layer of white snow that reflected the faintest glimmers of light.

Rather, the world was enveloped in a strange amber hue—like the last
sliver of dusk.

The snow poured faster from the sky, piling up on the ground. The
vehicles carved deep trenches into the snow, but neither side showed any
intention of stopping.

Howl!

A couple of shadowy, four-legged silhouettes burst forth from the amber
blue and closed in on Anna's vehicle.

Swoosh! Swoosh!

The clown threw two throwing knives, and the shadows collapsed before
they could reach the car.

Behind them, the Mobile Task Force had arrived, and they numbered quite
significantly. Their blurred figures moved rapidly through the
snowstorm.

A heavy air of tension weighed down on them. Both sides knew the
upcoming fierce battle was inevitable and prepared in grim silence.

Inside the violently shaking car, Tobba was hunched over the dashboard.
He seemed oblivious to what was happening and was excitedly fiddling
with the car's radio.

At the start, there were some muffled human voices, but it all soon
dissolved into white noise. Annoyance crept upon his face as he
muttered, "Ugh. This is a dead zone without any signal. Can't hear a
thing."

"You're trying to listen to the radio now?! In this situation?! Just
watch how I'll throw you out!" Anna snapped.

As soon as the words left her lips, the car jolted hard and suddenly
skidded to the side.

"Tire's gone! Ready to bail!" Jackal shouted. But he immediately noticed
a flash of red light closing in from afar. With a hard yank on the
wheel, he swerved the vehicle to the right.

A rocket flew past them and hit the ground, detonating with a deafening
boom. The aftershocks propelled the vehicle into the air, flipping it
upside down. The car then crashed down into the snow with its wheels
skyward.

Amid the swirling snowflakes, a group of humans and animals cautiously
circled in on Anna's vehicle.

The men were dressed in full snow camouflage, their eyes shielded by
anti-glare goggles to fend off snow blindness. Even their boots were
strapped to skis designed for combat mobility in harsh terrain. They
were clearly professionally armed for the operation.

The insignia emblazoned on their shoulders—a circle enclosing three
inward-facing triangles, and in the center, three jagged fangs gave off
their identity. They belonged to the IMF's Mobile Task Force.

Clank, clank, clank.

Barbed grappling hooks were latched to the base of the overturned
vehicle in an attempt to flip it back up.

Just as they were about to start pulling, one of them suddenly went
limp. He collapsed onto the snow and began spasming.

The agent beside him moved on instinct; he bent over to check the
collapsed figure only to suddenly draw the weapon from his waist.
Without a word, he aimed at the man on the ground and pulled the
trigger.

Two deaths.

Being well-trained, the rest of the task force members took quick action
against the sudden traitor. A snow wolf lunged at the man and sank its
teeth into his hand. At the same time, another task force member tackled
the traitor and held the latter's arms into a lock.

Before they could question the traitor any further, the man's head
twisted a full hundred eighty degrees until he was staring directly into
the eyes of the one pinning him down.

It was only then that the task force member realized in shock. A clown's
mask was somehow plastered on his comrade's face.

The man's eyes widened in horror. Taking this opportune moment, the
clown detached itself from its host and flipped through the air with
frightening speed. With a snap, it latched onto its new host.

The last thing the man heard was the crunch of his snow goggles being
crushed by an unnatural force.

Controlling its new body, the clown repeated the same modus operandi.

However, the surviving agents had already learned from their previous
mistake. They re-strategized and moved in to surround the clown in an
attempt to bring it down. But soon, it became clear that the clown was
not acting alone.

A pair of massive hands extended from beneath the snow. It locked onto
an agent's legs and yanked in under in one swift motion. He never came
back up.

Before the battle unfolded, Li Lu, Jackal, and the others had already
been transported to a safe distance by Anna. Now that the chaos had
ensued, they were lying prone on a snowbank and picking off targets one
by one.

At this moment, the hunters had become the prey.

With trembling hands, Gao Zhiming pulled the trigger. Evidently, without
any surprise, he missed. The recoil from the pistol was also so powerful
that it nearly wrenched his arm out of its socket.

However, he didn't give up. He gritted down on his teeth and fired
another shot. After missing a few times, finally, one struck an enemy.

When Gao Zhiming saw a fragment of skull blown into the air by the
bullet, he instinctively flinched. His mind went utterly blank. He had
thought he was ready to take a life, but reality proved him wrong.

After a few seconds of frozen silence, something inside him seemed to
awaken. His eyes reddened, and he bit down hard on his lower lip. A
crazed ruthlessness overtook him as he frantically fired again and
again.

On the other side of the battlefield, a sharp blade plunged into a
beating heart. Her hands twisted a neck and snapped it. As the coppery
scent of blood intensified in the air, Anna's pulse quickened with
excitement.

She loved this feeling—indulging in the surge of violence—where she
could kill at will. It reminded her of those times when she was still a
Dioite.

Realizing that the tides were turning against them, the Mobile Task
Force began to pull back from the battlefield. They had no intention of
being dragged deeper into Anna's rhythm. But then, for some unknown
reason, they stopped and returned to fight with Anna and her group.

Just as the thick blanket of snow was about to be dyed completely
crimson with blood, a new sound resonated in the distance. It was a
mechanical whooshing, like turbines slicing through the air.

A blinding light swept through the air. A hovercraft with its thick,
inflated skirt was rapidly gliding across the snow and approaching them.

The people on the hovercraft arrived before the vehicle itself. Just as
Anna was about to drag another task force member into the snow, the air
around her instantly froze, and her hands were locked in the
materialized ice.

The ground beneath her started to shift, and the soil rapidly compressed
and gave in to form a hollow chamber beneath her. Without any place for
footing, Anna dropped into the dark, rounded pit.

"Who are you? Why are you helping traitors?" A voice travelled into
Anna's ears. "The HQ is recruiting new blood. As long as you are willing
to cooperate with us, all that has happened can be written off."

Anna's dozen irises squeezed into her eye sockets. Instantly, she had
vision in the dark. A woman dressed in a white combat suit stood
opposite her. A helmet bristling with wire ports crowned her head.

"IMF's latest batch of Anomaly-absorbing test subjects? That's a rare
power you have there," Anna remarked. She took a swift glance at her
surroundings and noticed the chamber's perfect symmetry. She could
roughly guess her opponent's identity.

It seems like this batch has better control of their abilities than the
last batch.

Just then, a translucent, ghost-like figure phased through the stone
wall and materialized next to the woman. His gaze was filled with
caution as his eyes locked on Anna.

Under his scrutiny, Anna felt as though she had been stripped bare.
However, her expression gave away naught a sliver of her emotions. On
the contrary, the man's expression slowly contorted with shock as his
jaw dropped.

"You...You're 315? You're still alive?!" He questioned.

Anna's pupils shrank into needle points, and her gut twisted into a
knot. At his words, the gunfire overhead abruptly ceased. Clearly, his
remark had made its way through every open comms channel.

Swoosh!

A sickly green flame instantly enveloped the two figures standing before
Anna. Not even a sound escaped their throat before they were reduced to
ashes.

Anna emerged from the ground. Her eyes burned with wrath as she stared
at the hovercraft that was trying to turn and retreat. She dashed toward
it and commanded, "Kill them! Kill them all! Don't even let a single one
escape!"

Chapter 1076 - Battlefield

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

"AHHHHHH!" Gao Zhiming's eyes burned with rage as he furiously squeezed
the trigger again and again. In his crazed mode, he almost stood up from
cover.

Beside him, Jackal hurriedly yanked him back down and forced him into a
prone position on the snowy ground.

"Calm down! Do you want to get your head blown off?" Jackal chided.
Without waiting for an answer, he grabbed Gao Zhiming by the shirt and
dragged him away from the center of the battlefield.

All of a sudden, a thunderous explosion sounded from behind them,
followed by a surge of heat waves. It felt as though flames were licking
their backs.

Gao Zhiming looked over his shoulder to see that the snow-gliding
assault vessel had now turned into a giant fireball.

His gut wrenched into a knot, his instincts screaming at him to rush
over.

"Big Sister's there!" Gao Zhiming shouted.

"No! She won't die that easily! If you go now, you'll only drag her
down!" Jackal replied as he restrained the boy.

Just then, the blazing fire took on a green, sickly hue and turned into
a roaring inferno of emerald flames. Only then did Gao Zhiming let out a
sigh of relief. But almost at the same time, he felt regretful that he
could not be of much help.

I could help her... I just need to kill more people.

On the other hand, Jackal didn't have the luxury of time to be concerned
about Gao Zhiming's inner turmoil. He led the group to a shallow trench
that offered temporary cover before he scanned the battlefield and
analyzed the situation based on his past combat experiences.

The High Priestess, alongside the Clown, was engaged in a brutal clash
against the enemy. The IMF's Mobile Task Force could only scramble
around the fringe and could hardly deal any damage.

Regular hot weapons were useless against both Anna and the Clown. Their
real enemies were the ones who had descended from the hovercraft.

Each of them wielded different strange abilities from the Anomalies they
had absorbed. On top of that, they could even coordinate with each other
with uncanny precision, making them hard to deal with.

Fortunately, the tides were still in Anna's favor. Be it combat finesse
or mastery over the Anomaly-fused abilities, Anna outclassed them by a
long shot.

These amateurs were no threat to her. After all, they had been sent here
to pursue Tobba, and the IMF wouldn't have thought of sending the heavy
hitters to deal with an infant.

Having been through countless battles, facing these rookies was nothing
short of a one-sided slaughter for Anna.

After swiftly testing the abilities and limitations of these test
subjects, Anna began her counterattack.

Evidently, they couldn't hold the line and started to retreat. But the
scythe hanging over their heads was the fact that their evacuation
vessel had already gone up in flames. Escape was no longer an option.

After sitting on the bone-chilling snow for a while, Gao Zhiming
struggled to his feet again. His hands trembled as he reached out toward
his pistol. He still wanted to help Anna.

But now that the adrenaline coursing through him had faded, the pain
sank in. His hands hurt; they were numb and swollen. He struggled to
lift the pistol. The web of his thumb had split open, and his palms were
covered in deep, ugly bruises.

Even if it was a standard pistol, the recoil was still too much for a
child to bear.

A pair of slightly cold hands reached out from the side and gently held
Gao Zhiming's hands. They belonged to Olivia.

Cradling Gao Zhiming's hands, Olivia gently rubbed over the bruised
areas with gentle pressure in an attempt to ease the internal bleeding.

After all, she was still just a teenager. She couldn't help but
sympathize with the young boy upon seeing his condition.

"Πονάειακόμα?[1]" Olivia asked softly.

Gao Zhiming couldn't understand a single word she said, but the evident
kindness and warmth in her voice were unmistakable.

"Thank you," Gao Zhiming replied as he forced a faint smile.

Olivia felt sorry for Gao Zhiming and pulled him into a gentle embrace.
She planted a kiss on his forehead and whispered in her own tongue,
"Είσαιτόσοαξιολύπητοςόσοκιεγώ...[2]"

From the side, Tobba watched them with a pitiful look.

"Why does he get all the sympathy?" Tobba lamented. "Why don't you pity
me? I'm younger than him, you know. I'm the one who needs someone
comforting!"

"Alert! Something's heading this way! They're trying to flank us!"
Jackal shouted. Immediately, everyone snapped to high alert.

Without saying another word, Li Lu picked up her son and stuffed him
into the thick down jacket against her chest. She then reloaded her
handgun.

"I'm running low on bullets. How many magazines do you have left?"

Before Jackal could respond, several Mobile Task Force members glided in
from the side on snow sleds. Their weapons were raised and aimed at
Jacakl and his group.

Just as the mobile task force was about to open fire, something
unexpected happened. Without any warning, the agents' bodies went limp
and collapsed onto the snow. Motionless.

The polar creatures that had appeared with them mirrored the same
behavior. They, too, dropped onto the snow.

Even those who were hiding in the darkness couldn't escape from the same
fate. One after another, dull thuds sounded as body after body fell onto
the snow.

The perilous situation was resolved, just like that, in the blink of an
eye.

Sob...sob...

Tears streamed down Olivia's cheeks as she used her ability to stir and
dissolve the enemy's consciousness into paste. She really didn't want to
kill. Taking a life was a weight too heavy to bear for a high schooler
like her.

However, if she hadn't killed them, they would have killed her.

Li Lu stared at Olivia in surprise. "Where did Anna find this girl? How
is she so powerful?"

Beside her, Gao Zhiming's face was painted with envy. His desire burned
for such power. If he possessed such a power, maybe he could actually be
of help to Anna.

Very soon, the battlefield grew quiet. Those lying in ambush in the snow
dared not approach them anymore.

Fwoosh!

A sickly green flame ignited and burned yet another victim into ashes.
Anna stood in the middle of the encirclement. Her chest heaved as she
panted heavily. Her eyes were still locked onto her enemies. She looked
as though she was battle-worn.

However, it was all just an act. She was playing weak. She wanted to
sell an illusion to the IMF's forces—that they had a chance of defeating
her.

These people knew her identity now; there was no way she would allow
even a single person to walk away alive.

Controlling one of the fallen bodies, the clown moved in close and stood
back-to-back with Anna.

"I can handle them here," Anna whispered. "Go meet up with Tobba. Don't
let anyone near Gao Zhiming. He's the key to the Subterranean Sea.
Everyone else can die, but not him!"

The clown nodded in acknowledgment. He lifted the throwing knife in his
hand and slashed his own throat. He then peeled the clown mask off his
face and hurled it high and far into the air.

Just then, invisible silk threads drifted down from above, aiming for
Anna's joints.

Anna gritted her teeth and pushed off with her feet. She charged head-on
toward the encirclement before her. Green flames ignited and enveloped
her.

The corrosive flames served a dual purpose. They ignited any enemy that
came close. Yet, at the same time, even projectiles were scorched to
ash.

It didn't take Anna long to break through the encirclement. For a
moment, the IMF forces thought that 315 was retreating. But then, new
figures began to emerge from the snow.

They weren't Anna or anyone from her group. Rather, they were flesh
monsters—amalgamations made from the corpses littering the battlefield.

Anna had once regretted absorbing such an ability. Under normal
circumstances, there weren't many corpses for her to utilize. But now
that the ground was strewn with bodies, such a power had become her
greatest weapon.

The more corpses there were, the more allies Anna had. Moreover, every
one of the allies came armed with their own weapons.

Raising their firearms, the flesh puppets continuously pulled the
triggers.

Whoosh~

The clown came spinning back in the air and snapped itself onto the face
of a corpse beside Anna.

"Didn't I tell you to go watch over them? Why are you back here?" Anna
snapped in annoyance. She was busy creating more allies.

The clown crawled onto its feet and shrugged. Cards fluttered from its
left palm to its right palm. "They don't want help. Olivia killed anyone
who got close."

1. Does it still hurt ☜

2. You're as pitiful as I am. ☜

Chapter 1077 - Inferno

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

That lass is that strong on the battlefield? Seems like there's a lot
more potential to her than I thought. Anna was genuinely surprised that
Olivia had claimed so many lives so easily.

Just a while back, the young girl would hesitate for a good few seconds
before killing an animal.

Before Anna could think any further of Olivia's potential, the ground
beneath her suddenly gave way. She could feel that the gravity around
her had increased threefold.

Just then, a black blur descended from the sky, aiming for Anna.
Clearly, it was a perfectly coordinated ambush.

Anna had no idea what the shadowy figure was, but her instincts were
sounding the danger alarms. Corruptive emerald flames flared out and
formed a burning shield above her.

However, that was just a feint. The real threat came from her left. A
beam of violet ray shot toward Anna at an extremely fast speed.

Anna had encountered this attack before. It could hit her even if she
activated her high-frequency phase shift. At this critical moment, she
seized her nearest ally—the host that the clown latched onto—into the
beam's path.

Upon impact, the victim's body swelled grotesquely like a balloon before
eventually bursting into a firework of blood and flesh. Through the red
mist, Anna's multiple irises saw through the darkness and locked onto
her attacker in the distance.

A wordless command was disseminated to all her flesh puppets. Those
nearby immediately ceased whatever they were doing and surged toward
that particular agent.

This seemed to be the last card up their sleeves. Even their
best-coordinated strategy hadn't killed Anna. All hopes were gone.

Realizing that only death awaited them if they were to stay, the IMF's
Mobile Task Force decisively left a portion of people behind to buy time
while the others scattered into the dark.

Anna detached the bloodied mask and slapped it onto the face of another
corpse next to her.

"After them! Don't even let a single one escape!"

The ending was destined. If they couldn't outfight Anna as a group, then
they sure as hell wouldn't be able to escape as a group.

Anna's strange eye could easily pick out every fleeing figure within the
white snowstorm with ease. At the same time, Olivia's abilities worked
like radar and could easily pinpoint those who tried to play dead or
camouflage themselves in the snow.

"Thirty-four, thirty-five, thirty-six..." Anna carefully counted the
corpses on the ground. Every attacker had been accounted for—and that
included the animals they brought along.

Beside her, Olivia crouched down with a withered stick in hand. She was
sketching tally marks into the snow as she recalled the number of enemy
memories she had gleaned.

On the desolate plain, snowflakes poured. Various strangely stitched
together bodies were lined in tidy rows. Their faces were frozen in
agony and disbelief. None of them had anticipated their demise. It had
just been a simple mission to chase down two ordinary traitors.

They even accounted for Tobba and his potential threat. Then, they made
thorough, methodical, cautious plans. However, they had never thought
that they would run into Anna.

The corpses were soon piled together, and a spark of fire set the entire
heap ablaze. Human fat was the best fuel, and in the heavy snow, the
fire burned like a roaring inferno.

The flames cast their light upon everyone's faces. A calm and serene
smile graced Anna's visage. She reached out and ruffled Gao Zhiming's
hair as she remarked, "Smells good..."

Everyone else instantly turned their gaze onto Anna. Their expressions
betrayed their thoughts. They were afraid that Anna was going to do
something worse.

However, Anna didn't do anything.

Once the inferno had consumed everything and erased all traces of the
massacre, Anna and her group flipped the overturned off-roader back onto
its wheels.

They slapped on a fresh tire and were prepared to move out.

Before they set off, Anna reached her hand into the flames and pulled
out a handful of scorched remains. She then circled the fire and started
to etch strokes into the snow.

The result was a tangled ring of cuneiform-like script—each wedge-shaped
glyph fused into the next, forming an unbroken circle. She then drew a
twisted triangle outside the circle. The three black tips of the
triangle were like thorns piercing the white snow.

There were no distinct spaces between the symbols. They defied
comprehension, and it was impossible to decipher whether it was a
message or a labyrinth map.

As soon as Anna finished the last stroke, the overhead sky dimmed for a
split second before the light returned.

Anna climbed into the vehicle and instructed Jackal at the driver's
seat, "Let's go."

Beside them, Tobba stood on his toes and pressed his nose against the
rear window, watching the blazing wreck shrink as it faded into the
distance.

"Was that even going to work?" Tobba asked, "Who would offer sacrifices
after they're dead? You didn't even follow the correct steps. What a
failure."

"I don't expect Him to get it. There isn't even enough material for an
actual offering. I only drew it to mess with the IMF," Anna replied.

Li Lu was clearly confused as she asked, "What are you talking about?"

"Nothing," Anna said, "Just a little sacrificial ritual for one of the
Divinities in the Subterranean Sea. The Silent Sovereign of the Southern
Seas. He's mute. So it doesn't matter. Might as well draw a simpler
pattern."

Anna was clearly uninterested in continuing the topic. She turned toward
Olivia and asked, "You combed through their memories just now. Did you
find anything useful?"

Olivia shook her head with a bitter smile. "Nothing. All I saw was
drills and blind loyalty. Those people were just soldiers."

"I remember there were a few squad leaders," Anna muttered in a
regretful tone. "But I sniped them right away. Should've kept one
alive."

Hearing Anna's words, Roy, cradled in Li Lu's arms, finally said, "They
wouldn't know much anyway. Every department in the IMF operates
independently. You can dream on about figuring out the IMF's movements
through them."

Anna slowly raised her index finger and slid it into his open wound
before hooking him toward herself gently. A searing pain coursed through
Roy's body.

"Watch your place, kid. If it hadn't been for Tobba, you would have been
a corpse already," Anna said in an icy tone.

But even as her finger dug deeper, Roy never let out any sound.

Suddenly, it occurred to Anna. Torture of this level was merely a daily
activity for shadow agents like Roy. His weakness lay elsewhere.

The corners of Anna's lips curled up into a wicked smile. She yanked her
finger free before reaching out to grab Tobba by the neck and lifting
him up.

Tobba's soft pink face turned red and soon turned purple. His tiny,
stubby arms and legs flailed wildly in the air.

Roy instantly got nervous. "Wait! I wasn't trying to be disrespectful.
What I meant was that I have higher clearance than them in the IMF. My
memories are much more valuable."

Anna released Tobba and tossed him back onto the seat like a discarded
toy. "See, shouldn't you have said so earlier? You've already betrayed
them, so what's the point of clinging onto that pointless loyalty? Now,
start talking. What's going on in there now?"

"Based on the latest HQ directive, the IMF has entered Level-2 emergency
protocol. All mobile task forces are on wartime standby while the
higher-ups are secretly negotiating with the various governments."

"Say something that has more value. I could guess that much from
watching the TV news."

The opposing forces to the IMF were stirring and would do sudden
ambushes on containment sites. Of course, the IMF had to respond.

"They're gathering the Anomalies from every site," Roy began. "And
transferring them somewhere."

"Now, be specific about this somewhere."

"I'm not sure. I don't have that high of a clearance," Roy admitted.

Anna let out a scoff. "I could guess that much. They're probably
stuffing those Uncontrollable Anomalies into people and turning those
lab rats into walking weapons."

Roy shook his head. "I thought the same initially. However, some of the
Anomalies that were sent over... are weak. They could even be considered
useless.

"And they're also sending special items that are not Anomalies such
as..." A hint of pain flickered across Roy's face. "Such as my son."

Chapter 1078 - Others

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

A hint of understanding flashed across Anna's countenance as her lips
curled into a knowing smirk.

"All that high and mighty talk about loyalty and duty. But now that you
finally feel the blade, you start to back out, huh? Indeed, it always
hits differently when the knife's cutting into your own skin."

Roy's voice raised a notch in agitation. "I can sacrifice everything for
IMF—but not my child! What kind of parent could stand by and watch their
own kid die?!"

Silence fell upon the car. The only sound was the low hum of the engine.

After a long while, Anna finally broke the silence. Her tone was clearly
much softer and gentler.

"You're a good father. But unfortunately, the same can't be said about
everyone. Not every bastard who makes a kid deserves to be called one."

With evident agony on his face, Roy continued, "I have filed report
after report to prove that Tobba isn't a threat. But the higher-ups
ignored all of them. They even sent people to watch me.

"When Tobba knocked on my door out of nowhere that night, I couldn't
take it anymore. My request isn't overly outrageous. All I wanted was
for him to grow up safely."

Warmth and affection filled Roy's eyes as he turned to look at Tobba.

Tobba then turned around to meet Roy's gaze before flashing a bright,
innocent smile. "Thanks!"

Anna extended a hand toward Roy. "So, our interests are aligned now,
aren't they? That makes us allies from now on. We're just a bunch of
pitiful folks huddling together for warmth under the IMF's threat."

Roy scrutinized the woman in front of him. She was a complicated person,
and he really tried to read her—to guess how much of her words held
truth and how much was manipulation. However, he couldn't see through
this woman who was a monster beneath her human skin.

Just then, Li Lu leaned in and whispered something into her husband's
ear.

Roy then straightened up and asked, "Can I ask what your plan is going
forward?"

"After this, we will mainly operate around Subterranean London under the
disguise of hunters. We will lay low and watch the IMF and the other
opposing forces fight each other.

"This will be their show while we stay in the shadows and secretly grow
stronger while they bleed themselves dry," Anna answered.

"As for the next step after, we'll reassess and decide when one side
comes out on top. No matter what, only the ones with the stronger power
get a say."

A bitter smile tugged at Roy's lips. "Does your power include those who
survived that ritual in the Indian Ocean? With your temperament, would
you be willing to let the IMF off just like that?"

A hint of annoyance crossed Anna's face. "Just answer if you're in or
not. Or are you planning to crawl back to the IMF now, hand your son
over to them, and beg for mercy?"

"You know," Roy started. "From the very beginning, I felt that you were
different from those zealots. You only care about yourself."

Roy then pulled every ounce of strength in him to lift his hand and
clasped Anna's hand. "I'm in. As long as your goal isn't to annihilate
the world, we'll pledge loyalty to you."

With that, Tobba's family formally pledged their allegiance. Anna's once
weakened power was starting to return.

Jackal slammed on the accelerator; the massive off-road vehicle zoomed
across the desolate plains. The falling snow soon covered every trace of
their tire tracks.

***

Back in the Subterranean Sea, Lily sat on the ground, her arms curled
around her knees as she silently gazed at the scene ahead. Fleshy, blood
tentacles had invaded the buildings in the distance.

Fueled by bioelectricity, the entire island pulsed with life. Lights
were lit, and the machinery groaned back to life.

Humans with bulging eyes and grotesquely swollen heads—so large they had
to hunch forward just to support the weight—shuffled in and out of the
buildings. They were there to help Charles gain full control over the
island's technology.

At the docks to the edge of the island, the three Sottom Kings—Tobba
excluded—were being dragged off a warship. The captain leading this navy
was none other than Governor Bandages of Hope Island.

The long, luminous blue captain's coat draped over the black wrappings
on his entire body looked exceptionally matching.

Since he was here, naturally, he already knew what Charles was planning.

Bandages approached Lily and studied the frail girl before saying,
"Regardless…this works or not… thank you… for your…sacrifice… I'll make
sure…your parents…are well taken care of…

"No wonder…the captain…of the other plane…let you aboard his
ship…You're… a good kid…"

"Thank you, First Mate," Lily replied. She tried to force a smile, but
now, she already couldn't quite remember how.

She could no longer return to the past. The old Lily was no more.

She used to feel a strange distance between herself and Charles and the
others. There was always something that seemed to set them apart. But
now, she was finally the same as them.

"Can you ask Mr. Charles to come here? I've already chosen the other six
candidates."

"Speak. I'm listening." The ground beneath Lily's feet slightly
trembled. The entire island was now fully under Charles' control.

Lily pondered for a moment before she parted her lips and said, "Can I
choose the Lilies from the other planes?"

No matter which plane it was, a Lily was still a Lily. If she selected
other versions of herself, at least it could be ensured that the fused
Light God would be consistent in terms of temperament, mindset, and
soul.

"I would advise against that. If you were to merge with versions of
yourself who hadn't experienced the harsh reality of the Subterranean
Sea, you would just end up being a weak, indecisive, useless coward.

"A Lily who had never turned into a mouse is just an ordinary human. In
some other planes out there, some Lilies ended up as pirates, but you…
you are unique."

Lily's plan that had taken root in her mind was dismissed so
effortlessly, just like that. She couldn't help but feel a little
sorrowful, but she didn't have the luxury of time to mourn over it. She
needed to find the other six candidates as soon as possible.

"You'd better hurry," Charles urged. "This island holds profound yet
valuable knowledge. Because of it, my time is greatly shortened. I need
to leave by this afternoon."

Out of nowhere, she was being thrown the problem of finding six others
to merge into one to become the Light God. Time was really rushed and
where was she even supposed to start finding?

Anxiety ran through Lily as she stared down at the island beneath her
feet. She was eventually at her wits' end and asked, "Do you have any
suggestions?"

"You could try these," Charles replied. The moment his words fell, the
dark waters began to churn violently as the ghostly remnants of Project
Dawn—tethered together like a string of drowned stars—were pulled up to
the surface.

"Them?" Lily's eyes widened as she stared at the glowing spectres.

"Compared to alternate versions of yourself from other planes, I believe
these are a better fit. After all, you share the same origin."

"But… they're already dead. They don't even have physical forms."

"That's not a problem. I can fix it. I just need to borrow a little
something from you."

A portion of the divine energy within Lily that originated from the
Light God was being pulled out of her. Threads of it streamed into the
ghostly spectres.

They began to glimmer before they slowly started to blaze and merge into
what looked like a miniature sun. However, when Charles reached into
them, the light gradually dimmed.

When everything was finally over, the radiant sun in the air had become
a pulsating mass of flesh. It emanated a crimson glow that could corrupt
and cause mutation.

"Done," Charles declared. "They're alive for now. And with a physical
form."

"But…that's only five. We need seven to fuse into the Light God."

"I've already considered that. The Foundation didn't know they were
created. We're going to need a lot more materials."

As soon as Charles' words fell, a glimmer appeared in the distant
darkness. The light grew closer and brighter before eventually stopping
right in front of Lily.

She recognized it immediately. It was Dawn One that hovered above Hope
Island.

Chapter 1079 - The Beginning

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

The experiment that once created the Light God restarted again on this
very island.

As tentacles writhed forth and burrowed themselves into the body of Dawn
One, crimson and violet capillaries spread like eagle talons across its
circular form.

Dawn One hadn't made a single sound throughout the ordeal because,
unfortunately, Charles had taken away its ability to speak.

The same fate awaited the other five Dawn Spectres that had just gained
their fleshly bodies.

Lily looked up and watched the awe-striking spectacle unfolding
overhead. Her voice trembled as she muttered, "Without Dawn
One...there'll be no more sunlight on Hope Island."

The entire island beneath her trembled once again, and Charles' voice
echoed around her like noises from stereo surround, "Are you still
thinking for their sake now? It doesn't matter. Without the sun, there
would be no more idiots burning themselves to death every year.

"Also, even if Hope Island can no longer cultivate banana trees and

islanders will probably feel a little panic. But that's insignificant

Lily was no longer the naive child she once was, so she understood it
was not an insignificant matter as per Charles' claim. The disappearance
