its proximity for an extended period of time, the orientation of your
body will change randomly.

For instance, the direction of your hair growth, the length of your
finger, and your age may even reverse. Those are still insignificant
changes. 𝒇𝒓𝙚𝒆𝔀𝓮𝓫𝒏𝓸𝙫𝓮𝓵.𝓬𝙤𝙢

The dangerous part is that it can also alter the direction of the blood
flow in your blood vessels and disrupt your heartbeat's rhythm.

If things spiral to that point, I assume I don't need to explain too
much about the inevitable consequences. Therefore, my recommended advice
is to use the Anomaly for three months and then stop for half a year. Do
not try to change users to avoid the side effects.

The third owner tried that, and it didn't work. The burden left by the
previous user would be stacked onto the next user. His ending was
miserable.

Its applications are up to your imagination. With proper control, one
could use this Anomaly to fly freely in the air.

Thank you for consulting Three Crows Anomaly Shop. I'm the owner of this
shop. If you're ever interested in putting up any Anomaly for sale, feel
free to drop by or call me directly at 0414545…

Anna couldn't be bothered to read the rest of the long-winded sales
pitch. She raised her head and looked at Li Lu, Roy, and Jackal. She
then pondered over a question—Should she absorb the Anomaly herself or
give it to one of them?

Chapter 1090 - Mission

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

In the end, Anna decided against absorbing the new Anomaly. She already
fused many within her. There was no guarantee that there would be no
accidents if she were to add more.

Rather than fusing with more Anomalies, she would do better mastering
those that she already possessed.

The benefit of handing off the Anomaly to someone else was far better
than keeping it for herself. The strength of the entire team could be
enhanced.

Staring at the red ceramic goblet in Anna's hand, Roy, Li Lu, and Jackal
exchanged glances.

This was an Anomaly, and the absorption ritual's success rate was not a
hundred percent. The process of growing strong came with risk, and the
trio knew that well.

Li Lu gritted her teeth and was about to step forward when Roy moved
first and blocked her path. He reached out and took the goblet from
Anna's hand.

"Let me do it. I've been wanting to give the absorption ritual a try
ever since I heard about it."

Anna didn't object as she didn't really care who absorbed the ritual as
long as someone did.

Using a block of sea salt, the absorption ritual was rapidly drawn out.
With Anna leading the process, Roy's absorption process went smoothly.

Clutching his bloody abdomen, Roy slowly stood up. He tilted his head
upward to stare at the others now hanging upside down from the
"ceiling."

He had completely broken free from gravity.

Suddenly, he flipped and plummeted toward Anna and the others. Before he
could crash into them, his direction of gravity shifted again, and Roy's
fall reversed.

It was clear that he hadn't gotten used to his new ability just yet, as
he slammed awkwardly into the ceiling.

"There's not much space here. Let's take this outside," Anna commented.

Soon enough, the group arrived at the empty outskirts, and they watched
Roy take to the sky like a rogue firework.

By controlling the direction of gravity on him, he darted swiftly
through the air.

No matter whether he went left, right, or straight up, he moved at the
terminal velocity of a falling object.

A human would fall at a speed of 150 to 200 kilometers per hour. It was
way faster than any car. If Roy wanted to do so, he could use that high
speed to glide across the sky like Superman.

The only difference was that Superman flew head first while Roy flew
feet first.

Of course, such a power came with its dangers. If Roy couldn't control
it well, he could end up plummeting to his death.

Or the opposite could happen; he could soar past the atmosphere and
right out into outer space. He could then get first-hand experience of
spacewalking without any equipment.

With the help of a harness, Roy gradually adapted to the new power
within him. He then experimented with it and tested its limits. Its
potential was undeniable, but unlocking its full utility would require
finesse and patience.

Watching all of this from the ground, envy shone in Gao Zhiming's eyes.
He wanted that power, too.

After a few more trial runs and seeing that Roy was slowly getting used
to his new power, Anna called out, "Come down. I've got something to
announce."

Once everyone's attention was on her, Anna held up her phone and waved
it.

"I've just accepted a mission on the dark web," Anna said. "We'll make a
few preparations and head out right after."

By now, everyone had already gotten used to Anna's decisiveness—not that
they had the right to object or voice their opinion.

Gao Zhiming asked in curiosity, "Sis, where are we going this time?"

"Sri Lanka. An island south of India."

Hearing the location, both Roy and Li Lu's hearts sank.

Sri Lanka was right on the edge of the Indian Ocean. They both knew
exactly what Anna had done on those waters back then. 𝕗𝕣𝐞𝐞𝘄𝐞𝚋𝚗𝗼𝘃𝗲𝗹.𝚌𝕠𝚖

It was definitely not a coincidence that she picked that place for a
mission. Her true intent likely lay elsewhere.

"I don't care about the rest, but my family and I will never accept your
cult's brainwashing ritual!" Roy stepped forward and said with a grim
expression. "If that's what you are planning, then this is the end of
our alliance!"

Anna's lips curled into a faint smile as she looked at Roy. "Oh? So you
think you have the right to talk back to me now that you've gained that
one little ability?"

There was no apparent threat in Anna's voice, but tension gripped Roy
and Li Lu's mind like a vice grip.

"Relax," Tobba said as he stepped between them. "She won't do anything
with me here. And come on, Anna, is it that interesting for you to scare
people day in, day out?"

Tobba was trying to soothe the tense atmosphere. He could even be
considered the lubricant between Anna and his parents.

Anna walked over and gently pinched Tobba's soft cheek. "You'd better
prove your worth soon. I really hate having to pull a dead weight
around."

With that, she turned on her heel and climbed into the car.

Roy's guess was actually spot-on. Anna's true purpose in heading to Sri
Lanka was to check how much surveillance the IMF still had in the Indian
Ocean.

If they were too overwhelmed by all the things happening all around to
keep watch over this corner of the world, then it was time for her to
revive the Fhtagn Covenant.

After all, compared to the independent thinkers like Roy and Li Lu, Anna
much preferred devout zealots who obeyed without question. Who wouldn't?

Still, she had learned her lesson from her previous experience. She had
to stay low profile at all costs.

Directly going into the India Ocean was out of the question. But under
the guise of a hunter's mission, she could totally just survey the place
discreetly.

A plane slowly landed on the island nation nestled in the Indian Ocean.

The locals, much like their northern neighbors, had caramel-toned skin
and curled hair.

At a heavily guarded seaside villa, Anna and her group finally met their
employer.

He was a portly man with a thick gold chain around his neck. Lounging
across the velvet couch, he resembled a tanned version of Maitreya
Buddha as he grinned from ear to ear.

His tone rose and fell with each word as he spoke to Anna. However, Anna
didn't understand a single word. It sounded like Hindi.

A beautiful woman knelt next to the couch. Acting as an interpreter, she
relayed the message to Anna and the rest.

"Our chairman says that he's very pleased to meet you. However, he is
not too certain about your strength. He asks if you would be willing to
spar with his bodyguards."

Whoosh!

A burst of green flames erupted from Anna's fingertips and engulfed the
surrounding furniture, reducing them to piles of ash in mere seconds.

The scene clearly shocked the portly chairman. His rolls of fat trembled
as he leaped out of his couch in terror.

When he saw that Anna and the others had no intention of attacking him,
he quickly flashed a sheepish smile before he fumbled with his pocket to
pull out a phone with shaking hands.

Anna raised an eyebrow. An outsider? Does the dark web allow posting
from such people, too? It seems like even ordinary civilians are gaining
more access to the underworld.

However, Anna didn't really mind. After all, this mission was just a
cover for her.

The chairman shouted furiously into this phone for some time before he
turned to the interpreter next to him with a grim expression.

"Our chairman says that he's willing to trust you guys. If you can
complete this mission, he'll even double the payment."

"What do we need to do?" Jackal asked, his fake moustache twitching as
he spoke.

"Protect our chairman. He will be negotiating with a very important
individual on international waters. Your job is to ensure his absolute
safety.

"We strongly suspect that the other party's security team may include
individuals… like yourselves."

"I have killed many, but protecting someone? That's a first. Sounds
interesting," Anna responded.

Chapter 1091 - Target

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

A triple-deck luxury cruise glided steadily toward the international
waters. Its streamlined hull, accompanied by the curving of the waves,
presented a picturesque scene.

Men clad in black suits and earpieces in place stood guard across all
three levels. Their gazes were vigilant as they scanned the
surroundings.

Apart from the sheer number of bodyguards, what stood out was the two
small destroyers that flanked each side of the cruise ship.

Anyone rich enough could buy a luxury cruise, but warships were a
different story. Even if the destroyers bore marks of wear and tear,
their existence alone spoke volumes of the noble status of the cruise
ship's owner.

Naturally, the cruise ship belonged to Anna's latest employer. Having
taken a generous upfront payment, Anna and her group were now stationed
at various spots on the ship. They worked alongside the bodyguards and
were on alert for any possible threats.

Of course, to avoid drawing the attention of the IMF, all of them had
undergone complete makeovers. Li Lu had the most dramatic
transformation. She bound her chest tightly and completely disguised
herself as a man.

However, all of that wasn't part of Gao Zhiming's considerations. He
clutched the ship's railings with both hands and stood on tiptop to peer
into the vast sapphire waters beneath him.

A look of awe surfaced upon his face. An experience like this was the
first for him. Just then, a white gloved hand held out a triple-scoop
ice cream cone to Gao Zhiming.

Gao Zhiming turned and recognized the individual. With a beaming smile,
he accepted the ice cream and said, "Thank you, Uncle Mask."

No child could resist sweets, and that included Gao Zhiming.

The clown gave a theatrical clap and a cascade of poker cards, each
written with words fluttered between both hands.

Reading the text on the card, Gao Zhiming shook his head.

"I don't know what Sis is doing in the cabin. She kicked me out and told
me to stand guard outside. Instructed me to notify her if anything
happens."

The clown bent down and pressed a single finger to the deck. He then
performed a series of backflips and vanished from sight.

Gao Zhiming stared at the spot where the clown had disappeared before
turning his gaze to the tightly shut cabin door behind him. With a happy
hum, he continued licking the ice cream in his hand.

As he indulged in his ice cream, Gao Zhiming saw a woman with mirrors
for eyes approaching him. It was Olivia. She reiterated the same
question the clown had asked.

When she heard that even Gao Zhiming had no idea what Anna was doing
inside the cabin, Olivia's expression dimmed as she let out a soft sigh.

Ever since Olivia had heard from Li Lu that Anna had a way to convert
someone into a fanatically loyal cultist, she had been growing anxious.
She was afraid that she would be the first to go through the ritual.

No one wanted to lose their free will, and Olivia was the same. If it
was possible, she would beg Anna not to force her to go through with the
conversion ritual.

Just as she was wallowing in her own sorrow, Olivia suddenly felt the
gaze of the young boy in front of her. She looked up and saw that his
eyes were filled with admiration as he stared at her eyes hidden behind
her sunglasses.

She reached out and ruffled the boy's soft hand before persuading, "Gao,
you're still too young. You don't understand. This isn't some
superpower. It's more like a curse.

"If I wish to, I can read and sense almost anything anyone is thinking
right now. But the more I see… the more disappointed I am with humanity.

"Except for the pure, innocent children, no matter how kind, warm, or
amicable someone may appear, there's always a dark side to their
thoughts.

"I can even see all the horrible and terrible things they have done in
the past. Honestly, the more I see, the more I find myself hating
humans.

"I used to believe that there are good people and bad people in the
world. Now I realized that everyone has a good side and bad side to
them. It just depends on which side they show," Olivia concluded.

A flicker of disgust crossed Olivia's visage as she slightly turned her
head to look to her left.

The black-suited bodyguard who had been sneaking a glance at her butt
immediately turned his head away and pretended to be monitoring the
empty, vast sea ahead of him.

Gao Zhiming blinked at Olivia. She had said a whole bunch of things in
her own language, and he couldn't understand a single word. He could
only lick his ice cream in confusion and replied, "Sis, what were you
saying? I can't understand your language at all."

Olivia let out a sigh. If only she could project her own thoughts
directly into another person's mind, she wouldn't have to bother herself
with conversations.

Using body language to gesture to Gao Zhiming to stay put, Oliva turned
and walked toward her private cabin.

As she moved, the sleazy and vile thoughts of a few nearby men crawled
into her mind. She instinctively wrapped her trench coat tighter around
her thin frame.

Then, without warning, Olivia suddenly stopped and turned her head
sharply to the right. It was Roy with a thick beard as a disguise.

He stood next to the round porthole and had one hand in his pocket while
smoking a cigar with the other.

Olivia merely took a brief glance at him before lowering her head to
find her key from the pocket of her trench coat. She found it and
slipped it into the keyhole. Before she unlocked the door, she lifted
her head and looked at Roy once more.

"I wouldn't do that if I were you," Olivia said.

Roy's gaze didn't turn and remained on the sparkling blue waters beyond
the window.

"Since you can read my thoughts, you should be able to sense my doubts
as well. Can you tell me why?"

At his words, a trace of fear appeared on Olivia's face. "You didn't see
that scene and have no idea how powerful it is! You don't have any clue
how close the High Priestess is to Him!

"Humans are nothing before Him! In front of Him, any irreverent thoughts
are already a sin!"

With that, her visage was already pained with panic and horror. Her
hands trembled as she reached into her coat to pull out a small red tree
pieced together from LEGO bricks.

She bowed her head and muttered under her breath, confessing her guilt
and reverence to the tree.

After pouring out everything, Olivia felt as if a weight had been lifted
from her shoulders, and she felt a lot calmer.

"I've never read the High Priestess' memories, and neither do I dare to.
That's the end of this topic. We should avoid being seen together in
private from now on," Olivia said before she twisted the key firmly.
𝚏𝐫𝚎𝗲𝕨𝐞𝐛𝕟𝚘𝐯𝚎𝗹.𝕔𝐨𝗺

The door swung open, and she entered the room.

Just as she was about to close the door behind her, Roy called out.

"Don't go in just yet. Our target's here." Roy then tossed his
half-finished cigar into the sea.

Through the thinning haze of smoke, a decrepit vessel that looked like
it would break apart from a single gust was steadily approaching them.

Bang! Bang! Bang!

Gao Zhiming pounded on the cabin door. "Sis! There's a big ship coming
our way. Tobba's dad told me to get you!"

Anna opened the door to reveal a completely new look. She shifted to
block Gao Zhiming from seeing the blood-soaked ritual circle scrawled
across the floor behind her.

"Sis, what were you doing inside? Why does it smell so bloody?"

Anna didn't answer. Instead, she bent down and planted a light peck on
Gao Zhiming's lips before pulling him alongside her to the deck.

Although the conversion ritual reacted, it wasn't a complete success.
Seems like we are still too far away. If I want to carry out the ritual,
I need to get even closer. Anna thought to herself.

Seeing that another vessel was coming into view, the security team
snapped to attention. Even the defensive systems on both destroyers were
activated.

Despite the tense atmosphere in the air, the other party showed no
hostile response. Instead, the vessel lowered its anchor in a calm
manner.

Chapter 1092 - Deck

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Anna was taken aback to discover that the newcomers had white skin
instead of caramel-toned skin like her employer.

The leader of the group was a man with a big brown beard; his burly arms
were covered in many tattoos, and the symbol "1%" was on the chest of
his leather jacket.

The brown-bearded man's laughter arrived before him. He guffawed as he
approached, and as if he were picking up a ball, he scooped up Anna's
employer.

The two enthusiastically patted each other on the shoulders.

On the surface, they seemed as close as family, but in reality, they
were guarding against one another.

Anna was feeling curious about the kind of deal these two groups were
about to make that they had to commission hunters instead of just
ordinary bodyguards.

"Olivia, check our employer's memories and see what they're up to."

The freckled young woman immediately obeyed, but she soon shook her head
with a troubled face. "I can see his memories, but I don't understand
what he's saying. I only know that he's made a lot of phone calls and
met with many people recently. His emotions are dominated by nervousness
and excitement."

Since Olivia couldn't figure out what the other party was trying to do,
Anna turned around and walked away from the deck in disappointment. "I'm
going back first. Jackal, keep an eye on things."

Jackal's sharp gaze studied the followers that the brown-bearded man had
brought along with him. "Those people are giving me a sense of
foreboding. I'm afraid our employer might die if you leave."

Anna glanced at the chubby tanned man in the distance, who had his arm
around the brown-bearded man's shoulder, and indifferently said, "If he
dies, he dies. We've stolen so much money from our previous missions. Do
we still need that reward from him?"

This commission was just an excuse. Their main objective was to find out
whether the IMF had left any personnel to monitor this region.

When Anna turned around, her unusual behavior immediately attracted the
attention of the group of white men.

One of them frowned as he looked at Anna's back. He then pressed his
right index finger on his temple, and—

—the man howled in agony, and his face contorted as he collapsed to the
ground.

The agonized scream was like a signal for the battle to commence.

The two groups were extremely friendly toward each other just moments
ago, but they instantly became hostile upon hearing the scream. They
pointed their guns at each other, and thick, killing intent flashed in
their eyes.

The atmosphere was so oppressive that it was terrifying.

Everyone's nerves were stretched taut as if they were arrows nocked on
bows.

"उत्साहित मत बनो!"[1] said the brown-bearded man. Upon assuaging the
tense nerves of his subordinates, he cast a sincere gaze at Anna's
employer, saying, "मैन, मैं वास्तव में आपके साथ काम करता हूं, यह सिर्फ इसकी
गलतफहमी है"[2]

As soon as the brown-bearded man's words fell, the nervous, chubby
tanned man calmed down. He waved his hand lightly, and the guns in the
hands of his bodyguards were lowered.

The cannons on the nearby destroyers turned away as well.

When everyone had calmed down, the brown-bearded man and his
subordinates turned to the man who had fallen to the ground.

They whispered among themselves for quite a while until their gazes
finally landed on Anna.

Anna was standing quietly with her arms crossed. She no longer had any
intention to leave. She just stood there, waiting to see what they were
going to do.

The brown-bearded man said a few words to Anna's employer before walking
up to Anna with his subordinates in tow. He grinned, and he sounded
apologetic as he said in English, "I'm very sorry about that. It was my
subordinate's mistake. He shouldn't have randomly peeked at your
memories.

"However, I'm sure he didn't expect that someone is actually capable of
setting traps in their own memories."

Anna stayed silent, observing the dozens of people behind the
brown-bearded man. Their outfits weren't uniform. In fact, it looked
like they were a ragtag bunch of people.

The man earlier was apparently capable of reading memories like Olivia,
so Anna assumed that the people before her had their own special
ability.

"Hahaha, it seems that I underestimated Portman's intellect. To think
that he had invited someone capable of protecting his memories," the
brown-bearded man remarked.

The brown-bearded man glanced at Anna's employer. Obviously, he thought
that Portman had specifically recruited Anna in exchange for a large
sum.

Anna shook her head. "No, he doesn't know anything. We were just
recruited randomly."

"Then, the fact that he managed to find someone as capable as you means
that he has great luck. Anyway, do you mind telling me about your
identities?" the brown-bearded man asked, probing Anna.

Instead of answering, Anna asked back, "How about you? Where do you come
from? Why have you decided to look for this fat guy? And don't you think
you're making things difficult for me by asking that?

"It's better if we keep each other's identity a secret. You do your
deal; I do my job. It'll be great if we don't interfere with each
other," Anna said. She thought that the brown-bearded man would
tactfully back down upon hearing her reply.

However, Anna was surprised when the brown-bearded man directly revealed
his identity.

"We're mercenaries from the Wilson Group. My name is Hanks, and I'm sure
you haven't heard of us, but that's normal—our group was just formed.
You can think of us as hunters with a bit more military training than
the average hunter.

"We've come here to secretly take control of Sri Lanka together with Mr.
Portman. With the special abilities of my subordinates, taking control
of a small nation is as easy as taking candy from a baby."

Anna was astonished as he sized up the chubby tanned man in the distance
and the brown-bearded man in front of her.

Just how much liquor had these guys drunk? They were actually daring
enough to try and take control of a nation with their puny abilities? On
second thought, however, it wasn't really that strange.

If one wanted to make full use of one's special ability, the best way to
go about it would be to take over a country.

"Are you not afraid of the IMF dealing with you guys? What if their
shadows are among your people?" Anna asked a question that highlighted
something extremely important to the point of being fatal if not
addressed.

"Hahaha, they'll soon have no time to deal with us," the brown-bearded
man replied with a smug smile before adding," Anyway, I've told you who
we are, so can you tell me who you are now? Who are you?"

No time? Anna pondered over the brown-bearded man's words that
unexpectedly contained a ton of information.

"We're not worthy of your attention at all," Anna remarked. "We're just
a small group of hunters trying to earn a living. That fat guy said that
he'll pay us a ton, so we came here."

The brown-bearded man sized up the neither humble nor arrogant young
woman in front of him. His intuition told him that Anna wasn't just an
ordinary hunter, so it wouldn't be a bad thing to make friends with them
before anything else.

"Hahaha." The brown-bearded man laughed. "I understand your concerns,
but it's extremely dangerous to be a lone wolf right now. I'm sure
you've heard about the recent string of deaths in Subterranean London.
You've heard of it, haven't you?

"Think about joining us; join us, and we'll all be friends. No one will
dare to touch you by then," the brown-beaded man said, handing over a
silver business card to Anna.

Anna accepted the business card.

The brown-bearded man saw that and was about to say something when a
shrill whistling noise echoed from the distance.

Boom!

A thunderous boom echoed as an artillery shell struck the deck, and
flames shot up high into the sky.

Anna's figure was trembling at a high frequency as she crawled up from
the scorched steel deck. She turned and found the brown-bearded man
staring at her with wide eyes on the ground. No, to be more exact, what
remained of the brown-bearded man—a head and half a rib cage—was staring
at Anna.

The explosion had blown away the rest of his body.

1. Calm down! ☜

2. Man, we're on the same side; this is just a misunderstanding. ☜

Chapter 1093 - The Other Party

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Anna tensed up at the quick death of the confident, brown-bearded man.
She hurriedly pulled Gao Zhiming away from the chaotic deck.

"Let's not make a move just yet. Let's first figure out where the other
party is from," Anna said to the masked Jackal and the others. She then
vigilantly stared at the chaos on the deck through the bridge's glass.

The explosion was astonishingly powerful, but they weren't struck
directly. Except for Li Lu's minor leg injury, the others suffered only
temporary hearing loss.

However, the brown-bearded man's subordinates and those bodyguards
weren't so lucky. There were scattered limbs within the huge crater that
had formed on the deck.

Those unfortunate enough to survive the ambush were sprawled out,
wailing in pain, creating a terrifying sight.

Shwooong!

Boom!

A deafening explosion echoed as the destroyer next to them erupted into
flames. The explosion created powerful shock waves that shattered the
windows all over the cruise ship.

The last remaining destroyer retaliated, firing at something in the air.
Soon, debris from what looked like a plane came crashing down from the
sky, leaving behind a trail of black smoke in their wake.

Anna finally saw what was going on. The explosion wasn't caused by any
Anomalies. It was caused by an artillery shell.

How come the actions of this newcomer look so familiar? They're
suppressing us with artillery before going all-out and closing in on us.
Was I right? Could it be that the IMF had long known about their plans
to take control of a nation?

Jackal seemed to have seen through Anna's thoughts and shouted, "No,
this isn't how we do things! We always give a warning before making a
move! This ambush is more like an assassination attempt!"

An assassination attempt using missles? Who has that kind of power to do
something like that? Anna pondered.

Just then, the attacker appeared on the horizon. The other party was a
warship, but for some reason, there wasn't anything installed on it—no
gun turrets or any offensive-type weapons except for a radar and utility
equipment.

They also looked similar to the two old destroyers belonging to Anna's
employer.

When the warship was close enough, Anna realized that Jackal was
correct. The warship wasn't sporting the symbol of the IMF—a black
circle with three arrows pointing inward. In other words, the other
party wasn't a part of the IMF.

While Anna was busy observing the ongoing chaos, the door to the bridge
was pushed open, and the beautiful interpreter walked in. Her makeup was
all ruined from the crying.

"What are you guys doing here? Come outside and protect Mr. Raj! He's
injured!"

That guy is still alive? Anna pulled the female interpreter away and
rushed out.

Anna quickly located her employer. He was sprawled out on the deck and
was covering his bleeding wound while panting heavily. Compared to the
brown-bearded man, his condition was much better.

"Who are they?" Anna asked. She presumed her employer had some
information about their enemy, and Anna would never miss any intel about
her enemies.

"They're probably assassins hired by my father to deal with me. He must
have discovered that I'm attempting to overthrow his regime and decided
to eliminate me as soon as possible!" the interpreter said.

The warship showed no signs of stopping and rammed the bow of the luxury
cruise. The deck instantly tilted at a thirty-degree angle. Those caught
off guard were swept to their feet and rolled away.

"Has your father made any contact with people from Subterranean London?"
Anna asked, supporting herself with one hand on the ground.

Before she could receive any answer from her employer, the people
jumping onto the luxury cruise ship told Anna that she had guessed
correctly. After all, ordinary people couldn't possibly jump three
meters high.

A square-faced white man sporting sunglasses landed on the deck. He
scanned Anna and the others with clear arrogance in his eyes. "Ladies
and gentlemen, my apologies, but you will not live to see another day.

"If you want to blame someone, then blame yourselves for following the
wrong employer. Fret not, and feel honored. You are going to die at our
hands. The world ruled by ordinary humans will soon end, and humanity
will soon welcome their new gods!"

The square-faced man waved his hand, and the men in black behind him
hopped onto the deck and proceeded to kill anyone. Their movements were
precise and efficient; there weren't any wasted movements at all.

In addition to ordinary firearms, they wielded what seemed to be psychic
abilities, but it was unknown whether their special ability was from an
Anomaly or not.

Upon coming under heavy fire, the survivors on the brown-bearded man's
side and the black-suited bodyguards of the chubby, tanned man stood up
in defiance. They had no plans of sitting idly by and waiting for their
demise.

"Kill them! Avenge our boss!"

"Team 3, protect the employer! Team 2 and Team 4, provide cover!"

The deck was plunged into chaos once more as a harsh discordant mixture
of screams and gunfire pervaded the deck.

Anna instructed Li Lu to take Gao Zhiming to the cabin downstairs for
his safety before bringing the others onto the deck.

"If the IMF's satellites are overlooking this place, then they'll soon
notice such a big commotion. Let's end this quickly. They're probably on
their way here," Anna said before melting slowly into the ground.

The others glanced at each other before charging into battle.

Regardless of the other party's origins, the fact that they had come
with guns blazing meant that there wasn't any chance for negotiations.
Moreover, they were surrounded by the sea, so they had no choice but to
fight back.

Anna reached out with one hand and pulled a man into the floor.

The man struggled fiercely, and Anna kindly let him go, releasing him
from the influence of her Anomaly. Unfortunately, the man was left stuck
between the floors.

Anna proceeded to drag someone else into the floor. She repeated that
many times until the deck looked like a crop field with "crops" sticking
out of the floor.

However, the enemies weren't dumb enough to leave Anna unchecked,
considering how efficient she was at killing their people. They
dispatched a small team to handle her.

Anna grabbed someone's calf and was about to plant them into the floor
when the latter's figure twisted sharply to look at the floor.

They pulled out a book that seemed to be made of human skin and flipped
open the pages. The next moment, a bloody, gaping maw made out of paper
rushed out of the book and descended toward Anna hidden beneath the
floor.

Anna decisively let go and retreated, narrowly avoiding the bizarre maw.

Anna turned to look at her previous spot and was surprised to find that
a huge chunk of the deck had disappeared. Anna glanced at the book and
found the missing chunk in its pages.

Had she not retreated, she would have already become a part of that
book.

However, the attacks weren't over yet.

A dark yellow fog appeared out of nowhere and engulfed the deck.

Anna held her breath to avoid breathing in the fog, but before she could
do anything else, a group of people appeared to surround her.

Just then, a blurry figure flitted about the group of people and slipped
behind Anna. The figure had taken advantage of Anna's blind spot, and
the latter didn't notice them at all.

Just as the figure was about to pounce on Anna from behind, a mask flew
over and latched onto their face. As soon as the mask landed on their
face, they raised their right hand and rushed at their allies standing
next to them.

A strong gust of wind blew, dispersing the fog.

The clown used his new body to jump around the crowd. His mask would
sometimes latch onto the face of someone else, and he looked like he was
having fun wreaking havoc on their enemies.

The others around Anna were about to attack her when they suddenly fell
to the ground, convulsing with their heads tilted and eyes shut tightly.

The attacker was none other than Olivia.

Chapter 1094 - Daniel

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

It was clear that these people had just obtained their special
abilities, and it was proven by their rough control over their
abilities. Obviously, they weren't a match for Anna and her group.

As the chaotic battle continued, the scales of victory started tilting
toward Anna's side.

Daniel's face was unsightly as he observed the scene before him. This
was completely different from what he had imagined. They were supposed
to make quick work of these people based on the number of people he had
brought with him for this mission.

After taking a closer look, he noticed some strange things. Not everyone
on the opposing side was strong. Those bringing down his subordinates
were always the same few individuals.

The majority of the other side's firepower revolved around those few
people.

Someone was beneath the floor, dragging Daniel's subordinates into it;
someone was wearing a mask and was capable of possessing others; the
last one was nowhere to be found, but the bodies constantly falling to
the floor proved their existence.

This couldn't go on. Daniel knew that he had to take down those few
people, or they would fail this mission!

The clown was about to sever his own throat when a loud clang echoed
followed by sparks flying into the air. The clown was then blasted away
upon taking the brunt of the attack.

A black-suited bodyguard raised his gun to shoot at the attacker, but
his upper body abruptly shattered, exploding into dozens of meat chunks
before he could even pull the trigger.

Anna's heart clenched at the sight. Then, her figure trembled at a high
frequency, and she narrowly avoided getting hit by a bullet. The other
party clearly knew how to make use of Anomalies along with modern
weaponry.

"Roy, take me to their ship!" Anna exclaimed, turning to Roy.

Roy streaked across the skies and landed just above Anna's head. He then
reached out with his right hand to pull her up from the floor. The
gravity acting on the two changed instantly; the blue sky was on their
left, while the chaotic deck was on their right.

Roy and Anna rapidly plummeted, and it just so happened that the other
party's warship was right below them.

High-caliber bullets flew toward them at breakneck speed, but they
simply phased through their figures, all thanks to Anna's special
ability.

Soon, Anna saw rows of heavy anti-materiel sniper rifles standing
vertically on the warship to her left. She let go of Roy's hand, and
gravity's influence on her instantly returned to normal.

She flew like a meteor toward the sniper rifles down below.

Before Anna could arrive, her green, corrosive flames beat her to the
chase. The snipers and their spotters erupted into flames one after
another. In the blink of an eye, they were reduced to ashes, and the
long-range against the people on the luxury cruise no longer existed.

However, the fight wasn't over yet. Since everyone on the warship was an
enemy, Anna spared them no quarter—she killed everyone in sight.

The soldiers on the warship took out various modern weapons to fight
back, but unfortunately, Anna was completely immune to physical attacks.
They were left with no choice but to either die or jump into the sea out
of desperation.

Bodies fell one after another as Anna conducted a slaughter on the
warship.

Anna turned to the bridge and dashed toward it. Her plan was to engulf
the warship in flames. Before she could begin her plan, however, a
purple beam of light shot over from the right, colliding with Anna's
barrier.

Anna turned and saw the other side's leader. He was the same
square-faced man sporting sunglasses from earlier, and his face was
solemn as he stood in front of Anna while pointing a pitch-black,
three-fingered claw at her.

"Oh, you look familiar. Aren't you the one who said that humanity will
soon welcome their new gods?" Anna mocked with one hand on her hip. "How
much did you drink before coming here? You really think you're qualified
to say that?"

Daniel was well aware of just how powerful the Asian woman before him
was; Anna was strong, and to make matters worse, those powerful people
earlier were her subordinates.

He knew that they had hired a team of hunters, but he hadn't expected
this group of hunters to be so abnormally strong. Weren't hunters
supposed to be bullies who only knew how to prey on the weak but cower
before the strong?

Daniel took a deep breath and lowered the withered, three-fingered claw
in his hand, saying, "Hey, friend, there are no grudges between us, so
how about we talk this out? My lord can pay double the amount he offered
you."

If one couldn't beat one's enemies, then one ought to negotiate instead.
It was a reasonable course of action, but obviously, Anna had no plans
to negotiate with him.

"I can kill you, so why should I talk this out with you?" Anna asked,
and the glint in her narrowed eyes sent a chill down Daniel's spine.

"I have to admit that you're strong; you can manipulate flames, and you
can phase through things as well. You're also extremely good at wielding
your abilities.

"However, if you think that you can defeat us with just that, then I'm
afraid you're overestimating yourself!" Daniel declared, looking solemn.

Flames? He saw me use my green, corrosive flames? Anna quietly marked
Daniel for death.

"Or what? Are you telling me that you still have a trump card? You can't
set off a nuclear bomb on a ship, can you?" Daniel asked, smiling
coldly. Then, he threatened, "Your special abilities are from Anomalies,
but not all Anomalies are suitable for human absorption.

"Some particularly dangerous Anomalies remain particularly dangerous
even after being absorbed. One of those anomalies is currently being
held in the lower hold of this ship. With just one word from me, it will
be released, and we'll all be done for!"

"And that's why I'm giving you a chance—leave now, and it's a win-win
for all of us."

Anna pondered over it for a few seconds before laughing. "Oh my, is that
so? All right, then. Actually, I'm not really that loyal to that fat
guy. I'm just protecting him in exchange for money.

"Do you have a speedboat or something on this ship? Give me one. I'll
take my people and leave. You can then deal with that however you like."

"Of course." Daniel nodded and cautiously retreated toward the corridor
behind him. "Please follow me."

Just as Daniel turned around, a beam of purple light flew out of his
armpit and pierced Anna's glabella.

At the same time, Anna made her move. A cluster of green, corrosive
flames stuck to Daniel's back, setting the latter ablaze.

Right before the corrosive flames could reduce him to mere ashes, his
figure split open, and an antique bronze door burst out of him,
devouring Anna's corrosive flames.

When the antique bronze door closed, Daniel's figure was restored. He
looked at the woman in front of him with a solemn face. She had clearly
taken a direct hit from his strongest attack, but she was still
unscathed.

The two had clearly given up on talking this out. Since they were
deceiving each other, a conversation was useless. There was only one
thing they ought to do—fight to the death!

With a mere thought on Anna's end, clusters of green, corrosive flames
manifested and flew toward Daniel.

In response, Daniel took off his top, and the same antique bronze door
from earlier appeared on his chest. He made use of that door to devour
the corrosive flames.

The battle was a fresh experience for Anna, as this was the first time
she encountered someone unafraid of her corrosive flames. After all, the
corrosive flames she could manifest were from a god in the Subterranean
Sea.

Anna deduced that the bizarre door within Daniel wasn't from an Anomaly.

Amidst Anna's relentless attacks, Daniel wasn't idle. He raised the
withered, three-fingered claw in his hand, and different beams of light
flew toward Anna.

A beam of blue light phased through Anna's figure and struck a wall lamp
behind her. The circular wall lamp transformed into a toaster and slid
off the wall, falling to the ground with a loud clang.

Realizing that her corrosive flames were useless, Anna raised her gun
and fired.

Bang, bang, bang!

The sound of metal colliding against metal echoed as the bullet struck
Daniel in the face.

Just where did these guys come from? Anna muttered to herself.

Chapter 1095 - Killing

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Anna used all her strength to reach out toward Daniel's throat with her
hand wreathed in green, corrosive flames.

Daniel leaned backward to avoid the flames that seemed to be capable of
incinerating everything. The fire grazed the fine hairs on his skin,
reducing them to mere ashes.

The cluster of flames on Anna's hand condensed into a ball and flew
toward Daniel's back.

Daniel somersaulted to turn his chest toward the oncoming attack, and
the agape door on his chest devoured the fireball.

Pop!

A rivet popped out from beside Anna's foot, and it flew toward Anna's
calf.

It flew so fast that it left behind an afterimage. Naturally, its
extreme speed meant that the rivet would deal damage no less than a
bullet.

Before the rivet could bury itself into Anna's calf, a crystal popped
out of Anna's flesh, colliding with the oncoming rivet and causing a
crisp clink.

The quick but intense exchange of moves between the two left them
slightly out of breath. However, their expressions revealed no signs of
exhaustion as they faced each other once again.

Anna's expression turned solemn. The man in front of her was different
from those rookies earlier. His mastery over the special abilities
within him was clearly a notch higher than those people.

Moreover, the other party seemed to be probing her abilities.

"Was that telekinesis? You have quite a few things under your sleeve.
Did you obtain them by absorbing Anomalies? Probably not, right?
Ordinary human beings can't possibly handle so many extra 'organs,'"
Anna remarked.

Daniel remained unfazed, seemingly pondering over something.

"I assume your organization is new. What is it called? Perhaps I've
already seen you guys in Subterranean London," Anna added.

Daniel's earpiece vibrated slightly, making him feel a bit relieved.

"You clearly watched too many TV shows. Did you really think I'd be like
the villains in those shows and explain a bunch of things to you before
making a move?

"And if we're talking about villains, then you people are the real
villains. You guys are a group of terrorists trying to take over a
country with your anomalous abilities!"

Daniel's emotional words answered a part of Anna's question. "Oh, I have
no idea that you have quite a strong sense of justice. In that case, why
not join the IMF?"

Daniel's face stiffened, looking slightly stunned to hear that.

Anna noticed that, and she couldn't help but say, "Wait, could it be
that you guys are one of those anomalous organizations that the IMF has
absorbed? Are they so short of people that they have to recruit even the
likes of you?"

Instead of replying, a tinge of smugness spread across Daniel's face.

"Now," he muttered.

Anna staggered and fell to the ground, seemingly frozen in place.

Moments later, the third door to Anna's left was pushed open.

A woman wearing jeans walked out of the cabin with her arms crossed. She
looked smug as she walked up to Daniel and gave him a high-five. "Done.
She can't move a finger now."

Daniel walked up to Anna and pointed the withered, three-fingered claw
in his hand at her head. "Did you really think that I'd fight you
one-on-one? I'm sorry, but all's fair in war."

A purple light converged on the three-fingered claw, and it flew toward
the crown of Anna's head.

Anna's figure trembled at a high frequency, and the purple light phased
through her.

Before Daniel could react, Anna's hand pierced his abdomen. "You surface
dwellers really are ignorant. You think that mental-type special
abilities are rarely seen, so you often use them to deceive others.

"Unfortunately, I'm like your ancestor when it comes to mental-type
abilities. Defending against mental-type attacks is even easier than
eating and drinking for me."

A ruthless smile suffused Anna's lips.

Daniel retreated without any hesitation, kicking off the ground with
both feet. The speed at which Anna could summon those green flames
depended on how far she was from her target.

In other words, he absolutely couldn't stay near her!

Unfortunately, it was too late.

Anna tugged hard, and Daniel immediately felt something being dragged
out of his stomach. He looked down and was stupefied to find that a huge
chunk of his large intestine was being pulled out of his abdomen.

He couldn't let Anna control him, so Daniel decisively swiped at the
intestines using the sharp nails of the withered three-fingered claw in
his hand.

His large intestine was severed, and the pungent filth inside mixed with
blood splattered all over the ground.

The severe injury filled Daniel's veins with adrenaline. He couldn't
feel any pain, but he knew that his time was limited.

Knowing that he was going to die, a determined light flashed in Daniel's
eyes. His figure split open, and a bronze door drilled out of him,
pervading the entire corridor before rushing toward Anna like a runaway
train.

However, he was no match for Anna even without any injuries, so how
could he stand against Anna when he was severely injured?

Soon, the effects of adrenaline faded, and Daniel collapsed to the
ground, clutching his abdomen with no visible wounds. His face was pale,
and he looked utterly exhausted as cold sweat poured down his face due
to the extreme pain.

Daniel used all his strength to look at Anna and saw that the latter had
already murdered his female colleague using his intestines.

"Hehehe, how does it feel to have feces and blood filling up your
stomach? It's not a pleasant sensation, is it?"

Anna casually tossed the purple corpse in her hand to the ground before
walking toward Daniel.

The tables had turned.

Despite that, Daniel laughed boisterously, asking, "Do you really think
that you've won? I lost, but you didn't win, either."

Squelch!

Anna reached into Daniel's skull and directly pulled the latter's brain
out of his skull.

Splat!

Anna hurled Daniel's brain, dripping with cerebrospinal fluid, into the
wall, and it shattered instantly upon impact.

"I have no time to waste talking to you," Anna muttered. She bent down
and snatched the three-fingered claw from Daniel's hand. Then, she left
to help the others on the luxury cruise.

Anna had just taken a few steps forward when a door at the end of the
corridor behind her opened slowly, revealing a chubby man whose greasy
face was marred by acne.

The chubby man's gaze subconsciously landed on Anna's ample breasts, and
his face instantly turned red. He lowered his head, and his hands
awkwardly rubbed the hem of his clothes.

A few seconds later, he looked up again, sweeping his gaze over Anna's
exquisite face. The blush on his face vanished, and he looked more
reserved as he asked, "H-hello, my name is Jin Zhehan. It's nice to meet
you. May I know your name?"

"Huh?" Anna was momentarily at a loss. She had no idea where the chubby
man had come from.

"S-sorry, I-I must've come on too strong. It was rude of me to ask for
your name. I-I'm really sorry; I-it's because I-I have never really
talked to a woman before."

Anna pointed at the corpse on the ground. "Do you know him?"

"I don't know him. What's wrong with him? Why is he sleeping on the
ground?" the chubby man asked, casting a curious gaze at Daniel, who had
no visible wounds.

"All right, just stay by his side. When he wakes up, go up to the deck
and look for me," Anna said. Then, she cautiously made her way to the
deck. It doesn't matter where he came from as long as he stays quiet and
does nothing.

"P-Please wait a moment."

"P-Please wait a moment."

"P-Please wait a moment."

"P-Please wait a moment."

"P-Please wait a moment."

Anna's heart tightened upon hearing the cacophony of five voices with
varying pitch coming from behind her.

Chapter 1096 - Wave

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Upon hearing the Anomaly's words, Anna turned around swiftly, and her

"Wow!! I-Is that magic? That's so cool!"

"Wow!! I-Is that magic? That's so cool!"

"Wow!! I-Is that magic? That's so cool!"

Moments later, two pairs of feet wearing shiny leather shoes stepped

Two middle-aged men, a woman, and an old man walked out at the same

"So, what's your name?"

"So, what's your name?"

"So, what's your name?"

"So, what's your name?"

"So, what's your name?"

Anna realized that something was amiss. A chill ran down the back of her

Anna turned around quickly; she had no intention of paying any attention

Her purple face reflected a tinge of awkwardness as she rubbed her hands

The speaker wasn't just the woman before Anna, as the five people behind
Anna spoke at the same time as well. 𝑓𝓇𝘦ℯ𝘸𝘦𝑏𝓃𝑜𝘷ℯ𝑙.𝑐𝑜𝓂

A collective consciousness like the Moth? Or something else? Anna's
green flames manifested, engulfing the woman before her and the five

Realizing that they could be killed, Anna sighed in relief and rushed

Was that the so-called particularly dangerous Anomaly that he mentioned

Anna shoved open the door leading to the deck, and she was welcomed by

"Scram!" Anna exclaimed. Clusters of green flames manifested and linked

Anna instantly sealed her sea of consciousness. At the moment, she only

Meanwhile, Gao Zhiming was nervously guarding the door of one of the

"Ease up. It's all going to be okay," Tobba said, cradled tenderly

If one were on a battlefront and were on the losing side, one could

"Where's Sis?" Gao Zhiming asked nervously. He immediately searched for

"She went to that ship over there. Look, she's smiling on the deck. I'm

Upon hearing that, Gao Zhiming rushed to the side of the ship and waved

Anna responded and waved at Gao Zhiming as well.

However, Gao Zhiming noticed that something was wrong while Anna was

Gao Zhiming carefully observed Anna and the others. Then, he realized

Gao Zhiming's words attracted the attention of everyone else. They
turned to the distant warship and saw that everyone there was performing
the same movements at the same time.

A crew member took a closer look using a telescope.

"There are even dead people among that crowd!" the frightened crew
member exclaimed, "We have to get out of here now! Everyone on that ship
has turned into monsters!"

Anna had killed quite a few people on the warship, and those murdered by
her were all doing the same movements as her with their mutilated
bodies, creating a terrifying sight.

Everyone reeled in terror at the scene. The terrifying sight—coupled
with the fact that they had just survived an ambush—made the crew move
quickly, turning the ship back on its original route under the
employer's order.

"Wait, Big Sis is still out there! We need to go rescue her!" Gao
Zhiming roared, looking agitated, but no one listened to him.

As the seconds went on, the distant warship became even farther and
farther away.

The desperate Gao Zhiming took out the gun from his waist and pointed it
at the people before him. "Stop the ship!!"

The surviving bodyguards drew their guns and pointed them at Gao
Zhiming. "Kid, put that gun down. It's not a toy. Put it down, or you
better not blame us for being rude."

"Oh? Are you not going to be rude to us as well?" Jackal asked with a
cold smile. He was standing behind Gao Zhiming with the others next to
him.

The employer's face became ugly at the sight. The recent battle had just
proven the other side's strength, and had it not been for them, he would
have died from the ambush.

"He says that as long as you agree to leave now, the commission will be
multiplied by ten," the female interpreter said to Gao Zhiming and the
others.

"No way! We have to go back now! My big sister is still on that ship!"
Gao Zhiming exclaimed.

"No, we don't have to go over there," a crew member said. Then, he
pointed at the warship. "See? They're coming."

Everyone turned at once and found that the warship was moving toward
them at a speed much faster than the luxury cruise.

Chapter 1097 - Variable

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

"Speed up! Hurry up and speed up!!" Anna's employer shouted so loudly
that his voice cracked. He stared in terror at the approaching warship.
He had no idea what exactly had happened on that ship, but he had no
intention of facing what appeared to be undead monsters on its deck.

In stark contrast, Gao Zhiming was delighted to see the incoming
warship. He could finally attempt to save his sister. He turned around
and was about to say something to the others when he saw Jackal and the
others huddled together.

"There's something problematic on that ship, and it even has Anna under
its control. We have no idea how that happened, but the fact that they
easily took control of Anna means that they're difficult to deal with.

"I suggest we retreat. I can take you all with me out here. I can use my
ability to fly us all horizontally. I expect we'd reach the shore in
just one day at the fastest."

Gao Zhiming's heart sank upon hearing Roy's suggestion.

They weren't talking about how to save his sister. Instead, they were
talking about how to escape.

"What about Sis?" Gao Zhiming asked.

Roy turned to look at Gao Zhiming. His face was devoid of any expression
as he blandly remarked, "She can stay there for now. I don't think she's
going to die so easily."

Even though Anna was in danger, Roy's tone of voice remained calm as if
it had nothing to do with him.

"You want to betray the High Priestess?" Jackal asked, his voice
sounding cold as he cast a gaze full of murderous intent at Roy.

Roy instinctively gripped his weapon and shielded his wife and child
behind him while vigilantly eyeing Jackal.

"We're all criminals; what do you mean 'betray'? I simply gave the most
rational suggestion. Of course, if you have a way to save Anna, I can
help you. But look at those things and tell me—do you have a way to save
her?" Roy asked.

Everyone turned to look at the side and saw the warship getting closer
and closer by the second. Anna was on the warship's deck. She was
laughing jubilantly, and so was everyone else around her.

Their smiling faces were reminiscent of sunflowers yearning for the sun.

The cruise ship was truly built for luxury, as its speed couldn't
compare to the destroyer. A collision was imminent, and it was most
likely as close as thirty seconds.

Everyone could already foresee what would happen afterward.

Everyone began panicking. Those preparing for a fight to the death and
those intending to lower the lifeboats were moving about haphazardly.

"We're running out of time. Let's go!" Roy exclaimed.

He was already standing at a ninety-degree angle on a wall while holding
Li Lu up with him. He just had to jump down the wall, and the distorted
effects of gravity on him would pull them away from the ship.

However, neither Jackal, Olivia, nor Gao Zhiming moved to grab his
outstretched hand.

"You're no longer under her control, so what are you still worried
about? All of you are going to die in vain at this rate!" Roy urged.

Swoosh!

Poker cards with words written on them flew out of the clown's top hat.
"Wait a minute. I'm not human. Let me go and try to save her first.
Their ability might not work on me."

The clown jumped and bounced on the ground like a rabbit. He flew over
to the approaching warship, but as soon as his feet made contact with
the deck, he froze and began making the same movements as Anna and the
others.

Everyone's expressions became even more unsightly at the sight. They

Anomaly.

The other party's strength was still unknown to them, but they had
already lost two of their strongest colleagues to them. It seemed like

Gao Zhiming gnashed his teeth. He took out his pistol and fired at the
distant warship.

The crowd on the warship's deck stood there like scarecrows, seemingly
waiting for Gao Zhiming's bullets.

The space around Anna soon became more spacious, but Gao Zhiming's
attacks were ineffective. The whizzing of bullets did little to rouse
Anna; she was still standing on deck, smiling jubilantly.

The bizarre sight convinced Roy that he couldn't wait any longer. He

"Dude, don't run away. Without Anna and the others, we'll be like
sitting ducks once the Foundation[1] finds us."

Roy had long gotten used to his son's manner of speech, and regardless
of Tobba's identity, he would still treat him as his son.

"I'm just going to retreat for now. Once it's safe, I'll come back to
save them."

Tobba took out a glass marble and held it up to his eye to look at the
sun in the sky. "Hey, we're all family here, aren't we? Who are you

"She saved your life, and she saved us as well. We can't be ungrateful

"But what can we do even if we stay here?" Roy explained in a patient
manner.

Tobba played with the glass marble in his hand and said in a childish,

Boom!

As soon as Tobba's words fell, a deafening explosion reverberated. The
two ships had finally collided, hurling everyone on deck away like

The glass marble in Tobba's hand was inadvertently flung toward the
luxury cruise's deck. At the same time, the smiling Anna and the others

"Don't shoot! That's my sister. She's still alive!" Gao Zhiming roared,
sounding agitated.

However, no one listened to him. Those on deck raised their weapons and
immediately fired a hail of bullets toward the approaching enemies.

Left with neither a choice nor time to consider, Gao Zhiming made a
beeline for Anna, pouncing on her to bring her down to the floor.

Gunshots echoed like firecrackers as bullets whizzed past the air,
flying toward Anna and Gao Zhiming. There were so many bullets that
their deaths were virtually guaranteed.

Just then, one of the black-suited bodyguards slipped on a glass marble
and fell to the ground. The glass marble was kicked into the air by the
bodyguard, and it appeared amidst the flurry of bullets.

The glass marble was struck, and it shattered into a myriad fragments
that happened to hit more bullets in midair, slightly altering their
course.

The deflected bullets collided with the other bullets, and just like
that, a chain reaction was set into motion. As a result, the bullets
made holes in just about everything except for Anna and Gao Zhiming on

Just then, a small glass fragment happened to dig into Anna's hair. The
sharp pain instantly awakened Anna, who had completely sealed her sea of
consciousness to prevent the complete takeover of the personality
plague.

Using the final vestiges of her strength, she made herself tremble at a
high frequency. With Gao Zhiming in her arms, Anna phased through the
deck, the water tank, the engine room, and finally, she plopped into the
cold seawater.

Amidst the cold seawater, Anna's face reflected a jubilant, child-like
joy, but her eyes remained placid; it was like her eyes and face were
being controlled by two different entities.

Chapter 1098 - Anomaly

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

A wide variety of thoughts exploded in Anna's mind. Another personality
was tugging at her brain at the moment, but Anna could still handle this

In no time, Anna's expression stabilized, but instead of going to the

The cruise ship above her head moved away, and the bottom of the warship
earlier cast its huge shadow above the two of them.

Anna's trembling figure phased through the steel hull, but Anna found no

Anna wasted no time and found a lifeboat somewhere within the ship.
Then, Anna raised her hand, wreathed in her corrosive flames burning

Any hard material was nothing in the face of Anna's corrosive flames,

The warship was now taking on water, and its speed decreased
dramatically.

Soon, it was a safe distance away from the luxury cruise ship.

Roy promptly rescued the two, but even as they landed on the cruise

This time, her facial features were covered with tears. Hatred gleamed

In the end, she gasped and breathed in ragged pants. She rested for a

"It was really stubborn, but I've finally dealt with it."

Realizing that Anna had returned to normal, the others sighed in relief

Just then, Anna recalled something. She rummaged through the pile of

Anna placed the mask on a corpse's face, and the corpse soon stood up on

Anna sighed in relief. The clown was still alive, which meant that they

Despite having just survived a life-or-death ordeal, the others on the

Anna and the others watched the tilting warship in the distance, and

However, it had become dramatically slower than before. It was so slow

Just then, the clown's poker cards with words written on them shattered
the ice. "High Priestess, what were those things? They actually managed

"It's called a personality plague; it's a special type of mental

keep on replicating itself within the bodies of its hosts until it has
assimilated them all.

"That damned thing is extremely hard to defend against. It has a huge
range of influence, and it hits as abruptly as a plague. With enough
people, it can take over and contaminate an entire island in just an
hour.

"I just realized that it was born as a result of a human being fusing
with an Anomaly."

Just then, Gao Zhiming seemed to have noticed something. He turned to

"Haha, if that happens, it's going to be a really big issue. The IMF
will surely be busy by then," Anna replied, revealing a look of
schadenfreude.

Gao Zhiming was the type to always reply to his big sister's words, but

it?"

"Hmm?" Anna looked at Gao Zhiming in surprise. This was the first time
the young boy's opinion differed from hers. "Why?"

Gao Zhiming frowned, looking like he was facing a very important
question. "I think it's going to kill a lot of people. Many people will
lose their parents, and that's bad... that's really bad.

"If someone wants to hurt us, then we can go kill them. But ordinary
people... we shouldn't let them die."

Gao Zhiming's words drew the gazes of the others around him.

Li Lu was slightly astonished. The fact that Gao Zhiming had been with
Anna for so long yet hadn't been led astray was certainly a rare sight.

Anna lifted Gao Zhiming with both hands and placed him on the railings.
She stared straight into his eyes before asking, "Why do you care so
much about mere strangers? They don't even know you, so even if you save
them, they aren't going to say thank you."

"But... they're all living people, aren't they?"

Anna fell into contemplation upon hearing that. Then, she frowned
slightly and asked, "If you were given a choice to save either mere
strangers or me, who would you choose?"

Gao Zhiming fell into a daze, looking conflicted in the face of Anna's
question.

Anna shook her head in disappointment and raised her voice, saying, "Do
you know that the agents of the IMF in Subterranean London have another
important task in addition to tracking down and eradicating dangerous
organization?

"That task is to find anything that can threaten humanity—that ship
contaminated by a personality plague is an example of that. If someone
tells them the whereabouts of that ship, they'll certainly profit
handsomely."

With that, Anna turned around and walked toward her cabin without
looking back.

Gao Zhiming became anxious at the sight. He was about to chase after
Anna, but Jackal raised his hand, stopping him. "Stay. The High
Priestess has already agreed to your request. What she just said wasn't
for you to hear but for the others ."

The surviving subordinates of the brown-bearded man stared at the
distant warship with fervor rather than fear. 𝒇𝓻𝓮𝓮𝙬𝙚𝒃𝒏𝓸𝙫𝒆𝙡.𝓬𝓸𝒎

"They'll inform the IMF, and the IMF will deal with that threat. Don't
worry, no one will get hurt once the Anomaly Containment Team gets to
it."

Meanwhile, Gao Zhiming's astonishment was far less than Roy and Li Lu's
stupefaction. The couple walked away from the group and whispered to
each other.

"Did you hear that? Anna has actually decided to make a move that will
save many human lives. Am I dreaming here? When did she start acting
like a human being? I mean, I'm sure you still remember how she once
treated humans as mere ritual materials."

"I think Gao Zhiming is the reason behind it. She's been saying that
he's just a very important tool to her, nothing more, nothing less, but
I can feel that she really cares about him."

"What exactly is their relationship?"

"I have no idea..."

The annoyed Anna walked into her cabin and began sorting out her gains
and losses. First of all, she realized that the IMF wasn't closely
monitoring the Indian Ocean.

Otherwise, the IMF's forces would have long descended upon this place.
After all, the battle not too long ago had to have been visible to their
satellites.

Anna initially thought that the square-faced man and his people were the
IMF's outsourced mercenaries, but she was proven wrong when they
unleashed a personality plague as their trump card.

The IMF had too many Anomalies on hand, so there was no way they would
use such a terrifying Anomaly as their trump card.

Chapter 1099 - New Discovery

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

If they weren't from the IMF, then it would be great for Anna.

The commotion was huge, but the IMF had yet to send anyone. This meant
that they weren't actively monitoring the Indian Ocean, or at the very
least, they weren't monitoring it closely.

The shackles binding Anna's mind vanished at this realization.

The fact that she could now create as many Fhtagnists as she could meant
that her group was bound to become larger. Of course, once bitten, twice
shy—Anna would definitely not let the same thing happen again.

She was going to go about it as carefully as possible, and her group had
to be kept an absolute secret.

How about the materials she needed for the conversion ritual? Anna had
long set her sights on the pirates lurking around Somalia. No one would
really care even if those desperate people ended up disappearing.

As for whether their friends would avenge them, that was even less of a
concern.

Anna could just catch them all together.

The recent battle had allowed Anna to earn more than just financial
profit; she had also obtained an Anomaly in the form of a withered and
pitch-black three-fingered claw.

The finger was only the size of a human palm, and it looked like a
monkey's paw.

The three-fingered claw was that square-faced man's weapon. Anna had
made sure to pick it up before leaving that doomed warship.

Anna picked up the claw and tapped it lightly toward the wall. A beam of
purple light shot out from the claw and collided with the wall. The flat
and smooth wall immediately started withering as if it were a flower.

That must be a lethal attack. What about the next one? Anna folded the
middle finger and raised the pinky finger. Then, she aimed it at a
teacup on the table.

Pop!

The teacup transformed into a stack of used tissues. She tapped toward
it once again, and the tissues transformed into a mouse.

Anna had no living creatures on hand, so she couldn't do any experiments
on how the light just now would affect living beings. However, the fact
that her opponent had used that light against her meant that it couldn't
be good for living creatures.

Hmm... This thing's abilities sure are special. Anna imagined what she
could do with this weapon on hand. If the Anomaly's side effect wasn't
that terrifying, it would surely be a useful long-range weapon for Anna.
In other words, allowing Li Lu or someone else to absorb this powerful
Anomaly would be a pity.

Having tested two fingers out of three, Anna decided to test the final
finger.

Anna tapped the air with the final finger, and her surroundings
instantly dimmed.

Moments later, obscure and incomprehensible chantings echoed from the
bed, the door, and just about everywhere.

A colossal monster with a round shell on its back manifested slowly
before Anna. Its shell was covered with yellow-brown filth, and more
than a dozen grotesque-looking forelimbs flailed inside the shell.

Anna's heart began to beat rapidly in the face of the bizarre monster.
Her heart was actually trembling in fear. I'm afraid? I'm afraid of this
thing?

A cluster of green corrosive flames instantly engulfed Anna, but the
flames failed to provide a sense of security to her mind. Instead, her
heart began to beat faster and faster as the seconds went by.

Just as Anna was about to attack the monster in front of her, the
bizarre scene faded slowly. The monster in front of her became smaller
and smaller until it eventually transformed into a mouse on the table.

What on earth is this thing? Anna looked down at the Anomaly in her hand
and found that the final finger, which was supposed to be pointing
forward, had twisted and was pointing at herself.

The sensation just now was brought upon by the special ability of that
final finger.

Okay, now I know what this thing is and its side effect. Anna dispelled
the idea of using it as a long-range weapon. The Anomaly could suddenly
turn on its wielder, after all.

It was a good thing that the side effect had triggered on the final
finger. Had it triggered on the first finger, Anna would have ended up
killing herself.

Anna stood up and stretched lazily.

Well, that's it. This commission somehow managed to meet my
expectations, albeit just barely. Ah, right, there's also the reward
from that fat guy. That's still some money.

As a hunter, Anna had accumulated a large sum of money through shady
means.

However, one could never have too much money. With that in mind, Anna
walked toward the door with the intention of looking for her employer.

Wait. Anna grabbed the doorknob but stopped before she could twist it.
Something popped up in her mind, causing her to stop in her tracks. Why
did that Anomaly's influence on me weaken all of a sudden?

Anna sensed that something was amiss.

She had no recollection of whatever had happened while she was under the
influence of that personality plague, but one thing was for sure—Li Lu,
Jackal, and the others couldn't have dealt with such a dangerous
Anomaly.

Anna gathered the others and asked questions, but they all shook their
heads at her questions. Apparently, they weren't surprised that Anna
managed to free herself from that Anomaly's influence.

After all, she had always been a mysterious figure in their eyes, so it
wasn't strange for her to have some tricks up her sleeve that gave her
freedom from that Anomaly.

However, Anna knew that if she had some viable tricks up her sleeve
against that Anomaly, she would have used them before the Anomaly could
take control over her.

Why would she wait for the Anomaly to take control over her before
making a move?

Anna walked up to the deck and questioned the crew, who were busy with
the clean-up. Then, she stood quietly on deck and made her own
deductions about what had happened.

Anna also had Olivia cross-check everyone's memories. With Olivia's
help, Anna managed to reconstruct the scene and examine it closely for
anything strange.

In no time, Anna noticed something amiss. Earlier, the crew members had
raised their guns and fired upon them indiscriminately, yet not a single
bullet managed to pierce both her and Gao Zhiming.

That was strange.

Someone or something had to have been protecting them, and they were the
ones who had weakened the influence of that personality plague on her.

Is it him? Anna glanced at Gao Zhiming beside her, but she didn't get
any response. She then turned to Jackal. If Charles had left any
contingency plans, there was a chance that Jackal was involved in one of
them.

While Anna was stewing in her own doubts, Tobba was busy gnawing on a
piece of shortcake in Li Lu's arms.

Just as Anna's gaze landed on Tobba, the latter seemed to have sensed
something, prompting him to look up abruptly. The food in his mouth
sprayed everywhere as he exclaimed, "Wook! Shoowing swar!"

The people on the deck looked up at the same time, and they all saw a
black meteor streaking across the sky. The pitch-black meteor left
behind a long tail of light before plunging into the distant sea.

***

Charles made use of his human fleshly body to stare at his offspring who
had just returned from the Core. He listened to her talk about what she
had seen and heard up above.

The offspring standing before Charles looked like a three-headed,
feathered serpent covered in black hair. She looked absolutely
miserable, and some of her bones were even exposed to air.

The offspring didn't look like her father at all, but among those who
were sent to investigate the Core, she was the only one who came back
alive. Perhaps her survival was tied to the fact that she had no eyes.

Charles couldn't completely believe her words, so he split open the
Feathered Serpent's skull and devoured the three brains within.

Chapter 1100 - Reunion

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

One of Charles' offsprings, the Feathered Serpent, possessed no sense of
vision. Instead, she used her tongue to collect odorant particles in the
air to gather information about her surroundings.

In no time, the huge variety of odors that the serpent's tongue had
gathered appeared in Charles' mind. The air was extremely cold up above;
she tasted special molecules in the air as well as some tiny living
beings, so she decided to hold her breath to avoid inhaling them.

Charles was aware of where the Feathered Serpent was at the time, as the
eyeballs he had placed into the latter had failed at that moment.

The Feathered Serpent continued her ascent, and she encountered many
things. The siblings that had gone up with her perished and mutated one
by one until she was the only one remaining.

The higher she flew, the weaker gravity became.

In the end, she became capable of gliding for more than ten minutes with
a single flap of her wings.

Compared to her siblings, the Feathered Serpent was lucky. She almost
reached the upper limit of the map provided by the Foundation; she had
gotten infinitely close to 002.

Before 002, the only thing she perceived was a spicy taste and a rusty
odor that her tongue had picked up. The rusty odor was coming from her
tongue—it was melting.

Just as she was about to descend, the Feathered Serpent felt a powerful
gust of wind brushing across her skin. It felt like something enormous
in the sky had made a move.

002 was moving.

This was the only valuable information that she managed to bring back to
Charles.

Charles split open his belly, took out the brains inside, and placed
them back into the heads of his offspring before waving his hand,
dismissing her.

"Well, it seems that sealing off all senses is one way to approach It,
but if I couldn't observe 002, how would I know what It is and Its
current state?"

Charles was faced with a conundrum. He could safely approach 002 by
sealing off his senses, but how could he observe 002 without his senses?

He sat in place, pondering over the conundrum for a while. Eventually,
he decided to transmit that piece of information to his other selves
before doing anything else.

There were quite a few of them, and each of them had their own task to
deal with. His task was to monitor the Core with five other Charleses
and share any valuable information related to it—that was their duty.

Upon establishing contact with the other Charleses, Charles discovered
that two Charleses had disappeared, including the young Charles from
Lily's plane. It was highly likely that he had already become another
Edikth.

Charles wasn't surprised at all. During the previous meeting, the young
Charles' strength had increased significantly, and it was probably
because he had absorbed too much unnecessary knowledge.

"How much time do I have left?" Charles muttered to himself and made
some calculations using his constantly expanding brain. In the
proverbial blink of an eye, he found an answer. "1 month, 12 days, 4
hours, and 49 minutes.

"The fact that I have this much time has exceeded my expectations. I
thought I only had a few days left at most," Charles remarked with some
relief.

Just then, a cluster of tentacles covered in eyeballs with cross-shaped
pupils manifested behind Charles and embraced him from behind.

Tentacles covered in mucus sprouted from Charles' fleshly body and
patted the cluster of tentacles behind him. "Don't worry, Sparkle. I'm
ready."

Sparkle remained silent, but Charles no longer needed sound as a medium
to know someone else's thoughts. 𝘧𝓇𝑒𝑒𝑤ℯ𝑏𝓃𝘰𝑣ℯ𝘭.𝘤ℴ𝘮

"No, there's no need for you to skip your growth stages. You're my
daughter, not my shadow. You ought to have your own life, and you ought
to make your own choices rather than die with me."

"It's just like how human beings become more mature upon the death of
their parents. I was once afraid of becoming a god, but I am no longer
afraid of it, as I have no regrets and I have a clear conscience.

"I've decided to follow my heart, after all."

"Sparkle, you should also think about the meaning of your life once I've
passed. Think about what you truly want to do from the bottom of your
heart," Charles added.

Sparkle's enormous figure floated over and wrapped around Charles'
fleshly body in a powerful embrace.

A brief silence ensued...

The father and daughter pair enjoyed the rare moment of peace.

"Daddy, I think Mommy made a mistake back then."

"What mistake did she make?"

"She made a mistake teaching me these human emotions. If I couldn't
harbor these feelings, I wouldn't be so sad."

Upon hearing that, Charles thoughtfully replied, "I don't exactly agree
with what you're hinting at, but if you really don't want to feel those
emotions anymore, I can do something about them for you."

"No, there's no need. Besides, it's too late," Sparkle replied. Then,
the indescribable cluster of tentacles covered in fluorescent eyeballs
converged on one point, transforming into Sparkle's gorgeous figure.

A sofa rose from the ground behind them.

Sparkle grabbed Charles' hand, and the two sat on the sofa, silently
gazing at the distant pitch-black sea.

Charles could sense Sparkle's grief, and he said, "If you're still
worried about what is going to happen once you've truly grown up, then
don't worry about it anymore.

"Before I'm gone, I'll alter your body and make sure that you'll live
well."

Instead of responding, Sparkle buried her anguished face into Charles'
chest and shook her head gently. She truly couldn't bear the fact that
