her father would soon no longer be by her side.

Even more unbearable was the fact that Sparkle couldn't do anything
about it, as this was all Charles' decision.

The father and daughter pair soon fell silent, and they gazed at the
desolate sea once again.

Just then, a white mouse glowing with a faint light streaked across the
ink-black sea surface and made a beeline for them.

This mouse scurried to Charles, looking agitated and thrilled. The mouse
climbed up to his thigh and eagerly looked up. Its whiskers trembled
with emotion as it surprisingly spoke, saying, "Mr. Charles! I'm back!
I've come back to help you!"

Charles quietly looked down at the energetic white mouse, and he
couldn't help but recall his first meeting with Lily. Back then, she was
as energetic as she was today.

Unfortunately, things had changed drastically compared to back then. He
had changed a lot, but Lily's changes were even greater than his own.

Instead of continuing to face the mouse, Charles looked up at the
darkness up above, saying, "Lily, you should know that such tricks are
useless against me. I can easily see through your disguise."

As soon as his words fell, the mouse on Charles' leg dissipated into
innumerable specks of light. Moments later, an enormous sun manifested
in the sky, illuminating the sea and the nearby islands.

The enormous sun was none other than the true Lily.

"Mr. Charles, I'm back," Lily remarked, her voice sounding ethereal.

Charles frowned slightly and replied, "You shouldn't have come back. I
even wasted a wish to make you human again and make you leave for your
own plane for good."

"I can help." The enormous sun emitted a wave of radiant light.

"I don't need your help, Lily. I really don't. Go back. You're being
more of a hindrance than a help."

Lily noticed that Charles' feelings toward her were a bit off. "Sparkle,
what's going on with your daddy? I feel like there's something wrong
with him."

Sparkle buried her face into Charles' chest before replying softly, "He
has traded quite a few of his memories in exchange for some things."

Chapter 1101 - Lily

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Charles felt regretful as he stared at Lily standing in front of him.
The reason he sent Lily back to her own plane was so that she could live
a peaceful life like an ordinary child.

Charles had even made use of 005's power to make her human once again,
but it turned out that his efforts were all in vain.

Who did this? As soon as the question popped up in Charles' mind, he
immediately answered it himself. No wonder that kid became a god quicker
than expected; it turns out he created a Divinity!

A long-lost emotion—anger—surged in Charles' heart. The young Charles
from another plane had actually turned his crew member into a Divinity
without his consent.

He had even entrusted that kid to take care of Lily. Was turning Lily
into a brand-new Light God his idea of taking care of someone?! A human
being becoming a Divinity wasn't a good idea at all. To obtain
something, one ought to pay the price for it, and perhaps Lily had
already paid the price.

Meanwhile, Lily was busy thinking about something else.

"Traded? He has traded everything?" Lily muttered to herself, stunned
upon hearing Sparkle's words earlier. It seemed that too many things had
happened while she was away, and she no longer had a grasp on what was
going on.

The mundane days and the moments of suffering through life-or-death
situations with Charles were all... gone? Lily's heart was engulfed by a
complicated mixture of emotions, but overall, she was very displeased.

"Go back. Return to your own plane," Charles said calmly to the massive
sun before him. He sounded so calm that it made Sparkle feel a twinge of
heartache.

"It's me, Mr. Charles." Lily's ethereal voice echoed once again.

She couldn't believe that Charles had traded everything about her away.

"Of course, I know you." Charles nodded. "You're the gunner of the
Narwhale and my crew member, Lily. Unfortunately, you truly cannot help
me with the predicament before me."

Charles' voice no longer sounded calm; he sounded emotional as he said,
"No, you can't just go back like that. I need to strip away your power,
alter your memories, and make you human once again."

With that, Charles' human fleshly body decayed before melting into a
torrent of flesh and blood that fused with the massive flesh island
beneath Charles. Moments later, hundreds of flesh tentacles sprouted
from the cold sea.

The wind howled, and the sea churned as the colossal tentacles wrapped
around the radiant sun in the sky. The colossal tentacles were like
spires bursting from the seafloor as blood vessels and grotesque
rootlets proliferated across them.

The tentacles throbbed as the liquid pumping inside them made them drill
into the radiant sun.

However, the bizarre scene wasn't over just yet; more and more tentacles
burst out of the seawater, intertwining in midair to form a giant tree
trunk of flesh and blood.

There was a sun within the crown of the tree, making it seem like
Charles' flesh was nurturing a radiant sun.

Boom!

A thunderous boom echoed, and the enormous tree collapsed without any
warning. The sea churned, triggering massive waves that swept across the
entire sea region.

Charles' human fleshly body condensed next to his daughter, and he
looked up at Lily in the sky with a complex expression. "You're Lily?
No, you're not Lily. Lily has become just a part of you. You killed the
past Lily!"

A deafening silence descended.

Lily felt bitter. Charles' reaction was definitely not what she wanted
to see.

The silence was shattered by none other than Lily herself, saying, "Yes,
I've changed a lot since then, and let me ask you something, Mr.
Charles. Are you your past self, or did you also kill the past Charles?

"How much of your past self's memories do you still possess?"

Charles couldn't answer Lily's question.

Lily was right. He had indeed murdered his past self. He had changed—no,
everyone had changed a lot.

Charles was silent for a long time before asking, "What exactly do you
want to do?"

"I want to help you. The other Mr. Charles from my plane said that
you're all in trouble, so I'm here to help you. I want to help you, just
like how you helped me back then," Lily replied.

Charles stared deeply at the radiant sun, pondering over something for a
long time.

Eventually, he decided to agree. Lily had already progressed way further
than the point of no return. She had already become a Divinity, so it
was better to make full use of her.

"All right, you can stay here," Charles remarked.

Lily was genuinely delighted to hear that reply, and she felt like all
the hardships she had experienced were worthwhile. The radiant sun
descended and "joyfully" crashed onto the flesh island. 𝘧𝓇𝑒𝑒𝑤ℯ𝑏𝓃𝘰𝑣ℯ𝘭.𝘤ℴ𝘮

The radiant sun nuzzled up against the flesh island, instantly scorching
the flesh and reducing the flesh island's bones to mere ashes.

It was a behavior one would expect from a child, but Lily couldn't care
less. She wanted to nuzzle up to him—she really wanted to become that
carefree little mouse from back then.

"Okay, you're not a mouse anymore, so calm down. Anyway, you should get
used to your body as soon as possible," Charles remarked. Contrary to
his words, however, he truly felt like the little mouse back then had
returned to his side.

"By the way, what can you do?" Charles asked. He wanted to know her
abilities so that he could think about the role that suited her the most
in the inevitable end of the world.

"I can't quite describe the energy within me, but in a nutshell, I can
resurrect and bestow life upon everything," Lily remarked. Then, she
seemed to have recalled something as she added, "I'm going to resurrect
Grandpa Doctor and our friends, but I don't have their souls."

Upon hearing that, Charles was instantly reminded of the old ship
doctor. He couldn't help but start missing the old man, but that feeling
quickly faded away.

"He got to live till ninety-seven. That's a lifespan long enough for
humans, and death is the endpoint of humanity; it is inevitable. If you
disrupt that cycle, then he'll become something else. Believe me, that
probably isn't a good idea."

"If that's the case, then... Mr. Charles, why did you go to great
lengths to resurrect me back then?"

"Did I?" Charles asked. He pondered about it and realized that some
memories related to Lily were missing. Clearly, he had traded those
memories with 005 in exchange for a wish.

"Perhaps it was because I was too naive back then. Now, I've gotten much
more rational," Charles replied.

Lily felt like crying upon hearing that. Sparkle was right. He had
indeed traded away a good chunk of his memories. The Mr. Charles
standing before her had become too unfamiliar to her.

"No, it's not just his memories related to you. Daddy's humanity is
fading away as he gets closer and closer to becoming a god," Sparkle
remarked.

"What exactly happened?" Lily asked. He felt like too many things had
happened while she was happy.

"Don't worry about it; I'll tell you exactly what happened," Charles
replied. Then, one of his tentacles reached out to Lily, transferring
some information to her.

Fhtagn's movement, 002's changes in the Core, and the meeting of the
Charleses for the sake of saving themselves—Lily instantly learned about
them. Charles even transmitted those fragmented, apocalyptic scenes to
Lily's mind.

"Since I became like this, I've been seeing afterimages of the past and
the future on the people around me. And from what I can perceive, those
afterimages seem to depict the future of the world.

"We don't have much time left. We must do everything that we can to
ensure that we won't find ourselves in that hopeless and desperate
future."

Lily absorbed the torrent of information coming from Charles sent over,
and it took her no time to understand why Mr. Charles had been doing
this and that all this time.

"Mr. Charles, I'm going to help you. It doesn't matter even if you no
longer possess those memories and those emotions, you will always be my
Mr. Charles."

Chapter 1102 - Solution

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Charles had lost his memories, but the sacrifices that he had made for
Lily's sake couldn't be erased.

Charles' feelings toward Lily had disappeared, but the latter's feelings
toward Charles would never disappear, even though she had already become
a god. Regardless of what the future was going to be, she would face it
together with him.

"Lily," Charles said, calling out to the dazzling sun in his arms. The
radiant sun was searing him constantly, but Charles didn't seem to mind
it.

"Yes, Mr. Charles? I'm here," Lily replied.

Moments later, several towering tentacles stretched out from the ground,
lifting up the sun. "You should adapt to your new body as soon as
possible. Physical touch is a behavior that suits humans more than us.

"It is unnecessary and unsuitable for us, so you must change."

Lily hovered in midair, slowly orbiting the flesh island. "Mmhm, okay."

Charles nodded in satisfaction, and then he said, "Since you've become
the Light God, you shall inherit the Light God's plan."

If there was anything that could give humans a chance to survive on
other planets, it would undoubtedly be the presence of a sun.

"All right, Mr. Charles," Lily replied, just like how she used to reply
upon accepting the captain's orders on the Narwhale.

The Light God's plan was to select a sufficient number of candidates and
leave the Subterranean Sea when the time was ripe.

"My daughter, go help her," Charles said. He knew that Sparkle's special
ability was going to be helpful when locating great seeds. When needed,
she could also teleport those people before Lily.

"Okay," Sparkle replied. She tapped lightly on the ground with her high
heels and took to the sky, teleporting above Hope Island in an instant.

Of course, Lily was with Sparkle, and the former's radiant light fused
with the sunlight coming from Dawn One. The sunlight pierced Hope
Island's canopy, allowing Lily to "look" at her fellow crew members.

"I'm going to choose everyone!" Lily exclaimed. She thought of her
friends and fellow crew members before everyone else.

First Mate Bandages was quietly signing documents in the Governor's
Mansion...

Boatswain Dipp was working with his wife to track down criminals...

Chief Engineer Audric was drinking blood in an underground bar...

Ship Doctor Linda was using her special abilities to heal people.

Sailor Norton was in prison once again...

They were all Lily and Charles' most cherished friends. Thus, when Lily
heard that they needed to choose people, the first people who came to
her mind were them.

However, Sparkle's words were like a bucket of cold water poured over
Lily's head.

"No, Daddy said that you can only choose ordinary humans. Deep Dwellers,
vampires, immortals, and those who have turned into monsters cannot be
chosen."

"Why?!" Lily asked. She was clearly dissatisfied with Sparkle's reply.

"Because we're trying to save mankind. The crew members you have in mind
are no longer human," Sparkle said. Then, she quietly wrote Nene and her
mother, Donna, on her list of chosen people.

"But they're all our friends. No, I need to go ask him first." The
anxious sun turned around to leave but was blocked by an indescribable
cluster of fluorescent tentacles.

"Don't expect to change his decision using his feelings toward them in
the past. His humanity has been eroded to the point that he no longer
considers emotions as a variable to his decisions."

"No way that's true! And if you insist, then doesn't that mean that he
won't get to leave as well?" the huge sun replied, sounding agitated.

"That's right. He had never considered himself to be one of the chosen
ones in the plan. In just a month, he will become a god. His existence
will end in a month. The contingency plans are all for the sake of
what's going to happen after his demise."

The sun remained silent and unmoving.

Sparkle noticed that and realized that Lily seemed to be stunned by her
words. "He didn't say that to you? Mmm, now that I think about it, you
really don't know anything about that."

A fluorescent tentacle pounced on Lily, transmitting a torrent of
brand-new information into her.

"Then why is Mr. Charles not doing anything about that? He's going to
die!" Lily exclaimed.

Lily's words made Sparkle feel frustrated. "As his daughter, do you
really think I haven't done anything to make him do something? I also
thought of many ways to help and save him.

"Then, I realized that whatever I could think of, he could think of it,
too. The fact that he hasn't been doing anything about it means that the
problem is impossible to resolve.

"His fate was sealed the moment he became a god.

"The only thing we can do right now is help him—we have to help him so
that his sacrifice will not be in vain," Sparkle added in a low voice.

However, Lily clearly couldn't accept that kind of outcome. She had gone
through countless hardships to come back here, and she wasn't willing to
accompany Mr. Charles for just one short month!

The plan to select the seeds of humanity was immediately paused. Lily
was willing to help Charles, but the prerequisite was that he had to
live well. In her heart, Mr. Charles was more important than anything
else!

"We need to save him." Lily decided. The colossal sun left Hope Island
and headed toward the distant, pitch-black sea.

"How are you going to save him?" Sparkle really wanted to hear Lily's
suggestion.

"What if we keep stripping away the knowledge in his brain to suppress
his growth?" Lily asked upon instantly coming up with a possible
solution.

"I've been doing that, but it only delays the inevitable. And Daddy's
special ability allows him to see afterimages of the past and the
future. I can only strip away the current knowledge in his brain; I
can't remove his knowledge of the past.

"His knowledge of the past has been influencing him, and it has been
making him grow uncontrollably," Sparkle replied.

Realizing that the possible solution she had come up with wasn't going
to work, Lily quickly thought of another solution. "Then let's strip
away Mr. Charles' will and separate him from his divine body."

"Don't even think about doing that, Sis. Dad has already become a god.
The will of a god cannot be stripped away. He can't strip away your
will, and you can't strip away his will, either.

"And even if there were a solution, Daddy wouldn't want it. Back when he
had chosen to become a god, he had already made his choice. I guess the
Foundation's destruction has affected Daddy, even though he has been
saying the opposite."

Lily proposed several more solutions, but Sparkle rejected them all.
Charles had come up with those solutions as well, and they were all
problematic when performed on a god.

"Time! Let's modify time! Let time stand still for him, or make him fall
into a time loop. That might be a solution," Lily said, recalling how
she was once trapped in 010's time loop.

"Daddy had thought of that as well, but time contains too much knowledge
to gain mastery over. The vast amount of knowledge will instantly turn
him into a god. In fact, absorbing any knowledge is like ingesting
poison for him."

"I know, so why would he have to do that? He didn't have anyone else on
the same level as him back then, but doesn't he have me now?"

Chapter 1103 - 010

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Green fluorescent tentacles spanning hundreds of meters were piled on
top of the radiant sun that was more than ten kilometers in length.

The two streaked across the sky at breakneck speeds, leaving behind a
green tail that resembled a shooting star's tail.

In the end, Lily still managed to convince Sparkle. Even if the chance
was incredibly low, Sparkle had to attempt it on the off chance that it
could save her father.

She didn't want her daddy to die, and she also didn't want her daddy to
forget her.

"Are you sure that 010 is over here?" Sparkle asked. Since 010
disappeared from the entrance to the Core, it was never seen again.
Thus, Sparkle was doubtful whether Lily was truly aware of its
whereabouts.

The sun's booming and ethereal voice echoed in Sparkle's mind. "I'm sure
of it. I saw it in that plane, and many things happened due to its
special characteristics."

Lily recalled the decades that she had experienced in those time loops.
Soon, the warm sunlight of the sun illuminated the massive ice wall that
seemed to have engulfed the unknown sea region before them.

"Don't touch the snow and the ice here. They're very dangerous," Lily
said thoughtfully before increasing her surface temperature.

Beneath the scorching heat of the sun, the ice wall started melting, and
the humanoid fissures hidden deep in the ice rushed out of the ice. They
stared at the ball of light that was trying to snatch away its spoils.
𝑓𝑟𝑒𝘦𝓌𝑒𝑏𝑛𝑜𝘷𝑒𝘭.𝒸𝘰𝑚

However, the tables had turned completely between the two sides—the
humanoid fissures no longer posed any threat to Lily, unlike their
previous encounter.

As the radiant sunlight illuminated the unknown sea region, the
bone-chilling sea breeze began to turn warm and eventually became
scorching hot. The snow drifting in the air and the icebergs on the sea
surface melted rapidly in the heat.

Soon, 010 emerged from its ice prison and appeared before Sparkle and
Lily. The cluster of interconnected bubbles rapidly shifting colors was
a sight far beyond the limits of human perception.

Thus, this was Lily's first time seeing 010. She heard from Charles that
010 was a special entity capable of altering time. 010 was the reason
why Bandages managed to return to the past.

"What do we do? Do we ask it to control time for us?" Lily asked. She
had no idea what to do next.

"Personally, I suggest you do what Daddy did with the V12 Containment
Site's planar teleporter. Assimilate that thing and use it as if it were
one of your organs," Sparkle replied.

Charles couldn't travel between planes, but he had obtained the ability
to do so by assimilating the entire V12 Containment Site. Sometimes, one
ought to seize certain abilities for themselves, as some abilities just
couldn't be learned.

Before Lily could reply, the distant 010 stirred, expanding and
contracting rhythmically as it slowly faded away.

"Oh no, it's going to run to a different point in time!"

There was a flash of white light, and Lily instantly appeared next to
010. Tentacles covered with fluorescent eyeballs wrapped around 010.

As soon as those tentacles phased through the bubbles, the massive
tentacles retracted rapidly until they were just a few meters long.

010 had turned back time, making Sparkle's limbs regress to when she was
just born.

Meanwhile, 010's figure became almost transparent. It was about to leave
the present time. At the critical juncture, a radiant light erupted from
Lily, engulfing everything, including 010's semi-transparent figure.

Every ray of sunlight was Lily's eyes and limbs. She made use of the
sunlight piercing 010 to latch onto it, preventing it from escaping.
Lily had no idea when she had that ability, but she instinctively made
use of it.

As Lily walked closer and closer to 010, the radiant sunlight became
even more powerful.

The scorching sunlight extended and encroached on the parts of 010's
body, which had already disappeared into different points in time. She
followed the outline of 010's figure, forcing the other parts of 010's
body to manifest in the present time.

"I'm sorry, but I need your power. However, don't worry, I'm going to
let you leave once I've mastered it," Lily said apologetically to 010.
Of course, she had no idea whether the other party could understand her.

010's struggle became even fierce. Semi-transparent tendrils appeared
out of thin air and lashed at Lily, but the attacks were completely
useless against the latter.

"Please don't move, okay? I really don't want to hurt you!" Lily
exclaimed. Her light made the nested bubbles appear as if they were
covered with a layer of gold.

Just then, the surroundings rapidly changed, and Sparkle disappeared
along with it. The tiny icebergs on the sea down below also disappeared.
In no time, the desolate and pitch-black seafloor began to harbor things
that weren't supposed to be there.

Before Lily could react, she caught a glimpse of fishing boats and
exploration vessels scampering off with smoke billowing out of their
smokestacks.

Then, Lily witnessed a fierce battle at sea. However, the ships on the
chaotic sea looked antiquated, unlike the ships that were familiar to
Lily.

The ships down below seemed to rely on sails to move, while the sailors
fighting against each other were dependent on boarding the ships of
their enemies and fighting at close combat quarters.

It was a very peculiar sight for Lily. It had been many years since the
Albion Isles invented the turbine engines, so how could there be such
outdated ships in the Subterranean Sea?

Just then, the wooden ships disappeared, and an even more bizarre scene
unfolded before Lily's eyes. An aircraft carrier appeared before them,
and it was carrying what looked like a colossal key that was several
stories high.

The aircraft carrier's insignia and the unique clothing worn by the
people on the ship reminded Lily that she had seen them before—the
insignia belonged to the Foundation! And the ship was from the
Foundation, too!

The Foundation no longer exists, so why is their ship down there? Lily
recalled the bizarre scenes that she had just witnessed prior to this,
and she immediately came to a certain conclusion.

Oh no, 010 is taking me to the past. Time is going backward for us! Upon
realizing that, Lily immediately tightened her grip on 010. She
absolutely couldn't let go of it, or she would be completely abandoned
in this unknown point in time!

The colossal sun split open like a watermelon, engulfing the nested
bubbles. Then, Lily made use of her sunlight to permeate every inch of
010's figure and examine it closely.

She wanted to learn how 010 was manipulating time. 010's ability to
control time concerned not just Charles' hope for survival, but it was
also the key to whether Lily could return to the present time!

Analyzing a relic was utterly unthinkable for mere humans, but as a
Divinity, it was as easy as taking candy from a baby.

As Lily's sunlight pervaded 010's form, obscure knowledge streamed into
her mind. Lily's radiant figure was immediately tinged with 010's
colors.

This is fine! I just need enough time, and I can definitely learn and
master that power, Lily thought.

The scenery around Lily peeled away. This time, time was no longer going
backward. The flow of time was moving forward rapidly, and it was
getting faster and faster.

Upon realizing that it couldn't deal with Lily using the past, 010
decided to bring Lily to the distant future!

Chapter 1104 - Future

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Under the influence of 010's power, Lily and Sparkle moved rapidly
toward the future. 𝙛𝓻𝒆𝒆𝒘𝙚𝓫𝙣𝙤𝒗𝙚𝓵.𝙘𝙤𝙢

Lily had learned from Charles about what would happen in the future, so
she couldn't say for sure what would happen upon their arrival in that
future.

If Lily wanted to return to the present time, she had to master and
wrest control of time from 010. However, mastering time was still
difficult, even though she had become a god.

The seven-colored sunlight had already permeated 010. With that, Lily
began analyzing 010. Unfortunately for her, 010 didn't possess any organ
like a brain to store information, memories, and thoughts. Otherwise,
Lily would have instantly mastered the ability to control time by
reading its memories.

Lily had no choice but to analyze 010 thoroughly and work backward from
there. In no time, Lily finally saw 010's special characteristics. 010
existed not just at a single point in time; its entire body actually
spanned and overlapped across the entire timeline.

If one were to observe the entirety of 010, one would find that 010
wasn't just an orb made out of colorful nested bubbles. It was actually
a series of colorful bubbles nested along the timeline.

010 was like an elevator shaft, while Lily was inside an elevator that
was rapidly ascending in that elevator shaft. If Lily wanted to master
the ability to control time, she had to master not just the elevator but
the entire elevator shaft as well.

Lily was a god, but it was an extremely difficult task. However, it was
possible, and at this moment, Lily was working as fast as possible.

"Hey, are you okay? Where did you go? It took me a long while to find
you." A voice echoed from up above.

Lily's rapid calculations came to a halt. The voice belonged to Sparkle.
At some point, a cluster of green, fluorescent tentacles had settled on
her radiant figure.

"Sparkle? How did you come here?" Lily asked in surprise.

"I should be the one asking that. I couldn't find you for a long while,
and you suddenly appeared again. I was so startled when that happened,
but I managed to teleport onto you before you could disappear again."

Since Lily and 010 were heading toward the future, it wasn't strange for
them to appear in the present, which had become their future, as they
had come from the past.

Sparkle took advantage of that opening to teleport onto the colossal
sun. Upon hearing Lily's recount, the massive cluster of tentacles split
into hundreds and thousands of tentacles.

The tentacles squirmed and writhed as they dug into Lily's enormous
form.

"Hurry! We don't have enough time! We must go back to the present time
before we reach that doomed future!"

Fortunately, Sparkle was experienced when it came to something like
this, as she often did this to help her Daddy.

Soon, their memories and cognition were shared between each other.
Lily's comprehension rate increased drastically, and the arduous task
suddenly became easy.

Lily's sunlight gradually encroached on 010, which existed in many
different points in time.

Just as Lily and Sparkle were about to focus on the task at hand, they
sensed something, prompting them to cast their gazes outside.

Their sensory organs captured an eerie scene—the pitch-black
subterranean sea had dried up completely, and the rock layer that had to
have been there for thousands of years had disappeared.

The dark Subterranean Sea was no longer dark as purple light had
engulfed the surroundings.

At this point in time, the apocalypse that Charles had predicted had
begun and was, in fact, over.

The stupefied Lily was about to say something when the purple light
above them split open, revealing what looked like a pitch-black circular
silhouette. It was an eye so huge that it encompassed everything,
including Lily's field of vision.

The only thing that could be above the purple light was 002, residing
within the Core. Regardless of Its previous condition, It would awaken
along with the slumbering Fhtagn at some point in the future.

When Lily and Sparkle made eye contact with It, they froze instantly.
Then, they could feel it—something was drilling into their minds.

To make matters worse, they were powerless to do anything against it;
not a single thought of resistance, bidden or unbidden, rose within
their minds. The so-called gods were nothing before It.

No one knew how long it had been since they made eye contact with It;
perhaps it had been a thousand years or a mere few seconds, but the
colossal circular silhouette eventually disappeared, and everything else
vanished along with It.

The hazy purple light from the Core disappeared, and the dried, cracked
seabed vanished as well. A burst of radiant sunlight erupted in all
directions from Lily, but the sunlight made contact with nothing.

Sparkle and Lily immediately understood what was going on.

010's power was still in action, and it brought the two of them to the
future after the end of the world.

However, the pitch-black void outside engulfed Sparkle's heart with both
helplessness and despair. "No, this shouldn't be like this! This is
different from what Daddy and Mommy said! This isn't the surface world!

"I've never been outside the Subterranean Sea, so I have no idea what
they look like, but even if They had destroyed the Earth, the moon and
the sun should still exist outside!

"So why is there nothing here? Not to mention the moon and the sun,
there are no stars, either!"

Sparkle then came up with a terrifying deduction. "Are Fhtagn and 002
really that strong? Did their awakening end up destroying the entire
universe?"

With neither stars nor other habitable planets to live on, the fate of a
myriad beings was sealed.

Lily was so shocked that she stopped analyzing 010.

"This is... the future? This is our... future?" Lily muttered in a daze.
There was nothing after the apocalypse. In this place, even the concept
of time seemed to have become nonexistent.

Just like that, the world became deathly silent.

Without the concept of time, the two stood in a daze amidst the
suffocating environment for an unknown period of time.

Eventually, Sparkle shattered the ice, saying, "Lily, let's continue.
Let's master the ability to control time and go back."

The colossal, dazzling sun trembled slightly. "What's the point of that?
Are we going to accompany Mr. Charles and face this hopeless future
together with him?"

Upon witnessing the grim future, Lily lost all motivation. In stark
contrast, Sparkle didn't lose heart despite the despair-inducing sight.

She turned to Lily and encouraged her, saying, "You know what? When I
was young, Daddy was always outside, busy in his search for the exit to
the surface world, so he rarely had time to teach me anything.

"He wasn't exactly a competent father, but there's one thing I learned
from him—never give up, no matter the problem! If the future is full of
despair and nothingness, then let's take Daddy to the distant past!

"I don't want Daddy to die. I want him to live!"

Chapter 1105 - True Form

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

"The past... The past..." Lily's voice now carried renewed strength.
Sparkle's words seemed to have triggered a new sense of resolve in her

Her deep affection for Charles surged through her.

Sparkle was right. She couldn't give up now. As long as she could master
her control over time, they wouldn't have to head to the future to
accept an inevitable despair. They could evade that by returning to the
past.

Even though that just meant running away from the inevitable, at least
they would survive.

At this thought, Lily resumed her analysis of the 010 that was still
trying to escape. She had to complete her previous task.

Sensing Lily's revived determination, Sparkle immediately assisted on a
mental level.

Without anyone else to disrupt them, the duo made swift progress. As
Lily seized control of other instances of 010 in the other timelines,
she soon came to understand its buried knowledge.

At first, acquiring the knowledge was all too smooth. But it didn't take
long for her to reach a bottleneck.

Understanding time was easy. However, to master control over time was a
different story. Human cognition and comprehension were far from enough.
Under such circumstances, Lily had to change her way of thinking and
elevate it to another level.

010 had no consciousness or personality. Perhaps... she ought to do the
same. Perhaps only by becoming like 010 could she truly master time
itself.

Lily pondered for a few brief seconds before she decided to take this
crucial step. If that was the future awaiting them, then there was
nothing to hesitate about.

The process restarted; a tidal wave of vast knowledge overwhelmed Lily,
scrubbing away and diluting whatever was left of her previous self.
Filtering. Dissolving. Reassembling.

Finally, they began to congeal into a form that would never have existed
in reality.

As Lily absorbed the vast knowledge, her body, the massive sun, began to
form afterimages.

Before her, an amber-hued sunrise flickered into existence. Behind her,
a burnt-orange sunset mirrored it perfectly.

With a crisp crack, the rising sun and setting sun swiftly drew apart.
In the widening space between them, more solar afterimages emerged, each
with varying degrees of hue. They aligned themselves into a long line
before gradually curving to form a perfect ring where the head met the
tail.

What is time?

Time is the measure of the eternal movement of matter.

Time is a manifestation of continuity and sequence.

Time is a parameter invented by humans to describe the change of matter
or the occurrence of events.

As Lily embraced and understood the new knowledge revealed to her, the
sunrise and sunset began to fade—phasing into their respective
timelines.

That was when the truth dawned upon Lily.

010 could freely traverse time, not because it had mastered control over
time. Rather, it had merely mastered control over itself.

Time doesn't exist. It only comes into existence when one needs an
anchor. When one doesn't, it ceases to exist.

She also realized that despite the vast knowledge, they were merely a
key to open a door. Behind that door was everything—the ultimate
"truth."

Now that she held the key in her hand, she only had to open the door.

"Lily! Hold on! Don't forget our goal! You can already control time.
That's enough!" Sparkle cried out.

However, Lily could no longer hear her. The Lily of the past was
completely gone. All that remained now was a puppet guided by mere
instinct.

And the temptation of "truth" was one that no being could resist. None.

Just as Lily was about to take the final step to open the door, the
"key" in her hand was suddenly taken away without any warning.

Time lurched backward.

Everything that she had absorbed into her mind was being pulled out. The
old Lily returned once again. The one who had died in the pursuit of
knowledge now slowly returned to life.

"What's going on? Why? What was that?" Lily could feel that her sunlight
had illuminated something within her vicinity. Something that was
drifting at the edge of reality.

It was a long stretch of scenes, a tangled chaos from overlapping
dimensions and worlds. There was too much information twisted within it.
Despite holding the "key," Lily could barely decipher a sliver—a single
thread at the edge of the maelstrom.

And that was merely because a familiar silhouette had caught her
attention. It was Charles.

Infant Charles—abandoned in a bamboo basket, his tiny body was
shivering, and his lips were blue in the cold.

A dirt-stained Charles—dressed in a ragged, oversized top was standing
on tiptoe to rummage through a trash bin.

Nine-year-old Charles—collapsed on the floor and bloody with bruises.
Yet, he stood guard in front of a woman, and his eyes were burning with
determination.

Teen Charles—wrapping an arm around a woman's alluring curve, he raised
a gun in the other hand and casually pulled the trigger.

Sailor Charles—His back was hunched as he scrolled through the photos on
his phone while tears silently slid down his cheeks.

Captain Charles—Leading his crew, he stood valiant at the very front in
the battle against monsters...

And the scenes went on and on. Lily recognized some of them, while she
couldn't recognize the others.

Every version of Charles interwove into a spiraling, twisting line. But
this was just one line out of the many lines that rippled across the
entity in front of her.

But soon, a new change happened. As the "key" slowly disappeared, and
Lily's consciousness returned to normal, these scenes gradually
collapsed into flesh. The streams of memory fused together and morphed
into severed limbs and pulsing sinew with writhing tentacles.

When all of those things eventually collapsed into mangled flesh, Lily
finally saw the entity that had taken away her "key."

Droplets of green pus dripped from its body. Giant, yellow eyes bulged
and squeezed against each other. Writhing tentacles extended from the
layers of scales and wrinkled skin. All of them came together to form a
massive, spindle-shaped abomination.

Now, Lily truly understood why Divinities were always described as
horrifying and couldn't be written into words. What humans saw was
merely a facade of these entities' actual appearances.

The human mind could never perceive what it had never seen. When they
came face to face with something they could not understand, their
pitiful brains could only attempt to twist the incomprehensible into the
closest thing they could recognize—into monsters.

Lily couldn't recognize the Divinity, but Sparkle found it familiar.

With a hint of hesitation, she called out, "Daddy?"

The massive ochre eye embedded in the center of the spindle stirred
slightly before turning its gaze onto Lily.

Daddy? Is this really Mr. Charles? The Mr. Charles of the future?

Realisation suddenly dawned upon Lily. No wonder she had seen glimpses
of Mr. Charles in this gigantic spindle.

Wait.

Lily tried to recall what Charles had looked like before he had first
taken on this horrifying, monstrous form. However, no matter how hard
she tried, she couldn't recall anything.

Without the "key," she couldn't even remember Charles' true form at all.
Even if she was now a Divinity, there was still no exception.

"Daddy, is it really you? You didn't die in the future, right?" Sparkle
asked; a glimmer of hope suddenly lit up in her eyes. She extended a
dozen tentacles, each one covered with fluorescent green, cross-shaped
pupils.

Soon enough, the spindle responded. From all directions, titanic
tentacles spanning hundreds of kilometres surged forth and gently
wrapped around Lily and Sparkle before drawing them into its body.

Chapter 1106 - Time

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Despite the terrifying and appalling sight, Sparkle wasn't afraid at
all. Instead, her heart was engulfed with boundless warmth.

She reached out to the mass of flesh and blood to gently pluck an
eyeball about the same size as her colossal form. Then, she held the
eyeball in her arms. She was truly delighted. Her daddy was destined to
die, but he ended up defying that destiny and survived.

He was still alive!

While Sparkle was rejoicing, a faint sorrow pervaded the surroundings;
Lily and Sparkle's mood instantly plummeted as if the emotion had
resonated with them.

"Daddy? Are you sad? Why are you sad?"

Edikth remained silent. More and more flesh and blood rushed out of Him
as he embraced them tightly and forcefully.

The direct, physical contact with Edikth's tentacles made Sparkle fall
silent for a while. Then, she seemed to have understood something as she
hugged Edikth tight and wallowed in that faint sorrow together with him.

"Daddy, it's great to see you alive..."

Meanwhile, Lily sensed that something had been added to her mind. Upon
closer inspection, she discovered that it was a bunch of knowledge about
how to control time.

The knowledge was much more streamlined than Lily had expected, and she
could make use of it without transforming into a similar entity as 010.

Lily and Sparkle's minds were still connected, so everything they
obtained would be shared between the two of them. In other words, just
as Lily learned the method to control time, Sparkle became aware of it
as well.

"Now that we can travel through time, can we go look for Mommy, Daddy?
Let's leave this hopeless place together with her," Sparkle suggested.

Edikth's eyes froze. He seemed to be trying His best to remember
something, but in the end, He faded away, leaving behind Sparkle and
Lily.

Edikth had left as mysteriously as He had appeared, leaving the two
stunned. They couldn't grasp His intentions at all.

Just then, the two finally noticed that they were no longer in a void
full of nothingness. They had gone back in time, and it was proven by
how the rock layer overhead and the sea had returned to where they were
supposed to be.

"Was that really Mr. Charles? Why did he not talk to us?" Lily asked.
Edikth's departure without saying even a word made Lily feel troubled.

Meanwhile, Sparkle was already satisfied with the fact that Charles was
going to remain alive in the distant future. Of course, she also had no
idea as to why Edikth had remained silent.

"Don't think too much about it. Since we managed to obtain something,
let's go back as soon as possible. I think we've just obtained the key
to Daddy's survival in the upcoming apocalypse."

"What about the others? What about the humans? Mr. Charles cares a lot
about them, so what will happen to them? What about Hope Island? My
parents? And what about the entire universe?"

Lily obviously cared about a lot more things than Sparkle.

"We shouldn't think about that for now. Anyway, Daddy is still alive.
Daddy's going to take care of everything as long as he's still alive.
Our priority is to go back to that timeline. Don't forget that Fhtagn is
awake right now," Sparkle reminded.

Lily was so astonished by Sparkle's words that she shuddered. Then, she
wasted no time and turned back time to leave their current timeline.

As time moved backward, the devastated rock layer up above healed
rapidly, and the shallow Subterranean Sea became extremely deep once
again.

***

As Charles was still acting as the plug to the only passageway between
the Core and the Subterranean Sea, he had to use his human fleshly body
to stare at his creations.

They were headless spherical fleshly bodies with six pairs of wings on
both sides.

These entities were going to act as scouts, so they were created with
durability and nothing but durability in mind. They were so durable that
they could even withstand Charles' attacks.

In exchange, these creations possessed neither senses nor intelligence.
The sole reason behind their existence was to fly to the Core, get as
close to 002 as possible, and hopefully make it back to tell the tale.

Even though these things possessed no senses nor intelligence, they just
had to make it back to the Subterranean Sea, and Charles could acquire
the information that he needed from their afterimages.

"Protect them with all you have, and remember to get them as close to
002 as possible. The closer, the better. You weren't close enough the
last time," Charles said to the three-headed Feathered Serpent in front
of him.

Charles' other offspring stood behind the three-headed Feathered
Serpent, and their heads were lowered in submission. Their visual organs
had been removed, and their job was to deal with any danger before they
could start making their way to 002.

Charles waved his hand lightly, and the three-headed Feathered Serpent
took to the sky, taking everyone away with her. In no time, they made it
to the tunnel that seemed to be made out of flesh and blood.

Charles had opened it beforehand, allowing them entry into the Core.

Just then, Charles felt two figures pouncing on him from behind.

One of them was a gorgeous woman, and she looked thrilled as she hugged
Charles from behind. Three tentacles sprouted from the ground and
supported Charles, stabilizing him before he could fall to the ground.

"How many times do I have to tell you that physical touch such as
hugging is a human being's gesture of goodwill? It is unnecessary for
us," Charles said, reaching out to remove them from his back.

"Mr. Charles, we found a solution! If we trap you in a time loop, your
time will be extended indefinitely," Lily exclaimed excitedly while
hugging Charles' steel arm to her chest.

"I have long discussed that with my other selves. We don't have enough
time to grasp the knowledge of time."

"No, no!" Sparkle shook her head. "It's not like that. Lily has already
mastered time, and she can use her ability to control time on you."

"You..." Charles turned toward them and was surprised to find that there
weren't any afterimages of the future around them.

"What exactly did you guys do?" Charles asked.

Lily and Sparkle quickly recounted everything that had happened to
Charles. Upon learning that he was still alive in the future, Charles
was even more shocked than them.

I'm still alive? If I'm still alive, does that mean I just watched the
world end? Did I not do anything to prevent it? Or have I settled
everyone down somewhere else? Charles pondered. He was more interested
in what exactly his future self had done than the fact that Lily had
obtained mastery over time.

"Daddy! Let's go and try it! Maybe it's the key to your survival!"
Sparkle urged impatiently. Having found a solution to ensure Charles'
survival, Sparkle was understandably raring for him to try it.

"Lily has already learned to control time, so what's the rush? I need to
think about it very carefully."

Charles froze in place, and the eyes in his eye sockets disappeared as
well.

"What happened to Mr. Charles?" Lily asked, sounding concerned at the
bizarre sight.

Sparkle hopped and sat on her father's shoulder. At the moment, Charles
was like an immovable statue. "What else could it be? He must have
decided to meet with those daddies from different planes. He probably
thinks that it's too rash and risky to use that solution as it is, so he
decided to talk to them first."

Chapter 1107 - New Attempt

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Clearly, Sparkle knew Charles the best. Her guess hit the nail right on
the head. Charles was, indeed, seated at the rectangular table where the
multiple planes overlapped.

The Charleses from all the various planes were seated at the table in
silence. Their gazes were solemn as they absorbed the flood of
information transmitted directly into their minds.

"What do you guys think?" Charles broke the silence after a long pause.

Of course, he wasn't just enquiring about the time manipulation method
Lily had come up with. He was also asking for their opinions on
everything that Lily and Sparkle had experienced.

Compared to Lily's method of controlling time to ensure his survival,
the glimpses they saw of the future carried far more critical
information.

The destined apocalypse happened without any surprise. Yet, his future
self could actually recognize Lily, and even helped her.

This detail clashed with everything they had previously recorded. The
group of Charleses assigned to monitor the behavior of the various
Edikths had confirmed that after ascending to godhood, none of them was
capable of any emotions.

Then, there came the question. If he could help Lily in the future, why
hadn't he sent any helpful information to his current past self?

Was he afraid that it could cause a butterfly effect and disrupt the
meticulously constructed timeline? If that was the case, then would
helping Lily count as disturbing the timeline as well? Or...could it be
that Lily's time travel was all along a predestined event in this
timeline?

Questions and speculations spiraled in Charles' mind. Layers of theories
stacked upon each other endlessly. But whatever the case, all the
possible outcomes were far better than any of the doomsday scenarios he
had envisioned.

"If your daughter claims that she saw you in the future, then it means
you are destined to become Him. Then, perhaps, this time manipulation
method can allow you to become the future you," a blind Charles replied.

The Charleses from the other planes then offered their opinions and
advice.

"Don't overthink it. Now that we have a solution, we ought to give it a
try. The success rate is way better than any of our backup plans."

"Yes, my advice is to try it too. If you fail, we'll make sure to take
good care of your world."

With the layered reassurance and persuasion, Charles found his own
conviction hardening.

"Alright then," Charles said. "I'll temporarily share my vision with all
of you. If I fail, you can learn from my mistake. If I succeed, you can
then follow—"

Yet before he could complete his sentence, another Charles cut him off.

"No, we won't be able to follow your footsteps. We don't have a Light
God from another plane, and neither do we have a demigod daughter born
from a Dioite. You are the only one who can carry out this solution."

Charles's gaze swept over every single one of his counterparts before he
bowed his head slightly. His awareness then left this humanoid vessel
and returned to his true self.

Back at his own plane, Sparkle and Lily were still quietly waiting.
Every now and then, they would exchange a few words with a few of
Charles' descendants.

"Lily, come here," Charles called out.

Lily's heart skipped a beat as she joyfully approached Charles.

The island of flesh beneath them began to tremble slightly. The brain
that was swimming through Charles' immense form slowly rose to the
ground.

"Try your method on my brain," Charles said.

Trapping Charles in a time loop was Lily's idea. But truth to be told,
she was feeling rather nervous about it now. No one knew what the actual
outcome was.

"Don't be scared. Just like before, do what I tell you and it'll be
fine," Charles said as he patted Lily's head gently to ease her anxiety.

If the time loop method truly worked, then the fleeting time he had left
would be indefinitely extended. Originally, he had barely a month
remaining before becoming Edikth, but now, it could become hundreds of
years.

With the luxury of time, he would be able to deal with their current
predicament.

As for the endless boredom and suffocating stillness that would drive
any human mad? For the current Charles, it meant nothing.

"Okay, Mr. Charles," Lily answered.

Her humanoid form then slowly disappeared as beams of iridescent
sunlight shone upon Charles.

Just like the ice that had previously trapped Lily in a time loop,
Lily's sunlight now carried the same power to manipulate time itself.
Wherever her light touched, time would become hers to command.

As the sunlight grew more intense, the countless limbs on Charles began
to twitch and writhe. Charles began to silently calculate how much
information he had received.

The process went on for forty minutes before his entire world seemed to
come to a sudden halt. All the knowledge he had just accumulated
vanished.

A new cycle began!

The method worked!

Just as Charles thought that this method could truly sustain his
existence for centuries, everything abruptly stopped during the
fifteenth cycle.

Thrown into confusion, Charles lifted his gaze toward the hovering sun
above him. This wasn't what he had expected.

"Lily, what happened?"

"Sorry, Mr. Charles," Lily replied in an apologetic tone. "This is my
limit. I just realized that I could only reset time this many times."

Lily realized she had overlooked one crucial factor.

Time could imprison a mortal.

Time could imprison her past self.

Yet, time couldn't completely imprison Charles. After all, he was
considered a god now. No matter if one were to look from the physics or
time aspect, one would know that Charles had become far too "heavy."

"Don't panic. We'll think of another way..." Charles said, his mind
racing for other solutions.

Truth be told, a power like time manipulation had simply too many
experimental possibilities. It was as dangerous as it was intoxicating.

If it weren't for the fact that he would immediately become Edikth upon
seizing this power, Charles would really love to master time
manipulation and experiment with it himself.

A bold idea came to Charles' mind.

"If you can't trap me in time, how about trapping the entire
Subterranean Sea in a time loop?"

"That won't do," Lily immediately turned down the proposition. "If I do
that, everyone in the Subterranean Sea would be killed by my sunlight.
Also, there are too many beings in the Subterranean Sea that carry as
much weight as you do. Unless we exclude them all, I can't fully control
the time over the Subterranean Sea."

Charles' brows furrowed in deep thought.

The cogs in his divine brain turned. With Lily's new ability at the core
of his analysis, he began to formulate alternative solutions. If he
managed to survive in the future, it meant he had found one.

Just as he thought of his future self, a thread of insight flashed
through his mind and nearly slipped away. Fortunately, Charles managed
to catch it before it did.

Wait. If Lily already has the power to control time, isn't it too
insignificant to only use it to trap me in an indefinite time loop? Why
don't we go bigger?

"Lily, can you reverse the flow of time of the entire Subterranean—"

Lily immediately cut Charles off and shook her head violently. "Mr.
Charles, that won't do. That would reverse everything. Babies will no
longer grow up but will shrink until they return to their mothers'
wombs. The dead in the cemeteries will also come back to life one after
another. If time runs backward like that... it'd be terrifying.

"Just answer me this—can you do it?" Charles asked in a firm voice
without explaining further.

Chapter 1108 - Pirates

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

"It should be possible. I... can try, but if birth becomes death and
death becomes birth, then the world will be in chaos," Lily said,
sounding troubled. She was afraid that her ability would harm the humans
in the Subterranean Sea.

Charles looked down at the ground and remarked, "Perhaps compared to the
surface world, we're in chaos at the moment. I believe we're just
restoring the real timeline to its correct state."

Some knowledge about time rapidly drilled into Charles' brain,
drastically reducing the countdown until his ascension to a true god.

However, Charles decided not to throw them away. The knowledge he had
just obtained was very important to him, as it was related to his
destined future. He had just discovered a way that could allow him to
escape with the others.

"Lily, time is not immutable. It is not absolute. It is relative.
Different frames of reference will elicit changes to time.

"Your line of thought was a bit too narrow when you said that you'd only
take us away. We should think of doing something bigger. Perhaps we
might be able to send both the Subterranean Sea and the reason behind
the upcoming apocalypse back to the distant past."

***

At the same time, several modified and dilapidated fishing boats took
advantage of the silence and the darkness of the Indian Ocean to
approach a huge cargo ship in the distance.

"Hey, Hamar! Don't point your gun at your comrades. Damn it, you're
lucky you're my nephew; otherwise, I wouldn't have brought you here,"
said a tanned middle-aged man with his face covered by a black veil. He
sounded exasperated.

Hamar shuddered and lowered his trembling hands clutching a gun. Today
was his first day on the boat. Being nervous was unavoidable. His
comrades cast a gloating look at Hamar.

Hamar stared at the cargo ship in the distance. The cargo ship was so
huge that it was like a floating mountain. Then, he looked down at the
dilapidated fishing boat beneath him and felt worried. 𝗳𝚛𝗲𝕖𝕨𝕖𝗯𝚗𝚘𝕧𝕖𝗹.𝗰𝗼𝕞

"Uncle Jerry, is this really okay? I'm a bit scared."

Jerry pressed down on Hamar's head, making him crouch even lower. "Don't
be such a coward. You're afraid of something as trivial as this? I
thought you wanted to obtain a laptop for your little Isha at home?"

Jerry's words reminded Hamar of the latter's neighbors. As far as he
could remember, those people never returned from their endeavors with
empty pockets.

Hamar took a deep breath and nodded. "I'm not afraid at all!"

"Great. Anyway, a ship of this size only has a few dozen crew members,
and they can't watch over such a large ship spanning hundreds of meters.
Hopefully, there are a few more Americans this time. Hehe, Americans are
the most valuable."

"Isn't America really strong? Is it really okay for us to take their
people hostage?"

"So what? Why would we be afraid of them?! Actually, it doesn't matter
which country they're from; we call the shots out here. They don't count
for shit!" Jerry exclaimed. An old pirate like Jerry had long figured
out the thought process of the leaders of large countries.

They couldn't possibly dispatch the U.S Navy to save just a few
Americans, right?

Jerry concluded that such a decision would be incredibly wasteful; the
outcome wouldn't even be able to cover the fuel costs of saving a few
American citizens.

After all, the ransom was only going to be tens of thousands of dollars,
while dispatching the Navy could cost tens of millions or even hundreds
of millions.

Even a child would know what decision was the best.

More importantly, people as poor as them and were desperate enough to
risk their lives were a dime a dozen in a poor place like Somalia. Even
if one were to kill a batch of them, another batch would quickly
reappear.

In other words, killing them was a meaningless endeavor.

Therefore, most countries would obediently pay the ransom, and that was
it...

And just like that, these modern pirates would make a killing.

The cargo ship was slow, and the fishing boats quickly pulled up to its
side.

"Don't just stand there! Hurry up and move! Throw the grappling hooks!
This ship doesn't have any water cannons! It's a fat piece of meat!
Perhaps we might even find some women for you to have some fun with!"

With a swoosh, several ropes flew out and wrapped around the railings.

Hamar followed his uncle Jerry and climbed up.

Soon, more than ten people finally reached the deck, but the ship had no
lights.

Even the bridge was dark.

Hamar swallowed hard and remarked, "Uncle Jerry, something feels wrong."

"What's wrong? You think this ship is an aircraft carrier or something?
The crew of ships like this are just folks working for a wage. They're
also very timid. Threaten them with a gun and they might even piss
themselves!" Jerry replied. Raising the chipped, semi-automatic gun in
his hand, he slowly made his way into the cabin.

The others stayed silent and carefully followed after their leader.

There were lights in the cabin, but there weren't any people.

Leaving two people at the door just in case, the others conducted a
sweep, searching the rooms one by one with their guns in hand. They
meticulously looked for anyone, especially Americans, who were
definitely hiding at the moment.

Every single room was opened one by one.

Just then, Hamar stopped. He turned to his uncle and signaled that there
were voices within the room ahead of him.

Everyone carefully pressed their ears against the door. The voices in
the room were getting louder and louder; it sounded like they were
chanting something in an unknown language without any consonants.

The other party seemed to be chanting some kind of hymn in this
language. The eerie tone made them feel very spine-chilling when they
heard it.

"Iä! Iä! futar! Ugh! Ugh! Iä futua cf' ayak 'vulgtmm, vugtlagln vulgtmm!
Ai! Shub-Nigath!..."

"Don't be scared. Our prophet will protect us. There has only ever been
one true god," Jerry said. He then took the lead and charged over with
his gun.

He kicked the door open and was instantly struck by a wave of rust. The
smell of blood pervading the room was so thick that it seemed tangible.

It was absolutely fetid.

However, what was unfolding within the room stupefied Jerry more than
the smell of blood. He froze instantly upon clapping his eyes on what
was going on.

There, beneath the dim candlelights, was a group of people clad in black
robes. They were kneeling while devoutly chanting an incomprehensible
prayer toward a mural of a giant flesh tree.

Jerry trembled, realizing that he was in big trouble. He had no idea
what exactly was going on, but he knew one thing for sure—he was in big
trouble.

The chanting abruptly stopped, and the group of people in black robes
turned toward Jerry at the same time. Jerry finally discovered that all
of them were wearing gas masks.

The next second, everyone, including Jerry, went limp, feeling utterly
devoid of strength. The colorless and odorless anesthetic gas had
engulfed Jerry and his group at some point in time.

The terrified Hamar had just turned around to run away, but he only
managed to take two steps forward before collapsing to the ground along
with his other companions.

He couldn't move or speak, but he could see.

And the fact that he could still see made the experience even more
terrifying for Hamar.

The people clad in black robes walked over, and like picking up dead
pigs, they dragged them into the room filled with the thick smell of
blood.

Unable to move, Hamar could only watch helplessly as his uncle was
dismembered alive with a golden dagger.

They arranged his uncle's bones, skin, and flesh into a concentric
circle with some strange patterns in between. Then, Hamar came face to
face with his own fate. He was thrown into the embrace of his
dismembered uncle.

The chanting started again.

Soon, murmurs that plunged those hearing them into a frenzy reverberated
throughout the room. The murmurs and the chanting of the people in black
robes seemed to be resonating—resonating with something in the ocean.

Chapter 1109 - Enemies

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Then, Hamar felt it. He felt that indescribably powerful presence in the
deep sea—the Great One, Fhtagn!

In the face of such power, humans were insignificant. The intense fear
made him follow the instinct ingrained in him as a mere human being—to
worship anything incomprehensible as a divine being.

Only by doing so could he avoid becoming mad.

At this moment, Hamar felt like he could abandon even his wife and
children. In the face of such an indescribably terrifying being, nothing
mattered. If it could make Him stay a little farther away from him,
Hamar was willing to sacrifice his son on the spot.

When they saw Hamar trembling and desperately kowtowing to the colossal
flesh tree on the wall. The fierce-looking black-robed figures kindly
helped him up and draped a black robe over his body.

"Welcome, brother."

The kindness of his brothers and sisters assuaged the fear in Hamar's
heart. Ignoring his trembling legs, he began assisting them, afraid that
the great Fhtagn would scold him for not doing his job well.

He dragged over the people who had just been on the same boat as him and
started disemboweling them. He completely ignored their pleas.

"How many new ones have been added?" Anna sauntered in, nibbling on a
green apple.

"Esteemed High Priestess, we're using half of these pirates as ritual
materials, while half will become one of us. That will be nine people
converted in this batch, so we finally managed to break the two hundred
mark."

Anna nodded without saying anything and turned around to leave the
altar. She had never discovered the identity of her benefactor on that
ship. Aside from that marble, there were no traces of them.

In the end, Anna decided to set that matter aside for now, as she had
more important things to attend to.

Upon learning that the IMF wasn't monitoring the Indian Ocean, Anna
began to rebuild the Fhtagn Covenant. The Fhtagn Covenant was
indispensable at all times, as they were absolutely loyal to her.

To ensure that her movements would remain a secret, most of the converts
were pirates. Their lives were worthless, and no one would come looking
for them even if they ended up dying, making them the perfect targets
for conversion.

With the help of so many zealots who weren't afraid of death, Anna's
overall strength had grown even greater than before.

Anna returned to her room and opened the computer on the table. She
browsed the news, noting the locations disguised as chemical plant
explosions and terrorist attacks.

At this point, she had only one thought in mind—how come they had yet to
fight each other?

Even though she was far away in the Indian Ocean, she could feel the
tense atmosphere between the IMF and the anomalous organizations. The
air between them was becoming tenser and tenser as time went on, but
neither side had made a move just yet.

At the moment, Anna was just waiting for them to declare war on each
other, which would allow her to fish in troubled waters.

There was a creak, and the door to Anna's room opened.

Gao Zhiming wearing a blue T-shirt with a notebook in hand walked into
the room.

He seemed to have heard the screams from the altar.

"Sis, those people are—"

Anna pulled him over and set him down on her firm thighs before hugging
him.

"According to your beliefs, they're bad people, so don't worry about
them. Anyway, let me ask you something—where were you just now?"

"Oh, Sister Li Lu giving some lessons to Tobba, and she called me to
join the lessons," Gao Zhiming replied.

Tobba needs lessons? What doesn't he know? Anna thought. She opened Gao
Zhiming's notebook and saw that it was full of stories about familiar
love and friendship. Clearly, Li Lu came up with these stories on the
fly.

"Tsk." Anna immediately saw through Li Lu's intentions. She was
attempting to subtly change Gao Zhiming's personality, which would
inevitably influence even Anna herself.

He has already claimed several lives. What's the point of teaching him
these things? Anna tossed aside the notebook and placed the half-eaten
green apple with lipstick marks into Gao Zhiming's hand.

Then, Anna gently hugged Gao Zhiming as the latter nibbled on the apple.

Anna closed her eyes and pondered her next course of action. The current
situation wasn't too bad for those hiding in the shadows like them.
However, they still couldn't let their guard down.

Unless they were already as strong as the IMF, they absolutely couldn't
relax.

After a while, Anna decided to call Jackal.

"How's your recruitment going?" Anna asked. They were a hunter
organization, so they needed more hunters as cover. Anna had decided to
establish a large-scale hunter organization.

— It's not going well. Those we contacted through the dark web are very
vigilant. It's difficult to recruit people through the Internet alone.

"Just keep doing that for now. Once we have enough Fhtagnists, we'll
return to Subterranean London. You can recruit them again by then." Anna
believed that regardless of how wary those people were, they would
definitely join in the end.

Those alone were in danger amidst the ongoing situation. If those
hunters didn't want to be blown apart by what was coming, the best way
to do so was to find an organization that would protect them.

— Alright, I understand.

"That's it for now. Keep up the good work. You've worked hard these past
few days. In a few more days, I'll assign some Fhtagnists to assist
you," Anna said. She was about to end the call, but Jackal's words made
her pause.

— High Priestess, wait a moment. Among the pieces of information I have
just received from Subterranean London, there's a piece of news that you
may want to hear. I've emailed it to you.

"Oh? What's with the mystery? Even if you say you have roses for me
instead of great news, I'm still going to be happy," Anna remarked. She
moved the mouse and opened her inbox.

Anna's pupils constricted to the extreme upon seeing the news article
inside, and then her eyes became filled with murderous intent. It was a
blurry, candid photo.

The photo was blurry, but Anna instantly identified the figure in the
photo. 𝕗𝕣𝐞𝐞𝘄𝐞𝚋𝚗𝗼𝘃𝗲𝗹.𝚌𝕠𝚖

It depicted none other than her enemy—the Shattered God!

Actually, Anna's hatred toward the IMF wasn't that extreme. The
relationship between the two had always been antagonistic, after all.
They wanted to kill Anna, and she understood their reasoning behind it.

Anna also wanted to destroy the IMF and kill everyone within it to
ensure that she wouldn't have any headaches in the future. Anna also
believed that they understood her reasoning behind that desire.

Anna despised the despicable and shameless Shattered God more than the
IMF.

A traitor was always more hateful than an enemy. Anna rarely trusted
others, but she once trusted that pile of steel. However, death was the
repayment for her trust.

The Shattered God had destroyed what little Anna had built from scratch.
She wanted to torture the Shattered God until it was left begging for
her mercy!

"Sis, what's wrong?" Gao Zhiming asked, sensing that something was off
with Anna.

"Be a good boy and play outside. I have some business to handle," Anna
said. After shooing Gao Zhiming off her lap, she focused on the news
article.

The Shattered God's believers were pictured moving away their god.

It seemed that the IMF had given the Shattered God permission to move
freely. Of course, they had to have promised that they would never
contain it in the future, but this was just mere speculation.

Only the IMF and the Shattered God knew the details of their deal.

With a few clicks, the text on the picture was enlarged, revealing the
location where the Shattered God was photographed—New South Wales.

"Great, this is the perfect opportunity to test the strength of my
brand-new Fhtagnists." A bloodthirsty gleam flashed in Anna's eyes.

Chapter 1110 - Shattered

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

The night was dark, and the moon in the sky appeared particularly hazy
behind the clouds. It was the perfect weather for an ambush.

Anna led the brand-new Fhtagnists toward their destination deep inside a
forest just next to a national highway. The Shattered God itself was a
massive factory. As long as one was aware of its general location, it
was easy to track it down using the traces that a factory would
inevitably leave behind.

Moreover, the Shattered God had no need to hide at all, as it had
already made a deal with the IMF. With that deal, it no longer had any
enemies except for the late 315.

With the help of the clown and Olivia, the guards clad in camouflage
were quickly taken out. The grass and the trees nearby slowly took on a
yellow, withered color. In addition, the faint smell of machine oil
permeated the air.

Clank, clank, claaank!

Thunderous clanking noises echoed from the distance. A few minutes
later, Anna and her group found themselves staring at a large factory.

The lights from behind the windows and the dense smoke belching out from
the smokestacks were extremely conspicuous in the pitch-black forest.

The surrounding vegetation had died out due to the pollution, while
armed soldiers surrounded the facility. Anna also spotted a few
anti-aircraft weapons within the factory.

The commander in charge, Jackal, ordered everyone to stop. The Shattered
God's highly advanced equipment meant that they would instantly be
detected if they were to move any closer.

Everyone's eyes turned to their leader, Anna. They quietly waited for
her next command. Anna was twirling a lock of her hair with one hand
while gazing at the distant factory, pondering what exactly they were
doing.

What is that thing even doing here?

It didn't make sense for it to come here to avoid the IMF. After all, it
had sold itself out to become a VIP in the IMF. It could totally walk
openly under the sun.

It could be said that the Shattered God had become a completely
different existence compared to the other anomalous beings out there. It
was probably the only anomaly that wasn't under any surveillance.

The wind blew through the surrounding leaves, forcing them to sway left
and right. Anna's eyes narrowed slightly. Although she didn't know what
that thing was up to, her desire for revenge still burned brightly.

Recalling the Shattered God's ugly face, Anna's teeth trembled with
hatred. Anna raised her right hand slightly, and everyone's gaze locked
onto her delicate fingernail.

"Go in, and kill them all. Catch the Shattered God, and we'll
interrogate it later."

Under the clown, Olivia, Roy, and Jackal's lead, three hundred
Fhtagnists dispersed and silently approached the factory. Not all of the
Fhtanists held any weapons in their hands. In fact, some of them had
nothing on them.

Of course, weapons were unnecessary for them, as they had already fused
with an Anomaly. To become even stronger, Anna had used up all her
funds.

Gao Zhiming gripped his gun, wanting to go along with them.

"This is none of your business." Anna stopped him. "Just watch."

Gao Zhiming's lips twitched slightly as he engaged his gun's safety.

Tobba busy licking a lollipop remarked, "That pile of scrap metal is no
match for us at all. Plus, we have the element of surprise on our side.
My special ability also makes me feel that we're going to win."

Bang!

A gunshot rang out from somewhere, and the forest was instantly plunged
into chaos.

The Shattered God's array of artillery weapons came to life. The clown
danced his way into the enemy lines, and moments later, the roaring of
those was silenced.

Gunshots, explosions, and screams echoed nonstop; both sides were full
of fearless cultists, so their clashes were extremely brutal. Blood
splattered into the air, soaking the soil underneath.

However, Anna's side had the advantage. Olivia, capable of attacking the
minds of her targets, and Li Lu, capable of manifesting the abilities of
that withered three-fingered claw, were unstoppable on the battlefield.

After a while, the gunshots slowly died down. The Fhtagnists stormed the
bizarre factory before them, and the battle shifted from outdoors to
indoors.

Those people are killing others for the first time, but they've already
achieved such results. How impressive. Once they've adapted to combat,
they will be no worse than the IMF's mobile task forces.

Anna stepped over the corpses into the factory along with Gao Zhiming
and Tobba.

A rain of bullets whizzed past their heads as soon as they entered the
factory. The battle inside was far more intense and chaotic than
outside. 𝗳𝚛𝚎𝚎𝘄𝕖𝕓𝕟𝕠𝚟𝚎𝕝.𝗰𝕠𝐦

The Shattered God's believers relied on their familiarity with the
factory to engage in guerrilla warfare centered around the machines
within the factory.

Anna looked around and saw that some of them had rivets and gears all
over their bodies and heads—a clear mark that they had changed some of
their organs for mechanical ones.

They seemed unafraid and mostly unfazed by ordinary bullets. Despite
getting shot several times, they could still stand up and counterattack.

Anna had never seen anything like them before. They seemed to be new
products created by the Shattered God.

The stalemate didn't last long. The Fhtagnists hurled grenades, blasting
the machines into smithereens.

Roy streaking across the air flew up and ripped off the factory's roof,
exposing the factory to the outside world and allowing the flames below
to absorb more oxygen in order for them to burn even brighter.

The flames burned ferociously, creating a chain reaction that allowed
them to spread throughout the factory in the proverbial blink of an eye.
The scene was proof that—in case of a factory fire—one had to extinguish
it quickly.

Just as Anna thought that the entire factory would transform into a sea
of flames, some burning debris came tumbling out from the raging
inferno.

They struck the people nearby, emitting crackling and sizzling noises
upon making contact with their flesh.

Soon, a low, strange chanting echoed from behind the flames.

The chanting came from the Shattered God's believers.

"We do not fear the Lord, for It is shattered and incomplete like us. To
repair the body of God is to repair ourselves."

"Blessed is the one who frees the Lord from Its shackles. To free the
Lord from Its shackles is to free ourselves from our shackles. To follow
God is to do good. We glorify the Lord, we worship the Lord, and the
Lord will glorify us. To serve the Lord is to do good, and we shall
serve and protect the Lord."

"We, in ignorance, kneel and repair the Lord. The Lord shall rise from
Its shattered fragments, and the heretics shall tremble at the feet of
the Lord."

Amidst their chanting, a huge hydraulic claw emerged from the flames and
propped itself on the ground.

Moments later, a second, and then a third claw followed suit.

Finally, a bunch of exposed bronze steam machinery appeared. The densely
packed gears, pistons, and steam pipes were more reminiscent of the
style of the Albion Isles in the Subterranean Sea rather than of the
modern world.

A burning flame hovered in the middle of the machinery, and it gleamed
fiercely like the sun.

It was huge. It was almost as tall as the entire factory, and its
enormous figure swayed like a bloated hill made out of steel.

Chapter 1111 - The Trio

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

The constant opening and closing of the furnace door in the middle of
the pile of steel generated terrifying clanking noises along with
powerful heat waves.

Anna instantly recognized it. It was the Shattered God, but compared to
before, when it was just a large piece of steam machinery, the current
Shattered God resembled a movable steam fortress.

It seemed to have pieced together fragments of itself, becoming more
complete.

The Shattered God's betrayal had allowed it to obtain quite a few
benefits from the IMF.

The hollow and incomplete bronze doll was placed at the forefront of the
castle. The constant opening and closing of the furnace door cast
intermittent shadows on the doll's face.

It scanned everyone arrogantly with its eyes made out of bronze gears.
"Who? Who dares to offend the Great God of Cogs and Gears, Mekhane!"

The various machinery in the flames behind it came to life. The steel
plates on those machines clapped at once, extinguishing the flames in an
instant.

The steel glowing hot red from the flames converged in midair and pieced
together to form various weapons. Then, a grotesque, sizzling noise
echoed as they were installed into the Shattered God's believers.

The flames had scorched their bodies so badly that they lost every inch
of their skin, but they still propped themselves up using their
brand-new steel limbs.

Jackal's face turned grim at the sight. This wasn't what the High
Priestess had told him. The Shattered God was far more powerful than she
had described. 𝑓𝘳𝑒𝑒𝓌𝘦𝘣𝘯ℴ𝑣𝘦𝑙.𝘤𝑜𝑚

Just then, gun barrels sprouted from the Shattered God's figure.

"Die, foolish heretics." It drew a bead on its enemies and was about to
unleash a hell of bullets to slaughter the Fhtagnists when—

The Shattered God keeled over backward and fell to the ground.
