The guns swept past the heads of the Fhtagnists and aimed at the sky.

"What?" The Shattered God was shocked to find that its limbs had somehow
melted into the ground. Before it could pull them out, a green pillar of
flames burst out, piercing its bloated figure.

Its steel form slowly melted before being reduced to ashes. The
Shattered God moved swiftly and severed the burning parts of its body
before retreating from the green pillar of flames at breakneck speed.

Despite having survived the sneak attack, the Shattered God didn't
immediately counterattack. Instead, it retreated in fear; its previous
arrogance had vanished without a trace.

The face on the bronze doll revealed deep fear and hesitation as it
stared at the ground. "How is this possible? This is absolutely
impossible! I must be mistaken!"

"Mistaken? No, you're not mistaken. I'm indeed still alive. Long time no
see, my... ally."

The sight of Anna emerging from the ground as if she were walking up a
staircase left the Shattered God with no thoughts of resistance. It
turned around at top speed, preparing to flee for its life.

"315! 315 is still alive!" it roared, its voice filled with panic and
fear. It didn't fear 315, but it feared the existence behind 315. It was
so afraid of it that it couldn't muster even the slightest courage to
put up a bit of resistance.

The only thing it could do was reveal the truth to the IMF and let them
deal with 315.

Swoosh!

Green fireballs manifested before the Shattered God, forcing it to come
to a halt.

"Don't be in such a hurry to leave, my ally. To be honest, I've been
thinking about you a lot recently, and I've been wanting to have a
really good... chat with you."

The bronze doll pleaded, "Miss Anna, I had no choice! I really had no
choice!"

"Do you know? I hate being betrayed the most. I hate it more than my
husband!"

A wave of green flames swept across the Shattered God, and the steel
limbs that it had been using to support itself were severed. The massive
steel fortress collapsed to the ground.

The Shattered God desperately used its severed limbs to scoot backward
as if it were rowing a boat.

Anna grabbed the furnace door that was about to close and opened her
palm, revealing a green fireball.

"No! Don't!! I know I was wrong! I made a mistake!" the bronze doll
pleaded. It was truly terrified.

"So you finally know that you were wrong? Did you not have any idea that
you were about to do something wrong by selling me out?" Anna asked.
Then, she hurled the green fireball into the furnace.

The fiercely burning flames within the furnace were instantly polluted,
taking on a green hue. Unfortunately for the Shattered God, these green
flames weren't satisfied with staying only in the furnace.

They rapidly shuttled through the Shattered God's figure. Before long,
green flames spurted out from various parts of its body.

The Shattered God was going to die; it would suffer the fate of being
devoured by Anna's corrosive flames from inside and out.

"Ahh!! It hurts!!! It's devouring me!! Anna! Spare me, and I'm going to
become your subordinate! My abilities, my believers, and my everything!
They're all going to be yours! Save me!!" the Shattered God roared.

Faced with its pleas, Anna shook her head slightly and replied, "Without
loyalty, it's all meaningless. If you can betray the IMF as easily as
drinking water, you can definitely do the same to me."

Anna stared intently at the Shattered God. She was going to watch it die
a fiery death, but before she could even enjoy the show, the green
flames all over the Shattered God abruptly disappeared.

Then, its broken figure was restored.

What? Everyone present thought the same at the same time.

Anna was about to say something when she suddenly found herself unable
to speak. It wasn't a temporary loss of speech. Instead, her mouth had
disappeared completely. Anna touched where her mouth was supposed to be,
but she felt only a smooth patch of skin.

"Sis, watch out! There's someone behind you!" Gao Zhiming roared.

Anna turned around and saw three humanoid creatures with no facial
features rising slowly from the ground in front of her.

The trio clad in flowing white robes towered roughly three meters tall
as they looked down at everyone from above.

Jackal and the others were about to make a move when one of the trio
waved his right hand. Silver shackles appeared out of thin air,
shackling the hands and feet of everyone present.

The clown flew over, but he was engulfed by a box that was conveniently
just the right size for him.

The Shattered God turned toward the trio as if they were his saviors and
hurriedly shouted, "She's Anna! She's 315! 315 is still alive! She's
still alive! Hurry up and kill her!"

Anna was shackled along with everyone present. She couldn't see the
shackles, but she could feel that they were incredibly durable.

"You're Anna? 315?" the trio asked, looking at the disguised Anna with
some doubt. They found the Shattered God's statement to be hard to
believe.

Meanwhile, Anna stayed silent, pondering over something. The abilities
of the trio were strange—they weren't ordinary psychic abilities. In
addition, their abilities were familiar.

One of the trio waved his hand, and Anna's disguise was wiped clean.

The trio instantly became nervous upon realizing that they were indeed
staring at the real Anna.

"3, go and tell the others that Anna is still alive! Hurry!"

One of the trio, who was just a bit shorter than the other two, vanished
on the spot. The way he disappeared reminded Anna of someone.

Chapter 1112 - Dreamscape

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

"You guys... don't happen to have a colleague named Luvlyn, do you?"
Anna asked. Their abilities reminded her of that woman capable of
pulling people into a dreamscape.

Luvlyn was the reason Anna ended up having two fleshly bodies that
ultimately allowed her to survive the IMF's ambush.

The faceless two lift their heads in surprise.

They hadn't responded, but Anna instantly knew that she had guessed
correctly.

They were in league with Luvlyn. They all had the ability to manipulate
dreams.

Anna deduced that they were inside their dreamscape. Otherwise, it
wouldn't make sense how the Shattered God was still alive despite its
grievous injuries.

Now that she knew the trio's true nature, everything became much easier.
Their special ability was powerful, but it was incredibly easy to
counter.

Anna revealed a sweet smile. With a slight tilt of her head, a huge
steamroller came crashing down toward the trio. Since this was a dream,
anything was possible here.

Before the steamroller could land, it transformed into colorful
butterflies fluttering away in all directions.

Anna's imaginary attack was ineffective against them. After all, she was
in their dreamscape.

The two looked down and found Anna crouched on the ground. She was using
her own blood to draw something on the ground. Anna drew frantically and
quickly; before they could stop her, the array was complete.

It was a bizarre array containing concentric circles with an inverted
triangle inside of them, and the spaces between the circles were filled
with cuneiform lines of varying sizes.

"Iilth ...vwah! uhn'agth fhssh za!!" The incantation sounded like a
rapid tongue-twister, but Anna displayed extreme fluency in it. As she
chanted, the blood on the floor started to coagulate and turn black.

Anna had experience against someone like the two figures before her.
Thanks to her prior experience, dealing with someone like them again was
a piece of cake.

To break free from their dreamscape, one only needed to summon Edikth.

Although He could not be summoned to the surface world, His presence
could be brought out. The presence of the mysterious god would
contaminate their dreamscape and, in turn, contaminate the owners of the
dreamscape themselves.

A peculiar aura began to emanate from the array. The two tried to erase
it from the dreamscape, but the two faceless individuals ended up
clutching their heads with both hands.

The surrounding terrain changed drastically.

The factory and the earth beneath it cracked open, transforming into a
huge valley. Deep rumbling noises echoed as the high cliffs on both
sides moved toward Anna.

Unfortunately, the dreamscape was no longer under their control. It was
like Anna was wrapped in an insulating bubble. Nothing could affect her.

The chanting grew louder and louder, and a crisp noise echoed as Anna's
emaciated figure bent at an impossible angle. Her mouth spewed
incomprehensible and bizarre words, and she chanted while grinning as
widely as she could until the corners of her mouth were torn open.

She had fallen into a reverie.

As Anna's chanting reached a crescendo, the hearts of everyone present
were engulfed by fear. They could sense the presence of something
terrifying; the dreamscape was about to become a horrifying nightmare.

The two wanted to run, but it was too late.

A god's aura had contaminated their dreamscape.

"KYTH ag'xig yyg'far IIQAATH ONGG!!" Anna roared, and the two faceless
figures disintegrated. In the end, their heads exploded.

As soon as they disappeared, the sense of incongruity vanished, but
their surroundings hadn't reverted to their original state. Instead,
everything that had transpired in the dreamscape became real.

The terrain changed drastically; the Shattered God's factory was no
more; and the flat, expansive forest was split into two towering
mountains. Anna and everyone else found themselves standing between the
two mountains.

The moist, healthy soil beneath their feet had been stripped away,
exposing the hard rock at the bottom.

Edikth had transformed what was supposed to be a dream into reality.

Fortunately, the dreamscape had occurred right where they were standing
instead of somewhere else. Anna and the others didn't find a duplicate
fleshly body.

Before everyone could react, Tobba's voice echoed throughout the valley.
"Anna, look! That pile of scrap metal is trying to escape."

Everyone followed Tobba's finger, and they saw the Shattered God
desperately shaking away with its followers following closely behind it.

Knowing that it wasn't Anna's match, the Shattered God immediately made
the best choice possible.

Unfortunately, escape was futile. Before it could attempt to take
shelter in the depths of the two mountains, several green fireballs flew
toward it. 𝘧𝘳𝘦ℯ𝓌𝘦𝒷𝘯𝑜𝑣𝘦𝓁.𝒸𝘰𝓂

It frantically launched missiles to stop the green fireballs, but it was
meaningless.

In the end, the Shattered God was reduced to a burning hunk of metal. It
looked like an erupting volcano as green flames burst out of its figure,
splattering into the air.

The green flames landed on its followers, but the half-human,
half-mechanical beings didn't make any effort to dodge. Instead, they
pounced on the Shattered God and tried their best to extinguish the
green flames on their god's divine body.

When Anna finally caught up to the Shattered God, all of its followers
had already been reduced to a pile of ashes.

The traitor was dead, but Anna didn't feel like she had avenged herself.
She felt like she had just completed a mundane task.

Of course, she didn't really have the luxury of time to celebrate or
reminisce about the Shattered God's betrayal. One of the trio had
escaped, and she had to capture him before he could spread the news of
her resurrection.

Anna was confused as to why the Shattered God hadn't been hiding from
the IMF as well as why it was staying in such a remote place. Now, it
was clear that the Shattered God had a reason for meeting up with the
trio whose origins were still unknown.

"High Priestess, how are we going to find him?" Jackal asked, staring at
the gray, dense forest up ahead with a grim face. "I don't believe we
can find him like this."

It would be like looking for a needle in a haystack. The background of
the trio was unknown, but if he ended up revealing the truth to the IMF,
the consequences would be catastrophic.

"Don't worry, we'll find him." Anna reached into her pocket and pulled
out a translucent conch.

The bright yellow dextral conch was beautiful. The shell contained what
looked like a black substance that would sometimes converge and
separate, but despite its changes, one thing remained the same—the dot
inside it. The dot was unaffected by the changes and was moving toward
the edge of the conch.

"That's..." The others gathered around in surprise. They had been by
Anna's side for quite some time now, but they had never seen her use
such a thing.

The corner of Anna's mouth curled up, revealing a smug smile. "This is a
brand-new Anomaly that I imagined earlier. There's no way he can escape
when I have this."

Anna had already thought about what was going to happen next and decided
to prepare for it.

Edikth's presence could make things in the dreamscape come true, and
everything in the dream could be imagined. Since that was the case, why
not imagine a tracking Anomaly?

Chapter 1113 - Goal

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

If not for the fact that imagining someone would create a duplicate
fleshly body of the target instead of summoning them in front of her,
Anna would have immediately imagined her target to appear in front of
her.

As long as she was still within the dreamscape, anything imagined would
become reality, all thanks to Edikth's aura.

Unfortunately, the two faceless figures earlier had died too early. Anna
still had many things she wanted to imagine.

Anna stared at the patterns on the conch for a while before pointing to
the right. "Let's go. He's to the east. He's not that fast, so we can
definitely catch up."

While shuttling through the dense forest, Anna turned to the others,
saying, "Once we see him later, hold your fire. I want him alive if
possible."

She wanted to know the location of their headquarters. 𝙛𝓻𝒆𝒆𝒘𝙚𝓫𝙣𝙤𝒗𝙚𝓵.𝙘𝙤𝙢

"Your subordinate understands!" Jackal exclaimed, "We can capture him
and make use of his ability. By then, we will have countless Anomalies
in our hands!"

Jackal was so excited at the mere thought of it that he trembled like an
aspen tree. How could he not be excited? After all, they would surely
become even stronger than the IMF by then.

No one would be able to stop God Fhtagn's return by then.

"Hmph, who cares about a few more Anomalies? I'm going to imagine other
things," Anna said, revealing a rare moment of excitement.

Gao Zhiming in her arms looked puzzled. "Sis, what are you going to
imagine?"

"I'm going to imagine my original body. I've had enough with this
fragile human body! It's easily emotional and weak! It's utterly
useless!"

Just then, two green fireballs manifested in the air. They were like two
lanterns as they vanquished both the darkness and the large swaths of
greenery in the surroundings.

Anna stood at the helm of the group, stepping on the thick layer of
ashes before her as she moved forward. Their target was the last
remaining member of the trio from earlier.

According to the patterns on the conch, they were getting closer and
closer to their target. Anna couldn't care less about the other party's
origin. There was no way she was going to let them escape.

Of course, Anna was also planning on using their dreamscape to imagine
her original body.

Since Edikth's aura was capable of distorting dreams into reality, Anna
believed that there wouldn't be any issues materializing a Dioite's
fleshly body.

She had no idea whether it was going to work or not, but she was willing
to try anything as long as there was a sliver of a chance of success.

After all, the reward was just too tempting if the attempt succeeded.
Becoming a Dioite once again meant that Anna would obtain the ability to
freely manipulate human memories. In other words, she would no longer
have to hide like a rat, and she would no longer have any issues
altering Gao Zhiming's memories.

"Sis, what did your original body look like?" Gao Zhiming asked softly,
curious about why his big sister wanted to return to her original body.
For him, his sister was already perfect.

"My original body was beautiful, gorgeous, and... very powerful. You
will never be in danger again once I've returned to my original body,"
Anna replied with a faint smile.

Roy and Li Lu, who were walking behind her, exchanged glances.

Anxiety gripped their hearts, but Roy was even more anxious than Li Lu.
After all, he once saw Anna's original body in the latter's dreams. If
Anna somehow managed to return to her original body, humanity would
probably be in trouble.

However, Roy was utterly powerless to do anything at the moment.
Moreover, their interests were now tied together.

Just then, whirring noises echoed in the distance, shattering the
deathly silence in the dark forest. Moments later, more than ten
helicopters painted with the IMF's logo appeared before everyone.

The bright-white lights from their searchlights cast a long shadow
behind everyone.

"Anomaly 315!! Please drop your weapons, refrain from using any
abilities, and surrender immediately! Otherwise, we will be forced to
commence indiscriminate bombardment!"

"Anomaly 315!! Please drop your weapons, refrain from using any
abilities, and surrender immediately! Otherwise, we will be forced to
commence indiscriminate bombardment!"

A deafening voice echoed from the loudspeakers attached to the
helicopters.

Everyone's faces turned grim. The IMF was here, and they had arrived so
quickly.

Just then, the pitch-black forest came to life, and members of the IMF's
mobile task forces wearing camouflage emerged and aimed their weapons at
Anna and her group.

Among them was a small group of people clad in dark clothes seemingly
woven from dark red feathers. Strangely enough, their eyes were glowing
in the dark, making them appear as though they were yellow ping-pong
balls.

Jackal staggered, almost keeling over backward upon seeing them.

"It's over. We're dead. Even Phoenix is here," Jackal remarked.

Roy and Li Lu's clearance levels were low, but Jackal was a different
story. His clearance was high enough for him to know the true might of
the five-man task force—Mobile Task Force Omicron-24 ("Phoenix").

"Anomaly 315!! Please drop your weapons, refrain from using any
abilities, and surrender immediately! Otherwise, we will be forced to
commence indiscriminate bombardment!"

Upon hearing the warning, Jackal gnashed his teeth and approached Anna
with a solemn face. "High Priestess, hurry up and run away with your
special ability. We'll stay here and hold them off so you can escape.

"If you do manage to escape, you absolutely must not make contact with
any kind of flame for the next three years."

Despite the terrifying line-up, Anna showed not even a hint of panic at
the sight. She put her finger to her lips, bit it lightly, and
immediately started drawing something with her index finger.

Anna was drawing an array, and the peculiar cuneiform lines of varying
sizes within the concentric circles made it clear that she was drawing
the summoning array necessary to summon Edikth.

"You can fool them, but you can't fool me," Anna muttered, "And even if
you managed to inform the IMF, how could they arrive here so quickly
when you're not even an IMF member?

"There's also a fatal flaw in this dreamscape of yours, and that is the
fact that the IMF would never be so polite to me. They've always had
only one thought when facing me—to eliminate me by any means necessary!

"They would never ask me to surrender!"

With that, Anna began chanting the incantation to activate the array.

As soon as Anna's chanting pervaded the surroundings, the helicopters in
the sky and the mobile task force members dissipated like smoke. The
people and the helicopters were just illusions constructed to scare Anna
away.

When the illusion had vanished, Anna stopped chanting. However, Anna
hadn't actually defeated the other party. He had simply decided to
remove Anna from the dreamscape. In other words, Anna could no longer
use the same method that she had just used to contaminate the target's
dream.

However, the target's timely retreat revealed one thing to Anna—they
knew that the array she had just drawn could affect their dreamscape.
This meant that the only method Anna could use against the other party's
dreamscape had been revealed.

"We're speeding up, everyone! Since he managed to drag us into his
dream, he must be nearby!" Anna exclaimed. He looked at the conch in her
hand and started running deeper into the forest.

The last remaining member of the trio was slowing down.

Anna instantly saw her target as soon as she was out of the dense
forest.

It turned out that her target was a woman all along, and she was wearing
a suit. She flitted about the terrain with both hands on the ground,
moving like a leopard. Her destination was a village in the distance.

"Roy! Fly and shoot her legs! We absolutely cannot let her reach that
village!" Anna shouted anxiously. She couldn't let the earlier illusion
come true.

Chapter 1114 - Opportunity

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Gunshots rang out incessantly as Roy streaked across the sky like a
missile and unleashed a hail of bullets against the target. Thanks to
Roy's attacks, the other party didn't have the luxury to contact anyone.

Soon, Anna and the others followed the other party into the village.

It wasn't that difficult for over two hundred people to surround and
isolate just one individual. After a brief commotion, the black-robed
Fhtagnists had finally isolated the owner of the dreamscape—the dreamer.

Anna and the others finally saw her true appearance. Compared to her
three-meter-tall, featureless figure in her dreamscape, the dreamer
looked ordinary and unremarkable in real life.

However, she had one thing that was definitely unique compared to
ordinary people—the metal holes on the left side of her neck. A mere
glance was enough for anyone to tell that the holes on her neck were
ports.

The dreamer gnashed her teeth, glaring fiercely at the Fhtagnists eyeing
her like tigers while pointing their guns at her.

"It doesn't matter where you're from; you only have two choices—you can
either die on the spot or assist me by becoming my subordinate," Anna
said bluntly without wasting any time.

The dreamer put on a fake smile and replied, "Hah, do you really think
that you've won? You're bound to lose here! I've already passed on the
news that you're still alive."

Anna wasn't intimidated at all. "Do you really think I'll believe that a
paltry thief can remember the numbers of any police officers? I don't
believe that you have the IMF's number."

The dreamer hung her head in silence.

Just then, Anna sensed something and frowned. She snatched the gun from
Li Lu's hand and fired at the dreamer's limbs. Blood splattered, and the
dreamer collapsed to the ground.

When the dreamer's corpse landed on the ground, it turned into sand and
melted into the ground.

Bang!

A gunshot rang out as a bullet whizzed past Anna. Moments later, people
in pajamas with weapons in hand charged over to Anna and her group like
beasts.

"Another dream? Do you really think that the same trick will work on
me?" Anna knelt on one knee and started drawing. Once Edikth's aura was
drawn over, the dreamer had to cancel her dreamscape.

As soon as the eerie chanting ended, chaotic murmurs echoed in
everyone's ears.

Everyone present was affected, and they felt a bit agitated.

However, the attackers hadn't disappeared. It turned out that they were
all real.

Anna gritted her teeth. "Kill them all! She's controlling them all!"

With that said, she looked down at the conch in her hand and stared
deeply at it.

Anna left the Fhtagnists to deal with the villagers and led the others
to chase after the dreamer. Of course, Roy took the lead, as he was the
fastest among them.

They had no issues finding her at all. The dreamer's escape was just a
small incident in Anna's eyes, and before long, they found the dreamer
hiding inside a hay barrack.

This time, she wasn't so lucky. She was tied up and dragged to the
village.

The Fhtagnists dragged the dreamer into the village junction covered in
blood. She was tied up on the ground, and she couldn't do anything other
than wait for Anna's decision regarding her ultimate fate.

"You know, I'm actually quite experienced when it comes to
interrogation, so you better not make me angry. I'm trying my best to be
a good person here," Anna said, lightly patting the dreamer's face.

"Now, answer me—what form does your organization take? Is it a company?
A church? Maybe a huge family? How many people like you are out there?"

Anna had done some investigation about whether there were people capable
of controlling dreams in Subterranean London, but she found a big pile
of nothing.

Anna's questions went unanswered, but she wasn't in a hurry as she paced
back and forth on the sticky ground.

"Then, let's change the topic. Since you made a deal with the Shattered
God, why don't we make a deal as well? What do you need? Maybe we can
achieve a win-win situation here."

"You killed my companions, and you dare to talk about a deal with me?"
the dreamer replied, glaring fiercely at Anna.

"No, no, no." Anna wagged her finger at her. "You lot provoked me first.
It was also you who attempted to spread the news about me. It was just
self-defense.

"Anyway, I'm curious. Why are you so afraid of me? My reputation in
Subterranean London should be pretty good. And aren't you supposed to be
happy to see that the legendary figure daring enough to confront the IMF
is still alive?

"Wait, could it be that you're one of the IMF's dogs?"

The dreamer stayed silent and simply stared into Anna's eyes with a cold
smile.

Realizing that the dreamer had no plans of answering her question, Anna
smiled slightly. The clown noticed that and tacitly handed over a sharp
knife to Anna.

Anna used the knife to trim her nails lightly in front of the dreamer.
Then, she took out a notebook from her arms and tossed it over.

"Li Lu, take Gao Zhiming with you to study somewhere else," Anna said,
sounding a bit colder than earlier.

Li Lu seemed to know what Anna was going to do next, so she immediately
turned around to leave with Gao Zhiming in tow.

Anna grabbed the dreamer's chin and twisted it hard.

Crack!

The dreamer's jaw was dislocated, rendering her unable to bite her
tongue to end her own life or shatter the poison capsule on her teeth.

Her props were simple, but Anna could inflict great pain on the dreamer
using just about anything. Before long, all of the dreamer's fingernails
had been removed, and the fresh wound was then sprinkled with a layer of
salt.

The dreamer trembled from the extreme pain, but she still didn't yield.
The look in her eyes didn't change even in the slightest.

"You've got guts. Let's proceed with the teeth, then. Do you know? The
nerves in the teeth are extremely sensitive; they are much more
sensitive than any other nerve," Anna remarked.

Before the knife could explore the dreamer's mouth, the dreamer threw
her head forward. Shockingly, she melted like liquid into Anna's body.

Anna reacted quickly and was about to say something when a bloody hand
sprouted from her neck, covering her mouth.

The others tried to help Anna, but the bloody ground trembled like a
carpet, flinging them away.

The scenery peeled away immediately afterward.

Anna and the dreamer were in a dreamscape once again.

"Hehe, I've gotten a hold of your weakness. You can't break my
dreamcatcher if you can't speak or draw that array!"

Just then, rows of rusty torture devices emerged from the ground. Of
course, Anna knew why the dreamer had imagined torture devices. Anna
closed her eyes and imagined the torture devices as rows of large
candies instead.

The torture devices transformed, but they immediately returned to their
original appearance.

"315, I told you that you're bound to lose! I'm a god in this
dreamscape! Now, I'm going to pay you back tenfold—no, a hundredfold!
I'll make you suffer in this dreamscape!

"Those people are your subordinates, aren't they? Well, I'll have all
those men come over here and run you through!" the dreamer exclaimed.

Right as she was about to take revenge, however, a bizarre chanting that
sounded like a rapid tongue-twister echoed from afar. Everyone present
instantly felt agitated upon hearing the chanting.

In the distance, Gao Zhiming was chanting the incantation written on the
notebook in his hand. Edikth's summoning array was already drawn in
front of him, and a peculiar aura was wafting out of it.

A hint of amusement flashed in Anna's eyes. How could she not be wary of
the dreamer when she could unknowingly pull people into her dreamscape?

She didn't necessarily have to summon Edikth herself. Anyone could do
it.

Upon realizing that she wouldn't be able to obtain any information from
the dreamer, she concluded that it was better to imagine what she wanted
into reality as soon as possible.

The dreamer trembled upon hearing the familiar incantation. She
immediately tried to retract her dreamcatcher, but she despaired upon
discovering that she could no longer do so.

The bizarre and eerie array was like a thumbtack, pinning her dreamscape
in place.

Anna closed her eyes and carefully imagined what she wanted to obtain
once again. When she opened her eyes again, she found herself staring at
a monster.

The monster had gray tendrils all over its body; its huge eyes with
yellow cross-shaped pupils resembled lanterns, and it had over a dozen
writhing tentacles.

Chapter 1115 - The Diois Tribe

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

When the dream turned into reality, the dreamer disappeared. It seemed
like Edikth's aura had taken her away, but no one could say for sure. It
was unknown whether she was still alive or dead.

However, Anna couldn't care less about the fate of the mysterious
dreamer. She cared more about the wriggling monster before her, as it
was none other than a Dioite.

Anna was staring at her former body and former race. She was truly
powerful and beautiful back then. Anna had been feeling frustrated about
her weak human body, but she would no longer feel that way from now on.

She could finally abandon her fragile human body.

In stark contrast, everyone else cast wary gazes at the monster. Their
hands involuntarily gripped their weapons, including Jackal and the
loyal Fhtagnists.

Although they believed in the so-called Fhtagn, they weren't exactly a
fan of slimy, wriggling monsters. After all, they were still human
beings, and no human being could possibly like such a monster.

"Is that... your original body?" Roy asked, swallowing hard.

"Yes, what do you think? Isn't it beautiful?" Anna replied.

As soon as those words came out of Anna's mouth, everyone's wary gazes
instantly shifted to her. Even Gao Zhiming was staring at her the same
way as the others.

Only they were aware of their own thoughts, but the fear on their faces
made it obvious that their thoughts couldn't be anything good.
𝑓𝑟𝑒𝘦𝓌𝑒𝑏𝑛𝑜𝘷𝑒𝘭.𝒸𝘰𝑚

However, Anna was too lazy to care about their thoughts.

She was eager to use her ability as a Dioite for something.

In the bizarre Subterranean Sea, a Dioite capable of altering memories
and cognition at any time wasn't exactly a terrifying existence. There
were many beings far more terrifying than a Dioite.

On the surface world, however, it was a different story. The Anomalies
on the surface world were too weak and fragile, and there were also too
many humans for Anna to dominate.

Anna was confident that she could give the IMF a hard time once she had
covered her original body.

More importantly, she could easily alter Gao Zhiming's memories by then.
In other words, if Anna became a Dioite once again, she could easily
ensure Gao Zhiming's fall into the Subterranean Sea.

"Knife, give me a knife," Anna remarked.

The clown walked over and gave her a small knife.

Anna started cutting her hair immediately afterward.

As a former member of the Diois Tribe, Anna was extremely familiar with
a Dioite, and she knew that it was incredibly difficult to transfer a
human's consciousness and memories to somewhere else.

However, Anna had thought one step ahead, ensuring that the imagined
Dioite's sea of consciousness was empty. In other words, Anna just
needed to dig out her brain and place it into the empty space.

"Watch it closely, everyone. Don't look away, even for a moment. If it
shows any signs of transforming into a human body, shoot its tentacles
immediately," Anna said to the Fhtagnists while giving herself a
haircut.

The Dioite that she had worked so hard to obtain could become a human
being and run away, but Anna wasn't going to let that happen.

Fortunately, it seemed that her worries were unnecessary, as the Dioite
had never moved since it was imagined.

"Li Lu, come and help me. I can't shave the back of my hair," Anna said,
handing over the knife to Li Lu and lowering her head.

With Li Lu's help, Anna's thick hair was severed into large chunks. At
this moment, it was extremely quiet. There weren't any other noises
except for the sound of Anna's hair being cut.

Just as a lock of Anna's long hair was about to be cut, a hand covered
in scars snatched away the knife in Li Lu's hand.

"Anna, are you sure you want to do this? You should think about it
again." An exhausted voice echoed beside Anna. The voice sounded hoarse
and wasn't pleasant to the ears at all.

However, Anna had long grown accustomed to it. It was her man's voice,
after all.

She turned her head slightly, glaring impatiently at Charles standing
beside her.

"Are you kidding me? Have you forgotten what the IMF has done to us?
This is a great opportunity to become even stronger and fight back
against them, yet you're telling me to think about it again?

"I'm telling you this just in case, but there is no room for a
negotiation here. You better not force my hand! I still haven't talked
to you about my daughter! You absolutely can't be relied on for
anything!

"My fate is in my own hands, and I'm the one who's going to decide it!"

Charles was about to say something else when a panicked voice
interrupted their conversation. "High Priestess! That octopus monster
has disappeared!"

"What?!"

Everyone looked to the right and found that the motionless monster had
somehow disappeared.

Charles subconsciously touched the holster at his waist. "Don't panic,
we're in a remote place, so it couldn't have run away.

"It must be among us right now."

Charles swept his gaze across everyone's face.

Anna looked around, showing no panic on her face. Instead, a cold smile
tugged at her lips. "How interesting. That thing actually dares to play
hide-and-seek with us. Could it be that it hasn't discovered my original
identity through my memories?

"This is really absurd; it's like a novice showing off before an
expert."

"Let me do it for you," Charles replied, "You should rest and think
carefully about whether you really want to become a Dioite again or
not."

Upon learning the special abilities of the vanished monster, the people
nearby became uneasy as they exchanged glances with their companions.

Charles frowned and browsed his memories. In no time, he recalled the
unique characteristics of a Dioite and concluded a few things. Firstly,
the Dioite would surely target a core member of Anna's group rather than
just an ordinary Fhtagnist.

A Dioite's primary source of nutrients and energy was human. It had to
become a core member of Anna's group, or it would struggle to find any
food. Secondly, it could temporarily alter memories, but it couldn't
alter any photos.

With that in mind, Charles confiscated everyone's cellphones, and he
checked their galleries one by one. Unfortunately, Charles' method
wasn't effective. Among the people present, no one was idle enough to
take photos for no reason at all.

Charles remembered the footprints that they had made all over the
island, and he began looking for them. Sadly, the earlier chaos had
destroyed the trail, which meant that there were no footprints to
examine.

It was a stalemate, but the issue wasn't that big of a deal.

After all, there was only one Dioite, and they were aware of its special
abilities.

It would be a bit difficult to find it. After all, they weren't out at
sea, and no one would randomly write something similar to a navigation
log like back then.

"Why overthink it? Have you forgotten how you killed me back then? A
Dioite's blood takes on a different color compared to human blood.
Everyone just needs to cut themselves, and we can check them one by
one."

Anna stepped forward to take command over everyone when strange noises
echoed from the house next door. Charles waved his hand lightly, and the
others immediately walked over with a little girl in tow.

Chapter 1116 - Charles

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

"Maybe we were wrong, and that thing didn't join us but turned into this
little girl?"

Anna and the others looked at the little girl in front of them with some
doubt.

Anna wounded the little girl's arm, and red blood flowed out of the
wound.

"Are you sure the color of their blood is different?" Charles said,
sounding doubtful.

Anna glared at Charles and replied, "You made so many holes in me back
then. Do you not remember the color of the liquid that flowed out of me
at the time?"

Charles tried hard to recall the scene from that year, but he couldn't
remember the tiny details. After all, it had been such a long time.

"High Priestess, we should just kill her first, just in case," a
Fhtagnist said, taking out his dagger.

Charles raised his steel arm and stopped the Fhtagnist. "There's no need
for that. Since her blood is red, she's not a Dioite."

The Fhtagnist holding the dagger didn't reply but instead looked at
Anna, awaiting her orders.

Anna glanced at Charles and the little girl. She sounded surprised as
she asked, "Why do you sound anxious for her? That's funny. You're good
at protecting the daughters of other people, but you couldn't even
protect your daughter."

Charles looked at her with furrowed brows. "She's a person—a living
person. And don't talk about taking a life so lightly."

"Spare me the hypocrisy, will you? Do you not have any idea how many
people have died at your hands so far? And you're actually pretending to
be a good person despite that?" Anna said, expressing great disdain for
Charles' behavior.

Charles couldn't be bothered to argue with Anna. After comforting the
little girl in a low voice, he looked up at his wife and said,
"Seriously, do you really want to give up your human body and become a
Dioite again?"

"What do you think? I've been working so hard for this, and you still
think that I'm not sure?" Anna answered without any hesitation.

Charles walked up to her and placed his hands on her soft shoulders
before gazing at her with a complicated expression.

"If you have something to say, say it! Why are you being so mysterious?
We're still in this damned place; we don't have the luxury of time for
some chitchat. Perhaps that Dioite is hiding somewhere, enjoying this
show."

Anna was very dissatisfied with Charles' actions and behavior.

Charles fell into deep contemplation. After a long while, he sternly
said, "Anna, you're human now—a living, breathing human. You've finally
become a human being, and I don't want you to become a monster again."

"Did you really think I became human when you shoved me into a human
body?" Anna asked, smiling faintly at Charles. "When are you going to
change that bad habit of yours? You always take the liberty of deciding
for someone else."

"And what monster are you talking about? You're not much better than
me."

Charles took a deep breath and suppressed the annoyance within. "Anna,
since you've finally become human, stay human and don't go back down
here. I'll take care of Sparkle for us."

"You want me to rely on you?" Anna's face showed a hint of contempt. "If
you're that reliable, then send Sparkle over here. Otherwise, don't even
bother spouting nonsense!

"And you'd better show me some respect! Are you even qualified to give
me advice?" Anna shoved Charles away. At the same time, a cold gleam
shot out from between her fingers and grazed the back of Charles' hand.

A wound appeared on Charles' hand, but instead of a blood-red liquid,
some kind of translucent purple juice had flowed out of the wound.

Everyone instantly saw through Charles' identity. He was a fake; he was
none other than that monster.

Realizing that his cover was blown, Charles' scarred head cracked open.
The next moment, massive tentacles burst out of him as he ran
frantically into the distance.

However, the Fhtagnists instantly surrounded him. Unfortunately, a
Dioite with their cover blown wouldn't be able to escape at all.

Everyone pounced on him.

Soon, the Dioite was nailed to wooden stakes and lay spread-eagled on
the ground.

"As I said, I'm considered your ancestor when it comes to altering
memories. Don't you think you're underestimating me by altering just my
memories?"

The giant tentacled monster's flesh writhed and transformed into a
humanoid figure. The figure was none other than Charles. In the face of
its impending death, the Dioite created a temporary personality that
showed no fear before Anna.

Its emotions didn't really fluctuate as it said, "Anna, it's me. I'm
Charles."

"Stop pretending. You're just a fake."

"I'm a fake, but you're a fake as well. Am I wrong?"

The Dioite's words seemed to have touched Anna's reverse scale. She
snatched a gun from someone's hand and started shooting at Charles.
After just a few bullets, Charles was reduced to rotten pieces of torn
flesh.

"How dare a newborn like you compare yourself to me. I've been living
for many years, and I can confidently say that Anna is real!"

The rotten pieces of torn flesh wriggled and converged on one point to
transform into a human fleshly body. Charles looked at Anna with a
complex face and remarked, "You're pretending that you don't care, but I
disagree.

"You still care about your identity. I admit that you've become the real
Anna, but if you return to this body, you'll find yourself under the
influence of a Dioite's fleshly body. By then, will you still be the
real Anna?"

Surprisingly, Anna quietly lowered the gun in her hand without refuting
anything.

"If you return to this body, then you will be the same as me—a mere
amalgamation of a human being's dying thoughts. I really don't want you
to become like this."

Upon saying that, Charles' face revealed a tinge of realization as he
added, "You know what? When I realized that I was just a copy of Charles
from your memories as a Dioite, I got this one and only urge to change
your mind."

Charles had never thought about hurting others. Everything that he had
done so far was all for the sake of making Anna change her mind.

"Anna, you should cherish your current life. Don't go back there. I'm
begging you. If you return to the Subterranean Sea, everything will
repeat itself, and you will die!

"Stay up here and don't go back! I can shoulder the despair all by
myself!!"

Bang!

The barely formed human head was torn to shreds by a bullet.

Anna's eyes glimmered with extreme hatred as she exclaimed. "Do you
really think that you can make me let you off by doing all that? Dream
on! This trick will never work on me!"

With that, Anna walked over with a small knife in her hand. She
immediately peeled away the Dioite's sticky skin, revealing what was
inside its skull. Then, she reached in and grabbed the cerebellum
containing the Dioite's temporary personality.

The Dioite used all its strength to manifest Charles's torn upper body.

"Anna, I can feel it. You're slowly becoming a human being. Don't throw
that away, okay?"

"I don't need you to tell me what to do, you impostor!"

Anna pinched the cerebellum, and the newborn Charles collapsed into a
pile of rotten flesh, disappearing not even ten minutes after he was
born.

Chapter 1117 - Big Changes

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Anna's thirteen main tentacles quivered as she swept across everyone's
memories. With a mere will on her end, Anna could alter the memories and
twist the personalities of everyone present.

The feeling of having control over everyone's fate was simply wonderful.

Anna felt a kind of comfort that she had never experienced before upon
realizing that she was back in a Dioite's body. This was her true body;
her fragile, human body wasn't hers.

Anna's yellow cross-shaped pupils constricted, and she opened her
grotesque maw, letting out a spine-chilling, piercing roar. Licks of
green flames and clusters of crystals in a variety of colors sprouted
from her mucus-covered skin, making her appear even more terrifying.

The Fhtagnists present instantly knelt on the ground, praying devoutly.
The fear in their eyes had disappeared and was replaced by a hint of
fanaticism. Many people considered Anna's transformation as a reward
from Fhtagn to His High Priestess.

However, Roy and the others didn't share the same thought as the
Fhtagnists.

Their expressions were ugly as they stared at Anna from a distance.

They had long suspected that Anna wasn't a human being, but now that she
had transformed into a monster before them, they had no idea what to do.
In the end, they couldn't help but ponder what kind of trouble she would
bring to humanity.

"You don't need to think about it. You're my subordinates, so all you
have to do is obey. Do you understand?" Anna asked with a half-smile.
Her tentacles had retracted at some point, and she had returned to her
human form.

A powerful aura swept across everyone. They dared not raise even the
slightest complaint before Anna. In fact, they dared not entertain even
that thought as they realized that Anna could now freely examine their
memories.

"Of course, my little darling is the exception," Anna remarked, walking
over to Gao Zhiming with a smile. She picked him up and kissed him on
the cheek.

Gao Zhiming was in a daze. He still couldn't process what he had
witnessed. He had no idea why his grown-up self suddenly became a
monster, why his sister had sent herself into the monster's mouth, and
why the monster became his big sister.

"You don't need to know about what happened. You just need to know that
from now on, we won't have to hide anymore. We can live a much better
life," Anna remarked. Her steps were light, and she looked cheerful.

Just then, she turned to her first subordinate and said, "Jackal, our
goal has changed. We're going to fan the flames."

The Shattered God was no more, and it was the IMF's turn.

In the past, fighting the IMF would be like trying to crack open a
boulder with an egg, but Anna had become a Dioite once again. In other
words, she no longer needed to avoid them.

"Your subordinate understands, High Priestess!" Jackal exclaimed,
looking excited. He could feel that Anna was going to do something big.

While they were in the car on their way back, Anna told Gao Zhiming
about what had happened, her true identity, and what Dioites were. Anna
had previously said that she would tell Gao Zhiming everything, and she
was going to keep her word.

There was no need to hide such trivial matters from the little boy.

Soon, Gao Zhiming finally understood what was going on. He couldn't help
but feel afraid of his monster big sister. Then, disregarding his fear,
Gao Zhiming sounded slightly bitter as he asked, "Are you going to alter
my memories, Big Sis?"

Anna's smile faded. It seemed that it was time to alter Gao Zhiming's
memories. Gao Zhiming's childhood ought to be a happy and peaceful one;
the world of anomalous entities wasn't a world for children.

"Once my memories are... altered... will I forget you, Big Sis? Will I
forget everyone else, too?" Gao Zhiming asked, sounding downcast.

The young Gao Zhiming wasn't exactly sure of what was going to happen
once his memories were altered, but he instinctively felt sad.

"If that's the case, then... can you let me say goodbye to Tobba? He's
my one and only friend," Gao Zhiming said.

Anna was moved by Gao Zhiming's words. Compared to that scumbag, the
innocent Gao Zhiming before her was really hard to hate.

"No worries. We still have some unfinished business. Once we've dealt
with them, then we can talk about this again," Anna remarked.

Gao Zhiming jumped up in joy upon hearing that. He wrapped his arms
around Anna's neck and exclaimed, "Yay! So I'll get to stay by Big Sis'
side for a while then!"

Looking down at the squirming Gao Zhiming in her arms, a tinge of
hesitation flitted across Anna's face. Was altering Gao Zhiming's
memories—a decision that she had been postponing again and again—really
the right thing to do?

Unfortunately, it was inevitable, so perhaps the delay would only make
Gao Zhiming suffer more in the future. With that in mind, Anna
immediately got ready to alter Gao Zhiming's memories.

When Anna thought about it a bit longer, she realized that they weren't
in a position that would allow Gao Zhiming to live a quiet and peaceful
childhood. I can alter his memories now, but I won't be able to send him
back to that city.

It's better to wait until the IMF has been dealt with. Anna thought. For
some reason, she felt much more at ease upon making this decision.

***

Dipp was standing on a rooftop somewhere on sunny Hope Island. His arms
were crossed as he swept his gaze across the streets before him.

As always, the Deep Dweller was still covered in green scales. The only
thing that changed was that he now had three eyes on his forehead. The
three eyes resembling fish's eyes were looking in different directions.

Evidently, they were under the control of his other personas. Dipp's
vision had almost no blind spots. He could virtually see from every
angle. Dipp's three personas began to communicate with each other in his
mind.

"Haaa, this is so boring. There's hardly anyone willing to commit a
crime, especially in the central district. My feet are already numb from
all the standing that we've been doing." 𝓯𝓻𝓮𝙚𝙬𝓮𝙗𝒏𝙤𝒗𝙚𝙡.𝒄𝒐𝓶

"I think it's because they can see us here... that must be the reason
they dare not make a move. Why don't we go to the harbor district?
Remember that serial killer from a few days ago? Maybe we can find them
ourselves."

"That's not our case to handle. Let's not disturb their work. Anyway,
why don't we just go back inside?"

"No, we came out here for some fresh air because staying in there was
just too boring. It hasn't even been that long since we walked out of
there."

"I say let's just quit this managerial position. This kind of work
doesn't really suit us."

"Forget it. The First Mate gave that position to us, so let's just be
patient. Once a possible replacement pops up, we'll go back to District
3."

Dipp had been standing on the rooftop for quite a while now, so he was
feeling a bit thirsty. He raised the canteen in his hand and opened his
mouth full of sharp teeth to drink some water.

Just then, time itself seemed to come to a gradual halt. The stream of
fresh water falling from the canteen froze in mid-air. Then, it rushed
into Dipp's mouth before retracting into the bottle—again and again.

Dipp wasn't the only one witnessing such a bizarre scene. Similar scenes
were happening not just in Hope Island but throughout the Subterranean
Sea.

Chapter 1118 - Time

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

The bizarre temporal distortion affected not only Dipp but everyone else
as well. Just like him, they all froze in place, stuck at a certain
point in time, endlessly doing whatever they were doing in a loop.

The fresh water plunged and retracted dozens of times between Dipp's
mouth and the canteen. Eventually, time returned to normal, and the
water finally entered Dipp's mouth.

However, Dipp couldn't perceive the bizarre phenomenon, so he had no
idea that it even happened in the first place. After gulping down a few
mouthfuls of fresh water, he wiped his mouth clean and took to the
skies.

Kicking off against the roof, he landed nonchalantly on the street down
below and grabbed a copy of Hope Island Daily from the nearby newsstand.

A special advertisement was on the front page of today's newspaper.

Fantastic news! The miniaturized differential analyzer for civilian use
will soon be available for purchase! Dear readers, I'm sure you don't
know what a differential analyzer is, but I'm here to tell you that it's
a product ahead of its time!

This new product from the Relic Research Institute has been named
'computer' by the Governor; it can help you calculate, record, and even
solve your problems! I dare say that it's the first man-made,
controllable relic on Hope Island!

Most importantly, it can be mass-produced without any side effects. At
the moment, only two models are available for purchase—the X1 Series,
which runs on electricity, and the X2 Series, which runs on fuel.

The X2 Series' smokestacks are miniaturized, so please keep in mind to
use conventional fuel to power it. The products stated will be available
for purchase on September 2nd—

Dipp didn't bother to continue reading and simply flipped to the next
page. He pursed his lips and remarked, "Every day, they always say that
a product ahead of its time has been made. How many times have they used
that line at this point?

"Can't they come up with some phrases to use? I'm definitely smarter
than the writer of that ad."

The content on the other side of the newspaper was much more normal than
that on the front page, so it instantly captured Dipp's attention.

The Governor of Whereto's private physician has clarified the Governor's
inexplicable fainting—"Our esteemed Governor is in perfect health; she's
not under any curse nor has she been poisoned. The Governor's fainting
spell was caused by a recent episode of binge eating and drinking in
addition to her obesity.

The physician has also expressed confidence in treating the Governor's
obesity. The physician has stated that the Governor's health will be
restored in the next three to six months, and she will appear in public
by then.

I hope nothing serious will happen. The Governor of Whereto... isn't she
the Captain's woman? Should we go and visit her? Dipp thought. Just
then, the bizarre temporal distortion happened once more, and it was
even longer than earlier.

Like a tape was being rewound, Dipp flipped the newspaper back to the
front page and jumped back to the rooftop. He then opened his mouth, and
a column of fresh water rushed out of his throat, returning to the
canteen in his hand.

The time distortion continued, and one particular distortion pushed time
back so far that Dipp returned to his home and almost vomited his
breakfast back onto the plate.

Eventually, it ended, and the distorted time resumed its normal, slow
flow that resembled a calm river.

Lily slowly rose from the depths of the ocean and appeared next to
Charles' colossal figure. She stared at the enormous flesh island next
to her, and she sounded apologetic as she said, "I'm sorry, Mr. Charles.
The combined weight of the Subterranean Sea is too heavy for me. I can't
control them all at once, and it's also too difficult to separate
humanity's time from the time of their environment." 𝒇𝙧𝙚𝓮𝔀𝓮𝒃𝙣𝓸𝒗𝒆𝒍.𝙘𝒐𝒎

The recent temporal anomalies were products of Lily's experiments on
reversing time. One of Charles' fleshly bodies appeared next to Sparkle;
it opened its eyes and was frowning in contemplation.

Charles' brand-new solution was to turn back time for the entire
Subterranean Sea. As long as time was flowing backward, Fhtagn's
inevitable awakening would never happen.

The solution itself wasn't the problem; it was the execution.

Lily was too weak to execute the solution.

The idea of strengthening Lily popped into Charles' mind, but a question
appeared as well—how could he do that?

Charles could easily strengthen himself. In fact, it was so easy that it
was like taking candy from a baby. If he wanted to become stronger, he
just had to absorb as much knowledge as he needed to become strong
enough.

However, making Lily powerful enough to execute their solution was
incredibly difficult.

Charles severed a large portion of his own flesh and tried to make Lily
devour it, but she no longer had a mouth, making it impossible for her
to do so.

Charles tried a few more ways to strengthen Lily, but they all failed.
In the end, he could only resort to his last option. Soon, a huge,
bizarre array appeared on the fleshy ground before him.

The bizarre array was a summoning array for the Feaster.

Soon, 005 appeared before Charles. She looked the same as always; she
was wrapped in black bandages, and the Feaster was still in her arms.

"I want to make Lily strong enough to reverse time for the entire
Subterranean Sea," Charles said bluntly.

"Hmm... your wishes are getting more and more tricky to handle. But
first of all, I'm sure you're aware of my rules." 005 raised her right
hand slightly and said, "You help me; I'll give you a wish."

Sparkle's heart trembled upon hearing that. Clearly, her father no
longer had any more wishes. If he wanted 005's help, he would have to
offer something interesting for her.

Then, Sparkle reached a conclusion, and a trace of grievance appeared on
her delicate face. However, she still started walking toward 005.

Charles immediately grabbed Sparkle's wrist and pulled her back behind
him. He stared straight at 005 and reached into his old Captain's
uniform with his steel arm to take out a white ball.

When Sparkle saw the white ball in Charles' hand, she was stupefied. She
was so shocked that she wouldn't be as shocked had her father decided to
trade her away.

"Wait, Daddy! Why do you still have a wish? I thought you had already
used them all? And w-what did you trade for it?"

Instead of replying, Charles remained silent and even pushed his
daughter away with a flick of his wrist.

The white ball turned red, and Charles stared at the being before him.
"You've obtained it. Now, fulfill your end of the deal."

005 glanced deeply at Lily before speaking directly to Charles in the
latter's head.

"I need you to explain your wish to me."

"Do you want to reverse time for the entire Subterranean Sea, or do you
want Lily to obtain the ability to reverse time for the entire
Subterranean Sea?"

"Is there any difference?"

"Of course. If you want me to strengthen the man-made god before you to
the point that she's strong enough to reverse time for the entire
Subterranean Sea, then I'm sorry, but I can't do that.

"If you simply want to reverse time for the entire Subterranean Sea,
then... I'm sorry, but I can't do that either.

"And as I said, I can only grant a wish if it's within my capabilities."

Chapter 1119 - Suggestion

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

005's words were unexpected to everyone present.

In their eyes, 005 had always been omnipotent, and there were few things
she couldn't do. However, it seemed that they were mistaken. Otherwise,
she wouldn't have uttered such words.

It turned out that the gap between Charles and 005 had narrowed by a
huge margin without anyone realizing it.

Charles frowned as he extended his hand toward 005. "Give me back what I
gave you, then. Your words just now aren't worth a wish."

Instead of returning what Charles had traded away, 005 said, "I said I
can't help, but I can offer a constructive suggestion toward your goal."

With that, the white wish ball in her hand slowly disappeared.

005 looked at Lily.

Lily was suspended in the air, emanating a radiant but gentle sunlight.

005 gently reached out with her right hand and plucked the sun from the
sky before playing with it as if it were a ping pong ball.

"Regardless of her appearance, she's ultimately a mere part of Fhtagn.
Her potential is limited, and one with limited potential cannot possibly
reverse the boundless river of time."

005's eyes wrapped in black bandages turned to face Charles. "But you're
different. Your potential is unlimited. If anyone can reverse the time
of the Subterranean Sea, then it would be you. Even I cannot do it."

"I can't." Of course, Charles had thought about doing it by himself. If
he could do so, he would have done it long ago. "The knowledge to
control time is boundless, and I'm like a kettle that is almost full of
boiling water.

"I'm afraid I'll become Edikth before I can even grasp its early
stages."

005 suspended the ping pong ball in mid-air, and the colossal,
spectacular sun returned. The sun's dazzling sunlight cast two long
shadows of 005 and Charles on the ground.

"I know, so my constructive suggestion is that you devour this little
sun, transform her into an organ, and then freeze your time a second
before you become Edikth."

Charles, Sparkle, and Lily's hearts tightened upon hearing that.

Devour Lily? Charles thought.

"Yes, devour her. Transform her into an organ, and you will be able to
control time while also having the capacity to take care of other
affairs."

Lily was far calmer than Charles had thought. 𝒻𝓇𝑒𝘦𝘸𝑒𝒷𝓃ℴ𝑣𝘦𝑙.𝒸ℴ𝘮

"Mr. Charles, if it's going to save everyone, then I'm willing to do
it."

"No, don't be hasty. Listen to what she has to say first," Charles said.
He felt that 005 could provide a helping hand when it came to that.

"So I'll obtain enough power to reverse the time of the Subterranean
Sea?"

"No, even Edikth's power is insufficient to reverse the time of the
Subterranean Sea. What happens afterward is a gamble that you must
take."

005's words truly left Charles at a loss for words, but he said nothing
and simply waited for her to continue.

"You need to absorb enough power in an instant, and Lily is that power.
Take advantage of that opportunity to break through the barrier and
elevate yourself to a higher-dimensional existence than Edikth.

"If you're lucky, you'll obtain the ability to reverse time on such a
large scale."

"A higher-dimensional existence... what will happen to my thoughts and
cognition by then? Will I still have them?" Charles asked, instantly
grasping the key point. To become Edikth, he would have to discard all
traces of his humanity, so what would he have to sacrifice to become a
higher-dimensional existence than Edikth?

"Hmm, that's why I said you need to be lucky. You're betting on whether
you can reverse the time of the Subterranean Sea before your humanity
runs out. If you're lucky, you'll have a bit of humanity left by the
time you're done.

"If you succeed, the Subterranean Sea will be under protection. If you
fail, then the Subterranean Sea will have another god."

"What kind of suggestion is that? You just made that on the spot,
right?" Charles asked. In his eyes, 005's so-called constructive
suggestion was simply unreliable and too risky.

He wasn't afraid of death, but he had no plans of ending his own life
for a stupid gamble. The success rate of 005's suggestion was certainly
lower than the success rate of his other contingency plans.

"No, I didn't come up with that on the spot. In fact, someone else once
followed my suggestion, but he failed," 005 replied. Then, her figure
slowly became illusory. She had somewhat fulfilled Charles' wish, so she
was getting ready to leave.

The affairs of the Subterranean Sea were irrelevant to her. In her eyes,
she was just a passerby.

"You said before that you'd do your best to fulfill wishes, right? Since
you know that someone else has attempted to make use of that suggestion
of yours, don't you think you should do everything you can to help me?"
Charles asked.

He felt a bit reluctant to let 005 leave just like that. After all, it
could be said that 005 was the only external help he had found so far.

"Unfortunately, I can't help you. As I mentioned, this body of mine in
the Subterranean Sea is just a tiny portion of my main body. This tiny
part is too weak to help you when it comes to concepts involving time.

"My main body can come here and help you. That will significantly
increase the success rate, but you cannot afford to pay that price.
You'll need at least ten wishes for me to do that."

"Ten wishes..." Charles stood quietly. 005's words were no different
from a direct refusal. He had already traded away everything he could
afford to do so, so there was no way he could trade for ten more wishes.

"You never said that to me back then."

"The past is the past, and the present is the present. Everything is
changing, isn't it?" 005 replied. In the end, she left, leaving the
three "people" to quietly look at each other.

Whether 005's suggestion was feasible or not was yet to be seen, but it
was indeed a constructive suggestion. Moreover, it seemed like it was
the one and only way.

"Mr. Charles, I'm willing. If it's going to protect everyone, I'm truly
willing to do it," Lily said, sounding determined.

"No!" Sparkle exclaimed. Her attitude toward 005's suggestion was
inexplicably stern, and for the first time in a while, anger suffused
her delicate, fair face. "Daddy, let's go. Let Lily take us to the past!

"We'll surely have plenty of time to think of another way in the past!"

Sparkle was aware of the outcome of 005's suggestion. Regardless of
success or failure, her father would certainly disappear.

Charles shook his head in disagreement and replied, "No, it is useless
to keep running away. We have to try it. Once we succeed, we will be
able to defeat Fhtagn once and for all!"

"But you're really going to die!" Sparkle exclaimed, and her eyes turned
red.

Charles looked down at his fleshy body and smiled bitterly. "If I'm
going to die, then so be it. To be honest, I've lost too much—far too
much. What's the point of living like this?"

Charles tried hard to recall the past, wanting to find something worth
remembering, but he discovered that apart from a strong reluctance to
part with his daughter, his heart had become an empty void.

"What about me?! What am I supposed to do once you're gone?!" Sparkle
asked. Tears slid down her face as she tightly gripped the hem of her
clothes with both hands.

Chapter 1120 - Decision

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Charles looked at his daughter and reached out to hug her, but he ended
up hugging the air instead. Sparkle had teleported several meters away
and was glaring at him.

Obviously, Sparkle couldn't agree with Charles' plan.

"Sparkle, you know that children can't stay with their parents forever,
right? You must find your own path. It's time for you to spread your
wings and soar." 𝘧𝑟𝑒𝑒𝘸𝘦𝘣𝑛𝑜𝘷𝑒𝓁.𝘤𝘰𝓂

"Soar where?! I'm about to become an adult, but my future has already
been decided! The end of the world is coming, so where am I supposed to
soar?!" Sparkle exclaimed as she wept. Her heart throbbed with pain. She
felt like her father had decided to abandon her.

Charles' gaze was gentle as he stared at her, saying, "Sparkle, it is my
future that has been decided, not yours. There's no need for me to
escape, but you can and you must."

Having said that, Charles looked at Lily in the sky. This time, they
transmitted their thoughts into each other to ensure that Sparkle
wouldn't hear them.

Moments later, Sparkle disappeared. It happened so fast that Sparkle
couldn't even say anything. The only thing she managed to leave behind
for Charles was a face marred with surprise and anger.

Charles' heart ached. This was probably the last time he would see his
daughter.

"Lily, where did you send her?" Charles asked.

"Two thousand years ago. The Subterranean Sea was still very quiet and
peaceful back then. It was a good place to live."

Charles slowly exhaled. He could only send Sparkle to the past. The end
was approaching, and he had no idea if he would succeed. If he
succeeded, he would bring her back.

If he failed, then... living in the Subterranean Sea of two thousand
years ago was better than dying with him.

Having ensured the survival of his daughter, Charles' next course of
action was to proceed with 005's suggestion.

Charles raised his right hand gently, and the sunlight shooting in all
directions coalesced into a glowing young woman. The young woman walked
up to his side and gently leaned against him.

"Do you really want to do this, Lily?"

Lily's long lashes blinked. Charles' tone of voice was bereft of any
emotions he had toward her back then. In stark contrast to Sparkle,
Charles had forgotten too much of his feelings for Lily.

However, perhaps this was a good thing. If there were other emotions,
Charles would certainly find it difficult to make a decision.

"Yes, I really want to do this, Mr. Charles," Lily smiled and hugged
Charles' arm. She ought to be happy. She was finally helpful to Charles.
In addition, it wasn't necessarily a bad thing to become a part of
Charles.

After all, it meant staying with Charles forever.

"Hmm..." Charles pondered and hesitated, knowing the gravity of this
method.

He awakened one of his fleshly bodies in the Dark Abyssal Trench and
looked at the sealed Fhtagn. Fhtagn's posture had changed again. The
huge, rotting bat-like wings behind him had unfurled.

"We're running out of time..." Charles muttered and made up his mind at
the sight.

The success rate was low, but he no longer had the luxury of time—no,
the entire surface world no longer had the luxury of time to wait for an
even better solution.

This was his only way to fight Fhtagn.

Charles looked down at Lily and stroked her glowing hair.

"Lily, since you've made up your mind, let us begin, then."

"Mmhm!" Lily nodded vigorously. Charles' determined face made Lily feel
as if the Captain of the Narwhale, who had led them through countless
life-or-death situations, had returned after such a long time.

Charles patted Lily's head for a while before closing his eyes and
hugging her gently. At the same time, the sun in the sky descended, and
the flesh island wrapped around Lily.

Charles was absorbing her, but Lily didn't resist at all. In fact, she
wholeheartedly gave up everything. Charles' power grew at breakneck
speed, and some knowledge involving time popped into his mind, seemingly
out of thin air.

Instead of severing them and throwing them away, Charles was like a
sponge, absorbing them rapidly.

Unfortunately, the absorption of such knowledge meant the rapid erosion
of his humanity, and Charles could feel it. He could feel that he didn't
have much time left.

At this rate, he would become a true god in at most twenty minutes.

"Lily, does it hurt?" Charles asked, staring at the girl in his arms.

"It doesn't hurt at all. I actually feel secure," Lily replied,
burrowing her head into Charles' chest.

Meanwhile, Charles' eyes dimmed swiftly. His humanity was eroding as
swiftly as his increase in strength. In the end, Charles said nothing.
He simply lowered his head and kissed Lily's soft hair.

"Good girl... you're my good girl..."

***

Nene was riding her bicycle home amidst the bustling Second Street of
Hope Island. She had changed significantly compared to when she was
still living in World's Crown.

Thanks to the abundantly nutritious food available on Hope Island, Nene
had grown much taller and prettier. Those unaware of her past would
never think that she was once nothing but skin and bones.

Nene was in a great mood today, and she couldn't hide her smile at all.
Of course, it was understandable, as today was her birthday. Nene
believed that her parents had to have made a ton of delicious food at
home.

Moreover, tomorrow was Hope Island Landing Day, which meant that the
Landing Festival was going to be held. The school had also announced a
three-day school break, so Nene would surely be able to play to her
heart's content over the next few days.

It wasn't just Nene; any child wouldn't be able to stop smiling if they
were in Nene's shoes.

While Nene was thinking about what she would do tomorrow, a figure
suddenly appeared at the intersection, startling Nene. She hurriedly
squeezed the brake and barely avoided hitting the individual.

Nene looked up and saw a hunchbacked old lady standing before her. The
old lady was wearing a black robe full of patches, and its faded color
told her that the robe had been worn for a long time.

Nene got off her bike and apologized nonstop to the old lady.

The old woman said nothing and simply smiled at Nene.

Once Nene was done with her frantic apologies, the old lady said, "Nene,
long time no see. Do you still remember me?"

The old lady's squinted eyes widened slowly, revealing a pair of
fluorescent green cross-shaped pupils.

"S-Sparkle?" Nene muttered and stood there in disbelief. When she came
to her senses, she lunged at Sparkle and hugged her. Nene really felt
like crying. Her long-lost friend had finally returned.

"You finally came to see me. Where have you been all these years? I
really missed you, my best friend!"

Sparkle looked at her with a smile that contained a thousand words.

"Nene, I'm here to say goodbye. I'm leaving."

For a moment, Nene was stunned. "You're leaving? Where are you going?"

"To a place where there is no return."

Chapter 1121 - Sparkle

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

When Nene heard that, her excitement faded away and disappeared. "To a
place where there is no return? Does that mean we'll never see each
other again?"

The old lady, Sparkle, nodded gently. Her wrinkled, withered hand held
Nene's smooth hand, and their hands clasped together, just like when
they first met at World's Crown.

Back then, they were just innocent children. Under Anna's urging,
Sparkle decided to interact with humans, and Nene was the first person
to make contact with her.

From then on, they became best friends.

"Nene, you told me back then that you wanted your father to come back
and that you wanted to eat meat every day. Your wishes have already come
true. Are you happy?" Sparkle asked, her aged voice carried a certain
emotion.

Nene nodded forcefully, and her eyes were filled with reluctance as she
stared at Sparkle while gripping the latter's hand tightly. "Sparkle,
can you stay? I can't bear to see you leave. Didn't we promise to be
best friends for life?"

Sparkle cast a gentle gaze at Nene. "We are best friends for life, but
sometimes, even best friends have to part. Besides, it's too late to say
that now. I'm already there."

Sparkle reached out to caress Nene's smooth face with her other hand.
"Thank you, Nene. Your existence told me that everything Daddy had done
was all worth it."

"Sparkle, don't go, please! Stay with me on Hope Island!" Nene didn't
give up and tried her best to make Sparkle stay.

Nene really wanted to hold back her tears in the face of her best
friend's farewell, but when she thought that she would never see her
again, she burst into tears.

"Nene, do you know? I'm really jealous of you. I'm jealous that you have
enough time to grow up slowly, unlike me, who grew up too fast," Sparkle
said, "I used to imitate humans, but I was never human.

"I remember complaining to Mommy about why she even made me learn human
emotions, but now, I think she did the right thing."

"Human emotions are expansive and multifaceted; despair is one of those
emotions, and it makes people feel bad, but there are other positive
emotions like familiar love, romance, and friendship. One would never
know the beauty of those emotions without experiencing them."

"Nene, as your best friend forever, I've decided to protect your hope,"
Sparkle replied. Then, her figure began to emanate beams of white light.
Having bade farewell to her one and only best friend, Sparkle was
finally going to leave.

"Sparkle! Don't go!" Nene exclaimed and tried to hug her, but she phased
through Sparkle. The last sight she saw of Sparkle—one she would never
forget—was Sparkle waving goodbye to her.

There was a flash of white light, and Sparkle finally returned to her
father's side two thousand years later.

The flesh island had disappeared, and there was only a beating heart
covered in blood vessels that proliferated across the sea. The beating
heart was none other than Charles, who had merged with Lily.

Sparkle could feel that her father was at a critical juncture. It seemed
that she had arrived just in time.

Just then, one of Charles' fleshly bodies was casually thrown onto the
pulsating blood vessels on the surface of the sea.

Sparkle flew toward it.

"Daddy, I'm back. I'm ugly now. You're not going to find me disgusting,
are you?"

Upon hearing the familiar voice, Charles raised his head. His eyes were
dull as he stared at his daughter, but his gaze spoke more than a
thousand words.

"Why... did you... come back...?" The Charles of the past would be
thrilled to see Sparkle's return, but a huge chunk of Charles' humanity
had been eroded away, so his face showed barely any emotions upon seeing
Sparkle. 𝕗𝗿𝕖𝐞𝐰𝗲𝕓𝐧𝕠𝕧𝗲𝐥.𝚌𝐨𝚖

"Daddy, I'm here to help. I've been in the past for a very long time.
However, the past is useless and is only a means of escape. Only the
future can be changed. You were right. The only thing we can do is
confront it head-on," Sparkle said, smiling.

She reached into the pockets at her waist and took out what was inside,
revealing a dozen or so white balls. There was no mistaking it; those
white balls were 005's wishes, and a dozen or so wishes were in
Sparkle's hands.

Charles had made use of those wishes before, so he knew the price one
had to pay to obtain so many wishes.

"What did you sacrifice for them?"

Instead of answering, the old lady, Sparkle, just stood there with a
smile. Although his daughter hadn't said anything, Charles saw a few
fragmented scenes from her afterimages.

Charles almost keeled over upon seeing those fragmented scenes. It
turned out that the old lady in front of him was just an empty shell
delivering a message to Charles from two thousand years ago. His real
daughter had long become a god.

Sparkle's worshippers called her Eye of Truth, but Charles knew that the
real Eye of Truth had died a long time ago. In other words, his daughter
was long dead.

Charles' mouth fluttered open. His face twisted and distorted as he let
out a sonorous wail of extreme sorrow. Sparkle's sacrifice was far—far
greater than his own.

Just then, the white balls in Sparkle's hands took to the skies and
turned red, one after another.

Sparkle's face beneath the radiant red light revealed a brilliant smile.
Like a baby, she raised both her arms and reached out toward Charles.
"Daddy, can you hug me? You haven't hugged me for a really, really long
time."

Charles obliged. He sat on a chair made out of his flesh and held his
daughter on his lap, rocking her gently.

Charles tried hard to remember something. Then, his mouth opened
slightly, and he began to hum a lullaby that he had never heard before.

"Thank you, Daddy. I'm feeling much better now," Sparkle said, closing
her eyes in satisfaction.

A middle-aged man with a scarred face was gently rocking an old lady as
if she were a baby against a backdrop of a colossal, beating heart.

It was a bizarre yet heartwarming scene.

The wish balls in the air vanished and were replaced by black dandelions
that pervaded the skies in an instant. The black dandelions covered
everything that Charles and everyone else could see.

The black dandelions were 005's true form. In response to the dozen or
so wishes that Sparkle had accumulated and activated, 005 had descended
to help Charles.

Sparkle's wrinkled face nestled against Charles' time-worn Captain's
uniform.

"I'm sorry, Daddy. Even two thousand years couldn't stop me from coming
back here. You were right. Running away will never solve our problems.
Living in the past is like living in a pre-recorded film. It will never
solve our problems."

Charles gnashed his teeth, and his face twisted as he hugged the old
lady in his arms, but the old lady melted into countless light fragments
that scattered into the air.

"Daddy, none of this is your fault. My fate was probably decided the
moment I was born, but despite that, I really am happy... I'm really
happy that I was your daughter."

With that, Sparkle faded away and disappeared.
