Chapter 1122 - Ascension

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

When the final vestiges of Sparkle's presence disappeared from this
world, Charles no longer cared about anything. At this point, Charles
could only care about Sparkle, but now, even she was gone.

Charles had lost his obsession to protect the world, his feelings for
Anna, and finally, Sparkle. With an empty heart, he no longer felt like
he could even care about the world.

"I have descended in response to your wish. Are you going to proceed?"

A "thought" appeared out of nowhere in Charles' mind.

Moments later, hundreds and thousands of sensory organs drilled out of
Charles' proliferating blood vessels, and they all "looked" at the
sky—no, they were "looking" at 005's true form amidst the black
dandelions in the skies.

005 tasted like the sickly-sweet blood; she smelled like freshly cut
metal, and she sounded like the crackling of lava. For some reason, all
of Charles' sensory organs could perceive her except for his visual
organs.

In addition, what his organs had perceived couldn't be retained.

Charles tried to make sense of the being before him using the knowledge
that his organs had perceived, but every single idea born from his
cognition seemed to grow legs and walk away on their own, eluding him.

005's true form couldn't be stored in his memories, as even the vague
ideas born from his cognition about 005 belonged not to him, but to Her.

"Are you going to proceed? Time is meaningless, but I dislike
repetition." 005 urged.

"Proceed with what?" Charles said mechanically. All his sensory organs
retracted as he numbly continued to absorb the knowledge containing
everything around him.

"Wishes are my way of negotiating with humans, and your daughter has
wished for her father's success. Since she had done her end of the deal,
I must assist you in achieving your goal," 005 responded.

Before Charles could say anything, the dense black dandelions in the air
descended, enveloping Charles. They took away something—the final traces
of humanity within him.

Having lost the last vestiges of his humanity, Charles lost control of
his body. His flesh and swollen appendages proliferated endlessly
throughout the Subterranean Sea as if it were some kind of tumor.

Charles' body expanded rapidly. When it reached the rock layer up above
and the seafloor down below, it moved sideways.

The expansion was rapid, but it was not truly endless. When the
expansion reached its limit, yellow-brown eyeballs manifested all over
it before the entire thing collapsed inward.

Charles' worry had finally been realized.

He had crossed the threshold, becoming Edikth.

This time, however, unlike what happened to the Charleses in other
planes, 005 gathered Charles' scattered humanity and injected it into
him right as he became Edikth. Then, time came to an abrupt halt..

Charles was now in a superposition state between Himself and Edikth.

Meanwhile, the flesh island down below was also going through a
transformation, becoming an indescribable lump of flesh shaped into a
spindle, but the lump of flesh wasn't entirely solid.

It also looked like a layer of watercolor filter was applied to it.

The part shrouded in a watercolor filter was the organ Charles could use
to manipulate time.

The quivering flesh was riddled with yellow eyes of varying sizes as
well as deformed mouths screaming noiselessly with a black substance
dripping from the corners of their lips.

At the center of all this was a massive, bright yellow eyeball. Charles
remembered that eyeball. He had seen it many times before, after all.

However, it was only today when Charles finally realized that the
eyeball could see through the past and the future.

In Charles' eyes, there was no cause and effect, only cycles and unique
lines.

Perhaps the world in the eyes of the other Edikths was like this as
well, but there was a stark difference between Charles and the other
Edikths. It was the fact that he still possessed a bit of humanity
thanks to 005's help.

Charles had completely merged with Lily, obtaining complete mastery over
time.

Upon sensing that he truly possessed the ability to manipulate time, he
willed it, and the world around him moved in reverse as if it were a
tape being rewound.

In no time, he went back in time to a month ago and saw Sparkle. Out of
deep concern, Charles couldn't help but crawl out of the spatial rift.

Before Charles could make any move, Sparkle unhesitatingly stood in
front of him.

Flashes of white light flickered incessantly around them, along with
spatial rifts in a variety of sizes. The densely packed spatial rifts
formed a wall-like barrier separating Sparkle and Charles.

Charles' yellow-brown eyeball, which was as large as an island, focused
on Sparkle.

However, Sparkle couldn't recognize that she was staring at her father
in the future. In her eyes, the spindle-shaped lump of flesh was a very
dangerous god—a god that was perhaps even more powerful than her father!
𝘧𝓇ℯℯ𝑤ℯ𝘣𝓃ℴ𝓋𝑒𝑙.𝑐𝘰𝑚

"Whatever you are, don't you dare touch my father! Go away!" Sparkle
roared. She furiously waved her tentacles as if she were an enraged
octopus.

"Sparkle is protecting the past me, even though she knows that she's no
match for me," Charles muttered to himself. He could feel his daughter's
determination.

Looking at his daughter's glowing, luminous green eyes, Charles finally
made a move. He slowly extended a tentacle through the wall of spatial
rifts, and with a swoosh, the tentacle was cleanly severed by the
spatial rifts.

However, the result was within Charles' expectations. Despite getting
severed, the tentacle didn't fall to the ground. Instead, it flew toward
Sparkle at breakneck speed.

Flashes of white light erupted before Sparkle as she desperately tried
to teleport the tentacle away. However, Sparkle's reliable special
ability failed her for the first time.

The tiny tentacle ignored all of Sparkle's attacks and flew toward
Sparkle while leaving behind a trail of afterimages. In the end, the
tentacle came to a halt right above Sparkle.

The tentacle containing Charles' will curved slightly and patted
Sparkle's head repeatedly as if it were dribbling a ball.

Sensing his daughter's warmth and soul once again, Charles' icy heart,
which had become indifferent to any emotions, cracked open, scattering
into countless fragments.

A wave of tranquility was transmitted to Sparkle, soothing her in an
instant.

The tense air eased and was replaced by warmth. The wall made out of
spatial rifts vanished, and Sparkle's hundreds of eyes widened as she
stared at Edikth, asking, "Daddy?"

Charles' heart almost shattered once more upon hearing that. He wanted
to speak; he wanted to tell his daughter to stay away from him, the
farther, the better.

Just as he was about to say something, the world before him distorted,
becoming unstable. Upon ascending to become Edikth, Charles became
extremely powerful, and at this moment, he could see two different
futures.

As for which of them would come true... it all depended on whether he
would speak to Sparkle or not. Unfortunately, none of those futures
depicted a different outcome for Sparkle. Her fate... was still the
same.

She was still destined to leave him, just in a different way.

Chapter 1123 - The Past

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Edikth showed no reaction to Sparkle calling him Daddy. She then glanced
back to look at Charles in the house before she turned her gaze back
onto Edikth and called out, "Mommy?"

Mommy?

The word echoed in Charles' mind as it sparked a ripple that triggered a
series of vanished memories.

Though those memories had disappeared, Charles was now capable of
traversing time. Just like watching scenes from a movie, he could see
the threads of the past and rewatch the events that had happened in his
lifetime.

He saw himself back on an island where he met a Dioite disguised as a
human. The Dioite then took on Anna's identity and appearance and got
onto his ship.

He watched the entanglement that followed—their clash of love and fury,
desire and betrayal, and their fruit of it all—Sparkle.

Finally, after everything, he saw himself trying his all to prevent Anna
from ending up in his current state. He chose the path of destruction
and made use of 005's wish to send Anna to the surface world.

"Anna?! Anna... Anna!"

The knowledge came flooding back. Charles remembered even more. Yet, he
was exceptionally calm about the return of these memories. The emotions
he harbored for this person were never going to return.

Experience and memory were completely different. Due to the erosion of
his humanity, Charles had almost lost the ability to perceive emotions.

He knew who Anna was and knew that they had loved each other. Yet, he
could no longer feel that love that was involved.

"Sparkle, who are you talking to?" The Past Charles' voice called out.
He had returned from the overlapping planes. Organs surged together on
the flesh island to form a grotesque, bloated face.

His past self had sensed his existence; it was time for him to leave.

Charles took one final, lingering glance at his daughter before he
gently waved a tendril and leaped back through the timelines.

When he finally returned to his original timeline, 005 was still there
waiting for him. She had one final thing to do.

"For the final step, you need to absorb enough energy to break through
Edikth and ascend as a higher-dimensional existence. Only then will you
be able to rewrite the laws of the universe and reverse the time of the
Subterranean Sea."

Charles pondered for a brief moment and looked at 005.

"A higher-dimensional existence? You mean an existence like you?"

"No. I'm not quite there yet. You've found your way, while I'm still
searching for mine. This is also the reason why I've scattered fragments
of myself across countless universes," 005 answered with a candid ease.
After all, an existence of Her level found it pointless to lie.

Charles silently reiterated the plan in his mind and traced the steps he
had to take.

If I'm fortunate enough to become a higher-dimensional existence as per
Her words, then the first thing I need to do is to turn back time for
the Subterranean Sea to save all of humanity here and also on the
surface world.

Actually, at this point, Charles already felt no hint of empathy for
these beings whose lives were so infinitesimal compared to what he had
become.

In fact, he questioned himself over his upcoming actions. Why did he
have to do things that wouldn't benefit him? Everything he once
cherished had already vanished. He no longer had a reason or anyone to
fight for.

What did their demise have to do with him?

And yet, that fading sliver of his humanity held him back from the edge,
enough to stop him from turning into a god who would look at a dying
world and do nothing.

No. I can't let everyone's sacrifices, including my own, be in vain. I
need to complete the mission I set for myself back when I was still
human.

"Let's start. I'm ready," Charles declared to the endless swarm of black
dandelions floating across the sky.

"Great. Let's go." The swarm of black dandelions gathered and merged
into a mass of overlapping reality and illusion.

"Where are we going?" Charles asked.

"A thousand years into the past, when the Light God was first born. You
can find the vast amount of energy you need there. There's also
something else that we need to do there."

Upon hearing 005's response, Charles turned his massive eye toward the
distant past and saw it clearly. The pieces fell into place.

"Alright, let's go." With 005 in tow, the duo entered the currents of
time and raced backward.

They soon arrived at a moment one thousand years in the past, standing
upon the very island that housed the Foundation’s Laboratory One—the
birthplace of the Light God.

They remained hidden in the fracture of time and space as they watched
the events unfold on the island.

K9, also known as Nancy, was recording her final words in front of a
drone.

Charles waited in silence. He didn't ask 005 what his next course of
action ought to be. He already knew from the moment he traveled from the
future to the past. He had seen this all before.

Soon, the Divinity creation ritual began. Blinding rays of seven-colored
sunlight burst outward. With the help of a drop of divine blood from
Fhtagn, the Light God was thus born.

The light bathed the entire Subterranean Sea, its radiance falling upon
the faces of every human present. At this point in history, humans had
not yet fused with vampire DNA, so sunlight posed no threat to them.

However, this wasn't a blessing because the sunlight was beginning to
peel their very souls from their bodies.

The Light God intended to separate every soul from its flesh so that it
would be easier for Him to carry out His goal. He wanted to take all
inhabitants of the Subterranean Sea away from this wretched place and
head toward another star system to build a new homeland.

"It's about time for us to move."

Charles and 005 emerged from the spacetime rift.

However, the Light God's actions hadn't gone unnoticed. Other gods had
also sensed His actions.

These existences had appearances that defied language and human
comprehension. However, that did not come as a surprise to Charles. He,
too, was no different from them now.

More than a hundred of these beings existed, but most of them merely
watched in silence. However, Charles was not part of that group.

"Alright, let's do this," Charles announced, stopping time at his
moment. His will shot out and wrapped around the Light God, dragging Him
over.

However, he didn't get far.

From above, 004 emerged from the rocky terrain. It took on the form of a
serpent with dazzling colors, and its body twisted into a perfect
infinity loop.

Charles could see that time was in complete disarray around 004. Any
human who came too close would either rapidly regress into a child or
age into an old person within seconds. Regardless of the outcome, death
would soon follow in some tragic form.

Charles' ability to stop time clearly did not affect beings like 004. In
truth, once one became a god, very few could ever be frozen by Charles'
time-stopping powers.

Charles received no words or thoughts from 004. Yet, he understood the
latter's intent. It wanted to return the Light God to Fhtagn's side...
to prevent the slumbering titan from awakening due to the loss of a drop
of blood.

Just as the standoff teetered on a blade's edge, the onlookers moved.

Regardless of their purpose, some existences surged toward 004, while
others charged at Charles. However, the vast majority descended upon the
Light God.

The Light God’s intent to take all the humans of the Subterranean Sea
and flee had clearly overstepped a boundary that the other beings were
unwilling to ignore.

005's form suddenly expanded as an eruption of black dandelion tufts
bloomed across the entire Subterranean Sea and wormed their way into
every living body.

Under the influence of 005, the beings initially held back, but it
didn't take long before the restraint crumbled and chaos erupted. One
after another, the entities collided, merging into each other until
their forms and essences became indistinguishable.

Time and space were shredded beneath their power.

A full-blown war broke out.

Chapter 1124 - In the Past

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

The war amidst the frozen time lasted for a long time. Some of the gods
perished, but brand-new gods were born from those corpses, rekindling
the war. 004's slender figure was torn in half, but 004s from other
timelines joined the fight.

The Light God was far luckier than Charles thought. Although the
majority of the gods were targeting Him, He was still alive somehow.
Compared to Lily, the Light God before Charles was obviously much more
powerful.

Charles had anticipated the Light God's current level of strength. He
could also see the Light God's many futures, and none of them depicted
the Light God's destruction.

Of course, Charles hadn't come here for the Light God. He was here to
seek out an energy source. He needed enough energy to transform into a
higher-dimensional existence.

While They were busy above the sea surface, Charles was streaking across
the sea floor. Charles was transfixed on the gods' chunks of flesh that
had fallen into the deep sea.

Their writhing pieces of flesh that incessantly gave birth to brand-new
gods were what he needed. Those chunks of flesh contained a staggering
amount of knowledge, and each chunk of flesh was practically a library
full of knowledge.

Moreover, those chunks of flesh contained a part of both 004's
incomplete form and 005.

Charles had to obtain them. He cast his colossal eye capable of seeing
through the past and the future on those chunks of flesh. Whenever his
eye clapped on one, the chunk of flesh would gradually fade away before
disappearing completely.

At the same time, Charles' spindle-shaped figure would swell up. He was
"devouring" those chunks of flesh with a mere gaze. Charles refrained
from assimilating them. He wasn't in a good place to do so, after all.
It was incredibly risky to make such a dangerous maneuver when there
were so many gods nearby.

Upon realizing that he had accumulated enough knowledge to make future
breakthroughs, Charles stopped "devouring" the chunks of flesh and swam
to the surface.

It was absolute pandemonium above the sea. There were even spatial rifts
everywhere, occasionally spitting out bizarre and special creatures.

Some of those creatures were driven to madness by the aura of the nearby
gods before immediately dying, while those a bit luckier narrowly
escaped death, disappearing into the pitch-black Subterranean Sea with
their minds eroded away by the gods.

"It is about time we do our duties. The world will soon collapse if we
let them be." 005's thoughts popped up in Charles' mind.

"I saw it. And it is not a desirable future at all," Charles replied. He
could almost be considered omniscient due to his ability to see the
future. Of course, his foresight meant that he was long aware of what he
ought to do next.

"Since you've obtained what you needed, we will proceed with sealing the
Light God, and I ought to go back," 005 said. She seemed unwilling to
stay in the Subterranean Sea for a long time with her true power intact.

Charles' colossal figure disappeared and reappeared beside the Light
God.

The Light God's radiant sunlight illuminated Charles, illuminating him
inside and out. The Light God had become light itself, so He had no
physical form.

The Light God didn't linger and headed east. He wanted to run away.

Charles remained calm. His tentacles waved gently at the light in the
sky. "I'm going to send you to Fhtagn's side, so stop running away."

The streak of light abruptly came to a halt and coalesced into a blue
sun. A zealous voice filled with fanaticism echoed from it. "I can feel
your sincerity. Are you really willing to send us back to Fhtagn's
side?"

Before Charles could answer, the anxious voices of the other lights
echoed from within the blue sun. 𝕗𝐫𝐞𝕖𝕨𝐞𝗯𝚗𝕠𝘃𝐞𝚕.𝐜𝗼𝚖

"Hurry! Run!"

"Nancy, what are you doing?!"

"She has betrayed us!"

Charles' tentacles reached out and plucked the blue sun as if he were
picking a peach. The seven lights that made up the Light God were one.
Since the blue light was caught, the six remaining lights could only
struggle in vain.

"You cannot escape. Have you not noticed it yet? The blue light has been
contaminated by Fhtagn. You can take the humans here wherever you want,
but Fhtagn will surely follow suit."

The Light God ceased all struggle upon hearing. The lights understood
what Charles was talking about. K9 Nancy had been contaminated, becoming
a Fhtagnist.

Since the Light God had fallen into Charles' hands, the nearby gods
moved to surround him, and one of them was 004. 004's figure was so
colossal that It almost shrouded everyone else.

004 clearly wanted to obtain the Light God.

Charles could see that, but he naturally wouldn't give the Light God to
004.

"It is its fate to be sealed. There exists a future in which you've
consumed it, so I can say that you shouldn't eat it. Otherwise, you'll
suffer from indigestion."

However, 004 seemed to have no intention of communicating with Charles,
nor did it have any intention of giving up.

Its scales rubbed against each other, and the spacetime around Charles
distorted.

"Move aside." The swarm of black dandelions in the sky coalesced into
005's form, and She hovered behind Charles.

Upon seeing another god shrouded in fog standing next to Charles, 004
instantly realized that its chances of victory were zero. It turned and
drilled into the rock layer up above.

For some unknown reason, 004 took away the Foundation members throughout
the Subterranean Sea.

"All of you can leave now. Rest assured, I will send Him back to
Fhtagn's side. This will never happen again." Charles' thoughts were
conveyed to the "ears" of all the gods present.

004's retreat, and the fact that Charles had 005 on his side elicited an
immediate response from the other gods.

They quickly stopped fighting, but they didn't leave. Instead, They hid
in the darkness and observed. They wanted to see for themselves whether
Charles would truly send the ball of light back to Fhtagn's side.

The world teetering on the edge of collapse rapidly repaired itself, and
the world soon returned to its previous normalcy.

"Let's begin," Charles said to 005, the blue light, and the god shrouded
in fog.

The next second, the sea churned and split open, revealing the
slumbering Fhtagn within the Dark Abyssal Trench.

The three gods—Charles, 005, and a god shrouded in fog—together with the
blue light moved together, creating a seal to restrain the Light God
next to Fhtagn.

The countless futures Charles had seen told him that the Light God had
to be sealed. Otherwise, he wouldn't be what he was today. In other
words, he had to follow the predetermined future.

Upon seeing that Charles had indeed sealed the Light God next to Fhtagn,
the other gods turned and disappeared into the darkness.

Having achieved his goal, it was time for Charles to go back. He was now
at a critical juncture.

Charles returned to his own timeline with a belly full of flesh. The
chunks of flesh within him still radiated immense power, as they were
once part of a god.

Once Charles had assimilated them, their immense power would become a
part of him for the briefest moment, allowing him just one attempt.

Whether Charles could reverse the time of the entire Subterranean Sea
depended on this single attempt.

Regardless of failure or success, Charles would finally be able to rest.
He had struggled long enough, and he was utterly exhausted. If he could,
he really wanted to do nothing and just stay still.

Just then, 005's figure became illusory. Having completed her end of the
deal, it was time for her to leave.

Chapter 1125 - A Brand-New Existence

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

"You said you can see the future. Does that mean you can see your future
once you've assimilated them all?" 005 asked, her figure becoming more
and more illusory as the seconds went on.

"Yes, I can see two futures—I succeeded in one of them, while I failed
in the other," Charles replied. Immediately afterward, millions of
tentacles proliferated inside him, piercing the chunks of flesh within
him.

Charles felt a strong gust of wind. He had no idea whether it was real
or a mere illusion, but the wind was extremely powerful. It pierced his
skin and swept across his organs.

The wind was pitch-black, and the surroundings changed under its
influence.

The fierce black wind blew across Charles, blasting him across the dark
gulfs of space and gray regions of matter.

Charles could feel himself careering across a myriad of indescribable
landscapes and dark planets; he could feel himself swirl and shift,
traversing immeasurable distances across the boundless vast expanse of
the universe.

The twinkling stars were born and destroyed. They intertwined with one
another, transforming into a shimmering, hazy spiral, and then he saw
tiny asteroids streaking across the abyss deeper than the fabled depths
of Shung.

And then the wind came to a halt...

When Charles came to his senses again, he realized that he had broken
through a certain barrier.

He no longer needed to think linearly like humans—no, thoughts were no
longer necessary to him. Doubts and worries also ceased to exist for
Charles, as any answer he needed was simply right there.

Just then, a fish swam past Charles. The ordinary fish transformed into
a line in front of him. The one end of the line depicted the fish's
birth, while the other end depicted the fish's demise.

Charles had succeeded. He had become a higher-dimensional existence,
like what 005 had mentioned.

All traces of his humanity seemed to have vanished in an instant. Then,
with a will on his end, Charles began to modify the laws of time in an
attempt to reverse the time of the entire Subterranean Sea.

However, just as Charles was about to begin, he stopped, realizing that
he couldn't do it. For some reason, he still couldn't do it despite what
he had achieved.

Charles could feel that something was missing deep inside him.

What am I missing? As an almost omniscient being, Charles tried to find
the answer to his conundrum. He searched the entire Subterranean Sea and
soon discovered the answer—that final bit of his humanity was still
there.

The fact that he still had his final vestiges of humanity was a bad
thing. This wasn't what 005 had told him. Clearly, he had yet to truly
become a higher-dimensional being.

No wonder he still couldn't change the laws of time. He was still
missing something. He had to hurry up and find it.

Charles willed it, and time rewound like a tape. This time, he went back
to more than a decade ago. Back then, he had just arrived in the
Subterranean Sea.

A young man in a school uniform was on a wooden boat, and he was
nervously scanning the pitch-black sea surface using his phone's
flashlight.

As soon as Charles saw his past self, his past self saw His eyes in the
water.

The sight wasn't something the young Charles could withstand. When their
gaze met, the young Charles swelled and deformed rapidly before finally
losing consciousness.

However, Charles hadn't come here to confront his past self. He was here
to look for the passageway that his past self had used to reach the
Subterranean Sea from the surface world.

The passageway had long disappeared at this point in time, but Charles
simply rewound time even further until he found the passageway.

The passageway was small, but it was large enough to accommodate a small
wooden boat. There was also one thing for sure—it was artificially made.

However, Charles couldn't care less about the identity of the one who
sent him to the Subterranean Sea. All he wanted to do was complete his
task and leave in peace.

Charles made use of that passageway and finally arrived at the surface
world. If Charles were still obsessed with the surface world, he would
have been thrilled upon reaching the surface world, but he no longer had
any emotions.

Charles stopped in his tracks and looked around. The surface world
seemed a bit different from what he was familiar with. There weren't any
towering skyscrapers or any human beings. Even the sun and the moon had
vanished. 𝚏𝕣𝐞𝗲𝐰𝕖𝐛𝐧𝕠𝕧𝚎𝚕.𝐜𝚘𝗺

There wasn't anything around except for Charles and the black meteors
falling like rain, creating craters all over the ground.

The sky was devoid of any stars, and there weren't any celestial bodies
in outer space. There was nothing; it was like the surface world had
become an empty void.

Something's wrong. There isn't even any oxygen here. How did I even
survive this place as a young teenager? Charles pondered. His
"omniscience" seemed useless here, as everything was broken. In fact,
this couldn't even be considered a world.

While Charles was busy pondering what was wrong, he could feel someone
calling out to him. Who's calling out to me?

Charles closed his eyes and frowned. His vision was just connected to
something else—a towering flesh tentacle standing upright in a circular
lake. Charles turned and saw a fierce-looking young woman standing by
the lake.

The young woman was familiar. She was none other than his wife Anna,
whom he had sent to the surface world.

In the proverbial blink of an eye, Charles immediately knew how to face
her.

"Anna, it's been so long. I really missed you." A voice laced with
feigned affection echoed from the dozens of mouths on the flesh
tentacle.

Charles finally understood how 005 seemed to be a genuine human being.
It turned out that pretending to be a human being was extremely easy.

Charles watched as Anna walked through the crowd and stepped onto the
crimson lake. Charles controlled the crimson lake, transforming it into
a red carpet that carried Anna closer to him.

Upon reaching the giant flesh tentacle, Anna went berserk. She punched
and kicked the fleshly tentacle as if her life depended on it for the
sake of venting her dissatisfaction.

"You selfish bastard!! You've always only thought about yourself! Have
you ever thought of me before making any decisions?! I changed many
things about myself for your sake, and what reward did I get in return?!

"The reward was getting thrown to this damned surface!"

Just then, two palms grew from the tentacle, and they extended to
embrace the hysterical Anna. "I'm sorry, my love."

As soon as he made contact with his wife, Charles instantly understood
her emotions and everything there was to understand about her. He could
even deduce her actions. In his eyes, Anna had become just a mere
object.

Love... what an ironic concept... Charles tried his best to protect his
final vestiges of humanity.

Anna broke free and pounced on the tentacle before biting it viciously
as if she were a leopardess. However, it seemed that biting was not
enough to assuage her fury, as she even used her corrosive flames to
scorch Charles' flesh.

The part of the flesh tentacle that Anna was biting furiously
transformed into a mouth, and it moved closer to kiss her. Anna
struggled, but multiple appendages burst out of the flesh tentacle,
wrapping her in an embrace.

Anna's fierce struggles gradually mellowed out. In the end, she embraced
the giant flesh tentacle and began biting its appendages.

Do I really love her? Why should I even love her? What's the point of
such a low-level emotion? Charles wondered in the face of his wife's
bizarre behavior.

Chapter 1126 - That Thing

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

After a while, Anna got tired and spat out the bloody tongue in her
mouth. She glared fiercely at the flesh tentacle and exclaimed, "Now
bring me back!"

The eight mouths on the flesh tentacle opened at once. "I'm sorry, Anna,
but I can't do that. First of all, you're far safer on the surface world
than in the Subterranean Sea, and I want to keep you out of danger's
reach."

"Secondly and lastly, I'm on the surface world as well."

Charles turned his gaze to the side. Compared to the lush green peaks
here, the place where he was located appeared to be more barren. If the
surface world on this side was full of life, then the surface world on
this side was dead.

"Bullshit! You're on the surface world? Then how come I haven't seen you
yet?" Anna was convinced that Charles was lying to her.

Hearing Anna say this, the huge deformed tentacle slowly wriggled. More
and more eyes drilled out of it, looking in all directions with strong
curiosity, absorbing the knowledge of this world.

Upon hearing Anna's words, the giant flesh tentacle writhed, and more
eyes emerged from within it. The eyes were filled with intense curiosity
as they looked around. Then, they narrowed slowly.

It seemed that the answer Charles had been looking for was right here.

"Yes, that's exactly what I find strange. That thing doesn't exist on
the surface world where I am from.. This is problematic."

"Goodness..." Anna muttered, looking quite irritated as she paced back
and forth on the lake surface while kneading her forehead. "Just what
exactly have you been doing? You've yet to lose that recklessness of
yours after all this time?"

Charles didn't answer. The giant flesh tentacle grew, and eyestalks
emerged from beneath the flesh, rising upward. The grotesque flesh
tentacle transformed until it eventually became a hideous giant tree
made out of flesh and blood.

To make the sight even more bizarre, deformed appendages with eyestalks
were growing out of the tree's trunk, making it appear particularly
healthy if it weren't for the fact that it appeared to be made out of
flesh and blood.

"I'm talking to you! Do you hear?!" Anna exclaimed sternly.

The eyestalks froze and descended to look at her on the ground. "Sorry,
I got distracted. I'm listening now."

"I don't care where you are right now; I want you to bring me back NOW!
I don't want to stay in this damned place for even a second longer! I've
had enough of this fragile human body."

Anna's anxious voice echoed throughout the blood-red lake, sending
ripples in all directions.

Grotesque cracking noises similar to bones shattering at once echoed as
the massive tree swayed slowly. "Sorry, Anna, but you can't come back
here. I've seen fragmented scenes of the future. If you come back here,
you will meet an extremely tragic fate. I don't want you to suffer that
fate."

Anna's face distorted fiercely. She opened her mouth, seemingly to curse
and vent her frustration, but in the end, she only let out a soft sigh,
saying, "You told me that you don't want others to decide your fate, but
aren't you doing the same thing to me right now?"

Charles' eyestalks were staring at the starry sky and the earth at the
same time. "Those matters can wait, my love. First of all, we need to
understand what's really going on here. Your concerns are trivial
compared to this one."

Compared to saving the humans on the surface world and the humans in the
Subterranean Sea, sending Anna back to the Subterranean Sea was an
incomparably trivial matter.

Just then, Charles' eyes widened as he realized something, "Ah! I
finally understand. Time on the surface world and time in the
Subterranean Sea flows in opposite directions. We're both at the other
end of the line, but you're in the past.

"The gap between us is a thousand years. It seems that humanity's fate
has already been decided."

Charles instantly realized what was going on by merely observing the
surface world.

Anna adjusted her mindset and softly said, "A thousand years later, some
disaster will happen here, forcing the IMF to flee to the Subterranean
Sea to become the first-generation Foundation.

"I assume that's what's going to happen, but what does that have to do
with us? You're thinking too far ahead."

The giant tree swayed again, generating cracking noises. "No, that's not
the case at all. This is related to both of us, and it is related to
everything in the world."

Since time was flowing in reverse, it meant that he had succeeded. He
was indeed destined to become a true higher-dimensional being that 005
had mentioned.

Anna frowned, feeling that Charles wasn't exactly mentally stable at the
moment.

"How is it related to both of us? Explain," Anna asked.

Charles' tone of voice began to sound inquisitive as he replied, "I'm
not sure. I just have this feeling. Give me a moment; I need to see more
before I can give you any explanation."

The eyestalks attached to the giant tree rose once again, and they were
transfixed on the round moon hanging in the sky. For some reason, the
tree started bending and withering; its eyeballs caved in, withering
like flowers.

Once the eyeballs had disappeared completely, the huge moon in the sky
changed color, and it eventually transformed into an eyeball with a
yellow pupil.

The moon-sized eyeball turned to look at Earth. The moon-sized eye
opened, and as soon as it did, it saw every piece of knowledge on Earth
and assimilated it at the very same moment.

Then, all the mouths on the giant tree opened. "I see. I completely
understand it now. So that's what this is all about."

At this moment, Charles saw himself. He saw himself standing in a
crater. The two Charleses were connected. He saw through the laws of the
world as well as his true position.

It wasn't just the two Charleses. The Charleses from other planes
reached the same conclusion. As soon as they did, their thoughts
synchronized, and they all began moving upward.

He had always thought that he had reached the end, but he was mistaken.

His current self was just a small part of the tentacle.

As soon as Charles' words fell, the "branches" of the tree split open,
and the tree bloomed with extremely brilliant and dazzling "flowers." At
this moment, Charles seemed to have transformed into a different kind of
existence.

Every human being on Earth looked up at the sky, and their breathing
quickened as their hearts pounded wildly against their chest.

Their sanity decreased rapidly, and extreme panic began to seize
everyone's hearts. As they stared at the eyeball in the sky, they felt
like the apocalypse was nigh.

There weren't many beings capable of stopping what was about to happen,
but fortunately, Anna was one of them. "Hey, Charles! Calm down! Are you
planning on destroying Earth? I have to remind you that your past self
is still here!"

Anna's words had yet to finish echoing in the air when time itself came
to a halt before rewinding rapidly. The "flowers" on the tree closed and
retracted into the flesh tree; the shriveled and withered eyeballs were
restored, and the bright moon returned to normal.

Time rewound to three minutes ago when the massive eyeball in the sky
had yet to appear, thus leaving no one with any memory of having ever
seen it.

Then, the tree began to disintegrate, melting rapidly like a candle.

Chapter 1127 - Higher Dimension

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

The disintegrating massive flesh tree told Anna that Charles was about
to leave. She instantly became anxious. She couldn't let him leave so
easily, as he was probably her only way back to the Subterranean Sea.

"Charles, bring me back. It doesn't matter where you are right now; you
must bring me back!"

"Anna, I'm sorry. I know your feelings for me are genuine, but I can't
bring you back. There's nothing here—hope is fleeting; only deathly
silence is eternal," Charles replied. His voice was gradually losing its
warmth. Upon comprehending everything, he no longer wanted to pretend
that he wasn't on the brink of losing all of his humanity.

Indeed, there were barely any traces left of humanity in his heart.

The burning flames in Anna's heart erupted into a conflagration upon
being rejected once again. "Who do you think you are?! Do you really
think that I want to go back to get together with you? I want to go back
for my daughter, my Sparkle!"

As soon as Anna's words fell, the tree instantly fell silent. The
appendages and thin eyestalks growing on it drooped as they quietly
melted away. The air seemed to become a bit downtrodden.

The faint sorrow emanating from the giant tree was transmitted to
everyone present. Charles was supposed to have lost all emotions, but he
felt sorrow once more. After all, he only cared about Sparkle and no one
else.

Anna's words reminded Charles that he only ever cared about Sparkle.

Anna's heart skipped a beat upon sensing the sorrow emanating from the
giant tree. Then, she seemed to have come to a horrible conclusion as
her lips quivered ever so slightly.

Anna's trembling lips parted, and she sounded like she was pleading as
she asked, "You know that you're her father, right? Charles? How is
Sparkle doing?"

There was a loud crack, and the melting tree collapsed, creating waves
in the blood-red lake.

Charles' gaze was starting to return to where it came from.

Meanwhile, Anna's breathing became increasingly rapid. Then, she lunged
forward, jumping onto the giant tree. She thrust her hands into the
melting flesh tree and dug around before pulling out an eye the size of
a human head.

Anna held that eyeball in front of her face and roared, "Answer me, you
useless waste of space! Where is our daughter?!!" 𝑓𝓇𝘦ℯ𝘸𝘦𝑏𝓃𝑜𝘷ℯ𝑙.𝑐𝑜𝓂

In addition to sorrow, Charles also felt guilty. Those emotions were
fleeting, but Charles keenly sensed them. He cast his gaze on Anna's
timeline and used his hand to straighten out all the twists and turns.

At the same time, the yellow eyeball melted like lava that dripped
slowly into the lake. Then, the emotions in Charles' heart disappeared
as he said, "Anna... I'm sorry..."

Charles' gaze finally returned to that barren surface world. He blinked,
and the traces of the faint emotions in his heart vanished completely.
His conversation with Anna was also cast aside.

None of it mattered.

What was important was that he had finally found the missing part of
himself.

Just then, something pulled Charles' will out of his body, and as he
became "taller," he saw the Edikths from different dimensions and
different planes.

Their ascension drew out tentacles from many different dimensions and
planes. The tentacles pierced every obstacle in their way, and together
with Charles, they finally coalesced into a single point in the void.

There was neither time nor space here. This void was not part of the
material world. Except for Charles, there was nothing.

Charles came to possess a myriad of tentacles. When his gaze fell upon
one of his own tentacles, he saw a scene in the middle section of the
tentacle. The scene depicted him conversing with Anna just now.

His gaze extended downward, and he saw many Charleses.

He saw Charles who had just become a god...

He saw Charles fighting the Foundation...

He saw Charles searching for the darkness...

He saw Charles, who had just become the Governor of Hope Island...

He saw Charles struggling on an island, and then he saw a teenager
Charles. When his gaze finally landed on the end of the tentacle, he saw
baby Charles wrapped in swaddling clothes.

The myriad tentacles all over Charles likewise depicted a myriad of such
scenes. Each tentacle was its own timeline, representing a Charles,
representing an Edikth.

Charles finally understood why the Charles who had become gods seemed to
be moving solely based on their instinct and had ignored all of his
attempts at communication.

It wasn't that those Charleses couldn't communicate at all. They refused
to communicate, as it was simply a waste of effort.

His previous attempts to interact with the Charles who had become gods
were equivalent to the tip of one's fingers trying to communicate with
the middle section of another finger.

It was an attempt that needed no response. After all, regardless of
their location and circumstances, they would all merge to form his
current self.

Charles looked down at himself and saw that his body was now entirely
composed of the past Charles and the various laws of the world.

He was a part of the world, and a part of the world was Him. It could be
said that anything with a strong connection to Charles had become a part
of Charles' body.

The past Charleses depicted on the tentacles weren't mere illusions.
They truly depicted a real Charles living their own lives as depicted on
the tentacles.

Charles waved one of his tentacles, and the choices made so far by the
Charles depicted on the respective tentacle changed drastically.

Having become an omniscient being, Charles was aware of the effect of
the past and the causes of the future, and he could easily modify both
the past and the future through a single variable—Charles.

Now that he could manipulate everything at will, Charles finally became
a so-called higher dimensional being that 005 had mentioned.

It was only when Charles reached his current heights that he realized
how insignificant his past selves were. If one wanted to modify the laws
of the world and reverse time, one ought to become an existence like
Charles was right now.

Charles immediately made a move. Although he didn't know what benefit
this move would bring him, his disappearing humanity ultimately led him
to make this decision.

Time came to a halt. Then, his tentacles caved inward from the tips.
Once the tips became the tails, and the tails became the tips, the time
of every plane's Subterranean Sea would be reversed.

At first, it went smoothly, but something unexpected occurred. Something
hooked Charles' tentacles, preventing him from proceeding any further.

Charles "gazed" into the past and saw something foreign within him.

It was a pitch-black line, and the line was connected to the tentacle
tattoo on Charles' neck. Charles remembered the tattoo; the tattoo had
been completely inconspicuous throughout his career as a captain.

The tattoo hadn't been useful at all during the many life-or-death
situations he once found himself in.

The only time it had reacted was when he saw Dawn One and fell into
despair to the point that he had decided to end his own life.

The tentacle tattoo's origin wasn't anything complicated. To ensure that
Charles wouldn't die during his adventures outside, Anna had decided to
take out an insurance policy for him.

Charles turned to a tentacle. His gaze moved down the timeline until he
saw Anna conducting a ritual. The ritual required many sacrifices. In
addition to sacrificing human beings, Anna also had to sacrifice a huge
chunk of her own body.

Once Anna had offered enough to Fhtagn, Charles finally obtained the
tentacle tattoo on his neck.

Chapter 1128 - Endure

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Fhtagn, Fhtagn... He had been skirting around Him, but it was still
impossible to ignore the sole ruler of the Subterranean Sea.

Charles' gaze followed the pitch-black line, and his gaze eventually
landed on God Fhtagn. God Fhtagn was also in His true form.

God Fhtagn's true form was a dark substance that resembled both a tower
and a mountain. The dark substance seemed to have merged with everything
in the void, and it was a kind of darkness that couldn't be described by
any colors.

Charles couldn't determine whether the dark substance's darkness was
intrinsic or if it was a void left by the non-existence of a vast
expanse.

Human sensory organs couldn't perceive such an existence, so their
brains would distort Fhtagn's appearance into a winged, flesh monster,
or more often than not, any eldritch creature that their minds could
imagine.

The truth was that Fhtagn's appearance was not frightening at all. He
was also excessively quiet, as quiet as a real mountain.

God Fhtagn is different from me. Charles instantly reached that
conclusion upon seeing the other party. He couldn't see through the kind
of existence God Fhtagn was, one thing was certain—His path to godhood
wasn't the same as His.

There weren't a myriad of tentacles all over God Fhtagn, which meant
that he hadn't fused with the other versions of Himself from other
planes to become a god.

God Fhtagn was one and only. He was unique, and there wasn't anything
else like Him out there.

Charles' tentacles waved slightly, and the choices made by the people on
each timeline changed. The reality changed, and some events made Anna
too busy to leave a tattoo on Charles' neck.

Regardless of the changes, the outcome remained the same. Every single
Charles still sported the same, special tentacle tattoo on their necks.
Even the Charles in planes that had no Anna sported the same tattoo.

It was inevitable.

The pitch-black line attached to Charles wouldn't disappear.

Fhtagn, what is this? What is the meaning of this black line? Charles
asked, but it wasn't difficult for the current Charles to obtain the
answer to his question. After all, 002 was right behind God Fhtagn.

002 certainly looked different before, but regardless of its previous
appearances, it now looked like a black ball emanating a purple glow in
a net-like pattern.

002 living at the Core seemed to have become one with God Fhtagn.

However, Charles immediately noticed something amiss. 002 wasn't
originally a part of Fhtagn. Charles could still barely see 002's unique
afterimages, and they all depicted its ascension to godhood.

In the distant past, way before the Earth was formed, God Fhtagn had
fought with It, but God Fhtagn eventually won the fight. He eroded 002
and forced 002 to become a part of Him. Unfortunately, that tragic end
would soon befall Charles.

Perhaps God Fhtagn had devoured other gods as well.

Charles just hadn't seen Them, or perhaps He had already digested Them.

Charles remained unmoving.

Just then, a Fhtagnist inexplicably appeared around a young version of
Charles on a ship. Charles saw the Fhtagnist's sudden appearance through
one of his tentacles.

All of a sudden, the number of Fhtagnists around the young Charles on
the surface world increased drastically, and Charles noted that the
pitch-black line connected to his tentacle had grown longer and thicker.

Despite the abrupt changes, Charles wasn't flustered at all. In addition
to the fact that he could no longer panic, he had also become strong
enough to resist.

God Fhtagn was once a god that could not be gazed upon, but now, He had
become as strong as Him.

If I were still human, I'd say, 'fuck you!' I hate being under someone's
thumb. It doesn't matter even if you're a god.

God Fhtagn made no attempts to communicate and simply stayed quiet, but
Charles discovered that a Fhtagnist had become a part of the Narwhale in
the distant past.

Upon seeing that, Charles counterattacked in his own way. Their fight
distorted every single plane, and drastic changes occurred in each and
every one of them. It was to the extent that everything became
distorted.

Unfortunately, humans could never imagine nor participate in a fight of
this magnitude. The only thing they could do was endure—endure the
hardships and stay ignorant to live in the broken world left behind by a
battle between the gods.

***

Anna opened her eyes and fell into a daze. After looking at Gao Zhiming
in her arms, she got out of bed, opened the window, and started smoking.
She stared blankly at the distant trucks while assessing her current
situation.

She was busy building up her strength for the sake of dealing with the
Foundation in one fell swoop.

After the ritual in the Indian Ocean, Anna now had thousands of
Fhtagnists, but against the Foundation, they weren't enough. It didn't
matter even if they were unafraid of death.

If Anna wanted to increase their strength in the fastest way possible,
then making them absorb Anomalies was the quickest way to do so.

However, Anna couldn't find that many anomalies for them to absorb right
now.

If the plan was not feasible, then Anna could only try to overwhelm the
Foundation with numbers. She would rapidly expand the Fhtagn Covenant,
and then trade a life for a life against the Foundation.

Anna wasn't afraid of losing people. The most important thing for her
was the success rate. 𝕗𝐫𝚎𝗲𝘄𝐞𝕓𝐧𝕠𝘃𝕖𝐥.𝐜𝚘𝚖

Unfortunately, a group of Fhtagnists was an unstable group. Moreover, it
was clear that the Foundation had an overwhelming advantage when it came
to numbers.

How about we do them both? What if I carry them out both at the same
time?

After jotting down her idea, Anna opened her laptop and browsed through
some news.

Hello, viewers, a peculiar EY12 virus was discovered by doctors in
Switzerland. The virus has been analyzed and is deemed highly
contagious, but what's concerning is that the virus has been secretly
spreading for a long time before it was finally discovered.

To ensure everyone's safety, five permanent members of the UN Security
Council have taken the lead in closing ports and national borders,
prohibiting people from entering and leaving the affected cities.

Anna scrolled down and found an article about a powerful computer virus.
The virus was apparently so powerful that multiple countries were
prepared to disconnect from the internet at any given time.

Although the article was written in a serious and professional manner,
Anna saw through it at a mere glance. The two articles were both
smokescreens; they were fabricated.

The EY12 virus and the so-called powerful computer virus were all for
the sake of restricting contact between hostile forces. The IMF had to
have urged the countries to close their borders for the sake of rounding
up every anomalous organization in each country one at a time.

It sounded like bad news, but for Anna, it was actually good news.

The fact that the IMF had decided to create such a big commotion meant
that they were at a critical juncture. It seemed that the surface world
was about to usher in a new era, regardless of the victors.

"Big Sis, you're up so early?" Gao Zhiming asked, yawning while
scratching the scar on his forehead.

"It's just half past four. You can sleep a bit more," Anna said.

However, Gao Zhiming no longer wanted to sleep. After using the
bathroom, he began his physical training.

Gao Zhiming was just a twelve-year-old boy, but the daily high-intensity
training had bestowed upon him a physique that exceeded the average
human being's physique.

Chapter 1129 - Preparations

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Anna stood by the window with her arms crossed. She was staring at Gao
Zhiming hitting the punching bag in front of him. The muscles beneath
Gao Zhiming's skin had gotten even more robust, and his youthful face
was growing more and more similar to Charles' face back then.

The familiar face made Anna reminisce about what they had gone through.

"Big Sis, what's wrong?" Gao Zhiming asked with a teacup in both hands.

Anna returned to her senses and took the teacup before drinking its
contents in one go.

"It's nothing. Anyway, you should stop what you've been doing. That kind
of training at your level won't be of much help. And what I taught you
before is enough for you to defend yourself."

"But this is all I can do right now. If I stop it, what am I supposed to
do?" Gao Zhiming asked, sounding hesitant. Just then, he recalled
something, and his eyes lit up. "If you want me to stop training, then
can you teach me how to use those strange things, Big Sis? I mean, those
things they call anomalies.

"I realized that everyone became powerful once they started using those
things."

"No." Anna decisively rejected Gao Zhiming's request. Then, she lightly
flicked the cigarette between her fingers into the teacup.

An Anomaly could indeed make its user powerful thanks to its bizarre
abilities, but every Anomaly had its own side effects. Gao Zhiming would
indeed become more powerful with an Anomaly on hand, but the risk wasn't
worth it.

Gao Zhiming looked at the white ceramic teacup in his hand with an
aggrieved face. He quietly tapped the traces of red lipstick on it.

Upon seeing that, Anna reached out and pulled him into her arms. "Your
task is to live safely. You need to live until you fall into the
Subterranean Sea. You don't need to worry about anything else."

With food to eat, clothes to wear, and a good big sister by his side,
Gao Zhiming felt like he was in heaven. After all, he once used to
scavenge for food. However, he was already twelve years old, which meant
that he had slightly matured.

And even he could feel it—they were in the midst of a calm before the
storm.

"Aren't we about to go to war? I saw everyone preparing for it. And I
really just want to protect you, Big Sis."

"Did you not hear what I just said? Your task is to live safely. You
absolutely cannot be in any incident until you've fallen into the
Subterranean Sea. You're my only key to the Subterranean Sea."

Even though he could feel the oppressive pressure Anna was emanating,
Gao Zhiming still spoke, albeit his voice sounded like a mosquito's
droning. "A-am I just a tool to you, then, Big Sis?"

Anna was stunned. Was the little brat throwing a tantrum? She patted his
head with her hand. "No, of course, you're not a tool. You're my
husband."

"Then, do you care more about the future me than the current me?"

Anna looked at Gao Zhiming's eyes in surprise. Was he jealous? Was he
jealous of himself? He had grown up slightly, yet he was already so
troublesome. Charles was indeed Charles.

Anna lowered her head and gave him a light peck. Shaking her head, she
replied, "Of course not. The future you is a scumbag. Compared to him, I
prefer the current you."

Gao Zhiming licked his lips, savoring the sweet taste before grinning at
Anna's response. He spread his arms and lunged at his big sister.

"I like you, too, Big Sis. It would be great if I could be with you
forever."

Just as Anna was about to say something, there was a knock on the door.
She stuffed the cup in her hand into Gao Zhiming's arms and walked over
to the door.

When she opened the door, she saw Jackal standing outside.

"High Priestess," Jackal said respectfully, "The leaders have arrived.
We've investigated and confirmed that they do not have any Anomalies
protecting their minds."

"Great. Take our people and move. Don't keep our allies waiting."

An hour later, Anna went to a five-star hotel and met with a burly man
wearing sunglasses. Several fierce-looking men in military uniforms were
standing behind him.

Bizarre creatures resembling wireworms sprouted from their cuffs and
collars. The wriggling "wireworms" looked absolutely disgusting, yet
they couldn't be bothered to conceal them. After all, this was also a
form of intimidation.

"I heard you wanted to discuss some business with me? I really want to
know what kind of business a group of flies huddling together for warmth
has for me. Isn't it better to keep circling that pile of shit? I'm
talking about Subterranean London, of course," the burly man said
contemptuously.

To make matters worse, the burly man's eyes behind the sunglasses
brazenly sized up every inch of Anna's gorgeous figure.

Anna's mouth curled up slightly. She sat in front of the burly man and
grazed the curves of her slender figure with her fingers. "This is a big
business. I need some cannon fodder, and your men are perfect. I need as
many of them as possible."

Despite hearing the word "cannon fodder," the burly man didn't get angry
at all. "Sure, but can you afford to pay us? Our fees are very high."

"John, do we really have to talk about payment?" Anna's tone suddenly
changed, sounding disappointed as she said, "Have you forgotten that you
still owe me a big favor? If I hadn't intervened, your child would have
died."

The burly man's face turned ugly. He frowned and pondered for a long
time before finally clenching his fists and slamming them hard on the
table. "Damn it! This is the last time! After this, we're even!"

With that, he stood up and walked toward the back door with his
subordinates.

When Anna stood up, a female Fhtagnist behind her pulled out a chair.
Then, she held out the document in her hands with both hands.

The document was full of names, and more than half of those names had a
checkmark drawn next to them.

Taking a pen, Anna drew a big check mark next to the words "John V.
Furlan."

"Hmm... they're too easy. Since they don't bother putting up any
protection for their minds, they're like sitting ducks for me to
control."

Jackal walked up to Anna and asked, "High Priestess, this batch of
people only numbers a little over a hundred, and they aren't that
strong. This subordinate of yours believes that those people won't be
that useful against the IMF."

"I agree, but even though they're useless, they can still muddy the
waters by then," Anna replied and looked at the names on the document in
her hand.

The document was a list of people whose memories she had altered, and
they were all leaders of anomalous organizations.

The surface world was like a barrel of gunpowder, and these people were
the fuses Anna had found to ignite the powder keg.

Once it was time, those people would make a move together with her
Fhtagnist, and the powder keg would surely explode. The effects of herd
mentality also meant that they would move according to the plan.

Anna had done all these, as she believed that skirmishes were
meaningless. If she wanted to avenge herself, she had to start a world
war. Anna could already foresee the IMF's fate by then.

"We're almost done. There are only five people left, and once I'm done
altering their memories, we'll light the fuse three days later," Anna
said in satisfaction. She then threw the document back at the female
Fhtagnist.

Chapter 1130 - Dawn

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

"High Priestess, isn't this too hasty? I suggest we refine the plan even
further. Three days is too soon."

"It can't be delayed. Otherwise, those incompetent fellows will lose
heart. And I can only alter memories, not distort the reality. If we
drag this out, those a bit clever might come to their senses."

"I understand, High Priestess." Jackal nodded respectfully and walked
ahead to lead the way to the next meeting place.

Just then, Anna turned to the quiet Olivia and said, "You've seen me
alter the memories of so many people, so you must have learned a thing
or two, right? How about you give it a try on the next guy?"

Olivia's ability was similar to her own, and if she managed to learn
Anna's techniques, Anna's camp would have two Dioites.

Olivia panicked and waved her hands frantically. "I can't do it, High
Priestess. My mastery over my special ability has been improving, but
I'm way too far from your level. If I try it, I'm afraid that I'll burn
the target's brain."

Anna was a bit annoyed by her lack of confidence. "Why are you even
panicking? With me by your side, no one is going to die, so just go
ahead and give it a try."

"Hmm..." Olivia lowered her head, quietly enduring the pressure.

Anna shifted her gaze to the couple, Roy and Li Lu.

"The final battle is about to begin. When the time comes, you will lead
the way to the IMF's sites. I know that you know where their sites are
located."

The IMF's containment sites and laboratories were scattered all over the
world, and for the sake of safety and secrecy, they were built in places
rarely visited by people. In other words, those who weren't a part of
the IMF had no idea how many sites were out there.

Roy was holding his fast-asleep son. His face was solemn as he remarked,
"Li Lu and I are just shadows in the IMF with Level E- clearance, so we
don't really know the specific locations of the IMF's facilities and
sites."

"It's fine. Just tell me what you know. Don't be too nervous. I know
you're not lying." Anna walked over and flicked Tobba's chin with her
finger.

"Stop it, so annoying..." Tobba mumbled, burying his face deeper into
his father's embrace.

Anna turned to Li Lu, who was now holding Tobba, and said, "What's with
that bitter face? I'm sure you know that I treat my subordinates well.
Once the IMF is under attack, they won't have any time to deal with us.

"When the time comes, you can go wherever you want. I won't stop you."

Looking at her child in her arms, Li Lu's voice quavered as she asked,
"Is it really necessary for us to go this far? Many people are going to
die, and the entire world will be embroiled in war."

"Feeling guilty now? Well, you and your husband can still stop me. It's
still not too late," Anna said with a faint smile. She raised her right
hand and grabbed the strands of hair in front of Li Lu's face before
tucking them behind the latter's ear.

Li Lu tilted her head slightly to avoid Anna's white, slender fingers.
She knew that Anna's kindness was merely on the surface. After all,
there was a monster residing beneath Anna's human form.

Upon being reminded of that monster, Li Lu hugged her son even tighter.
"We're going to leave and stay away from the conflict. We're going to
raise Tobba in a place where no one can find us."

She had given up everything for her child, so at this point, Li Lu only
cared about her son.

"So you're going to retire? That's not a bad idea. A simple life is the
best."

Li Lu was quiet for a long while before finally speaking once again.
"Were you telling the truth when you met with my child in the future?
How was he? Was he doing well?"

Li Lu's words sounded like a bunch of nonsense, but when Anna was indeed
a colossal, tentacled monster, Li Lu couldn't help but partially believe
her words.

Anna took out a white-filtered cigarette and started smoking. "He's
doing well, and your child will go through many things. You don't need
to worry about him at all. He's definitely going to live until he dies."

"Tsk, you don't say?" the sleeping Tobba mumbled.

Just then, a poker card flew in front of Anna.

"You look happy today."

"Of course I'm happy. It feels great to no longer be under the mercy of
anyone," Anna said, taking in a deep breath. She truly hated the feeling
of being forced to hide like a rat.

"Are there many beings like us in the Subterranean Sea? And have they
formed some kind of society?"

Anna wrapped an arm around the clown's shoulder and cheerfully said,
"Calm down. Once we're down there, I'll give you a tour. That place is
the true home of beings like us, and I'm going to look out for you once
we're there."

The clown turned his head and looked at Anna's side profile.

"You're my companion, so wherever you go, I will go."

Anna looked at the clown and chuckled. It was a genuine chuckle with no
hidden motives. If one were to ask if Anna had a friend on the surface
world, then Anna would say that the loyal clown counted as half a
friend.

Anna had many people around her, but they all had their own agendas.

However, the clown was unlike them.

"As I said, I'll repay you tenfold—no, a hundredfold for what you've
done for me, and I still remember them all," Anna said, patting the
clown's shoulder before letting go.

"Actually, there's something I need your help with once everything is
over."

For the first time, the clown had actually asked for help.

"Sure, let me know when the time comes." Anna readily agreed.

Roy standing next to Anna opened his mouth to say something when a
frantic voice echoed from his earpiece. The voice was coming from the
guards outside.

"In Fhtagn's name, what on earth was that?! Be careful, everyone!
Something just flew past us!!"

Anna's figure vibrated at a high frequency, melting into the floor.

Before she could disappear into the floor, the attack landed.

Boom!

A violent explosion occurred, and Anna was blasted away.

Taking to the skies, Anna saw the enemies. They were a group of people
wearing pure white streamlined armor, and their faces were covered with
what looked like blue holograms. 𝚏𝕣𝐞𝗲𝐰𝕖𝐛𝐧𝕠𝕧𝚎𝚕.𝐜𝚘𝗺

They were enveloped in white light as they descended, making them look
like angels.

Anna returned to her human form amidst the expansive ruins, and she
found herself surrounded. Clearly, this group of people was here for
her. Soon, green corrosive flames manifested and suspended in mid-air
like anti-personnel mines

Anna cast a wary gaze at the attackers.

"Who are you people? Shouldn't you communicate first before attacking?"
Anna was certain that she had never encountered this group of people
until now.

Unfortunately, they seemed to have no intention of offering any
explanation as they flew toward Anna at breakneck speeds.

Chapter 1131 - Killing Move

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

The bustling city center was instantly plunged into chaos. People from
the collapsed hotel were fleeing in panic in all directions.

The deafening explosion reminded everyone present of the September 11
attacks that happened not long ago.

Everyone tried to stay as far away from the collapsed hotel as possible.
The cloud of dust shrouding the ruins of the hotel made it hard for
everyone to see what was going on.

A bladed weapon shrouded in a lightning arc swept toward Anna's arm at
breakneck speed. A burst of green blood spurted from the stump, but the
slender, fair arm streaking across the air transformed into a tentacle
covered in gray tendrils.

With her severed arm in hand, Anna was transfixed on the attacker. Her
will, like invisible tentacles, stretched toward the attacker. Anna
wanted to alter their memories in the blink of an eye to turn them into
an ally.

That was what she wanted to do, but reality proved different. The blue
holograms around their face seemed to be protecting their mind. As soon
as Anna's will made contact with their head, they retreated frantically
away from Anna.

Before Anna could switch targets, every figure in white armor around her
sensed something, and they retreated promptly, leaving nothing behind
but a mere afterimage.

The enemies were so well-trained and coordinated that Anna's heart
unknowingly tensed up. These figures in armor were completely different
from the other anomalous organizations.

Perhaps they were stronger than the IMF's Mobile Task Forces. An
organization wealthy enough to afford such state-of-the-art gears and
equipment meant that they were a powerful organization comparable to the
IMF.

Damn it, where the hell did these guys even come from? How come I've
never heard of them before? Are they the IMF's trump card? No, that's
impossible! They aren't sporting the IMF's emblem!

And their equipment's level of technology is clearly beyond this era...

In the face of the unknown and powerful threat, Anna no longer held back
and reverted to her true form.

Her delicate face split into six petals as if it were a flower, and
eight wriggling tentacles sprouted from within. A glimmer flashed in her
yellow, cross-shaped pupils, and the green, corrosive flames in the air
transformed into a giant net.

The giant net of flames pervaded the battlefield in an instant. Then,
Anna took advantage of that opening to make another move. Her tentacles
slammed the ground, propelling her toward one of the figures in white
armor.

Addressing the root of the issue was better than using a band-aid
solution, and Anna knew that well. In other words, she understood that
her goal was to shatter their encirclement and figure out exactly where
they were from.

Knowing the enemy and knowing oneself was the key to victory in every
battle.

The other figures in white had yet to make another move, but Anna was
already next to one of them. Anna's tentacles wrapped in green,
corrosive flames swung at her target as if it were a whip.

However, the target was clearly ready. A laser that moved like a rope
chopped at oncoming corrosive flames.

However, the bizarre green flames from a Divinity couldn't be easily
blocked. The corrosive flames engulfed the figure in white streamlined
armor, and he was reduced to ashes before he could even scream.

The death of one of the enemies allowed Anna to shatter the
encirclement. She took a moment to look around and noticed that Li Lu
and the others were under attack as well. Clearly, they wanted to make
sure that Anna wouldn't receive any help from her subordinates.

Their calculated move told Anna that they were thoroughly prepared and
had considered Anna's every single possible move.

"So you came prepared, hehe. However, I really want to know how I ended
up offending you people," Anna said. Then, her monstrous, tentacled form
vibrated at a high frequency before melting into the ground.

Of course, her enemies weren't the only ones capable of launching a
sneak attack.

Tentacles covered in gray tendrils sprouted from the ground. It grabbed
one of the figures in white armor before pulling them. Moments later,
another one was dragged underground, followed by a third one.

In response, the figures in white armor tapped their right palms a few
times. The blue holograms before their faces flashed red, and they
instantly learned Anna's location.

In the blink of an eye, they all distanced themselves from Anna.

Realizing that the enemies could see through solid ground, Anna snorted
inwardly. Their gear and equipment were a bit too advanced. Where
exactly were they from?

However, Anna already had a way to find the answer to her question.

After all, she was holding two figures in white armor at the moment.

Taking advantage of the brief lull, Anna's will instantly scoured their
memories, and two nearly identical sets of memories flashed before Anna.

They were clones, and they were completely identical in both appearances
and heights. The only difference between them was the barcode on their
necks.

They were immediately brainwashed since they were born and were
constantly under hypnosis as they grew up. Amidst their sea of memories,
Anna couldn't find anything else except for deeply carved hypnotic
suggestions.

Their personalities, way of thinking, and even their bodies were
extremely similar that it was like they were made from the same mold in
a factory.

These perfect warriors whose bodies would never reject any cyberware
were the ultimate result of genetic engineering.

It was a useful piece of information, but apart from that, Anna didn't
find anything else useful in their memories. In fact, their minds were
cleaner than robots.

Sigh, let me just take care of these guys first. Having made up her
mind, Anna rushed out of the ground.

The figures in white streamlined armor were well-trained and powerful,
but Anna had already learned everything there was to know about their
cyberwares, so they were no longer that difficult to handle.

Moreover, the clown and the others had already shattered their
encirclement and were starting to help Anna.

Soon, the dawn of victory was starting to shine on Anna and the others.

Anna was convinced that she had cracked open the secret to their attack
patterns, but before she could deal with the figures in white armor, her
scalp suddenly went numb.

A strong sense of crisis engulfed her, making her feel cold all over.

Then, the lantern-sized eye at the center of her body opened slightly
and looked up. Moments later, the singular eye socket was crowded by
more than a dozen yellow, cross-shaped pupils.

As Anna's line of sight took to the skies, it took her no time to find a
missile heading toward them at dazzling speeds.

"Run! Run away from them! They're the targets!!"

The battlefield plunged into chaos immediately afterward as everyone
frantically ran away from the figures in white armor.

Anna ordered the remaining Fhtagnist to bring up the rear before taking
the slowpoke Olivia into her arms and disappearing into the distance.
𝐟𝐫𝕖𝗲𝘄𝚎𝗯𝕟𝐨𝕧𝐞𝚕.𝕔𝕠𝐦

Anna ran desperately, instantly running five hundred meters away from
the figures in white armor. Despite that, the sense of crisis hadn't
diminished in the slightest.

The oncoming missiles were definitely not to be reckoned with.

Once she was a kilometer away from the figures in white, Anna stopped
and turned to find a black dot descending from the sky. First, there was
a bright white light, and then a "sun" was born.

Anna immediately went all out, drilling as deep as she could into the
ground.

Despite being five meters underground, she could still feel the violent
tremors from the surface.

Anna stayed underground for a long time, emerging only when the tremors
had faded away. Upon appearing outside, Anna was stunned. The world
outside had darkened, and the sun in the air was obscured by a huge
mushroom cloud.

The black dot just now had turned out to be a tactical nuke!

Those bastards from God knows where had actually dropped a tactical nuke
to deal with her!

Chapter 1132 - Reconcile?

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Considered the peak of human technology, nukes were extremely powerful.
Anna had no doubt that the figures in white armor, the remaining
Fhtagnists, and the civilians in the city center had been obliterated.

Anna was underground during the explosion, so she was unharmed. However,
the radioactive fallout was gnawing away at her body even at this
moment. Of course, Anna didn't have the luxury of time to care about
such a trivial issue.

She was more concerned about the identity of her attackers. The fact
that they could mobilize a weapon of mass destruction just to deal with
her meant that they could surely mobilize more than just that kind of
weapon.

Where exactly were they from?

Just then, the crisp sound of leather shoes echoed behind her,
interrupting her train of thought.

Anna put Olivia down on the ground and cast her massive eye toward the
noises.

The newcomer was a middle-aged man in a suit and leather shoes, and he
was walking confidently toward Anna while occasionally raising his right
hand to look at the watch on his wrist.

"As I said, Plan 3 isn't reliable. Saying that a tactical nuke can
easily deal with the world-renowned 315 is the same as underestimating
her too much," the middle-aged man said. He sounded hoarse and tired as
if he were a chain smoker.

"Hehe, so someone in charge has finally arrived?"

The tentacles retracted, and Anna's slender figure appeared before
everyone once again.

"I'm pretty sure I've never encountered you guys before, both in the
Subterranean Sea and on this surface world. What's with the hatred so
intense that you sent me such a tremendous gift for our first meeting?"

The middle-aged man chuckled. He put his hand down and straightened his
tie before saying, "You sure are humorous, Miss Anna. Anyway, let me
introduce myself first. You can call me Dr. Wilson. I'm a member of the
Foundation, and I'd like to keep my position confidential."

"The Foundation?" Anna's pupils constricted. The name didn't bring back
any good memories for her.

They're not the same Foundation as the one in the Subterranean Sea, are
they? Anna thought before asking, "Who exactly are you people?"

"Hmm, how should I explain it? You know that the IMF's core philosophy
is everything for mankind's sake, right? Well, our core philosophy is to
protect the IMF.

"Just as humans cannot perceive the IMF's existence, the members of the
IMF cannot perceive our existence as well.

"The fact that extremely dangerous Anomalies are just one layer away
from the human world is too much of a risk for us to bear, so it was
decided that two layers of protection are imperative.

"Memetic agents, anti-memetic anomalies, and crises that are truly
capable of threatening mankind are considered Crimson Threat Level
events. Of course, it is our responsibility to handle such events."

So they're like an IMF that protects the IMF? Anna found it absurd. She
never thought that such an organization existed on the surface world.

"No need to be surprised. Actually, our members have made contact with
you a long time ago. The three dreamers you killed were our members.
Honestly, when we received their transmission, we really thought we had
misheard.

"You should know that all of us have analyzed your fleshly body. It was
a genuine human body, and we never thought that you actually had another
fleshly body.

"Miss Anna, you're an enemy, but I must say, I really am in awe of your
methods."

Anna's eyes narrowed upon hearing that. The so-called dreamers were
actually members of their organization. Their ambush finally made sense.

The enmity between both sides hadn't appeared out of nowhere, as they
had been paying attention to Anna for quite a while now.

Just then, the air screeched, and Roy landed in front of Anna with his
wife and child in his arms. His face was unsightly as he remarked,
"There's an army outside. We can't get out. We've been surrounded."

It seemed that the enemies had prepared more than just one way to deal
with Anna. Clearly, they had gone all out just to kill Anna, the
calamity of humanity.

Although the conclusions Anna had reached on her own were just baseless
assumptions, she could say one thing for sure—she was in big trouble.

Just then, Wilson uttered words that were completely unexpected.
"Actually, I'm here to talk to you. I'm hoping that perhaps we can
reconcile."

"Reconcile?" Anna pointed at the huge mushroom cloud in the distance.
Her tone carried a hint of sarcasm as she replied, "You're talking about
