wanting to reconcile despite seeing that? Don't you think that's a bit
absurd? Reconcile? To hell with that!"

On the outside, Anna was furious, but this was just her pretense. At
this moment, she was carefully prodding Wilson's mind in an attempt to
alter his memories.

Of course, Wilson's mind was clearly under protection, but Anna had no
issues dealing with that as long as she had enough time. Indeed, what
she needed at the moment was time.

"Will anger even do you good at this point? You've been surrounded, so
the advantage is ours. Actually, it was difficult for us to put down our
weapons and talk to you like this," Wilson said.

He raised his right hand to glance at his watch before putting it down.

"How about this? Come back with us and tell us more about your species,
the so-called Subterranean Sea, and God Fhtagn. Join us and assist us in
completing our research, and I guarantee that no one will make a move
against you in the future."

"Of course, you can also—" Wilson's calm face suddenly became extremely
solemn.

Then, Anna heard a faint A.I voice echoing from Wilson's earpiece.

"Time anchor set; multi-space locks closed; reality modifier off; and
the Slayer has been equipped with 091. Teleporting in five, four,
three..."

A cold shiver ran down Anna's spine before engulfing her. At this
moment, she felt like her natural enemy was targeting her. It turned out
that the other party had no intentions of reconciling with her at all.

They were determined to kill Anna from the moment they attacked her.

Anna was buying time, but Wilson was doing the same? His frequent
glances at his watch were to check the time. He had proposed a
reconciliation to placate Anna and make her lower her guard, which would
increase the success rate of their mission.

Anna had no idea what was going to happen, but she knew that it couldn't
be good. Her figure vibrated at a high frequency to melt into the
ground, but she remained unmoving!

They clearly knew Anna's special abilities, so how could they not be
wary of them?

Clearly, an Anomaly had directly sealed Anna's special ability.

Anna took to the skies and streaked across the air, fleeing into the
distance. But no matter how fast she moved, the sense of crisis followed
her as if it were her shadow. It persisted and grew even stronger as
time went on.

As soon as Anna covered herself in her green, corrosive flames, the
sense of crisis engulfing her heart finally reached its peak.

Crack!

The ground cracked open, and barbed chains moved like vines, wrapping
tightly around Anna's legs. Then, as her vision blurred, her cognitive
functions slowed down before seemingly shutting down.

Just then, a portal in the shape of an inverted triangle appeared above
Anna.

Revealed within the portal was an old woman whose eyes, nose, mouth, and
ears were sealed. The old woman's slender arms were so long that they
nearly touched the ground, and her right hand was dragging a frightening
three-meter-long saber.

Chapter 1133 - Now

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

The old woman raised her saber and smashed it down. The saber moved so
fast that it left afterimages, and Anna's corrosive flames that seemed
to be capable of reducing everything to mere ashes were easily dodged.

In the blink of an eye, the saber was right in front of Anna.

At this moment, Anna's figure seemed to have been locked at a certain
point in space. She couldn't move, and even her will was immobilized.
She could only watch helplessly as the saber descended toward her head.

The impact came first before the saber could physically hit Anna. Her
fair skin cracked open, revealing tentacles underneath. Even the
tentacles split open into mangled flesh and blood.

However, the damage wasn't over yet.

Just as Anna thought that she was going to die, a black shadow flew over
and covered her face.

Clang!

Sparks flew as the sound of metal colliding with metal rang out.

Everything was settled in an instant.

A long, narrow ditch was gouged out behind Anna. The ditch was hundreds
of meters long and was unfathomably deep. Anna stood there, stunned. Her
encumbered will and senses were gradually returning to her as she
realized that she was actually still alive.

Before she could feel relieved, however, the black shadow on her face
split into two and fell powerlessly to the ground. Anna looked down and
saw the clown mask.

Loosely clumped and jet-black Origin Essence flowed out of the mask and
dissipated into nothingness. The clown mask had blocked the fatal blow
for Anna, and it died on the spot.

A tremor ran across Anna's heart at the realization, and an emotion she
had never felt before tugged at her heart.

Anna slowly turned to look at Wilson as well as the old lady, whose
facial features were sealed shut. Her lips fluttered open, seemingly
saying something. Immediately afterward, a grotesque noise echoed as
Anna was split open from the middle before falling to the ground.

Anna's greenish-yellow blood seeped into the ditch behind her. Her
figure twitched a few times before it completely stopped moving. She
looked absolutely miserable, except for her head; every other part of
her body was neatly cut in half.

The meticulous Foundation would never employ just one method against
their target. This was a perfect assassination that ended before anyone
could even react to it.

Wilson instantly breathed a sigh of relief at the sight. He tapped his
earpiece and muttered, "Target deceased; terminate all backup plans."

Roy and the others beside him were frozen. They had no idea what to do.

The fact that Anna was dead meant that it was meaningless for them to
resist.

Just then, several helicopters flew over and landed. Figures in white,
streamlined armor rushed out of the helicopters and cordoned off the
area.

Once it was confirmed that the area was safe, some people walked up to
Wilson and began talking to him. They seemed to be Wilson's colleagues,
and they were probably discussing the details of today's operation.

"We have to leave now. The radiation here is too high. I haven't had any
children yet. Let's leave and talk later," Wilson said. Then, he started
walking toward the helicopter.

Swoosh!

The air let out a shrill cry as a cold gleam swept past everyone.
Shockingly, the quiet old lady had abruptly slashed out with her long
saber. Those nearby revealed shocked faces, but before they could even
react, they collapsed to the ground.

They were neatly cut in half on the chest, and blood wantonly poured out
of their corpses.

The people in the helicopters sensed that something was amiss and began
to take flight. However, it was too late. There was a flash of cold
light, and the helicopters were chopped into two.

"Watch out! Disconnect from the network! Everyone, initiate passive
mode!!" Wilson exclaimed at the figures in white armor. Then, he ripped
his earpiece off his ear and made his way toward the downed helicopter.

He rushed into the helicopter and walked out of it with a medical kit in
hand. Then, he approached Anna and immediately started treatment with a
solemn face. After a while, Anna's two halves were sutured together, and
the massive wound was smeared with a thick layer of what looked like an
ointment.

However, it seemed that Wilson was still dissatisfied. He dragged the
corpses of his colleagues and stuffed them into Anna's monstrous maw.

Roy, Li Lu, and the others stared blankly at the sight.

Everything happened so quickly that they had yet to recover from the
shock.

How come that old lady with a saber suddenly attacked her comrades, and
what was wrong with that man called Wilson? Instead of returning to
base, why was he treating Anna's injuries?

Everyone present was confused, except for Olivia. She was capable of
peeking into anyone's memories, so she instantly realized what was going
on.

"Anna's still alive! When they let their guard down, she immediately
took advantage of that and transformed them into our comrades!" she
exclaimed. Then, she ran over and helped Anna up.

The wriggling tentacles retracted as Anna returned to her human form.
She then lay quietly in the young woman's arms.

The figures in white armor made no movement whatsoever. They weren't
going to make a move until Wilson had ordered them to do so. Thus, a
bizarre silence descended, and everyone's gaze was on Anna.

Anna was silent for a while. She trembled and struggled to stand up.
Once she was standing up, she walked two steps and grabbed the clown
mask on the ground.

Without its Origin Essence, the mask became extremely fragile. It was so
fragile that it began to crumble in Anna's hand.

Anna was reminded of the times she had spent with the clown.

She wasn't really aware of the clown's past, and she had been treating
it as a mere subordinate, but the clown's senseless and meaningless
death made Anna realize that she wasn't really indifferent toward the
clown.

"You wanted to ask me for a favor, but you hadn't said anything about
what you wanted me to do for you. It'd be great if I were aware of it,"
Anna muttered. She lowered her head and put on the broken mask.

Then, she gripped the mask tightly. The mask crumbled slowly in her
hands until it was finally reduced to a pile of rubble.

A tentacle lashed out. It moved so fast that it left afterimages in the
air as it landed on Wilson's face. The powerful impact allowed Wilson to
break free from Anna's control, but any efforts he mustered to defend
himself were useless.

He was destined to have his head twisted off by Anna's tentacle. A
tentacle covered in gray tendrils rolled up the head and placed it in
Anna's hand. She hugged the head and began gnawing on it.

At first, she gnawed on it slowly, seemingly enjoying it, but soon, she
began taking bigger and bigger chunks of it as her face became ferocious
to the extreme.

For the final bite, she went all out, crushing the skull and sucking in
every ounce of brain matter.

Anna's lower jaw parted like a flower, and a shrill, ear-piercing roar
filled with uncompressed fury rushed out of her maw covered in
razor-sharp teeth.

When Anna's roar faded, her lower jaw closed. She swept her gaze across
the figures in white armor standing frozen like statues and smiled
coldly. "Since you already know that I'm still alive, I'm not going to
hide anymore."

Anna looked around and saw Jackal in the distance. He was covered in
dust.

"Contact those whose memories I've altered and tell them to light the
fuse."

The confused Jackal stared in a daze at Anna. Then, he glanced at the
faces of his surviving comrades and asked, "Light the fuse? When?"

"Now!!"

Chapter 1134 - Start

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Roughly twelve hours after the ambush on Anna, a huge chunk of the
world's television signals was hijacked. Everyone's screens briefly
faded into static, including a few big monitors in Subterranean London.

It'd be easy for them to resolve the issue, but everyone froze upon
seeing the face being depicted on their monitor. The face belonged to
none other than 315, which meant that she was still alive.

"I don't think I need to introduce myself, do I? That's right, I'm still
alive. The IMF can't kill me that easily," Anna said calmly to the
camera, but her face was solemn.

Everyone was silent, but their breathing started to become rapid. They
could feel it; they could deduce what Anna was about to say.

"The IMF is almost done creating mobile task forces whose members have
absorbed Anomalies. What are you all waiting for? Are you waiting for
them to discuss peace with you? If that's the case, then wake up!

"Stop dreaming and wake up! All of you have been fighting the IMF for so
long, so how come you haven't realized it yet? In their eyes, we're
nothing but livestock!

"Once they've mastered the Anomaly fusion ritual, they'll definitely
treat us like cattle and sheep!!"

Anna leaned forward slightly, bringing her face closer to the camera.
Then, her bewitching voice echoed. "All of you know how to perform the
fusion ritual, so this is an opportunity! A fleeting golden opportunity
to make a move!

"Believe in me! The IMF is at its weakest, and we can do what they can
do! We're all standing at the same starting line as them!

"I know that you don't want to stick your necks out, as you are afraid
of the IMF's retaliation, and that's fine! I'm going to stick my neck
out first!"

Anna lifted the camera and walked out of the dim room.

Everyone's pupils constricted to needle points upon seeing what was
outside.

The minimalist architectural style of the building and the emblem
depicting three black arrows pointing inward told everyone that Anna was
in one of the IMF's sites.

Shockingly, the site was in a mess with corpses all over the place.
𝒇𝓻𝓮𝓮𝙬𝙚𝒃𝒏𝓸𝙫𝒆𝙡.𝓬𝓸𝒎

Figures in black robes were busy cleaning up the battlefield.

Just then, the Subterranean London received news of attacks on the IMF's
sites.

To make matters even more shocking, more than ten IMF sites were
attacked at once instead of just a few sites. 315 was telling the truth!
The mere thought of the opportunity to fight the IMF made everyone feel
restless.

"Comrades! Rise up and resist! The IMF's hegemony should have ended a
long time ago! Do you still want to continue living like rats in a
sewer? If you want true freedom for yourself, then go seize it
yourselves!"

"A brand-new era is nigh, and it will belong to us! We ought to stand
above humanity."

The video ended there, but its influence was far from over. Soon,
Subterranean London became empty, and there were only a few people on
the streets.

The IMF instantly became aware of what Anna was trying to do, but it was
too late for them to stop 315's message from spreading throughout the
world. Soon, reports of attacks came in from their sites all over the
world.

Indeed, their sites scattered all over the world were being attacked at
the same time. The stalemate that was supposed to last for a long time
was destroyed by Anna.

Every single anomalous organization with even the tiniest bit of enmity
against the IMF banded together with a common goal in mind—revenge for
the way the IMf had treated them by attacking the IMF's sites amidst all
the chaos.

Of course, they were also planning on seizing Anomalies for themselves
to become even stronger.

The chaos happening all over the world at the same time was impossible
to hide, and in no time, the entire world became aware of the existence
of Anomalies.

Tobba was clicking the mouse with his chubby right hand. He was browsing
through articles depicting images of Anomalies and the fights between
anomalous organizations. These reports had gone viral all over social
media.

The battles between monsters and people with incomprehensible, special
abilities made all sorts of rumors fly around, eliciting panic among the
common populace.

This was no longer just an issue between the IMF and Anna.

The entire human society was already involved.

Just then, the screen before Tobba froze. Those in power had declared
martial law, and they had just severed everyone's access to the
Internet.

"Haaa... "Tobba let out a long sigh. He raised his right hand—still
holding the mouse—and grabbed a baby bottle from the nearby table. He
stuffed it into his mouth and gave it a hard suck.

Wiping his mouth clean with the back of his hand, Tobba looked down at
the banana in his hand and chewed its top off. Then, he threw the banana
high into the air. When it descended, it became a peeled, hard-boiled
egg.

The egg rolled and was about to fall off the edge of the table when Li
Lu caught it with her hand.

"Darling, do you not like bananas?"

The egg was a banana once more, but Li Lu was unaware of the banana's
transformation, as the banana had always been a banana.

Tobba ground the banana with his teeth and swallowed it forcefully.
Then, he muttered, "Hmm... now that things have become so chaotic down
here, it's time for those up above to make their move."

Li Lu immediately became nervous upon hearing Tobba's inexplicable
words. "Darling, what are you talking about? Can you tell Mommy?"

Tobba stretched out his chubby arms toward her, letting her pick him up
into her embrace. "There's no point telling you, as you aren't even
qualified to perceive them, much less witness their fight."

Tobba looked around, sweeping his gaze across the room. The room's decor
hadn't been like this just a few moments ago. The influence was growing
stronger and stronger.

Li Lu frowned slightly in confusion. She lowered her head to kiss
Tobba's soft cheek, and then she carried him toward the door. "Let's go
to Daddy, okay? It's bad to stay cooped up in your room all by
yourself."

"No, I want to go outside and see how Anna is doing," Tobba replied.
Then, she struggled desperately to move toward the nearby window. There
was a commotion outside.

Clearly, Li Lu didn't want Tobba to get too deeply involved with Anna.

"Darling, listen to Mommy, okay? Let's stay here for now."

"No!! I want to go outside! If you dare stop me! I-I'll never eat
again!! I'll starve myself to death!!" Tobba exclaimed. Then, he pouted
with furrowed brows and crossed his arms in front of his chest.

Li Lu sighed softly. She knew that Tobba could actually do what he said.

"Then, let's just look outside through the window."

When the porthole was opened, a bustling port appeared before them. The
black-robed Fhtagnists had all gathered by now, and each of them led
large groups of people to many different ships.

They had employed not just passenger ships, but just about any vessel
that could carry people, and they all had one destination—the Indian
Ocean.

They were so many of them that they looked like moving ants, and of
course, they were all candidates to become Fhtagnists.

"There are so many people... there must be at least several hundred
thousand here," Tobba said, marveling as he leaned closer to the
porthole.

Li Lu became somber as she stared at the crowd. "Yes, there are so many
people, and at least half of them will die to convert the rest into
Fhtagnists."

It had started, and Anna needed loyal people on her side. This was the
first batch of the so-called "loyal" people, and there were certainly
more batches to come.

Chapter 1135 - Disappointment

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Having abandoned all pretenses, Anna no longer had any reason to hide.
She decided to take advantage of the chaos to obtain as much strength as
possible before launching her attacks. Since they had already torn off
all pretenses, there was absolutely no need for Anna to hide anything
anymore.

As the ships made their way to the Indian Ocean, senior Fhtagnists
wearing pendant-sized silver statuettes of Fhtagn began sharpening some
golden "tools."

The deck was cleared of people long ago, and the ritual would soon
begin. Anna's decision was both pragmatic and reasonable, but there was
a small problem. She had ignored the feelings of the only person whom
she cared about on the surface world.

Gao Zhiming walked out of the bridge, gripping the railing as he made
his way to the deck.

Rows upon rows of people with bound hands were on his right, and their
eyes reflected nothing but fear. They swayed back and forth amidst the
waves. They could barely muster any strength to stand due to the despair
and the lack of food as well as water.

However, this wasn't really out of place. The majority of these people
were going to die anyway, so giving them food and water was a complete
waste of supplies. They just had to stay alive, and that was it.

Gao Zhiming was familiar with them. Just days ago, they had been
students, office workers, and from all walks of life, but now, they had
all become nothing but slaves.

"Water, I want some water... I'm so thirsty..." a young woman muttered,
pleading to Gao Zhiming with lips so dry they had turned white. She
collapsed to the ground before Gao Zhiming could respond.

Gao Zhiming was once a vagrant, and he knew the agony of thirst. He
couldn't refuse her request, so he rushed to his room, grabbed a cold
drink from the refrigerator, and stuffed it into her arms.

When there was one, there would be two. Everyone lunged at the young
woman, clamoring for the cold drink in her hands. Some of them reached
out toward Gao Zhiming as well. In the end, he couldn't take it anymore
and fled in panic.

He knew that he couldn't help everyone, but tears still dripped down his
cheek. He cried and cried until he broke down into uncontrollable
sobbing.

Gao Zhiming huddled in a corner as if he had been abandoned. He buried
his head in his folded arms, venting the emotions in his heart by
sobbing loudly.

Just then, a soft hand reached over his chin and lifted his head.

What appeared before Anna was a face covered in snot and tears. However,
she felt not even a tinge of disgust as she took out a tissue and wiped
him clean.

"Big Sis, they're not like those bad people from people. They're not
bad! They're good people! Can we just let them go? Please?" Gao Zhiming
asked, almost begging for Anna to let those people go.

Anna said nothing. She pulled Gao Zhiming to his feet and walked toward
their room.

"Big Sis, they're not a threat to us! Can we just let them go? Can we go
and catch pirates instead?" Gao Zhiming said. He grabbed the hand
pulling him and yanked with all his might, but he was too weak compared
to Anna.

"Say something. Big Sis!"

Anna squeezed Gao Zhiming's hand until it was black and blue, but she
still hadn't said a word.

Gao Zhiming went from struggling to biting. He frantically tore at
Anna's arm, trying to make her let go.

Anna threw Gao Zhiming onto the bed, eliciting a loud noise. Gao Zhiming
sat up quickly and saw Anna closing the door. Clearly, she was going to
lock him up inside this room.

"Y-y-you liar!" Gao Zhiming roared. He shouted so loudly that his voice
cracked, and his voice was filled with both disappointment and anger as
he added, "You're the bad person! You're a monster! You're a murderer!!"

"Pfft! You really are dumb. You just realized that?" Anna asked with a
sneer. Her body then split open as she reverted to her true form. "I'm a
member of the Diois Tribe, and humans have always been nothing but our
food.

"I really have no idea what kind of fantasy you've been harboring about
us.

"You made me human and made me live in human society, but did you really
believe that I'd eventually take pity on my food, Charles? Dream on!!"
Anna's monstrous maw split open as she roared at Gao Zhiming.

Gao Zhiming cried even louder. At this very moment, his idealized image
of Anna collapsed. He had killed quite a few people before, and he had
been deluding himself into thinking that those people were bad people.

However, the faces of the people outside made Gao Zhiming realize that
he couldn't do it anymore—he couldn't lie to himself anymore.

Bang!

The door was slammed shut and was also locked from the outside.

Anna's face was as cold as ice as she made her way to the bridge. Her
eyes were fixed on the coordinates being displayed on the instrument
panel.

Just then, a Fhtagnist pushed the door open just a bit rougher than
usual, but Anna instantly tore him in half and stuffed him into her
stomach.

Anna wasn't hungry. She was simply venting.

The blood splattering in the air made everyone fall silent. They dared
not speak nor make any large movements. They were terrified of following
in the unlucky Fhtagnist's footsteps.

Amidst the extremely oppressive atmosphere, several ships finally
reached their destination. Anchors weighing over ten tons were lowered,
crashing into the seabed.

Everyone stopped and turned to Anna, awaiting her next order.

Anna reached for the microphone connected to every ship. Through the
bridge's glass, she swept her gaze across the people on the deck of
every ship.

Looking at their terrified faces, Anna realized that she was actually
hesitating. She discovered that she couldn't sacrifice these people as
easily as she had sacrificed those two million people in the Eastern
Seas. 𝗳𝚛𝗲𝕖𝚠𝚎𝚋𝗻𝗼𝕧𝗲𝐥.𝚌𝚘𝐦

Impossible! That fragile human body of mine couldn't have changed me!
Just then, Gao Zhiming's disappointed gaze flashed through her mind. Do
I really care about what he thinks? What a joke! If I want, I can make
him think whatever I want him to think!

"Everyone, do it!" Anna's voice was transmitted to all ships. The golden
"tools" in the hands of the qualified Fhtagnists were raised high at
once before plunging into the chests of the sacrifice.

Human flesh and organs were pulled apart before being arranged to form
the array dedicated to Fhtagn. The sacrifices were the elderly, the
children, and the women, while those who were going to be converted to
Fhtagnists were all young adults.

Their eyes were filled with intense hatred and agony as they walked into
the array, but upon walking out of the array and receiving their black
robe, their eyes contained not even a trace of the previous hatred and
pain.

Instead, they gleamed with a fanaticism bordering infinitely closer to
obsession.

Desperate screams and cries pervaded the skies above the Indian Ocean.
There was so much blood dripping from the deck that the sea around the
gathered ships had turned red.

Anna smiled in satisfaction at the steady stream of converted Fhtagnist.
This was merely the beginning. Her army of fanatics was bound to
multiply at breakneck speed. The IMF's defeat was inevitable!

The ritual was bloody yet boring.

Anna turned and left the bridge, walking toward the room she shared with
Gao Zhiming.

Forget it. That brat's emotions have been a bit too volatile recently. I
should explain things to him. Anna thought as she walked down the
corridor. She reached out to the doorknob to open the door, but she
froze.

She could smell something strange inside the room. Anna tore down the
locked door, and a shocking scene was revealed before her.

Gao Zhiming was lying on his side on the bed, and a pistol hung loosely
in his hand.

There was a huge, gaping hole on his head. The powerful gun had
obliterated everything above his neck, and his bones, brain matter, and
eyeballs splattered all over the wall, creating an exquisite but
grotesque painting.

Chapter 1136 - Laplaces Demon

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Anna stared at Gao Zhiming's corpse for a few seconds before launching
into a tirade of curses.

"You cowardly scum! Are you even a man?! Why did you kill yourself over
such a trivial matter?! Do you know how hard I've been working just to
go back? My plans! My hardwork! My everything! You've ruined everything,
you useless scum!

"I should have broken all your limbs and turned you into a tool! A mere
tool for me to use!"

Despite being left breathless after the fierce round of cursing, Anna
was still not satisfied. She swung her right hand and slammed Gao
Zhiming's corpse into the wall.

She threw the corpse so hard that it bounced off the wall and splattered
on the ground. Anna gnashed her teeth and paced back and forth in the
room. Her breathing grew more and more rapid, while the fury in her
heart burned fiercer and fiercer.

Right as her fury was about to reach its peak, Anna froze and realized
one thing—Gao Zhiming was dead. He was really dead. She walked up to Gao
Zhiming's corpse and picked up the headless body.

Gao Zhiming's crimson tongue swayed left and right.

"Gao Zhiming... is dead?" Anna trembled. For the first time ever, she
panicked. "No, he can't be dead! He's supposed to be the future Charles!
How could he possibly die?!"

Dozens of tentacles whipped rapidly around the room, tearing down the
"painting." Anna had enough knowledge of human anatomy that she easily
reassembled the pieces of flesh into a makeshift head that she piled
onto Gao Zhiming's headless corpse.

"You can't be dead! I mean, how could you even die? You're supposed to
become a god in the future!" Anna's agitated words seemed to be directed
at the deceased Gao Zhiming, but they were actually meant to comfort
herself.

Her trembling tentacles pressed hard on the flesh, seemingly trying to
shut the gaps between the bloody flesh. As Anna pressed even harder, the
gaps seemed to have disappeared.

Gao Zhiming's head looked as if it were still alive. It was like he was
about to come back to life. The next moment, however, Gao Zhiming's head
exploded like a watermelon, and bits and pieces of him scattered
everywhere.

As Anna looked down at the headless corpse before her, she soon began to
accept the harsh reality. She held Gao Zhiming's corpse in her arms as
yellowish-brown tears flowed from her eyes.

Anna tried hard not to cry out loud, but it was futile. At this moment,
she finally understood how important he was to her.

The reason Anna was furious when Charles sent him to the surface world
wasn't because she had lost the opportunity to become a god. Instead, it
was out of worry. She was worried about Charles.

Anna's grip on Gao Zhiming in her arms tightened, and it was like she
wanted to fuse him with herself.

Anna's emotions spiraled out of control. First, she became hysterical,
and then she apologized to Gao Zhiming, promising that she would listen
to whatever he said as long as he came back to life.

Afterward, she began reminiscing about the moments they shared together.
Then, she hurled a hail of curses at him before kissing him
passionately. After an unknown amount of time, Anna finally went silent.

She knelt there motionless as if she were dead.

Just then, Anna heard someone vigorously sucking on something. Her
pupils turned slowly until she saw Tobba leaning against the door with a
baby bottle in hand.

"Tsk, tsk, tsk, what a horrible death. This one definitely ranks in the
top five," Tobba remarked. He was talking while drinking milk, so his
round belly was instantly soaked with milk.

Swoosh! 𝘧𝓇𝑒𝑒𝑤ℯ𝑏𝓃𝘰𝑣ℯ𝘭.𝘤ℴ𝘮

Anna appeared in front of Tobba in the blink of an eye. She then grabbed
him with both hands and lifted him up. Her murderous glare was on him as
she asked, "You know a way to save him, right? You definitely have a way
to save him, right?"

"No way. Dead is dead," Tobba replied, looking and sounding extremely
calm.

"No way that's true! You're the one who saved me last time, so you can
definitely save him, too!" Anna exclaimed. She had long figured out the
bizarre series of events at the time, and she had simply remained silent
about it.

"Hmm, you're putting me on the spot here. I'm not really good at this
kind of thing. For something like this, you should go look for Lily,"
Tobba replied, looking innocent as if Anna had yet to catch on.

"Y-you remember everything that happened? Otherwise, how do you know
about Lily? Wait, you remember everything?! Since you've recovered your
memories, why did you not warn me that Gao Zhiming would end his life?!

"You've always believed that I could foresee the future, and even I
thought the same of myself, despite losing my memories, but the truth is
that I really cannot foresee the future. How should I describe this
special ability of mine?

"Have you heard about Laplace's demon?"

"What?" Anna asked, sounding stunned. She felt that the Tobba in front
of her was different from the Tobba she was familiar with. Pressed
against the wall, Tobba couldn't really struggle, but he still managed
to throw his baby bottle.

The baby bottle collided with Gao Zhiming's eyeball on the ground, and
the eyeball bounced up before Anna.

Anna's pupils constricted and trembled upon seeing the intense emotions
frozen within those eyeballs just moments before Gao Zhiming pulled the
trigger.

Taking advantage of the distraction, Tobba easily escaped Anna's arms,
and he landed steadily on the ground.

"My body has always been that of an ordinary human being. Unlike you
all, my special ability only activates upon my death. Whenever I die, I
will return to the moment of my birth, retaining all my memories to
start a new life.

"I'm like Lily after she made contact with that iceberg. The only
difference is that each cycle lasts only forty minutes for her, while
mine is an entire lifetime.

"Laplace's demon... it is a thought experiment made by a human being. If
there were a being that could know the cause and effect of all events,
then they could know everything that will happen in the future through
deduction."

Tobba looked up at Anna. "By repeating the same life over and over
again, I became thoroughly familiar with everything about you all,
including all the cause and effect in the world. If I wanted to do so, I
could easily change the outcome of all things by altering seemingly
insignificant details.

"In other words, I am Laplace's demon."

Tobba's reveal contained too much information for Anna to process at
once. "What does any of that have to do with anything?! What does that
have to do with you not telling me that Gao Zhiming would commit
suicide?!"

"It does matter, as they're already fighting. Thanks to them, this world
has become illogical and unstable. This world is doomed. Since you've
already chosen this path, just keep walking down on it. There's no
medicine for regret in this world, anyway. Goodbye."

Tobba lashed out with his foot, and a small fragment of Gao Zhiming's
bone flew toward the wall. The bone fragment bounced off the wall and
flew toward Tobba, piercing his neck with precision.

Anna watched as Tobba slowly collapsed to the ground and bled to death.

Meanwhile, in an unknown place with neither time nor space, Charles
calmly watched as one of his tentacles changed colors, becoming the
exact same pitch-black color as Fhtagn's figure.

Gao Zhiming in the timeline being depicted by the tentacle was dead, and
he had completely disappeared. Everything on that tentacle had become a
part of Fhtagn.

This wasn't an isolated case. In addition to the tentacle that had
turned completely pitch-black, some black spots had also appeared on the
other tentacles.

Chapter 1137 - Anna

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Upon seeing that one of his tentacles had become a part of Fhtagn, the
omniscient Charles instantly realized what had happened.

"Well played. Besides the tattoo on my neck, you actually have other
tricks up your sleeve. Your level of skill in this chess game is
impressive, considering how you don't even have any consciousness. I'm
truly impressed.

"However, if you think that you can assimilate me with just that, you're
being far too presumptuous. We're not that different."

Charles transmitted that information over to Fhtagn, and every line on
his body began to glow faintly. Soon, he found another tentacle with the
smallest difference in attributes and appearance from the pitch-black
one.

The white tentacle made out of matter, time, and space rotated, wrapping
around the pitch-black one.

The two tentacles had clearly distinct colors at first, but as they
moved even closer together, some commonalities between the tentacles
overlapped.

Completely identical planets, completely identical time, and completely
identical human beings. The entangled colors were no longer distinct,
becoming a mixture of black and white—gray.

The two lines were almost identical, except for one difference. Gao
Zhiming was dead in one of the lines, while he was still alive in the
other.

When Charles dragged over the living Gao Zhiming from the other line, he
partially regained control over the blackened tentacle that Fhtagn was
supposed to have completely assimilated at this point.

Thus, what was supposed to be predetermined was changed.

***

Anna was standing in the bridge of a ship somewhere on the Indian Ocean.
There was a microphone in her hand as she cast her gaze on the
Fhtagnists awaiting her orders on the deck.

The Fhtagnists were ready, and the ritual would immediately proceed once
Anna gave the green light.

The sacrifices on deck trembled with anxiety and fear in their eyes. It
couldn't be helped. Just one word from Anna would decide their fate,
after all, and Anna would instantly obtain hundreds of thousands of
loyal subordinates.

The plan was to obtain more Fhtagnists by ordering the new Fhtagnists to
capture more people. With that plan, her forces would surely expand
exponentially. If she were to do that, neither the IMF nor the
Foundation would stand a chance against her.

Soon, no one would be able to ambush, suppress, or chase after her. It
was a plan that had all the pros but no cons. There was no reason not to
employ the plan, but the Fhtagnists had yet to receive any word from
Anna.

Anna's heart wavered upon recalling Gao Zhiming's roar and his extremely
disappointed gaze. She had no idea why, but she just couldn't do it. She
had sacrificed two million people in the Eastern Seas without batting an
eye, but now, she couldn't bring herself to sacrifice not even half of
those people at the time.

Gao Zhiming and her human body's influence on her were far greater than
what Anna had imagined. She struggled for a long time before putting
down the microphone in her hand and turning around to head to their
room.

There was a knock at the door of their shared room. Gao Zhiming
suppressed his sobbing as he opened the door with tears streaming down
his face.

Anna cast a mocking gaze at him. After a while, she sounded like she was
mocking herself as he said, "I really don't know why you think of me as
something beautiful when I'm a man-eating monster.

"Are you blind again? Did you not see my true form?"

Silence descended and lasted for a while. Upon realizing that there
weren't any screams outside and Anna wasn't covered in the pungent smell
of blood, Gao Zhiming realized something that made him smile.
𝑓𝘳𝑒𝑒𝓌𝘦𝘣𝘯ℴ𝑣𝘦𝑙.𝘤𝑜𝑚

He was now crying and smiling at the same time. The big sister in his
heart had returned. He spread his arms and rushed forward, hugging
Anna's legs.

Anna patted his head, and a hint of helplessness appeared on her face.

"I really don't know where you learned to do something so useless, and I
never taught you to do any of that. You don't want them to die, but we
don't have enough people. We need enough people, or we're probably going
to die.

"There's always a better way, and... I'll do everything I can to protect
you, Big Sis!" Gao Zhiming exclaimed decisively.

"Tsk, you're still wet behind the ears, so how useful can you be?" Anna
replied, clicking her tongue. She then pushed him hard enough to make
him stagger backward.

Regardless, Anna knew that the ritual couldn't proceed anymore.

She badly wanted to proceed with the ritual, but as she stared at Gao
Zhiming standing in front of her, she realized that she truly couldn't
be cruel enough to make the so-called angel in Gao Zhiming's heart
become a murderous demon.

Forget it. The world is already so chaotic, so there's no need to get
more Fhtagnists. Let's think of another way. After all, my goal is to
return to the Subterranean Sea, not to rule the entire world, Anna
thought. Then, she took out some tissues and leaned down to clean Gao
Zhiming's tear-stricken face.

Just then, Li Lu walked up to them with Tobba in her arms.

Tobba saw everything unfold in the distance, and he couldn't help but
express his amazement. "Great, you've already changed your mind? You're
so whimsical, just like a kid."

Tobba attempted to squeeze himself out of Li Lu's arms, but he realized
that he couldn't move at all. When he blinked, he found himself staring
at the ceiling. He had somehow become a corpse sprawled out on the
ground, and his body was growing cold fast.

He had died many times before, so he knew that this scene wouldn't last
long.

"This is the first time I've seen something like this. An overlap of two
worlds? How interesting."

Just then, Anna stood up and cast a furious gaze at Gao Zhiming's
headless corpse.

"Do you really think that dying will solve everything? Dream on! You may
have died, but I can still bring you back!!" Anna roared. Her sorrowful
gaze gradually became cold and fanatical. At this moment, she became a
true Dioite.

Anna stomped hard on the ground, and her figure vibrating at a high
frequency shot upward. She phased through the roof and arrived at the
blood-soaked deck.

Her hundreds of thousands of loyal subordinates had been waiting for her
outside.

"Brothers and sisters! The great God Fhtagn has bestowed upon me an
oracle!" Anna's voice echoed from the speakers of every nearby ship.
"The almighty Great One wants to test us! He wants to know if we are
indeed loyal to Him!

"To prove our loyalty to the Great One, we can only do one thing! We
ought to sacrifice the seven billion human beings on the surface world!
We must sacrifice them to the Great One!!"

"My goodness! What is she trying to do?!" Tobba exclaimed in horror.
Then, he realized that he was staring at a cause and effect that he had
never seen before.

"Darling, what are you talking about?"

Li Lu's words brought Tobba back to his senses. He finally realized that
he hadn't gone anywhere. He hadn't become a corpse; he was still in his
mother's embrace, and Anna was still there, speaking quietly with Gao
Zhiming.

Tobba frowned deeply and scratched his head, which barely had any hair.

Once everything was settled, Anna held Gao Zhiming's hand and walked
away.

Li Lu nervously came to her side and asked, "Anna, what are we going to
do about the people outside?"

"What else can we do about them? We're going to return them, of course.
What? Do you really expect me to fulfill the basic needs of so many
people?"

Li Lu breathed a sigh of relief upon hearing that. She really thought
that Anna was going to sacrifice all these people for her cause.

"Stop the nonsense and pick up the pace. This commotion must have
already attracted the IMF's attention, so we need to leave as soon as
possible."

Chapter 1138 - War

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

The IMF was surely feeling overwhelmed at the moment and could most
likely not spare any resources to look for Anna. However, Anna had never
been the one to rely on mere chances.

"Anna, what's our next step?" Li Lu asked, sounding a bit anxious. She
was truly afraid of what Anna could do, and she was scared that she
might come up with some new idea.

"Our next step? Since we've decided to avoid increasing our numbers for
now, we'll go and look for the IMF's command center. We have to take
their command center down, or this war will never end. 𝕗𝚛𝚎𝚎𝐰𝗲𝗯𝗻𝚘𝚟𝚎𝗹.𝕔𝐨𝕞

"The IMF is a massive organization, so it definitely has a command
center somewhere. We just have to find its command center and obliterate
it. After that, we only have to wait. The others will help me bring down
the IMF."

As a former member of the IMF, however, Li Lu was opposed to Anna's
plan. She quickened her pace and blocked Anna's way to the deck. She
looked a bit anxious as she said, "Anna, you've got to think of another
way. That won't work.

"The higher-ups are unknown to us, and we don't even know how many
people are a part of the command center. How can we find them when we
don't know anything about them?"

"Don't get ahead of yourself. A human organization can never be a
monolithic entity with no room for individual input. Since they can
plant spies among us, then we can certainly plant spies among them,"
Anna replied.

She glanced at the sacrifices squatting near the railings, and her gaze
landed on a young woman. She was the one who asked Gao Zhiming for some
water earlier.

Anna effortlessly accessed her memories and found that everything was
normal.

It was so normal that it was bizarre.

Anna dug a bit deeper and found something in the depths of her sea of
consciousness. Indeed, the young woman was one of the IMF's shadows.

Instead of exposing her, Anna walked away with Gao Zhiming. While the
two of them were walking away hand in hand, Anna began altering the
young woman's personality and memories as if she were fiddling with a
machine.

Anna was undoubtedly an expert at anything related to memories, as
before they had gone far, the young woman was already Anna's double
agent. Shockingly, the young woman herself had no idea that she had just
become Anna's double agent.

Some days later, the ships returned to port, and Anna realized that a
double agent with a pitifully low clearance level wasn't going to be
very useful. Knowing that there was strength in numbers, however, she
decided to convert every shadow the IMF had sent her way into her double
agent.

While waiting for their report, Anna made the same move as the other
anomalous organizations. She attacked the IMF's sites, seizing Anomalies
to bolster the strength of her forces.

Since she couldn't overwhelm the IMF through numbers, she could only use
a crude method to deal with them, for now.

Fortunately, the world was in so much chaos that neither the IMF nor the
Foundation behind them seemed to be willing to stake everything they had
to fight Anna in a deathmatch.

And unfortunately, it was already too late for them to go all-out
against Anna.

After all, she had already opened Pandora's box. Even her death wouldn't
help assuage the chaos. In addition, Anna was still alive despite quite
a few assassination attempts. The IMF and the Foundation were no longer
certain if they could truly kill her by simply attempting to assassinate
her again.

Just like that, a long war began. There was a battlefield everywhere,
and many people died every day.

Some organizations even made contact with Anna upon learning that she
was the legendary 315, and many of them expressed their willingness to
follow her orders for the sake of defeating the IMF.

It seemed that Anna had become a spiritual leader among the anomalous
organizations. Although they weren't really that strong and the majority
of them simply wanted to ride on her coattails, Anna still accepted
them.

Something was better than nothing, and besides, she no longer had to be
afraid of the possibility that the IMF's shadows were among them.

Under Anna's leadership, they captured and plundered several IMF sites,
becoming severalfold stronger than before. Unfortunately, the bigger
they grew, the fiercer the IMF's offensive became against them.

One day, the IMF mobilized its task forces and attacked Anna's group
along with a government armed force.

Both sides suffered heavy casualties, and even though Anna and her
people wielded powerful, special abilities, they couldn't possibly
withstand a carpet bombing.

In the end, Anna neutralized the bombers, obtaining a hard-earned
victory for herself.

The IMF had lost, but Anna's momentum was curbed as a result of the
heavy losses. However, the IMF's decision to involve a government's
armed forces made Anna realize one thing.

If the IMF could do that, couldn't we do the same? Was it really hard to
take control of a government armed force?

Many more things happened afterward, and Anna's group grew even larger.

At this point, it became clear that the IMF was weakening. It couldn't
be helped. After all, the entire world was in chaos, and they were
running out of manpower. They could mobilize to secure a site, but they
would end up losing another site somewhere else.

The war between the two sides was undoubtedly fierce.

Each and every nation decided to stand with the IMF at first, but some
decided to change their mind upon learning that they could obtain
special abilities upon absorbing an Anomaly.

Those with bigger ambitions even began seeking more ways to become even
stronger, and some even began cooperating with anomalous organizations,
turning against the IMF in the pursuit of immortality.

Those seeking to profit handsomely decided to abandon the sidelines and
jump into the fray, adding to the chaos.

Just like that, a year and a half passed. At this point, the majority of
the IMF sites had long been obliterated. Without the IMF's containment,
quite a few Anomalies ran free, causing many incidents.

Anna thought that the chaos would continue without any changes until the
IMF's fall, but she received shocking news from her double agents. They
had found traces of the IMF's command center.

Unfortunately, the news couldn't be any worse. The members of the
command center had gathered to talk about using an A+ Keter Anomaly to
restart the world itself.

Clearly, the IMF had a contingency plan on the off chance that the world
became completely out of control.

Upon hearing the news, Anna knew that they were done playing. The IMF
was no longer going to entertain them and was planning on shutting down
the game by flipping the table.

Anna had no plans of letting them do as they wished. She contacted
everyone and made a beeline for the command center. Regardless of what
exactly the A+ Keter Anomaly was, they had to seize or destroy it before
the IMF could flip the table.

Gao Zhiming was jolted awake by a speed bump, and he immediately reached
out for his weapon in response.

Everything that had happened over the past year and a half led to Gao
Zhiming maturing rather quickly, but he had also indeed grown older. He
was now thirteen years old, after all.

Realizing that they weren't in immediate danger, Gao Zhiming let go of
his weapon and looked at Anna beside him. He had grown a head taller,
and since Anna was rather petite, he would soon catch up to her in
height.

Anna was leaning on the soft seats of the car, and her black hair was
spread out like a cloud in the sky.

Gao Zhiming's gaze traced down her long lashes and rosy lips before
finally settling on her exposed shoulders, which were as white as milk.

The sight made Gao Zhiming breathe a bit rougher. Clearly, he was no
longer an ignorant child. Anna's refreshing curves as well as her faint,
distinct fragrance made Gao Zhiming feel a bit restless.

He hadn't hugged his sister for a long time because inappropriate
thoughts would always pop up in his mind whenever he hugged her.

"What are you looking at? Wanna touch?" Anna asked.

Gao Zhiming's face instantly turned bright red.

"Big Sis, can we... sleep separately from now on?" Gao Zhiming asked
nervously with his head down.

Anna opened her eyes and picked up the phone next to her.

"Control yourself. The final battle is nigh, so now's not the time for
that. Once we're done dealing with them, I'll let you do whatever you
want."

Chapter 1139 - Overlap

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Gao Zhiming's face turned even redder. Then, in a voice that sounded
more like a mosquito's droning, he said, "B-big Sis, what exactly are
you talking about..."

Smack!

Anna gave Gao Zhiming's cheek a light smack, leaving the latter in a
daze.

"Let me say this again—focus. This isn't a game. Don't drop the ball on
me," Anna remarked.

Gao Zhiming stewed in embarrassment for a while. Fortunately, the car
finally came to a halt. They had arrived at their destination.

Gao Zhiming was about to alight from the car when Anna grabbed him and
stuffed a golden ball covered with ornate, special patterns into his
hand. "Open this as soon as you notice something amiss. Do you
understand?"

"Yes, I understand," Gao Zhiming replied and held the ball firmly.

Finally, the two alighted from the car and saw that the Fhtagnists were
already at their positions along with a large group of armed soldiers.
These Fhtagnists had become far more useful than before when they had
nothing but fearlessness and an obsession with Fhtagn.

These Fhtagnists would do anything for Fhtagn's sake. Anna looked up at
the dark skies. Today's weather was gloomy, and it was probably going to
rain. As Anna's gaze descended, she saw a city shrouded in dark clouds
in the distance.

The IMF's command center—the higher-ups of the IMF—was located in the
distant city. Anna knew that they weren't blind, and they definitely
knew that she was here. Unfortunately for them, they had nowhere to run.
Their best site capable of defending against just about anything was
right over there.

"Don't be nervous. I was told that it takes them a long time to activate
powerful Anomalies, and it is very expensive for them as well.
Unfortunately, they've run out of time to make any changes. They're
destined to lose."

Just then, the doors of several armored vehicles nearby were opened, and
figures with familiar faces alighted from those vehicles.

The cruel, long-standing war had greatly changed everyone, but if
someone were to ask Anna as to who changed the most, she would
undoubtedly say that it was Olivia.

Olivia's mastery over her special abilities reached unprecedented
heights thanks to Anna's guidance. She had access to as many
experimental subjects as she could, so Olivia eventually learned to
alter memories, cognition, and personalities as she wished, just like
Anna.

Unfortunately, there was no free lunch in this world. Every anomalous
ability came with its own drawback. Having become intimately familiar
with human nature, she became completely disillusioned with humanity.

Olivia had become an extremely cold and radical woman. She was becoming
more and more like Anna as time went on. She looked down on everyone. In
her eyes, her fellow humans were just beasts capable of communication.

Jackal had changed greatly as well. He wasn't just Charles' first
believer anymore. He had become Anna's right-hand man, assisting Anna
with all affairs related to their organization.

His efforts allowed him to occupy a lofty position second only to Anna
in the coalition force that the latter had founded against the IMF. His
lofty position was exhausting to maintain, but Jackal remained
absolutely loyal, completing Anna's orders with nary a mutter of
complaint.

As for Roy and Li Lu, the couple was considered core members of Anna's
organization. After all, they were Anna's longtime associates. Anna
could see the selfish desires in their hearts, but she couldn't care
less about it.

As long as they obeyed her orders, she couldn't care less whether they
were being sincere or not. It wasn't like the couple could still get off
the ship. The ship had already sailed when they decided to stay with her
at the time.

Last but not least was Wang Sheng, the second-generation nouveau riche
whom Anna had stumbled upon on the surface world.

When Anna restored Wang Sheng and Wang Jianshe's memories, they
resolutely joined Anna's cause. The father and son duo were incredibly
helpful during the early stages of Anna's plan.

Jackal pressed his earpiece and exclaimed, "Deploy the radars! Mobilize
Anomaly 3687 and Anomaly 781! Now!"

The people behind him got out of their vehicles and sprang into action.

After delegating some of the affairs to his deputy, Jackal walked up to
Anna and said, "High Priestess, I've contacted the Yellow-Robe Alliance,
the Lightkeepers, and the Green Flames Brotherhood. They've arrived at
the eastern, western, and northern sides of the city."

Anna nodded with satisfaction. Against the IMF's strongest and final
site, she wasn't going to rely solely on herself.

If one had the complete advantage in strength, then the safest way to
achieve victory was to advance steadily and crush the enemies with one's
overwhelming strength instead of cooking up risky and unnecessary plans.

"Let's go. We've been fighting them for so many years now. It's time to
settle this once and for all," Anna said.

Everyone immediately got moving.

Rumbling noises pervaded the air as fighter jets flew overhead, making a
beeline for the distant city. Artillery shells descended like rain,
bombarding the city.

Naturally, the IMF made a move, sending surface-to-air missiles to
obliterate the fighter jets streaking across the skies. However, the
fighter jets were merely a part of the prelude.

When one of the aircraft engulfed in flames struck the ground, a
four-meter-tall humanoid figure crawled out of the flames. The slender
and emaciated figure with blood-red nails and no facial features was a
terrifying sight to behold.

The thin humanoid figure let out a shrill cry and slaughtered everyone
around it.

The remaining fighter jets crashed one after another, and all sorts of
bizarre "things" crawled out from within the debris. These things were
the most bizarre and dangerous Uncontrollable Anomalies.

These Anomalies couldn't be absorbed, so Anna repurposed them as weapons
of mass destruction. The methods and means of warfare on the surface
world had changed long ago, and victory depended on who could best use
Anomalies with modern weaponry.

Once Anna made her move, the rest followed suit. Soon, the city was
engulfed in the scorching flames of war.

Anna took in the grand scene before her and turned to Tobba in Li Lu's
arms.

"Magnificent, isn't it?" she asked.

Tobba was sucking on a lollipop, and his eyes were utterly cold as he
stared at another overlapping scene before him—a scene in a
superposition state.

Rain poured down hard, and there was no smell of gunpowder in the air.
Instead, the air itself felt heavy and suffocating. The distant city was
dyed pitch-black due to the sheer number of black-robed Fhtagnists
standing within its confines.

Against hundreds of millions of Fhtagnists, the IMF's last stand was
pitifully weak, and they were defeated in the proverbial blink of an
eye.

Anna reverted to her true form and devoured the members of the IMF's
command center, ending the IMF's hegemony. Then, the Fhtagnists gathered
the survivors and tied them up in the city square.

There were so many Fhtagnists that by the time Anna arrived at the city
square, the colossal array that spanned the entire city was already
complete.

Like a starfish, Anna sprawled out on the ground, and her eyes with
yellow, cross-shaped pupils were cast at the skies above her.

There was no one beside her. She was alone, utterly alone.

The rain relentlessly pelted her eyes, but she remained frozen with her
thoughts unknown. Finally, she raised her tentacles, and they swayed
like seaweed in the air. Soon, her chanting reverberated from the
speakers throughout the city.

Anna had won, and it was a decisive victory by an overwhelming margin at
that. Now, it was time to proceed with her next plan. Anna's voice
pervaded not just this one city. Through the Internet, her voice reached
every corner of the world.

Her chanting contained the crazed whisperings of the gods, making it
impossible to understand, but Tobba could understand it.

Chapter 1140 - Entrance

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Anna's crazed yet unnervingly composed voice echoed throughout every
corner of the Earth. She had hijacked every available communication
transmission.

"Hear my call, Oh Lord of the Endless Void! Shifter of Stars! Firm
Foundation! Creator of Suffering! The Great One! Father of the Void and
Chaos! God of Primordial Darkness! Lord of Dimensions, Keeper of Gates!
Pathbreaker! Primordial Almighty Lord of Eternal Life! Your servant is
calling upon Your name!"

The hysterical madness underlying the incantation sent a shiver down
Tobba's spine. This lunatic was really planning to sacrifice all seven
billion lives on the surface world.

"Hey, what are you staring at?" Anna called out, jolting Tobba back to
his senses.

The sight before Tobba vanished. The Fhtagnists were gone. He looked to
the distant city and saw nothing. The only sound that could be heard was
the thunderous explosions.

Anna wasn't standing at the edge performing any ritual. Instead, she was
right next to him and glaring at him, her eyes filled with slight
annoyance.

"N-nothing. I just... found you too beautiful and got stunned," Tobba
said, cooking up an excuse.

Anna rolled her eyes; she couldn't be bothered to entertain the nonsense
coming from the mouth of a two-year-old brat. She turned and made her
way to the nearby makeshift tent to check on the latest battle reports.

"Don't worry, High Priestess. Most of the IMF's mobile task force
members have been wiped out. They've already lost," Jackal reported
calmly, his expression unfazed despite witnessing the downfall of the
very organization he once served.

As she watched the high-definition combat footage transmitted from a
military radar drone, Anna gently patted Jackal on the shoulder.

"Don't underestimate the enemy. This is the IMF we're talking about."

As soon as the words left Anna's lips, a few crimson flares soared into
the sky and erupted.

Jackal's eyes lit up with apparent glee as he exclaimed, "High
Priestess! The outer perimeter of the underground stronghold has been
breached! They can't hold out much longer. If we advance—"

Bzzzzt!

Jackal's words were cut short as Anna yanked Jackal down with a sharp
pull.

Out of nowhere, a blinding beam of energy sliced through the air. A thin
red line was drawn across the necks of everyone standing inside the
tent, including Anna.

A silent second passed before the sickening squelch of severed flesh
rang out. Heads fell to the ground as the tent collapsed inward.

"High Priestess?" Jackal called out in a crouched position. His eyes
were wide with apparent despair and horror as he stared at Anna's
headless body.

Just then, Anna's body twitched slightly before a new head pushed its
way out from the severed stump of her neck.

Unlike humans, her fatal point was not her head. In fact, her entire
Dioite body was just a mere deception. A severed head was nothing more
than a snipped tendril tip.

"What did I tell you earlier? Don't underestimate them. Someone's
coming. Looks like they're pulling that decapitation operation again."

With that, Anna stepped out of the collapsed tent and looked toward the
distant city. However, she didn't see anyone approaching. Rather, the
enemy had attacked from the sky."

She slightly tilted her head upward, and dozens of irises squeezed into
her eye sockets. She saw it then—hundreds of space drop-pods, ignited
from friction and in flames as they tore through the atmosphere.

"All to your stations! Anti-air ready!" Projectile after projectile shot
out from the columns of missile barrels and soared toward the skies,
aimed at the descending drop-pods.

Yet just before they could hit their targets, the flames surrounding the
drop-pods seemed to have turned controllable. With a roaring whoosh,
their embers lunged toward the flying missiles.

While the anti-air projectiles exploded in the air like a grand display
of fireworks, the very last drop-pods crashed heavily on the earth.

The door of a drop-pod slid open and several individuals emerged from
within. Their identity was a given from the distinct phoenix insignia on
their shoulders.

A hint of understanding flickered across Anna's eyes.

I see how it is... The IMF has placed its most elite task force in outer
space. No wonder I have never come across them.

After the Phoenix Task Force, the other drop-pods opened one by one.
Each held a mobile task force, their shoulders branded with a different
insignia. Indeed, they were the final trump cards the IMF had up their
sleeves.

With a will on Anna's end, her mind-controlling thoughts spread out like
a wave toward the approaching enemies.

However, this was the first time that her powers were rendered
ineffective. Among these hundreds of mobile task force members, someone
or something countered her mind-control ability.

The four task force members of the Phoenix suddenly raised both their
hands, and everything in the vicinity capable of combustion ignited into
flames. The tongues of their flames spread rapidly.

"Kill them all," Anna ordered in an icy tone.

Immediately, her followers charged forward.

The distant battlefield had finally reached home turf, with both sides
clashing in fierce combat. Now, battles raged simultaneously across the
strongholds of both factions.

Yet, no one had expected what came next. Just as they were engaged in a
chaotic clash, Anna, with a handful of her followers, boarded a vehicle.
They stealthily slipped away beneath the earth and headed for the city.

"High Priestess, are we leaving just like that?" Jackal asked as he
glanced over his shoulder with traces of unease in his eyes.

"Since we already know that they're here to kill us, why should we clash
with them head-on?" Anna replied. "How will that benefit us?"

"They're merely pawns. We need to find the leader," Anna added, her eyes
fixated on the city that was drawing closer.

The IMF had deployed its final elite forces, which also revealed a fatal
vulnerability. Their command center was now undoubtedly left poorly
defended. All she needed to do was strike at the heart, and the battle
would be over.

The car soon entered the city, where firebombs and unleashed anomalies
rampaged unchecked.

Anna closed her eyes, allowing her thoughts to spread out as she combed
the city for anything that was still alive.

With the memories of these living creatures, Anna quickly locked into
the right direction.

Upon reaching the entrance of the underground stronghold, she was
greeted with a scene of brutal carnage.

People clad in clothes with different insignias were engaged in a
vicious battle with the IMF operatives. Bodies littered the ground,
their limbs twisted at odd angles.

"Move! Charge in!" Anna commanded.

Her squad surged forward.

As soon as they saw Anna, several IMF operatives abruptly switched sides
and began to attack their own allies. These moles, whom she had planted
within the IMF for years, had finally been activated.

Thanks to them, Anna faced sparse resistance, allowing her to move
deeper into the IMF site.

The situation within was just as savage, with corpses strewn and piled
up along the corridors. Clearly, both sides fought fiercely in the
cramped environment.

Due to the cramped space, progress was rather slow.

Just as Anna was ready to pivot and find a detour, a hint of delight
flashed across her face.

"Hah. To think that I would find one here."

Anna's form swiftly split apart, as a massive tentacle writhed out of
her back and shot toward a man in camouflage.

Startled by the attack, the man hurriedly shouted, "Miss 315! What are
you doing?! I'm a Flamebearer of the Green Flames Brotherhood!"

"Nice acting—but that's all there is to it," Anna remarked. "I don't
judge someone by their appearances or insignias. I only look at their
memories. Sorry to say, but you're no Flamebearer. You're one of those
up there, Nightwatch."

Realizing that Anna had seen through his disguise, the man's anxious
expression vanished instantly. His lips parted; wave after wave of
wriggling maggots poured out from between before scattering in all
directions.

As the maggots squirmed away, the man's body shriveled up, his skin
collapsing inward, as though those worms made up his true form.

For anyone else, an ability of this level would pose a significant
threat. Yet, it was barely effective against Anna.

Emerald flames roared to life and surged forward to form a blazing wall
that surrounded the escaping maggots. A series of sickening pops and
sizzling sounds resonated through the air as the maggots burst into
charred pulps down to the very last one.

Chapter 1141 - Thirteen

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

"Hmm... One of the Thirteen is down," Anna commented, and the corners of
her lips curled up into a satisfied smile. Upon scanning the man's
memory, she discovered how many of them formed the upper echelons and
obtained knowledge of their names as well as their exact locations.

With a thought, she instantly disseminated the information to all her
fellow allies. Like an infectious plague, her thoughts rippled out into
the minds of the others.

"Find them. They're our targets. Kill them all and it's our victory,"
Anna's voice rang out in the minds alongside the newly acquired intel.
𝗳𝚛𝗲𝕖𝚠𝚎𝚋𝗻𝗼𝕧𝗲𝐥.𝚌𝚘𝐦

Her words were like an instant trigger; everyone entered a crazed state
as they rushed toward the enemy. Every single one of them understood the
significance of the information.

Those thirteen—now twelve—were the core of the IMF's command center.
With them gone, the remainder of the IMF had no authority to deploy or
rearrange their forces. Surely, that was a win for Anna and her allies!

With Anna and her group joining the fray, the rebellion forces moved at
a faster pace than before, gaining more and more territory at an
increasing speed.

Soon enough, the Phoenix and the other mobile task forces finally caught
wind of the situation and turned back toward the IMF command center.

Yet, it was too late. One by one, the pillars of the IMF were rooted out
and slain.

With each kill, Anna harvested their memories and added them to the
intel database that she already had of the IMF. Secrets were revealed
with great clarity. Nothing was hidden from her at this point.

The shrill wail of emergency alarms howled throughout the underground
stronghold. Red warning lights flashed and flickered, seemingly
signaling the final moments of the IMF.

However, the situation didn't last long. The death toll of the IMF
operatives was spiking uncontrollably. Collapse was imminent.

Before absolute power, nothing else mattered. All the IMF personnel
could do now was to continue their futile resistance in hopeless
desperation.

Splurt!

A sickening pop sounded as one of Anna's tentacles pierced into a
containment chamber and writhed around. The skull of a
translucent-headed creature inside had burst apart.

Another one of the Thirteen had fallen.

A trace of doubt flickered across Anna's gaze as she pondered over the
situation.

What's going on? This is...too easy. I haven't even utilized any of my
contingency plans?

To Anna, it would be worrisome if things were going wrong. But it was a
totally different kind of worry if things were going too smoothly. She
still remembered clearly the layers of fail-safes that the Foundation
had in the Subterranean Sea.

They were the Foundation, after all. As the guardians of humanity, they
couldn't possibly be this weak and fragile.

Just then, the ground beneath her trembled, the afterwaves causing the
buildings to rock as well. Moments later, the group was hit by surprise.

A corner of the war-torn city began to tilt. With each passing second,
the angle increased further.

Eventually, like a pancake, the entire city flipped over with a woosh.

This particular stronghold was actually a double-sided city.

The city above the ground was now inverted; the buildings hanging
upside-down like mechanical stalactites.

Meanwhile, the underground buildings were now bathed in sunlight. Those
who had been fighting in the city, along with every Anomaly caught by
the sun's rays vanished in an instant.

Built almost entirely of metal and gray concrete, a fortress that was
armed with all sorts of weaponry down to the teeth appeared before
everyone's eyes.

Silence hung in the air for a brief few seconds before a deafening roar
echoed.

The next moment, every weapon mounted on the fortress unleashed a
furious barrage, raining destruction on the enemies surrounding the
city.

In an instant, its sheer might forced everyone to duck for cover.

Anna, who was supposed to be in an underground facility, now found
herself standing on the upper floors of a building, after the city was
inverted.

Watching the scene unfolding outside through the window, she let out a
sigh of relief.

No matter what, the IMF had finally made its move. The first piece had
been played—and to Anna, it was the unknown that was always the
deadliest.

"Drop those things," Anna instructed. "Bomb this tin bastard into
scrap!"

Anna’s command was instantly relayed to the squadron of warplanes
circling thousands of meters above.

In perfect formation, the aircraft dove sharply as their bomb bay doors
slid open with a hiss.

Whoosh!

A missile—clearly heavier than anything deployed before—plummeted
straight toward the heart of the military fortress.

The enemy's air defense systems immediately responded. Interceptors shot
up in an attempt to stop the missile midair.

The moment they came into contact...

An explosion thundered, and a new sun bloomed in the sky.

The dropped shells weren't like any of their predecessors. They were the
latest, most advanced weapons on the surface world.

Anna tilted her head up to take in the view of the rippling explosion,
her lips curling up into a grin.

"Bring it on. Don't worry, there's more coming. Give me one, and I'm
going to give you ten in return!"

Fwoom!

A ball of flame abruptly streaked past Anna before latching onto her
thigh.

Clearly, this was no ordinary fire. No matter how hard Anna slapped at
it, it refused to be extinguished. Even worse, her hand caught fire.

With a snarl, Anna lashed out with a tendril, tore the burning chunk of
flesh away, and tossed it to the side.

The moment the burned flesh left Anna's body, the flames roared with
fiercer intensity.

The fire bloomed into an inferno, and four figures soon emerged from
within. Clad in crimson uniforms, the phoenixes on their shoulders
marked their identity.

The Phoenix Mobile Task Force had finally arrived, and they stood in
front of Anna.

Boom!

Another thunderous explosion ripped through the air as yet another
miniature sun bloomed in the sky,

The shockwave, saturated with lethal radiation, whipped Anna's long hair
into a wild dance.

A cold sneer appeared on Anna's visage. "So you have finally shown up?
But isn't it a bit too late now? There are only seven of the Thirteen
left."

Staring at the origin of all evil before him, the Phoenix Mobile Task
Force Leader replied in an icy tone, "You don't actually believe that
there's an Anomaly that can reset the world, do you?"

Next to Anna, Gao Zhiming's pupils contracted sharply, and his heart
skipped a beat.

Flanked by her allies, Anna stood in her spot.

Suddenly, the surrounding buildings around them shifted and transformed
like steel titans waking up from their sleep. Armored fortresses rose
from the earth and encircled Anna and her group.

A searing laser shot out from one of the steel titans and struck the
crowd. It then ricocheted off a couple of mirrored surfaces in rapid
succession, striking the crowd again and again.

The sizzling sound of burned flesh and screams of agony filled the air.
The blast had a significant impact on Anna's ranks.

Then, something stranger happened. For some unknown reason, they were
shrinking, their forms getting smaller.

"This is a trap! We've been ambushed!" Jackal roared, the veins on his
forehead bulging as he gritted his teeth in fury.

The very next second, a battle broke out.

Bullets tore through flesh, and a cacophony of sounds—screams,
explosions—resonated throughout the battlefield.

At the same time, the IMF's execution squad arrived. However, their
target wasn't Anna this time. Rather, it was Gao Zhiming, who was right
beside her.

"Gao Zhiming is 315's weakness! Initiate Plan C-3! Capture him at all
costs!" A female voice rang out through every comm unit worn by the IMF
mobile task forces.

In a mountain bunker five hundred kilometers away, intel analysts and
tactical staff packed the place.

Rows of supercomputers beeped and blinked.

This was the IMF's command center. Here, the upper echelons of the IMF
watched the live footage transmitted back from the mobile task forces.
Their faces were grim as they tracked the war playing out in real time.

Chapter 1142 - Conversion

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

"Don't kill 315! We just need to hold her down! Remember, our target is
that Asian boy, Gao Zhiming!" A woman with rimless glasses and a
commanding presence shouted the order across the comms unit to the IMF
members on the frontlines.

Her name was Golia, and she was the overall commander for this trap
operation of the IMF.

Right from the beginning down to the end, this operation had been a
trap. A trap targeted at Anna. To achieve their goal this time, the IMF
had made tantamount sacrifices.

Golia had no choice but to offer every colleague of hers based in this
city as bait.

It was a sacrifice that had to be made. 𝑓𝘳𝑒𝑒𝓌𝘦𝘣𝘯ℴ𝑣𝘦𝑙.𝘤𝑜𝑚

Having been through the past few months and suffering so many losses,
the IMF was stretched thin on every end. Besieged on every front, they
could only react instead of taking any sort of initiative.

The only solution to getting rid of this chaos was to cut off the tumor,
the origin of it all—315. She was the visionary leader of the opposing
forces.

If the IMF managed to take her down, the rebels would lose their
motivation.

After all, they were just a bunch of rogue factions, each fighting for
their own purposes. Without a leader, they were nothing more than a mob.

"Release Variant 431-2! This is our last chance!" Golia commanded.

In the heart of the warzone where the fight was the most intense,
corrosive emerald flames surged around Anna to form a blazing wall,
stopping the mobile task forces from advancing.

Using the brief reprieve, Anna shouted at Gao Zhiming, "Now! Use what I
gave you!"

Gao Zhiming nodded without a word and pulled out a small golden sphere
from his pocket.

He was just about to activate it when he felt a searing pressure on his
chest.

His eyes opened wide in horror as he watched his shirt being easily
shredded by something coming out from within his chest.

It was a deformed infant with a bald head and sharp, pointy ears. An
eerie smile surfaced on the infant's face. The next instant, it raised a
sharpened finger and slashed down.

Blood sprayed, and Gao Zhiming's right hand, which was holding the
golden ball, was severed.

Adrenaline rushed through Gao Zhiming. Before the pain could catch up,
he fiercely kicked off the ground and lunged at the fallen sphere on the
ground.

Seeing that Gao Zhiming had been attacked, Anna's figure vibrated at a
high frequency in an attempt to assist him.

However, the IMF had already rehearsed this scenario thousands of times
before. The operatives immediately closed ranks to block her path.

Swish!

Several of Gao Zhiming's outstretched fingers were cleanly severed.

The sphere was slipping further away. Desperate, Gao Zhiming threw
himself forward and tackled the creature on his chest to the ground.

Veins bulged across his face as he opened his mouth wide and clamped
down on the golden sphere to swallow it whole.

"Initiate Reality Override Protocol!" Golia's calm voice echoed through
the command channels. "Redirect Anomaly 864 to seize control of all
spatial jump anchors in the vicinity. 434, lock onto the target's
trajectory. Once he leaves, deploy our backup orbital unit to pursue."

The entirety of the IMF was united for a single mission, so their sheer
might was simply staggering.

They had sacrificed too much for this sneak attack. They had accounted
for everything and prepared themselves for every possible scenario...
except for what happened next.

It was out of their anticipation. Gao Zhiming wasn't teleported away.
Instead, he began spasming violently.

"Big...Sis...Anna...?" Gao Zhiming choked out, as violent spasms coursed
through him. His eyes widened in disbelief at his beloved sister as his
figure began to slowly dissolve.

Chunk by chunk, his flesh disintegrated until all that remained was a
pool of blood. The crimson liquid continuously expanded its range and
devoured the nearby corpses until it eventually filled the space the
size of a basketball court.

Anna then poured in a swirl of eerie green pigment. The pool of blood
churned and bubbled violently. It stretched downward to merge with the
darkness below. From its depths, something began to stir, growing,
forming, and incubating.

Anna watched it all unfold. Then, she turned to the chest cam of a
nearby task force operative and flashed a smile.

"You didn't really think I'd bring my weakness onto the battlefield, did
you?" Anna taunted, her voice laced with apparent mockery. "With my
abilities, it's all too easy to manipulate someone into thinking that
they're Gao Zhiming."

As soon as her words fell, bloodied, grotesque creatures clawed their
way out of the pool. They immediately pounced on anything that moved,
regardless of whether the target was an ally or enemy.

And with the IMF forces making up the majority on-site, the victims of
these creatures were clear.

The tides of battle had abruptly turned.

This was an Uncontrollable Anomaly, and its presence usually indicated
