extreme danger.

"Mission failure! Adjust objective! Contain 315!" Golia commanded
remotely, her face marred with solemnity.

Before they could clearly understand how Anna had returned from the
dead, they had no intention of killing her.

Just for this day, they rehearsed many times and trained hard. Every one
of Anna's abilities had been recorded, analyzed, and simulated through
countless drills.

Although an unstable variable, a wild card, appeared and threw the
battlefield into chaos, the IMF had not lost!

And that was thanks to the sacrifices of all the mobile task force
members.

When she saw the hole being blasted through the chest of the traitor Roy
and heard Li Lu screaming Roy's name in anguish, Golia allowed herself
to ease up a little.

That Gao Zhiming had been a decoy, but the others, including Anna, were
real.

The IMF only had to deal with them. Their mission hadn't failed yet.

However, the thought had barely entered her mind for a mere fraction of
a second when a violent shudder suddenly rocked the underground command
center. Dust and ash rained down from the ceiling.

A monitor flickered and switched to the feed of one of the perimeter
cameras. A fully armed soldier appeared on screen and shouted, "General
Golia! We're under attack! They can phase through walls and are almost—"

Before the man could complete his sentence, the feed cut to static.

Golia reacted instantly. She turned to the council of the IMF's command
and instructed, "Everyone, the situation has changed! Evacuate! Board
the Leviathan and return to the space station at once!"

"Well, well... Here I was thinking, what's going on? So all of you were
hanging in the skies. No wonder I searched for so long and couldn't find
you." A taunting voice echoed through the chamber.

"Good thing you finally came down," Anna said, her lips curled up into a
sweet smile as she stepped through the stone wall by the side. Behind
her, an army of grotesque abominations, their flesh twisted and armored
with embedded crystals, followed.

"315?! How is this possible!" Golia stared in disbelief, her gaze
darting between the Anna before her to the Anna still on screen.

Including the corpse of 315 that had been preserved as a specimen, there
were now three identical 315s.

What was she?! Could she possibly replicate herself endlessly?

Anna's gaze slowly swept across every face present. Most of them were
elderly, and they numbered sixteen. Each of them bore distinct Roman
numeral tattoos on either their hands or faces.

Compared to those fake council members in the city, these individuals
were undoubtedly the true deal.

And if she could consume them all, she would have achieved her objective
for this attack.

Anna turned her gaze back onto Golia. Her eyes glinted with a malicious
glee, and she said, "Take a guess."

Of course, Anna wouldn't explain anything to her enemies. The truth was
that she had never intended to forsake the human body that Charles had
gifted her.

She also knew that to bait these cunning IMF foxes, she had to use
herself as bait. Otherwise, they would never fall for her trap. Thus,
Dioite Anna had created another Anna, and she had been controlling the
decoy at the same time.

Back on the battlefield, the mobile task forces were engaged in an
intense fight when they received the distress signal from the HQ. They
immediately mobilized back to assist.

However, it was way too late.

Anna raised one finger. The crystal-flesh abominations behind her burst
forth from the walls and pounced on the enemies.

Chapter 1143 - The Foundation

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

I, II, III, IV...

These individuals marked with Roman numerals were the upper echelons of
the IMF, the decision makers, and the actual authority behind the
international organization.

But for some unknown reason, they were different from the Foundation in
the Subterranean Sea. They hadn't fused with any monstrous creatures.
Neither do these surface dwellers bear any alien mutations. In addition,
they didn't seem to have assimilated with any Anomaly.

Naturally, people like them were no match for Anna. Under the assault of
Anna and her bloodied flesh army, the IMF management had more than half
of their ranks either killed or injured.

The tide of the battlefield was moving exactly the way Anna had
envisioned.

As she watched them fall one after another, a rush of vindictive
satisfaction surged through her chest.

The death of the clown and the grievances and oppression she had endured
all these years could finally be unleashed in full at this very moment.

"Die! All of you, die!" Anna roared.

Corrosive green flames danced in the air, reducing the fleeing enemies
to ashes. Even the operation commander, Golia, was consumed by the
searing tongue of fire.

Anna reveled in the taste of victory. She had always loved this feeling,
and she had loved it since the moment of her creation.

Several round objects that resembled crystalline fruits were tossed into
the chamber. Explosions rang out, causing a storm of arctic wind that
swept through the battlefield and flash froze everyone in sight.

This was probably the IMF's contingency plan, but it did nothing to stop
Anna.

Her form vibrated at a high frequency, and she easily emerged from the
solid ice. She then approached an old man who had the numeral VII
tattooed just below his right eye.

"So now what? Where's the almighty Foundation that is supposed to
protect the IMF? Where're they? Are they not coming to save you? And to
think I actually wasted time preparing for them."

"I've no idea what you're talking about. Monster, do you think killing
us will—"

Blood sprayed. The old man's eyes were wide with overflowing defiance.
His head spun in the air and hit the ground with a dull thud.

Anna moved toward her next victim. He was a middle-aged man. Despite
Anna's approach, his eyes bore no fear. Instead, he tried to reason with
the enemy before him.

"Anna, look outside! Look at those victims! Is this really what you
wanted? We've analyzed you. You once gave up summoning Fhtagnists for
that child's sake. Deep in your heart, there must be—"

Before he could complete his sentence, Anna's corrosive fire had burned
him down into ashes.

A cold, mocking chuckle escaped Anna's lips. "What did all of you do
when I first came to the surface? Let's put it straight out there. You
only want to reason when you've lost.

"I'm so sorry, but since I'm the one with the power now, I'm not going
to listen to your pathetic excuses. You guys taught me that.
Moreover..."

Anna's lips twisted with fury. Her red gums bared as she let out a roar
of fury. The malice in her expression shattered her beautiful face into
a mask of wrath.

"Not only did you kill my people, but you even killed me! How dare you
try to reconcile with me now!"

Another head hit the floor. His face was marred with the same
unwillingness. A fine line of blood arced through the air and landed on
Anna's cheek.

Now, only three remained...

Anna disregarded all words that came from their mouths. Her twisted
smile widened as she walked toward them.

"Anna, maybe... that's enough."

All of a sudden, a familiar voice rang out from behind her.

Anna turned around. It was Tobba, clad in child's clothes, and his tiny
shoes squeaked with each step as he entered the room.

She wasn't the least fazed by Tobba's sudden appearance. If anything,
she was curious why Tobba had been playing dumb for so long, only for
him to drop the pretence now.

"Enough?" Anna questioned. "Look at the monitor. Look at whose body is
that! That's your father! Your father died in their hands. And you're
telling me to let them go?"

Tobba looked up at the monitor screen. It depicted a lifeless Roy and Li
Lu weeping uncontrollably as she held him in her arms. A bitter smile
surfaced upon Tobba's innocent face.

"What can I do about it? People die... That's life."

Anna moved toward her next target. A razor-sharp crystal blade emerged
from her palm. Just as she raised it and was ready to swing it down, a
single empty shell casing soared through the air and struck Anna's wrist
with perfect precision.

It was just a casing, but it hit the right spot. Anna's hand instantly
went numb, and her attack was forcefully halted.

Anna slowly turned to look at Tobba. Her voice was laced with hints of
threat. "Are you trying to stop me?"

As soon as her words fell, the crystal-flesh, four-foot monsters around
them snapped their jaws toward Tobba and bared their sharp, ivory fangs.

"They are the order. The surface can't survive without them. If they
cease to exist, many people will die. And what do you think Gao Zhiming
will feel if he sees that scene?"

Anna suddenly froze upon hearing Tobba's words. She stared at the
cartoon print on Tobba's shirt and asked, "What did you see in the
future?"

"I saw death," Tobba whispered with his head bowed low. His eyes were
locked on the shoes with flashing soles that his father had bought for
him. "Also, I saw something far more terrifying than death. Too much of
it."

"I can forget about my death. Then what about the Mask's death? Was his
sacrifice for nothing?!" Anna questioned with restrained rage.

"Sometimes, death is inevitable. This world was never meant to be
perfect. If everything were perfect, this wouldn't be reality—it'd be
heaven. But heaven... doesn't exist."

"And if I still plan to kill them anyway?" Anna's voice sharpened. "Are
you planning to step in to protect them?"

"No, I won't," Tobba answered. "That's your choice, and you have to bear
the equivalent consequences of making that choice."

Silence gradually took over the command center. No one uttered a single
word. Everyone simply waited quietly for Anna to make her decision.

Anna pondered for a very long time. Time ticked by, and the forces from
the city began to arrive before she finally raised her head.

Her eyes glimmered with determination, and she was just about to speak
when a deep rumble shook the place.

The wall to the right of the command center suddenly split open to
reveal a glowing, triangular portal.

Anna was immediately on her guard.

She recognized this portal. It was the same portal that had appeared
when the IMF attempted to assassinate her. This portal belonged to the
Foundation!

So these guys finally decided to show their hand at this very last
moment? The thought ran through Anna's mind.

She then swiftly sifted through all the contingency plans she had
prepared and wondered if she had overlooked anything.

The first to step out of the portal was a Foundation soldier clad in
sleek, pitch-black armor with seamless curves.

With her keen senses, Anna realized that the soldier had at least eight
Anomalies that complemented each other.

This guy is difficult to deal with. A conclusion immediately settled in
Anna's heart.

Dozens of pupils squeezed into Anna's eyes. Emerald flames surrounded
her. Just as she was about to strike first, the armored man staggered
and, without warning, crashed to the ground.

What's going on? What's happening? The same thought echoed in every mind
present.

But that wasn't the end of it all.

Another fell out of the portal.

And another.

And another.

More and more bodies squeezed out of the portal.

It was not one, nor ten bodies. Rather, a flood of corpses spilled from
the triangular portal!

Anna recognized their uniform all too well. These soldiers belonged to
the Foundation.

The very organization that was supposed to protect the IMF was massacred
by some existence.

Chapter 1144 - Anna

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

Anna and the others stared in alarm at the triangular portal on the
wall.

Only that, now, the portal was no longer a triangle. Its edges began to
distort and warp outward. It seemed to have turned into the gaping maw
of hell itself.

A flood of mangled, disfigured corpses poured out of it like a geyser.
The portal vomited wave after wave of flesh and decay.

The corpses piled higher and higher. Anna swiftly retreated. The rest
immediately followed suit. Whatever grievances they held toward each
other just minutes earlier were meaningless now.

When they had escaped from the chamber, the mobile task forces that had
been locked in bitter battle in the city, as well as Anna in her
monstrous form, had also arrived.

They were just in time to witness the unexpected scene.

Just earlier, the two opposite sides had been tearing each other apart.
Yet now, they stood side by side in silent peace.

Something was wrong—very wrong.

In fact, both groups even suspected that their allies were under mind
control.

"You did this? Who are those dead bodies?" one of the remaining IMF
leaders asked with a solemn expression.

Clearly, the IMF knew nothing about the Foundation.

Anna couldn't even be bothered to look at him. Her gaze remained fixated
on the mountain that began trembling violently,

She initially thought that the ones emerging through the portal were
reinforcements to assist the IMF. She never expected that they actually
died, and a gruesome death at that.

It was a one-sided execution. Whatever their enemy was within that
portal, they overpowered them by a great margin.

Who the hell did they provoke? Didn't they claim to be the guardians of
the IMF? The war has escalated to this extent. What could possibly be
more important than the IMF losing control of the world's balance of
power?

Anna's thoughts were then cut off when the mountain before her rumbled
even harder.

Crack!

The rocky layers of the mountain began to split apart. A jagged fissure
raced from the base all the way to the summit in an instant.

As soon as the fissure appeared, a distinctive sound erupted from within
and entered the ears of everyone within a two-thousand-mile radius.

It was a unique sound. It was a cascading melody of high-frequency
vibrations that couldn't be born of any human vocal cord or man-made
instrument.

Everyone, Anna included, dropped to the ground with their hands
clutching their heads and their expressions contorted in agony.

As that all-too-familiar yet utterly alien sensation tore through her
mind, Anna's expression twisted into one of deep dread.

It's a god! They were killed trying to stop a god from breaching the
surface. Which god has reached the surface?

But how is that possible?! This has never happened before. Never had any
god reached the surface. How did It do it? Who is It? 002? 004? Hypnos?
Edikth? Giant Bird of Suffering? Fhtagn? The All-Seeing Eye?

Under the agonized gazes of everyone, the mountain erupted like a
volcano.

Instead of lava, what erupted were bodies. Thousands of corpses, each of
varying shapes and sizes, spewed into the sky. Their faces bore the same
expressions of extreme despair and agony.

Judging by their somewhat similar uniform, these deceased humans were
once part of the Foundation.

Once the rainfall of corpses was close to ending, a thick, inky black
water began to gush forth. It was viscous and pitch-dark, resembling
molten tar more than water.

The black liquid churned and bubbled as it gathered into a writhing mass
before coalescing into long tentacles, each lined with yellow,
cross-shaped pupils. However, they flickered in and out of form and
looked extremely unstable.

The unique sound from earlier emanated from deep within the black water.
This was the body of that god!

The moment the monstrous tentacle creature revealed itself, the brightly
lit sky instantly dimmed to a suffocating black.

A torrential downpour of black meteors tore through the atmosphere like
a curtain of doom and blotted out the sun. The entire surface world
plunged into an apocalyptic night.

"Ah... there are still so many humans here? Say... If I offer this world
as another sacrifice, surely the almighty Great One will return Gao
Zhiming to me, won't he?"

A sultry voice laced with traces of madness slithered into every ear.
Every single person recognized it instantly.

That was clearly Anna's voice!

"What... What is going on?" Anna stared in disbelief at her own image,
another her who had ascended into godhood. 𝙛𝓻𝒆𝓮𝒘𝙚𝙗𝒏𝙤𝙫𝓮𝒍.𝓬𝒐𝙢

Her thoughts spiraled into chaos.

"Don't you get it?" Tobba shouted. "She's you from a different timeline!
She sacrificed billions from the surface world on her side to Fhtagn in
exchange for power!

"Since the beginning of their existence, the Dioite Tribe has always
worshipped Fhtagn! Now, she's planning to collapse your timeline so that
this timeline will be under the complete control of Fhtagn!"

Before Tobba could say anything more, his expression shifted.

"Fuck! Screw this—"

As soon as the words left Tobba's lips, a tentacle lined with eyeballs
shot up from the ground. Like a harpoon, it pierced Tobba and hoisted
him into the air.

Back in the dimension beyond time and space, Charles watched everything
unfold on that particular tentacle. Now, he was omniscient. He was
omnipotent. Yet, the future woven into this tentacle was constantly
changing. He couldn't see the end of it.

Fhtagn was wrestling Charles for control, and He wasn't restricting
Himself to just one tentacle.

Soon enough, all of the other tentacles began to shift and twist as new
changes were introduced. The trajectories of every single one of them
skewed toward demise, toward ruin.

All instances of Charles at varying points of time on each tentacle
encountered other unexpected things.

In one, Charles never met the Hook from the Fhtagn Covenant. He hadn't
taken the man's commission and hadn't earned enough money to afford the
Narwhale. In another, Charles lost focus on his initial goal, indulging
in the comforts of Hope Island.

And in one of the darkest, Charles broke under the pressure of the
Subterranean Sea. He surrendered to his despair and to seek solace, he
converted to become a devout follower of Fhtagn.

Charles certainly wouldn't sit by and allow any of those endings to come
into fruition. He lashed out with every tentacle. Each one snapped
forward like a whip and coiled tightly around the massive, dark mountain
before him.

Fhtagn's form was no longer pure. His abyssal darkness writhed with
impurities, or rather bits of Charles.

Fhtagn was assimilating Charles' form, while Charles, in turn, was
stealing the very fundamental law Fhtagn had used to construct his form.

The two abyssal entities, born from the deepest chaos, boiled and
clashed at the heart of infinite nothingness. Their battle had descended
into complete and irredeemable madness.

The collision of their divine laws resulted in terrifying pulses, the
impact of which birthed fleeting, unstable worlds at the edge of
existence.

In these short-lived worlds, raw primordial matter surged together to
form unidentified, terrifying cosmic abominations.

However, these creatures were pitiful. Their fates were sealed, after
all.

They were born as collateral of the conflict between gods; they were
doomed to die in the same crossfire.

At a battle of such a scale, laws and timelines could no longer be
destroyed and could only be assimilated.

And as things stood, Charles held the upper hand. However, as he
consumed more and more of Fhtagn's laws into himself, the last remnants
of his humanity crumpled into dust.

At that moment, Charles finally ascended, becoming a true god. He no
longer possessed emotion, only pure logic, omniscience, omnipotence, and
omnivision.

Upon reaching that, Charles suddenly shifted his focus onto 002, which
was still lingering on Fhtagn.

Although 002 had been completely consumed by Fhtagn, Charles noticed
something strange. There was a thread that linked 002 to Charles, and
the connection was tied a long, long time ago.

Charles followed the thread and at its end, he saw Tobba suspended on a
writhing tentacle.

The tentacle twitched, and the next moment, Tobba's tiny two-year-old
body exploded into a fine mist of blood.

Chapter 1145 - Transformation

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

The two Annas stared at their doppelganger that had ascended to godhood.
They clenched their teeth and struggled to get on their feet.
𝒻𝘳𝘦𝘦𝘸ℯ𝒷𝘯𝘰𝑣ℯ𝑙.𝘤𝑜𝘮

However, in the face of the writhing tentacles, even the simple act of
standing felt like an impossible feat.

God Anna didn't spare them a glance. For her, they were nothing more
than insects. Her eyes were set on the entirety of mankind on this
world.

The black meteors had already crashed to the ground. Anything they came
into contact with was erased from existence. Everything that made
contact with them crumbled into nothingness, and the world was teetering
on the edge of annihilation.

The ground ruptured and split apart. From the chasms, foul-smelling,
dark green seawater surged upward and flooded the land.

By the side, Jackal's eyes suddenly widened in wild ecstasy. He pointed
at the encroaching waters and screamed hysterically, "Look! God Fhtagn
has arrived in the waters. The Great Fhtagn has come to save us!"

Of course, Anna knew who Jackal was referring to. Her heart clenched.

If the seawater from the Subterranean Sea is pouring into the surface
world, does that mean Charles is here, too?

However, when she turned her gaze toward the water, the flicker of hope
within her withered away. The waters were pitch black; there was
nothing. It had just been another hallucination on Jackal's part.

Jackal had always claimed that any body of water contained one of
Charles' branches. Unfortunately, hallucinations were merely
hallucinations.

"Look! Our omniscient, omnipotent God has finally arrived!" Jackal
shouted. He was delirious with madness in their current desperate state.
His eyes were bloodshot as he pointed a trembling finger at the waters.

A tentacle lashed out from Dioite Anna and coiled around Jackal.
Accompanied by the crack of breaking bones, she dragged Jackal close to
her.

"How many times do I have to tell you? It's just an illusion! A
hallucination! That guy had forgotten about us long ago!"

At that moment, Anna's face was so close to Jackal's that she could see
the reflection in his eyes.

However, she couldn't see her reflection. The only thing she saw was a
writhing tendril of blood and flesh.

Reflection?

A strange thought suddenly rang in Anna's mind. The next second, she
moved like a mad woman and ripped the skin from Jackal's scalp before
prying open his skull.

Amid the gray and white brain matter, both Annas saw the fleshy branch.
It was a part of Charles that he had left behind.

In that instant, everything suddenly made sense. She finally knew why
Jackal had been claiming that he could see Charles' branch in just about
any body of water.

It was all because those were really reflections, and the actual branch
was buried in Jackal's brain all along.

"So... this is your contingency plan? Your parting gift for me?" The two
Annas spoke in unison at the bloodied branch.

The bloodied branch of flesh didn't sprout any vocal organs. It merely
swelled and grew. It rapidly expanded and devoured Jackal's corpse. And
then it was Anna.

Everything was lacking in this place, but corpses were in abundance.
While the branch hungrily feasted, the towering tsunami from afar was
drawing close.

The dark green seawater crashed down, drowning everything in its path,
burying the world in its depths.

As soon as it came, the waters churned and surged before a massive,
mountainous bulge surged upward and breached the ocean's surface.

A deafening explosion rang out as a colossal tree of flesh rose from the
depths of the waters and pierced into the skies.

Its trunk was covered with inverted limbs and exposed organs, while its
canopy was lined with eyes with yellow cross-shaped pupils.

Those eyes opened in unison and locked onto a single target in the
distance—God Anna.

No words were exchanged. Both sides collided at blinding speed.

Neither wielded their most familiar ability—mind manipulation. They knew
themselves too well. Their usual reality-warping tricks had no power
here.

Flesh intertwined with limbs; shriveled eyeballs were gouged out and
rained down on the ocean below.

As they wrestled with each other, their two worlds blurred and blended
into one. Their consciousness encroached on and stacked on each other.

They began to understand. Anna saw Gao Zhiming's death and felt that
same grief. An unbearable clarity showered upon her. She now understood
why the other Anna had done what she did, and her feelings made her feel
conflicted.

As the wrestle of consciousness intensified, the world, teetering on the
edge of breaking down, began to go completely out of control.

The earth cracked open, and verdant lands bursting with life became
endless deserts as well as frozen wastelands. Each scene flickered like
slides in a broken projector.

Ruptures appeared in time and space. Time would accelerate and then
suddenly reel backward the next moment. Objects lost their figures;
nothing could maintain their forms. Nothing remained real.

At this moment, their beliefs or emotions no longer mattered. The only
thing that remained was the raw will behind their power.

Finally, the battle reached its zenith.

Just then, everything collapsed into a single, blinding singularity. An
unknown amount of time later, the singularity exploded.

Anna gasped awake to find herself sprawled atop a mountain of corpses.
She spotted all kinds of bodies around her, including her own Dioite
body.

As soon as her eyes snapped open, she scanned her surroundings in search
of God Anna. However, she saw not even a trace of the black, molten tar.

There wasn't any black meteor shower, and there wasn't any sickly green
seawater bleeding from the cracks in the ground. There weren't any
traces of God Anna as well.

Everything had returned to the way it was. The distant mountain that had
splintered was back as well. It was now whole again.

Anna felt that something was off. There seemed to be something different
about her memory and personality. Then, realization dawned upon her.

The others hadn't vanished. They were her. She was them. The three Annas
had completely merged to become one.

And it wasn't just that. Everything was gone. Be it the cursed blessings
from Fhtagn, the corrosive flame of the Primordial Fire, or the
Anomalies she had absorbed. All of it had vanished.

Even her age and appearance had regressed. Now, she looked like a
fourteen-year-old girl, an ordinary human.

Anna's long lashes fluttered slightly as she lifted her small, fair
hands to her face. Her lips curled into a bitter smile.

"Charles, so is this your solution? To turn me back into a human?"

Something suddenly stirred in the distant pile of corpses. The IMF
survivors crawled out. Olivia dragged a wounded Li Lu out of the
carnage.

The forces on both sides exchanged glances, but no one said a word.

Days passed, and the surface world remained embroiled in chaos. No one
truly knew what had happened or what was happening.

Everyone moved with blatant ignorance, keeping themselves busy with
their affairs. Like the hunting instinct of wild beasts, they were
fueled by self-interest and fought to maximize their benefits.

When news spread that the IMF had mysteriously vanished, a couple of
factions acted. They took control of the military and toppled regimes.

Just as they attempted to wrestle for more power, every screen
flickered.

Every signal was hijacked.

All of them showed the same feed.

A woman was on her knees with her head bowed low. She was covered in
wounds, looking mangled and bloodied. Clearly, she was subjected to
brutal torture.

Just as the world was trying to guess the identity of the woman, a hand
seized her by the hair and yanked her head up. The woman was 315, Anna.

The woman who had led their resistance against the IMF had ended up
getting captured by the IMF.

"No way! I heard that her faction was expanding rapidly, and they even
managed to raid the IMF HQ and escape unscathed. How could she get
caught?"

"That must be a fake! I've interacted with her! The real 315 is so
powerful. The besieged IMF couldn't possibly defeat her!"

"Oh God... if even she can't win against the IMF, then I..."

"No! I must go rescue her. At such a critical moment, nothing must
happen to her!"

However, none of those thoughts mattered.

A deep, male voice suddenly crackled through the screen.

"Everyone, take a good look. This is your 315, the instigator of this
rebellion. The visionary leader of a bunch of fools."

A wide-bore handgun was then raised, and under the watchful gaze of
billions across the planet, the barrel pressed against Anna's temple.

Bang!

A gunshot echoed through every screen, and almost every single living
person in the world instinctively shot to their feet at the sight of it.

Her delicate head exploded like a shattered watermelon. The bullet tore
through her skull. Blood splattered. Her skull peeled off with a
sickening tug as the hand lifted what remained of her scalp for the
world to see.

The camera panned upward to show the grotesque mountain of corpses
behind Anna's body. They were the dead Fhtagnists.

Some viewers recognized them. These were once her loyal followers, but
now, they were all dead.

The same voice returned. "Look around you. Look at those who died in
this war. Is this truly what you want?

"You keep saying that the IMF is oppressing you, but have any of you
ever stopped to think about why we do what we do? Because this is our
mission! The IMF is the will of humanity. We exist to protect all of
mankind!

"Our home—this world we live in—is beautiful, but just as fragile. Just
this year alone, in your attempts to strike at us and purge your
so-called enemies, you've unleashed sixteen Anomalies of the highest
threat level, EtzhaChayi.

"Sixteen. And not to mention the nuclear weapons you've stolen from
national armories and detonated.

"If this continues, our world will be utterly destroyed! There are no
victors in war. We are all losers. Ask yourself, is this truly what you
want?"

Silence fell. Across continents, no one spoke a single word.

"We acknowledge that the IMF's past foreign strategies were too
aggressive. We admit our mistake and are willing to step back. We're
open to dialogue. But this chaos cannot go on!

"You know where to find us. If you agree, send a representative. If you
don't..."

The camera sharply cut back to Anna's mangled corpse. The speaker's
voice now dripped with uncompromising resolve.

"Then this will be your fate! Anyone who threatens the stability of
mankind will be treated as an enemy of the IMF!"

Chapter 1146 - School

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

The intercepted broadcast cut off abruptly. In the wake of the latest
information, the opposing factions made varying responses.

Some plotted to find the IMF for revenge. Others considered peace talks.
There were also a few who opted to wait and see. And some began to lie
low and prepare themselves for the darker season.

Unknowingly, their singular goal of defeating the IMF that gathered them
into one united front had fragmented and broken down in this instant.
Everyone now had differing agendas.

But regardless of their thoughts, Anna's death left a significant
impact. The chaotic world was slowly giving way to the return of order.

When the major forces—the Fifth Church, the Horizon Initiative,
Serpent's Hand, and Prometheus Lab—began to reach out to the IMF, the
remaining insurgents realized the futility of resistance.

Soon enough, the tide of chaos receded. The world went quiet, but not
without major scars.

However, that didn't pose any difficulty to the IMF. They had already
made contingency plans for situations of this magnitude.

It didn't take long before construction teams equipped with Anomalies
partnered with the major countries to rebuild the world's
infrastructure.

All nations attempted to bury the cruel history. All countries had
united for this singular goal, so nothing proved to be hard.

Within a year, law and order were restored. Peace returned, and borders
were reopened. The Internet was back again.

People filled the streets again in celebration that the horrible
"pandemic" was over. They also mourned those who had lost their lives
during the pandemic.

However, not everyone had forgotten. There were bound to be some who
slipped through the cracks. By accident or fate, their memory hadn't
been altered.

And one fine day, at 8 a.m., just when the world was waking up, a
strange new post found its way on an Internet forum.

I don't care anymore! I have to let the world see the truth that has
been buried. Do you even know what really happened in 2004?

Forget about searching it up. There's nothing on the net. They deleted
every single thing. That very day, they obtained new tech from the
corpse of that tentacle creature! They now have the tech to do
large-scale psychological suggestions and memory rewriting!

I know every one of them is a hero. They gave up their lives in exchange
for our valuable, peaceful times. I hope every one of you can take this
matter seriously and respect their sacrifices.

However, I also feel that the public has the right to know! The lower
rungs of society shouldn't be left blind and manipulated by the top!

The post then continued with a raw, unfiltered account of the war from
the perspective of a nameless IMF grunt.

Soon, comments poured in. They neither supported nor doubted the post's
content. Rather, the replies were laced with mockery and sarcasm.

Humble person: You got it wrong. Humans discovered higher intelligence
creatures on Centaurus ω. This is our first contact with an outer space
race that surpassed the intelligence and technology of homo sapiens.

I. M. Pixchu: yes, yes, yes. I've heard about it too. That year, the
Centaurus System and Solar System signed a mutual defense pact to
jointly resist the threat of the bullshitariens and their allies.

Kaylee Jackson: Damn it! All you bastards STFU! Did you forget the NDA
in 2004? How can you do this to the Organization?

KENRBIX: Wth. Are you guys that bored to be recycling such an old joke?
Are you a loser living in your mom's basement?"

I. M. Pixchu: @KENRBIX bro, shut your damned mouth. Who TF do you think
you are? Some chinless NPC noble from a French Revolution side quest?
When they chopped off Louis XVI's head, how the hell did they miss
yours???

With that, an intense battle of words erupted on the thread. Every
curious onlooker who clicked on the post was drawn to the comment
section. No one even remembered why they came to the thread to begin
with.

With her cheek propped up with an elbow, Anna lazily scanned the thread.

"The IMF's Disaster Control Department is certainly capable. In a world
drowning in entertainment, no one is concerned about the truth behind
some emerging hot news. This is much easier and straightforward than
just deleting the post."

Yes. Anna was still alive. The Anna who died live was merely a decoy.

After everything that happened and everything that Anna had shown them,
the IMF no longer harbored the hallucination that they could kill her.

Anna was an untouchable existence that they couldn't afford to mess
with.

With that in mind, both sides struck a deal. They made a secret pact
that only they would ever know.

"Big sis, everyone has forgotten about that war," Gao Zhiming's voice
sounded from behind her. Now, he stood the same height as Anna, and his
face revealed complicated emotions.

"Why? Are you unhappy about it?" Anna asked as she opened her inbox and
checked the congratulatory message from Olivia.

"Of course not. I'm happy. I just don't know what to do next." Confusion
clouded Gao Zhiming's face.

Having lived those turbulent times with Anna, he had already grown
accustomed to instability. Peace felt foreign to him.

Anna turned to look at Gao Zhiming. She raised a hand to gently caress
his cheek. In a soft, gentle whisper, she said, "It's time for you to do
what you should do. Go back to school."

"School?" Gao Zhiming found the word extremely unfamiliar. It had been
so long since he last heard this word.

Anna's delicate fingers intertwined with his as she pulled him a little
closer. A warm smile spread across her lips. "Yes, school. I'll go with
you."

Gao Zhiming's pupils shrank ever so slightly. He stared at the peerless
visage before him for a few seconds before he smiled and nodded firmly.

"Okay."

They returned to their home country and moved back to the same
neighborhood they once lived in.

"Class, we have two new students joining us today. Let's give them a
warm welcome!" the homeroom teacher announced.

Under her lead, the students clapped enthusiastically while regarding
the newcomers with curious eyes.

"Both of you, please introduce yourselves," the homeroom teacher
prompted. "You'll all be classmates from now on."

"I-I'm Gao Zhiming. Nice to meet all of you," Gao Zhiming greeted,
albeit nervously.

Sensing Gao Zhiming's anxiety from his side profile, Anna gently reached
out a hand and held his. She waited for him to relax before turning her
gaze to the rest of the class seated behind their desks.

"Hi everyone," Anna greeted, "My name's Zhao Jiajia."

Compared to Gao Zhiming's reserved demeanor, everyone present,
regardless of gender, was drawn to Anna in an instant.

The sole reason was that she was too strikingly pretty, so she didn't
look like any ordinary junior high school student.

The teacher slightly pushed her glasses at the bridge to adjust them
before saying," Alright, let's encourage each other in our days again
and aim to get into a good university. You two can sit in the third
row."

Class passed by in a flash, and soon, it was time to go home for lunch.

"How do you feel about school so far?" Anna asked as they walked home
together.

"I feel like... they are all kids. Their thoughts are so childish," Gao
Zhiming said, voicing out his first impression of his classmates.

Anna let out a soft chuckle and curled her index finger to flick Gao
Zhiming's forehead."They are kids. And do you think you're not? Don't
act like an adult, you little punk."

Gao Zhiming was stunned for a moment upon seeing the radiant smile on
Anna's visage. This was the first time he ever saw her smiling so
carefreely. In the past, her smile was always tinged with traces of
mockery and condescension.

He very much preferred the current Anna.

"Big Sis, the lessons are really hard. I can't understand a thing," Gao
Zhiming said, catching up to her.

For someone who had only been through a year at an elementary school,
suddenly jumping into junior high was too much of a stretch.

"It's okay, I'm right here. I'll teach you. There's plenty of time, we
can take it slow."

Hearing Anna's words of assurance, Gao Zhiming turned to look at her.
His eyes were filled with tender affection.

"Thank you."

"No need to thank me. What do you want for lunch? I'll cook when we get
home," Anna said as she skipped ahead with light steps and twirled on
the sidewalk as if she were dancing.

Gao Zhiming ran to catch up with Anna. "I want to eat mala chicken
drumsticks!"

"Okay! But you have to wash the dishes!"

Chapter 1147 - Confrontation

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

The peaceful and quiet life of Gao Zhiming and Zhao Jiajia began. School
days were far from exciting. Their usual routine was just travelling
between two points: school and home.

But in comparison to their previous chaotic lifestyle, this current life
was much better.

They went to school together, came home together, did their homework
together, and shared every meal together.

As the simple days passed, their bond grew stronger and closer.

Time at junior high flew by in the blink of an eye. With Anna's help,
Gao Zhiming barely passed the high school entrance exams and managed to
get into a high school.

"Here," Anna said, holding up a half-eaten bun to his mouth. "The
steamed buns here are pretty good."

Gao Zhiming received the bun and took a large bite. "It really is! Big
sis, let's eat out next time. I think breakfast outside tastes better."

"Hmm? Are you complaining that my cooking is bad now?" Anna asked with a
raised eyebrow.

Gao Zhiming flashed a mischievous grin before nodding and swiftly
running away.

"You little brat! You think you're all grown up now and have the guts to
rebel, huh?" Anna shouted. They laughed and bantered as they made their
way to the school gates.

Like any high school, there was bound to be a group of kids who thought
highly of themselves.

Near the entrance, a group of girls dressed caked in heavy makeup and
clad in colorful, skimpy outfits clustered around the boys with tattoos.
They tossed around smug words and occasionally erupted in cocky
laughter.

One couple was even making out in full view of everyone else, while
their friends cheered them on. They weren't the least bothered by the
crowd around them.

As Gao Zhiming watched their display of affection, his eyes shifted,
almost instinctively, toward Zhao Jiajia. As soon as her eyes met his,
however, his cheeks flushed red, and he quickly turned his head away.

When he was much younger, hugs and kisses between them came naturally.
But now that he had grown older, they hardly did those things anymore.

A mischievous glint darted through Zhao Jiajia's eyes. She tugged on Gao
Zhiming's sleeve and led him into a quiet alley beside the school.

"Big sis, what are we doing here?" Gao Zhiming's breathing quickened.

Zhao Jiajia tilted her head up to look at him. Gao Zhiming had grown up,
and his resemblance to Charles was becoming uncanny.

The only difference between him and Charles eight years later was the
lack of exhaustion on his youthful face and the hopeful glint in his
eyes.

Looking at the face before her, something in Zhao Jiajia stirred. She
suddenly had the urge to tease him. 𝓯𝓻𝒆𝙚𝒘𝓮𝙗𝓷𝒐𝓿𝙚𝒍.𝙘𝓸𝙢

The two lingered in the alley for a long while before they emerged. By
then, class had long started, but they weren't in a rush. Their fingers
were linked, and when their eyes met, their lips curved into a knowing
smile.

For ordinary students, high school was the most critical turning point
in life.

But for Gao Zhiming, this was the first time he tasted the sweetness of
love, and it was utterly intoxicating. He fell hard, swept away in its
current, and before he knew it, he was in too deep.

These were the happiest days of his life.

They didn't need to do anything. Just sitting by Anna's side and
watching the delicate curve of her side profile was enough to make his
heart brim with joy.

Soon, freshman year passed, and then the sophomore year ended. They were
finally in their crucial senior year, signaling the inevitable: it was
soon time for Gao Zhiming to depart for the Subterranean Sea.

Their lovey-dovey days were coming to an end.

Neither of them talked about it, but some things could not be ignored
forever.

As that day crept closer and closer, the pressure weighing down on Gao
Zhiming's chest grew heavier and heavier. It was so suffocating that he
almost couldn't breathe, and it wiped the smile from his face.

In the cosy living room, Anna and Gao Zhiming were having dinner. No
words were exchanged, and the only sound that filled the room came from
the television.

"What are you thinking about? You've been so quiet. I've heard good
reviews about that recently released movie. Want to go see it together?"
Anna asked in a gentle voice.

"Jiajia, what happened to those people who used to be with us?"

Gao Zhiming's question caught Anna off guard. She hadn't expected this
question at all.

"Most of them are dead," Anna answered calmly. "Jackal, Roy, Wang Sheng,
even Tobba... They're all dead. Even the survivors... Li Lu has
completely lost her mind."

"She went crazy?"

"Yeah. She went crazy. They don't know if she was affected by the
presence of a god or if it was the shock from losing her son, Tobba.

"Olivia tried to use her powers to heal Li Lu, but... judging from the
latest updates, it hasn't been very effective."

"Then what about Olivia?"

"Olivia? She's doing great. I've asked her to stay nearby and always be
on standby."

"On standby to alter my memories?" Gao Zhiming's words pierced through
the elephant in the room. The atmosphere thickened with tension.

"Jiajia... I don't want to go down there," Gao Zhiming said. This was
the first time he decided to defy her orders. "I don't want to go to the
Subterranean Sea! I don't want to go to that terrible, oppressive place!
I just want to live like a normal person!"

Zhao Jiajia reached out with a soft hand and grabbed his trembling
fingers. "Don't be afraid. I'll come along."

Gao Zhiming's breathing turned ragged, and emotions flickered across his
face.

"Jiajia, don't you get it?! I don't want to go down there. Neither do I
want you to go down there! It's such a dangerous place. I don't want you
to take the risk. Can't we just stay here and peacefully live out our
lives? Why do we have to go there?!"

Zhao Jiajia's expression faltered. She realized now that she had been
oversimplifying the situation. She had never considered Gao Zhiming's
feelings over this matter.

Gao Zhiming suddenly threw his arms around her, trembling as he held her
tight.

"I don't want to end up as the madman that you spoke of. I don't want
our memories to be erased. Also..." Gao Zhiming's voice trembled and
cracked. "Jiajia, I love you. I don't want to leave you."

Zhao Jiajia lowered her head and nestled in against the curve of his
neck.

In a soft voice, she said, "Eight years. In the eighth year after going
down there, you'll meet me again. We'll have an adorable and beautiful
child. Her name is Sparkle.

"If it were just the two of us, then nothing else would matter. Screw
the time paradoxes. However, this doesn't concern only us.

"We have to consider Sparkle. Because she is in danger in the
Subterranean Sea. We have to go back for her. She is our responsibility,
our love."

A tremble coursed through Gao Zhiming. He parted his lips, wanting to
say that he didn't give a damn about a daughter he had never even met.
However, the words wouldn't leave his mouth.

After a long silence, he slowly let go of Zhao Jiajia and took another
look at the girl he loved.

Her arms were pale and soft like lotus roots. Her legs were smooth and
slender. Accompanied by her serene presence, she sat there like a
painting rendered in elegance and light.

Gao Zhiming leaned in again and took her lips. He kissed her softly at
first before deepening it.

He let the love swelling in his heart overflow, and he expressed it with
every touch, every breath. That night, he gave her all of himself—his
tenderness, his longing, and a deep love he could no longer hold back.

When it was all over, Gao Zhiming lay beside her on the bed. With his
arms wrapped around Zhao Jiajia's silky, porcelain skin, he planted a
tender kiss on her smooth forehead and listened to her narrate stories
of what was to come in the Subterranean Sea.

"Jiajia, I'm willing to have my memories erased. I can also go to that
hellish place. But can you promise me one thing?"

"What is it?"

"The Subterranean Sea is too dangerous. Don't come along with me. As for
Sparkle, don't worry. I'll protect her with everything I have. I swear
upon it."

At that moment, Gao Zhiming suddenly felt some sort of resonance with
his future self. He finally understood why his future self had sent Zhao
Jiajia to the surface world.

It was all because the love his future self had for Anna was no less
than the love he currently felt toward her.

Traces of conflict clouded Zhao Jiajia's gaze. "I... I need to think
about it. I need to think it through..."

Chapter 1148 - Deal

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

For their final two months together, the two decided to stop going to
school and go on a honeymoon. They visited the most beautiful places on
the surface world and ate the most delicious food available out there.

They even did thrilling activities such as bungee jumping and skydiving.
Gao Zhiming did his best to experience everything that life had to
offer.

Unfortunately, good days were often fleeting, and even though they were
truly reluctant to part, it was about time for them to set sail.

"Bleeeck~" Zhao Jiajia retched, hunching over the sink. After a while,
she smiled bitterly and placed a hand on her flat stomach.

Just then, the door was opened with a click, and Olivia bound to wear a
pair of sunglasses forever quietly walked into the bathroom.

"High Priestess, the ship is ready, and we can leave any time."

Anna nodded gently and sat back at the vanity table to do her makeup.
Today, Gao Zhiming was going to fall into the Subterranean Sea, so Anna
wanted to look more beautiful than usual.

When she emerged from the bathroom, she saw that Gao Zhiming was already
dressed, and he appeared calmer than Zhao Jiajia had imagined.

At first, Gao Zhiming was unsure how he would face this inevitable day,
but now that it had finally arrived, he realized that he was calmer than
he thought.

Zhao Jiajia stared at Gao Zhiming with complex emotions churning in her
eyes.

"Okay, I promise that I won't go down, but you must also promise me one
thing. You must protect our daughter and bring her back safely, even if
you end up losing your memories!" she declared.

Gao Zhiming smiled and nodded firmly. "Mmhm!"

Zhao Jiajia walked up to Gao Zhiming and stared straight into his eyes.
"Think before you answer! Think about the weight behind this promise!"

"I've thought about it, and I still promise you! Even if it costs me
everything, I will protect our Sparkle," Gao Zhiming declared and pulled
Zhao Jiajia into his embrace.

The two left the neighborhood and got into the car waiting for them.

They headed straight for the port.

While the car was moving through the streets, Gao Zhiming suddenly asked
the driver to stop. He pushed open the door and pulled Zhao Jiajia
toward a breakfast stand by the roadside.

"Tobba was right. You can't do anything without eating breakfast. Let's
go and eat something."

While the two were busy eating Gao Zhiming's final meal on the surface
world, their car on the road blocked the traffic on the streets,
eliciting honks from the annoyed drivers behind them. The piercing honks
roused a young man on the second floor of a nearby building.

Having pulled an all-nighter, the furious young man shoved the window
open and cursed at the drivers.

Swoosh!

A gust of wind rushed out of the young man's room through the window and
took to the skies.

Thanks to the electric fan, the gust of wind was much stronger than
usual, and after going through many hardships, this particular gust of
wind eventually merged with a storm over the Atlantic two months later.

The storm was massive. It was so massive that a storm of this scale was
usually seen only once in a century.

The humongous storm changed directions as it crossed the Atlantic.

Storms were always impactful, but a storm was just that—a storm.
However, this once-in-a-century storm was more impactful than any other,
as it drew Charles' attention to it as well as to the intricacies behind
it.

In Charles' eyes, the storm was the tip of a line. He followed it
backward and saw that its other end was connected to 002. The omniscient
Charles instantly understood everything upon seeing 002.

He realized that Tobba was 002's Chosen One, and he had been doing
everything to save 002.

Upon comprehending that, Gao Zhiming tore off all his tentacles and flew
toward 002. Once his tentacles were connected to 002, the latter's empty
shell, which was about to be devoured by Fhtagn, came under his control.

Having lost all of its tentacles, Charles' body began moving on its own,
and clusters of interconnected bubbles shining radiantly beyond the
limits of human sensory perception emerged all over it.

More and more interconnected bubbles appeared, and as they stacked upon
each other, they eventually transformed into an indescribable, endless
spacetime continuum composed of nothing but itself.

Everything was within it, and It was within everything.

The exchange was complete, and 002 was resurrected in Charles' body.
Upon Its resurrection, 002 immediately "pulled" Charles beyond Fhtagn's
influence before Fhtagn could devour him.

Fhtagn's control over his divine law was weakened by 002's escape, and
Charles also obtained a new ally. The extreme shift in balance meant
that the fight was over.

002 and Charles quickly divided Fhtagn's divine law—the law that gave
Fhtagn control over life and death.

Unlike Charles and 002, Fhtagn was born a god, so despite losing his
divine law, He didn't disappear. Moreover, He was indestructible, so the
only way to silence Him was to make Him sleep forever.

002 briefly cast Its will on Charles, bestowing upon the latter a
portion of Its law.

Charles began to transform upon taking in the divine laws of Fhtagn and
002. The tentacles all over Him became ethereal and interconnected, but
each tentacle was its own unique, independent existence.

Upon "seeing" that, 002 got ready to leave. Its body grew larger and
larger until it engulfed the entire world.

Once 002 was no longer around, Charles realized that, even though this
place was still devoid of space, it already possessed time. In the very
distant future, this place devoid of space would witness the birth of
many things.

Charles could see this place's future, and he saw that it was no longer
destined to be deathly silent. It was changing and would keep changing
in the future.

As for 002's exact identity, Its origin, and the reason behind Its
conflict with Fhtagn, Charles didn't care at all.

The crisis was over, and according to the previous plan, He had to
reverse the Subterranean Sea's time and make every part of Fhtagn across
all the timelines fall into a permanent slumber.

And that was when he noticed one thing—why would he do something like
that when there was absolutely no need for him to do that?

Why must I think? Why must I have a name? Charles? No, I'm not Charles.
Charles had died a long time ago. Everything has become completely
meaningless to me.

With that, Charles immediately moved according to his thoughts. His
thoughts and his will started to dissipate, while his tentacles started
linking head to tail, regressing to their most primordial state.

Just then, he noticed something summoning one of his fingers, Edikth.

The disturbance was significant, and it affected all of his tentacles at
the same time. This kind of summoning couldn't be performed by mere
human beings. As soon as he thought that way, his will had already made
a move.

Beneath an upside-down ocean, he found the summoner of his fingers—a
woman wrapped in black bandages.

005's bandages split open as she took out things that once belonged to
Charles.

A piece of Charles' arm, a chunk of his stomach, eye, heart, ear, a jar
full of organ specimens, and finally, his skull and scalp. These things
were what Charles had traded in exchange for 005's wish balls.

In addition to these tangible objects, Charles had traded other
things—his despair upon seeing Dawn One, his love for Anna, his
unforgettable yearning for his family on the surface world, and his
memories with Lily.

The omniscient Charles instantly realized 005's goal. "Why should I make
a deal with you and exchange my experience of becoming a god for those
things? They are all meaningless to me."

"Are they really meaningless? Your daughter had sacrificed a lot for you
over the years. I implore you to not betray her sacrifice and your past
self," 005 said. She then took out a wish ball that was emitting a green
light.

Charles' will froze upon seeing the green ball. Then, his gaze traversed
through many years before eventually falling on the deal between Sparkle
and 005 a long time ago.

Sparkle had refused to give up, and before her inevitable demise, she
was still busy making plans for him instead of enjoying her remaining
time.

The objects around 005 floated over to Charles. As soon as he made
contact with them, he was enlivened. He could feel and harbor emotions
once again; he was Charles once more.

However, these emotions were fragile and fleeting. He had to hurry.
Charles immediately stripped away the dissipating humanity within him
and merged it with what 005 had given to him, creating two brand-new
versions of himself.

This wasn't enough, but Charles instantly knew what was missing. He
extracted everything belonging to Charles inside him—Charles' humanity,
memories, emotions, and everything belonging to him.

Then, he stuffed them all into the two Charleses before him. Afterward,
He placed the two Charleses harboring their own unique emotions into the
Subterranean Sea and the surface world.

The traces of His humanity faded rapidly with the disappearance of the
two Charleses. Despite that, he remained calm as he looked at 005.

"Thank you," he said and transmitted the information that 005 needed
from him.

005 remained silent all throughout. Upon receiving the goods, 005 said
nothing and simply disappeared.

This was merely a deal, after all, nothing more, nothing less.

Charles stayed quiet amidst the nothingness for quite a while.

Then, he began thinking about what to do with the final vestiges of
humanity within him. Strictly speaking, this wasn't His humanity, as he
had already stripped away everything that belonged to Charles within
him.

In other words, He was no longer Charles. He had become an empty shell
containing absolute divinity.

Amidst his fading humanity, He cast His gaze on the two Charleses that
he had thrown into his tentacles.

Chapter 1149 - Charles (End)

❈──────•◈◈◈•──────❈

An eldritch island made out of flesh and blood broke through the cold
surface of the Subterranean Sea. The island was none other than Charles.
He could now reverse the time of the entire Subterranean Sea.

His gaze pierced the perpetual darkness of the Subterranean Sea and
landed on Hope Island. In an instant, he saw the faces of his crew, who
had overcome many crises with him.

Dipp, Linda, Bandages, Audric, James...

Their faces reminded Charles of the past, and he couldn't help but smile
warmly as he reminisced about those days. Of course, they weren't his
only crew members. Some of his crew were in other places, some were
dead, and some were still with him.

Charles willed it, and his fleshly body split open. A sun slowly rose
from within him.

"Mr. Charles."

"Lily." Charles' hundreds and thousands of eyes looked at her. "You
should go. Find your own path out there. I won't be able to stay with
you any longer."

The massive sun orbited around the flesh island, and Lily's ethereal
voice echoed in his ear. "I'm not going anywhere. I'll go wherever you
go!"

Upon hearing that, the memories that Charles had just recovered popped
up in his mind, and he remembered having once fulfilled Lily's wishes.
Charles remembered the joyful white mouse that wouldn't stop jumping
around him.

"All right, let's go together, then. I'm going to keep protecting you.
Now and in the future," Charles remarked. Five tentacles made out of his
flesh burst out of his body and reached out toward Lily as if they were
fingers of a palm.

"Mmhm!" Lily responded in delight and chuckled to herself. She nuzzled
up against the tentacles and landed heavily on the flesh island.

Lily was engulfed by Charles' flesh once again as the latter began to
ascend. He rose slowly until he reached the Core through that colossal
hole in the dome overhead.

Having been freed from Fhtagn's grip, 002 was no longer around the Core,
and as Charles ascended, he eventually settled in 002's previous
location.

"Charles. Position: Captain. Duty: Responsible for the safe
transportation and administrative management of the vessel. Ensures the
utmost safety of the ship, the crew's lives, and property. Decisively
and prudently handle all affairs in case of emergencies! Narwhale, set
sail!!"

The Narwhale docked at Hope Island seemed to have sensed something just
then, and its steam whistle tore through the air. 𝒇𝒓𝒆𝒆𝙬𝒆𝒃𝓷𝒐𝓿𝙚𝙡.𝒄𝓸𝒎

HOOONNKK!

At the Narwhale's piercing cry, the Subterranean Sea became a ship that
started sailing toward the distant past under Captain Charles' lead.

***

At the same time, a cruise ship was sailing across the ocean somewhere
on the surface world. The ship's deck was full of students wearing
various uniforms, and the majority of them were busy taking photos of
the scenery around them.

Thanks to Olivia's special ability, these people couldn't see Anna and
Gao Zhiming.

Gao Zhiming's fall into the Subterranean Sea was nigh; the moment of
parting was fast approaching. Gao Zhiming gripped his phone tightly,
took a deep breath, and walked toward the ship's railings.

Anna clenched and relaxed her hands repeatedly as she tried her best to
ensure that her tumultuous emotions wouldn't seep out on her face. She
cupped her hands around her mouth, and her lips trembled as she stared
intently at Gao Zhiming's back, shouting, "Remember! Zhao Jiajia will
always love Gao Zhiming[1]! And promise me! You must come back here!"

Gao Zhiming turned around and grinned. Then, he nodded firmly and
exclaimed, "Mmhm! I promise you, Jiajia! Olivia, let's do it."

Olivia pointed at her temple, and all of Anna's memories about Charles
on the surface world were extracted from her mind. She then replaced Gao
Zhiming's memories with the memories she had just extracted from Anna.

Gao Zhiming's passionate eyes and smile gradually disappeared. When the
alteration was done, Gao Zhiming became a teenager on a sightseeing
tour. The azure ocean instantly captured the attention of the naive
young woman.

"Wow! The scenery here is amazing! No, wait! I've got to take some
photos here. I have to make Gao Suling feel jealous!" Gao Zhiming held
up his phone and frantically took photos of the beautiful scenery.

He approached the railings to take better pictures, but the massive
cruise ship suddenly lurched forward, and Gao Zhiming lost his balance.

Gao Zhiming screamed as he fell into the sea, but before he could hit
the water, a pitch-black hole somehow manifested at the right time as if
it was a part of a script.

Zhao Jiajia rushed to the ship's railings to check on Gao Zhiming, but
she saw nothing except for the frothy tops of the sea waves. Clearly,
Gao Zhiming had fallen into the Subterranean Sea.

Anna's grip on the railings loosened, and she slumped to the floor with
her back against the railings. She covered her face and cried hard. She
had been trying her best to suppress her emotions, but she could no
longer contain them.

As a strong and steady woman her entire life, Anna was never the type to
lose her composure, but for the first time ever, she lost control of her
feelings as if she were just an ordinary human being.

"Jiajia." A familiar voice echoed, and Zhao Jiajia's heart trembled. She
looked up and saw a bizarre face.

It was a face covered in old scars. The figure was clad in a time-worn
Captain's uniform, and he was also missing his right arm, right eye, and
right ear.

The figure was none other than Charles, the Charles from the
Subterranean Sea. A never-before-seen gentle smile tugged at Charles'
lips. He walked over, knelt on one knee, and used his remaining arm to
embrace his wife.

"I kept my word. See? I'm back."

Zhao Jiajia finally came to her senses. She wasn't dreaming; this was
real life!

Anna grabbed Charles and bit down hard on his neck. She tore at it as if
she were mad, but the familiar taste of Charles' blood told her one
thing. The figure before her was indeed Charles, and he had returned to
keep his promise.

"Let's go... Let's go home." Charles wrapped his arm around Anna's
slender waist and lifted her gently.

"Wait, give me a moment. I'm feeling a bit dizzy." Zhao Jiajia lay in
Charles' arms and closed her eyes, kneading her temples with her hand.

Charles waited patiently for her to recover. He had plenty of time now,
and he could afford to wait even an eternity for her. After a long
while, Anna looked up, and the two smiled at each other.

Charles lowered his head to kiss her, but a soft palm blocked his lips.

"Wait, where's Sparkle?" Zhao Jiajia asked, and as soon as the words
left her lips, her eyes widened in disbelief. A violent tremor ran
through her, and her eyes darted around. She looked everywhere, but she
couldn't see a girl with fluorescent green, cross-shaped pupils.

"Answer me!!" Anna exclaimed. She grabbed Charles' collar and tugged
hard. "Where's Sparkle?! Where's our daughter?!"

"Shhh, keep your voice down. Be careful not to disturb our Sparkle," Gao
Zhiming said, moving his calloused left hand to Zhao Jiajia's flat
abdomen.

Zhao Jiajia's trembling hands settled on Gao Zhiming's rough hand.
Together, they closed their eyes, focusing on the pulse of the tiny life
underneath as the wedding ring on their finger reflected each other's
gleam.

***

"Wheeew, why is it so cold? Aren't we in summer?" Gao Zhiming muttered,
shaking his head to shake the dizziness away. His movements shook the
wooden boat beneath him, eliciting splashes of water from somewhere.

"W-wait... where am I?" Gao Zhiming looked around and saw an oil lamp at
the bow. The light from the oil lamp told him that he was no longer on a
massive cruise ship. Somehow, he was on a wooden boat roughly six meters
long.

Except for the two oars on the side, there was nothing on the boat.

Gao Zhiming gazed around in disbelief in an effort to discern his
whereabouts, but to his disappointment, the pitch-black surroundings
blended perfectly with the dark waters around him, providing him with
nothing to observe whatsoever.

The instinctive fear of the dark inside him made him move closer to the
one and only light source, a swaying oil lamp.

I've got to call for help! Gao Zhiming thought. He reached for his phone
in his pocket, but he saw that there was no signal at all.

"Where the hell am I?!" Gao Zhiming cried out, looking to be on the
verge of tears. He was just on a simple sightseeing tour, so how did
something like this happen to him?

Just then, a gust of wind blew across the sea. The fishy and salty smell
in the wind reminded Gao Zhiming of something. He dipped his finger into
the ink-black water and tasted it. A bitter and salty flavor instantly
pervaded his mouth.

This is seawater! I'm still out at sea? Gao Zhiming began to panic. If
he were on a lake, he would be able to row his way to shore, but since
he was out at sea, how could he row his way out when he had no idea
where he was in the first place?

It's fine. I'll just wait until morning. Gao Zhiming suppressed the fear
in his heart and decided to wait it out.

Time went by just like that, and Gao Zhiming eventually noticed
something amiss.

There's something wrong here. It's been so many hours, so how come there
hasn't been any light at all? He looked down at his phone and saw that
it was already in power-saving mode.

Just then, he realized something and looked up. There weren't any stars
in the night sky. Instead, he saw an unknown and seemingly endless
darkness.

"Where the hell am I?! Where the fuck are the stars?! Hey, is anyone out
there?! Heeelp!!" Gao Zhiming let out a heartwrenching cry of despair at
the pitch-black darkness around him.

Unfortunately, it was clear that there was absolutely no one around
here, and the realization made him fall into despair.

Gao Zhiming deduced that he wasn't on any ocean on Earth, or perhaps he
was probably not even on Earth in the first place. After all, if he were
on Earth, he would definitely see stars in the sky during nighttime and
the sun during daytime.

Gao Zhiming went hysterical and screamed nonstop until he collapsed to
the floor, gasping for breath.

His eyes filled with pain, he looked at the darkness overhead with
extreme confusion. But as the confusion in his eyes gradually
disappeared, he pulled himself together again.

No, I have to go back. I can't die here. It doesn't matter where I am...
I have to go back because... Gao Zhiming trailed off and stayed silent
for a long time.

The gleam in his eyes shifted erratically, sometimes confused, sometimes
determined. Then, at one time, he looked as if he had remembered
something, but he ultimately couldn't recall anything.

After a while, he decisively declared, "Everything I have is back
there... my family is there, so I must... go back there!!"

Gao Zhiming's eyes blazed with determination. He grabbed the nearby oar
and gnashed his teeth before desperately rowing in a chosen direction
amidst the perpetual darkness around him.

Shrouded Seascape

-The End-
